# 201: Chapter 198: I Like Koraidon—and Everyone Else (4k)

Year 198 of League Calendar, July 25th, Autumn.

As time went by, the temperature in the air gradually dropped.

People took off their thin short sleeves and put on warm long-sleeve jackets.

D was no exception. As a farmer, because he often needed to work in the fields, most of the clothes he bought were sturdy overalls and jeans.

"Achoo!"

Early in the morning, D was about to go out to milk the cows when he suddenly felt a cold wind blow through and couldn't help but sneeze.

The sudden drop in temperature was something he wasn't quite used to yet.

The Pokémon in the ranch were doing much better.

Koraidon and Dragonite were both thick-skinned, Pawmot was covered in a layer of thick fur, and as for Glaceon, it wasn't afraid of the cold at all.

Only Cyclizar seemed a bit unaccustomed.

It stood next to D, and the moment after D sneezed, it also sneezed, looking quite listless.

"Caught a cold?"

D touched its forehead, saw it wasn't running a fever, and gave Cyclizar a day off, telling it to stay indoors and avoid the wind.

Cyclizar could only sit listlessly on the sofa.

Sister Indeedee thoughtfully brought it a cup of hot tea to warm it up.

D sniffed, then walked out of the house, greeted Dragonite and Dragonair who came to work, and then moved the bucket and milker with Koraidon to the Gogoat pen.

Without needing a wave, the three Gogoat sidled over and then knelt down to make it easier for D to milk.

During the process, D took the opportunity to check on the mutated World Tree.

Compared to half a month ago, this sapling hadn't changed a bit. If he couldn't see the days to its maturity decreasing, he might have thought it had stopped growing.

Even with the Gogoat constantly helping to take care of it, the date on which this World Tree would mature remained unwaveringly fixed above 99,900 years.

"Phew! Done!"

The milk bucket was filled, and D gently patted the three Gogoat on the side. They retreated and lazily lay on the grass.

D placed the milk bucket properly, then moved the MooMoo Milk milked by Miltank to here, placing it together with the goat milk.

By the time everything was finished, it was already 9:30 in the morning.

Remembering the uncomfortable Cyclizar, D returned to the living room and asked, "Feeling any better now?"

If not, he would take Cyclizar to the Pokémon Center for a check-up.

"Grawrr..." Cyclizar still looked very weak.

D touched its forehead and found that its temperature was scalding hot, reaching quite an alarming level.

D's brow immediately furrowed.

He turned and went to the bedroom to fetch Cyclizar's Poke Ball, putting it inside.

The Pokémon in the ranch were alerted and seemed very worried.

Even Glaceon's usually cool and calm face was filled with concern.

"Looks like Cyclizar has a good relationship with everyone!" D raised an eyebrow.

Before, when he was working, the Pokémon would play on their own, and he only joined them for games like flying disc and ball when he had time.

In their usual interactions, he wouldn't interfere.

After thinking about it, D said to the concerned Pokémon:

"Don't worry, it's just a fever. I'll take it to the Pokémon Center, and it will be back soon."

When he said this, a flash of emotion crossed Koraidon's face.

It had never realized before how worried the Pokémon on the ranch were about it.

But it was understandable.

After all, when it was still Cyclizar, and when this happened, it had already been put in the Poke Ball and was groggily unaware of what was going on outside.

And afterwards, these guys wouldn't show it, but would instead teasingly play with it.

This time, from a different perspective, Koraidon saw something it had never noticed before.

"Let's go!"

D's voice called Koraidon back from its emotional moment.

It immediately transformed into a sprinting mode, carrying D and its former self towards Porto Marinada Town.

Koraidon's speed was fast, and in just a few minutes they arrived at the Pokémon Center in Porto Marinada Town.

There weren't many people here, and upon hearing D mention the Pokémon's fever, Miss Joy immediately put on a serious face and with assistant Audino pushed Cyclizar into a room.

This scene was very much like pushing a critically ill patient into an operating room.

D inexplicably felt a bit nervous.

Beside him, Koraidon looked at him and then asked, "Gaa-sis?"

D was slightly taken aback and turned to ask, "What's up, Koraidon?"

Koraidon looked at the treatment room where treatment was happening: "Gaa-sis..."

D roughly understood: "Are you also worried about Cyclizar?"

Koraidon: "..."

Thinking of Koraidon's usual habit of being jealous of Cyclizar's attention, D couldn't help but chuckle and said:

"Who would have thought you two have quite a good relationship!"

Koraidon didn't actually mean that; it wanted to ask if D was very worried about Cyclizar.

Suddenly, it really wanted to know what it meant to D back when it was still a Cyclizar.

In the time after, many Pokémon came to the ranch. At first, as a Cyclizar, it was a bit foolish and naive.

Later, as it slowly grew up, it gradually came to understand many things.

D had limited energy, and there were so many Pokémon on the ranch.

---

On the surface, D seems to treat every Pokemon well and likes them.

Koraidon worries that compared to itself, D might like other Pokemon more.

Fluffy Pawmo, the caring Indeedee Sibling, the soft and cute Dragonite who knows how to Charm, the silly Psyduck, the loyal and reliable Garganacl...

Compared to them, it seems to be good for nothing other than farm work.

Koraidon's heart felt a bit gloomy.

At that moment, it heard D beside it say in a comforting tone:

"Actually, don't be deceived by how calm I look now, I was quite startled when I first saw Cyclizar like that!"

Koraidon looked over: "Argh?"

D laughed: "Cyclizar is still young; it was the first Pokemon I ever encountered. For humans, the first Pokemon is always special."

"It's sensible, reliable, and has been helping me with the work. To me, it's like my own child."

Saying this, D recalled the events of the past few months with Cyclizar:

"At the beginning, Cyclizar knew nothing, whether it was plowing fields, planting, or transporting things, I taught it slowly, just like humans raising a child. Barring any surprises, we'll live together for a long time."

"The only regret I feel is not being able to witness Cyclizar hatch with my own eyes."

D felt quite regretful, and then thought of the worried expressions on the Pokemon in the ranch just now:

"If one day Cyclizar isn't around, both I and Glaceon and the others will be very sad."

Saying this, D had thought Koraidon would be angry or jealous.

But when he turned around, he saw Koraidon with tearful eyes, as if about to cry at any moment.

D: "???"

Koraidon, learning from Dragonite, came up for a hug with visible grievance.

It thought to itself: "Seeing you all so worried about me, the wait over the years was worth it."

D thought Koraidon cried from grievance because he mentioned liking Cyclizar more.

He felt amused, yet still patted its back: "I like Koraidon too, along with everyone else."

Koraidon murmured: "Argh..."

What Koraidon? It had always been Cyclizar...

At that moment, the door to the treatment room opened.

Miss Joy walked out: "Luckily, it was brought in timely; it was likely a chill caused by the sudden temperature drop. Don't worry, the fever has gone down now."

"Thank you."

D sighed in relief, and under Miss Joy's guidance, walked into the ward, looking at the sleeping Cyclizar on the bed.

"When you get back, you need to pay more attention, preferably not letting Cyclizar battle in the next two days, keep it warm. Cyclizar is a temperature-sensitive Pokemon that fears the cold," Miss Joy advised from the side.

D nodded: "I understand. Thank you very much, Miss Joy."

Miss Joy gave a soft smile: "No need to thank me; it's our duty."

Another trainer came to the Pokemon Center for treatment, and after giving some instructions, Miss Joy left the ward to attend to the visiting trainer.

At this point, Cyclizar woke up.

D hurriedly went forward: "Awake? How are you feeling?"

"Gao..."

Cyclizar seemed a bit embarrassed.

D laughed: "Alright, it's time for us to go home!"

He took out a Poke Ball, recalled Cyclizar into it, and after paying Miss Joy the treatment fee, left the Pokemon Center.

When Koraidon arrived at the ranch, D saw Keri's big truck at the entrance.

Keri was playing with a timid Dragonair by the farm.

Seeing D return, Keri stood up and said: "Went to town?"

D replied affirmatively: "Cyclizar had a fever, I took it to the Pokemon Center for a check-up."

Keri: "Is it alright now?"

"The fever's gone." Having said that, D handed the Poke Ball to the sister Indeedee, letting her take Cyclizar inside to rest, and reminded: "Remember to close the doors and windows, don't let the wind blow in."

"Ai." The sister Indeedee nodded gently, indicating she understood.

Seeing that Cyclizar was fine, Keri joked: "You! Isn't Cyclizar less than a year old? You've been letting it follow you around working in the field every day, and now it's worn out and got sick, right?"

D: "..."

Upon careful consideration, he realized he had indeed been somewhat overworking a young Pokemon, so he earnestly replied: "Thanks for the reminder, I'll pay more attention in the future."

Keri, knowing he cares about Cyclizar too, didn't dwell on it and instead switched the topic to business:

"You see, the batch of glass materials I mentioned before finally arrived, I just happen to have time today. Are you sure you want to build a Glass Greenhouse?"

"Yes, I'm sure." D nodded. Over time, through selling cow's milk, goat's milk, and Rock Salt, he had saved up a considerable amount of money.

Though he still couldn't build a large greenhouse, a Glass Greenhouse was feasible.

Seeing his nod, Keri said: "Alright, have you chosen the location? If so, I'll start the construction!"

D had already made plans in his mind. He led Keri to the southwest corner of the ranch, right opposite the entrance of the Berry Garden: "Here is good!"

Keri immediately set flags in the area.

Turning around, she saw in the Berry Garden, rows upon rows of trees already laden with various kinds of unripe Berries.

---

"Looks like your orchard's harvest is pretty good this year!"

D: "It's a pity the berries are so cheap. The entire orchard's berries aren't worth more than a few barrels of my milk."

"Then why not make them into Poffins and sell them at your Pokemon Tavern!" Keri had just seen a group of Dragonair happily eating Poffins.

These days, her Machamp comes to the tavern for a drink almost every day.

As Machamp's Trainer, she felt a bit guilty for letting Machamp freeload off D, so afterwards, she always gave a few League coins to the two Machamps to hand over to the tavern owner.

"That's a good idea too." D thought about it, but in the end, he didn't go for it.

Because Poffins are Dragonite's salary, and the amount he and his sister Indeedee make in a day is limited, selling them at the tavern wouldn't cover Dragonite's wages.

Anyway, Keri was just making a suggestion.

After confirming the location for the Glass Greenhouse, she moved a bunch of construction materials there and started the work.

Seeing that he wasn't needed there, D turned around and went back to the living room.

In the living room, Cyclizar was wrapped in a blanket, looking very warm.

And next to it was a large group of concerned Pokemon.

Alcremie, Pawmot, Dragonite, Glaceon...

Cyclizar looked bewildered. It didn't know what was happening, and it felt quite embarrassed under everyone's gaze.

Seeing D coming over, Cyclizar immediately looked at him with pleading eyes.

Seeing this, D laughed, "Alright, Cyclizar is fine. Don't crowd around it; let it rest for a bit!"

The group of Pokemon obediently backed off.

D checked Cyclizar's condition and, seeing it hadn't been feverish again, finally relaxed.

But in the next moment, he heard a sneeze from Alcremie.

D: "..."

"You can't ride Naclstack around the ranch these days! You're swallowing cold wind into your belly!" D admonished, "If you want to play, go to the Underground Cave where there's no wind."

"M'ma~" Alcremie stuck out its tongue, a bit embarrassed.

Then, it sneezed again.

D couldn't help but facepalm.

He sighed and said to the smaller Pokemon:

"Just stay at home for the next couple of days, don't run around. Pawmot, you don't need to go to the chicken coop; I'll handle it."

Dragonite quickly asked, "Awwo awo?" What about it?

D looked at its huge frame and chubby body, "…You wouldn't get sick even if you slept outside, right?"

For the past few days, Dragonite had been sleeping on the grass outside.

But Dragonite didn't like hearing this.

It immediately started chirping, insisting that it could indeed get sick!

D: "..."

Alright!

He knew Dragonite just wanted some attention, so he went along with it, "Dragonite, take good care of yourself too, don't get sick later."

Only then was Dragonite satisfied, promising to be obedient.

Koraidon: "…" Too childish.

Next in line was Glaceon.

Looking at the Ice Type Glaceon, D fell silent.

"Glaceon, you shouldn't be afraid of the cold, right?"

"Bu-y…"

"Alright, you can play inside too, no Pokemon will fight you for the Ice Stone over there."

With that, D turned to the two Indeedees:

"I'll leave them to you. You can let them watch TV or play hide and seek; I'll go see if Dolliv wants to come over."

Saying this, D went out to ask Dolliv.

But Dolliv gently shook its head to refuse, clearly happy with D's concern, and then said that it sleeps beside Gogoat every night.

Gogoat is fluffy, with thick wool that blocks the wind, making it very cozy.

"Alright then."

D felt a bit frustrated. Smoliv used to be so clingy, but after evolving, its personality changed.

This truly fits the saying—children grow up and become independent.

Even though Dolliv is a girl, and he's not its mother.

But no matter what, as long as Dolliv is happy.

D sighed.

He stood in the pasture, listening to the sounds of construction in the distance, when an autumn breeze blew by. He shook his head and suddenly thought of ginger tea with brown sugar, which dispels cold and warms the body.

Maybe drinking this, the Pokemon on the ranch would feel much better.

There was no ginger at home; D rarely cooked, so he hadn't bought any.

But he remembered there was a place up in the back hills where wild ginger grew.

With a little thought, D put on a backpack and rode a Gogoat into the back hills.

---

---

---

---

# 202: Chapter 199: Brown Sugar Ginger Tea

The autumn back mountain is quite different from the spring and summer seasons.

The leaves on the trees are no longer lush green, showing a hint of yellow.

When the autumn wind blows, the dense canopy overhead rustles with sound, followed by the gently spiraling descent of the yellowing leaves.

A few wild Tarountula dangle upside down from the treetops with silk threads, scurrying back into the foliage and cautiously observing their surroundings when D walks by below.

D, however, doesn't pay them much mind.

He hops off his Gogoat, approaches a tall old tree, and picks a few mushrooms from among the fallen branches and leaves.

This must be the upside of autumn.

In this season of harvest, a variety of wild mushrooms sprout in the back mountain.

Whether dried and stored or used to brew a delicious broth, they are a very good choice.

In just an hour, D has harvested half a basket of wild mushrooms.

Some he recognizes, like boletes, russula, coral fungi, morels, yellow boletes, and weeping bolete.

Of course, there are some he doesn't recognize among them as well.

But thanks to his Golden Finger ability, D can still learn the names and details of these mushrooms.

Among them are quite a few containing toxic elements.

D originally wanted to throw away these mushrooms to prevent harm from eating them.

However, after looking closer at their information, he gave up on that idea.

[Red Mushroom: A special ingredient, carries toxic properties, causing Pokemon to fall into a Toxic state when consumed. Cooking with it may yield some unexpected effects.]

"Huh?" D is a bit surprised, "A special ingredient? Does it mean ingredients with toxins?"

This is the first time he's seen such an ingredient.

Looking at the description that suggests unexpected effects, curiosity rises inside D.

Why not... take it back and give it a try?

Even if they're toxic, they simply need not eat it.

However, based on previous cooking experiences, D thinks these toxic mushrooms might end up making a dish that boosts Poison Type Pokemon.

"Alright then, let's give it a try!"

D still decides to pack away these toxic mushrooms separately.

Then he rides his Gogoat for a little further exploration, finally finding the ginger he wanted by a moist riverside.

This ginger is very spicy, and aside from certain Pokemon, most aren't interested in it.

Therefore, this cluster of ginger remains almost perfectly intact.

D digs them all up from the ground, then packs them into the basket on his back.

After doing all this, he rides his Gogoat back to the ranch.

The wild mushrooms can wait; right now, the ginger needs handling first.

D opens the cupboard for an inspection and finds there's still some red sugar left, what was left over from an earlier dessert session with Alcremie.

Fortunately, he doesn't need to go to town to buy more.

With the autumn wind howling outside, D huddles in his warm home with a group of Pokemon, brewing red sugar ginger tea for them.

While preparing the ingredients, a bunch of Pokemon are playing in the living room.

All he has to do is look up, and he can see them.

The TV's sound mixed with the growls and howls of the Pokemon make the house lively.

From time to time, a few Pokemon look up in his direction.

D catches their gaze, then smiles softly and continues cooking.

The process of making red sugar ginger tea isn't complicated.

First, peel the ginger and slice it, then pour clean water into a pot, bring it to a boil, add red sugar and two red dates, then simmer for twenty minutes, finally add the sliced ginger and simmer for another five minutes.

The finished red sugar ginger tea has a bright color, and just from the rising steam, you can smell the blend of sweet and spicy scents.

D tries a sip first, and as he swallows, his whole body warms from the inside out.

"Amazing."

He looks down, and the information about the red sugar ginger tea appears simultaneously.

[Red Sugar Ginger Tea (A): A beverage made with red sugar and ginger, infused with the creator's heartfelt emotions which significantly boost its effects.

Cooking Effect: Warmth lv3, after drinking, Pokemon will dispel coldness and feel warmth in body and mind, increasing the efficiency of move practice for 12 hours.

Review: Your best friend for banishing cold and keeping warm, a top choice for chilly winter days!]

Hmm?

Is this... the second emotion-infused dish?

D was a bit surprised, recalling the quiet and serene feeling he had when making the red sugar ginger tea.

A peaceful, soothing atmosphere that made him feel relaxed and warm deep inside.

Thinking about this, D gives a knowing smile.

In the past, after creating a permanently lucky Lucky Pudding for Teddiursa, he tried to cook intentionally tapping into his emotions.

But he failed every time because he was too deliberate.

Then he realized this state comes unexpectedly, best left to chance — encountering it was a pleasant surprise in life, and if not, he wouldn't be disheartened.

"It's ready, everyone come on over!"

D shakes his head, putting his mixed feelings aside, then pours the brewed red sugar ginger tea into bowls, lining them up on the stove in front of him, and calls out towards the living room.

Soon, a group of Pokemon obediently line up to drink tea.

In their minds, every dish D makes is undeniably delicious.

Therefore, without D having to say a word, a few Pokemon pick up their bowls and down them in one go.

The very next second, the living room fills with a series of coughing sounds.

Evidently, the spiciness overwhelmed them.

D looks helpless: "You have to sip this slowly!"

Pawmot's face turns red from choking, tears welling in its eyes: "Pawmo..."

The two Indeedee wear similar expressions.

Especially the brother, who knits his brows tightly, clearly not liking the taste.

The brother Indeedee was about to say something, but in the next moment, it felt warmth spreading from the inside out.

This astonished it: "Indeedee?"

The other Pokemon felt it too, as the spiciness in their mouths faded, their attention shifted entirely to the changes in their bodies.

Watching this scene, D smiled and asked: "Feeling warmer now, right?"

"Gawoo!" Cyclizar nodded dumbly.

Under the curious gazes of all the Pokemon, D began to explain:

"This is red sugar ginger tea. It can drive out the cold within you, making it less likely for you to get sick and helping you grow up strong and healthy."

The sister Indeedee suddenly recalled that D rode Gogoat up the back mountain.

She quickly asked: "Indeedee?"

D understood what she meant, nodding with a chuckle: "Exactly, it's made from the wild ginger growing on the back mountain. Just today, I went with Gogoat into the mountains to find them."

Hearing those words, the Pokemon in front all looked moved.

The red sugar ginger tea carried the maker's emotions.

So, after drinking it, they could also feel the intention contained within.

Koraidon suddenly felt like crying.

Even many years later, when it reminisced about the past, it would clearly remember today — it had caught a cold, and D went up the back mountain with a basket to find ginger for it.

---

---

---

---

# 203: Chapter 200: Curiosity

After D finished drinking all the brown sugar ginger tea in front of him, he poured out another dozen cups.

He handed these cups to his sister, Indeedee, and asked, "Can you help me deliver these to the Miltanks?"

"Aye!"

Sister Indeedee gladly agreed.

Not only her, but other Pokemon also helped, carrying the cups outside.

Watching their backs, D smiled and then started dealing with the half-basket of wild mountain mushrooms.

He separated different types of wild mushrooms, marking which ones were suitable for stir-frying and which for making soup.

There was still some time before noon, so he wasn't in a hurry to cook lunch. Instead, he turned his attention to the pile of special poisonous mushrooms.

D picked and sorted through them, and after careful inspection, he found that every mushroom here would put a Pokemon into a toxic state if consumed.

That's right, a toxic state in the literal sense.

This also meant that even if poisoned, it could be treated with antidote berries and antidotes.

"It shouldn't... kill anyone, right?"

Red cap, white stalk, eat them and lie down flat together.

D rubbed his chin, hesitating inside, but ultimately couldn't resist his curiosity.

"Oh well, I'll just give it a try; if it's toxic, I can always throw it away."

He went for it!

D gritted his teeth, began washing the mushrooms with determination, and then started the fire, pouring all the poisonous mushrooms into the pot to make soup.

As time passed, the once calm pot began to boil gradually.

The red mushrooms tumbled up and down in the pot, and D stirred them continuously with a chopstick.

Watching this scene, he suddenly felt like an evil witch from the Middle Ages concocting a potion.

D: "..."

But speaking of which, the art of cooking does have its harmful methods.

Using the principle of mutual reinforcement and restraint between different ingredients to gradually deplete the consumer's health.

After about half an hour, the toxic mushroom soup was finally ready.

D poured it into a basin and then lowered his head to examine it.

[Poisonous Mushroom Thick Soup (B-): A mushroom soup brewed with toxic wild mushrooms, although it smells fragrant, don't be fooled by its appearance, beware! Consumer!

Cooking Effect: ???, after consumption by humans and Pokemon, they will see another different world, and some unexpected things may happen.

But at the same time, they will fall into a toxic state and must be detoxified within an hour using a Pecha Berry or antidote!

Rating: Extremely dangerous dish, please consume with caution!]

D: "..."

Sometimes humans are like this, the more you forbid them from doing something, the more they want to do it.

Moreover, this cooking effect seems to deliberately entice people to eat it, doesn't it?!

Seeing a different world, is it the Spirit Realm inhabited by many Ghost-type Pokemon?

And the part about unexpected things happening.

Although the method is quite clumsy, he couldn't deny that he was tempted.

The Pokemon World's another world is clearly not that simple.

However, he still had to be careful, after all, this thing really could be harmful.

After much deliberation, D retrieved several Pecha Berries from the berry storage.

He lowered his head and examined the information about these pink Pecha Berries in his mind.

[Pecha Berry: A magical berry that can detoxify.]

Then, D called over the Indeedee siblings and shared his plan with them.

After listening, Brother Indeedee showed a disapproving look.

Sister Indeedee looked very worried.

"Don't worry, I know what I'm doing. I'll just look for one minute, and after one minute, pour the Pecha Berry Juice into my mouth," D said.

The cooking description said there wouldn't be a problem as long as detoxification occurred within an hour.

To prevent the two Indeedees from worrying, he assured them it would only take one minute and that there would be no problem.

Seeing his determined attitude, the two Indeedees exchanged a glance and reluctantly agreed.

So D sat on the sofa, scooped a bowl of soup with a spoon, and drank it down.

The next second, he felt everything in front of him begin to change.

The room's decor became colorful, with patches of color constantly peeling off, and D felt dizzy as if the world was spinning, like he was falling.

Gradually, as all the surrounding colors faded away, he found himself in a space filled with a sense of deathly silence and void.

This was a very special world.

Floating in the void were various-sized floating islands, with upside-down trees, waterfalls hanging downwards, and even upside-down water surfaces.

Everything here seemed unaffected by gravity.

D was stunned.

Suddenly, four large words appeared in his mind.

Reversed World!

He finally realized what the cooking effect meant by seeing another different world!

This wasn't the Spirit Realm or hallucinations caused by the poisonous mushrooms but an opposing yet balanced counterpart to the real world, the Reversed World!

This was the domain of the mythical Pokemon, the Netherworld Dragon Giratina!

"So this is what the real Reversed World looks like," D thought, feeling it was worth the trip.

He quickly scanned his surroundings, hoping to catch a glimpse of Giratina, the legendary dragon banished here for its fierce temperament.

Unfortunately, there was no trace of Giratina nearby.

D wanted to take a walk and have a look around.

But the next moment, he felt someone feeding something sweet and fruity into his mouth.

Everything around him quickly retreated like a tide, and D's vision returned to reality, where he saw the worried faces of the Indeedee siblings beside him.

"I'm fine!" D first reassured them.

Then, he felt a little uncomfortable, like he was weakened after a long day of farm work.

D realized that this was likely a sign of poisoning.

He took the glass of Pecha Berry Juice from Sister Indeedee and drank it, after which the weakness gradually subsided.

Sister Indeedee still looked worried, and seeing D's excited expression, she began to worry that the poisonous mushroom had affected his brain.

The two Indeedees exchanged a glance, their eyes full of undisguisable worry.

D noticed: "…What kind of expression is that?"

"Aye..." Sister Indeedee said.

D casually grabbed a melon seed cake to eat, finally understanding what Sister Indeedee meant.

She was suggesting they go to a human hospital for a check-up to ensure there were no adverse effects.

The two Indeedees looked at him so worriedly, leaving D unsure whether to continue indulging in the poisonous mushroom soup.

Seeing his reluctance to part from the poisonous mushroom soup, Brother Indeedee began to ponder whether to inform Koraidon and the others about this.

When that time comes, neither Koraidon, Dragonite, nor Pawmot would probably waste any time rushing over to hold D's legs to stop his self-harming actions.

D: "..."

D relented: "Okay, I promise you, I won't drink it anymore."

But he really wanted to see Giratina move!

Meanwhile, in the Reversed World.

A massive figure swiftly darted through the void.

It was a large, dragon-like creature with a golden half-ring encircling its neck from its back, with black and red stripes horizontally across the front of its neck, and blood-red eyes quietly surveying the void in front of it.

It sensed that a stranger's aura had just intruded into its domain.

---

---

---

# 204: Chapter 201: Giratina, Light and Shadow (Double-Length)

If he gives up, then this pot of Poisonous Mushroom Soup would be wasted.

Thinking of this, D felt quite regretful.

But the toxins in the Poisonous Mushroom Soup are no joke; if he insists on consuming it, it might cause some irreversible effects on his body.

Just to be safe, D decided to go to the town hospital for a check-up and consult with a doctor.

So, that afternoon, D rode Koraidon to the human hospital in town.

After a series of tests, the doctor, dressed in a white coat and wearing gold-rimmed glasses, looked at the test results and said:

"No problem, your body is as healthy as a Tauros running on the vast plains!"

D: "..." What a miraculous metaphor?

The doctor noticed his expression and was somewhat puzzled: "Is there something you're not telling me?"

"Not really." D quickly shook his head and explained: "I just wanted to ask, if a human is poisoned and then uses an Antidote or a Pecha Berry to detoxify, will there still be any irreversible effects on the body?"

Upon hearing this, the doctor pondered: "Ah, this involves the field of Pokemon medicine. Although we're human, in some ways, we are similar to Pokemon."

D: "For example?"

The doctor spoke confidently: "For example, if humans are poisoned, they can use a Pecha Berry for cure, and if burned, they can apply Rawst Berry Juice to the burn, and it will heal quickly."

D had anticipated this but couldn't help being a little surprised: "So, in that case, humans are actually a type of Pokemon?"

The doctor: "Some people believe so, but many think humans are just humans. It's like the ancient scholars arguing about geocentrism and heliocentrism."

"Personally, I believe humans are just humans. After all, we don't have the magical powers Pokemon possess. Haha, I'm digressing here."

"Don't worry, even if it's toxic, as long as you detoxify before it's critical, it won't have any effects on your body. You have to believe in the power of the human body!"

D: "..."

He nodded but didn't say much.

However, he didn't quite agree with the doctor's words.

Because, in one of the Pokemon movies, a human named Coco used Zarude's special move, Jungle Healing.

Moreover, there are a few people in this world who can use Psychic powers, like a certain Gym Leader in the Kanto Region and an Elite in the Unova Region.

Nonetheless, he had achieved his goal for the trip.

——As long as he detoxifies in time, the Poisonous Mushroom Soup won't have any adverse effects on him.

D's heart began to stir again.

He immediately rode Koraidon back home, then placed the pot of Poisonous Mushroom Soup from the insulated container onto the dining table.

He sat at the table, looking down at the pot that could connect his vision to the Reversed World.

Even though several hours had passed by this moment.

But, thanks to the insulated container, the Poisonous Mushroom Soup was still steaming, with a scent unique to mushrooms.

The two Indeedees crowded around as soon as they saw D bring out the Poisonous Mushroom Soup again.

"Indeedee!"

D cleared his throat: "Well, I actually just went to the hospital for a check-up. The doctor said that even if poisoned, as long as detoxified in time, it wouldn't affect the body, so..."

However, the Indeedee Sibling still wasn't very agreeable.

They had learned about poisoning while studying at the manor and knew that a Pokemon poisoned with toxins would suffer greatly.

D ate a piece of Translation Nuts Cake and then understood their meaning, suddenly feeling a bit bewildered.

"You think I would be in pain, and that's why you're stopping me from drinking it?"

The siblings nodded.

But he didn't feel any pain!

Except for the strong dizziness when his vision switched, and once awake, he'd feel weak...

D had a sudden flash of inspiration: "I got it!"

The two Indeedees looked over.

D: "It's probably because this Poisonous Mushroom Soup was made by me!"

Upon hearing this, the siblings immediately understood what D meant.

They also knew well that the food prepared by D always possessed various magical powers.

D said happily: "Don't worry! It will be fine! Just detoxify me quickly when needed!"

Seeing D so resolute, the two Indeedees reluctantly agreed.

The sister Indeedees worriedly reminded: "Indeedee." (If you feel unwell, be sure to tell us.)

"Okay." D readily agreed.

Meanwhile, Koraidon, who had watched the whole affair, was completely confused.

What just happened?

While it was still bewildered, D already took a second sip of the Poisonous Mushroom Soup.

He agreed with the two Indeedees that this time, they would give him fifteen minutes.

After swallowing the Poisonous Mushroom Soup, D immediately felt dizzy, and the scene before him rapidly changed.

When his vision gradually returned, D realized he was once again in Giratina's Reversed World.

Only this time, instead of a void, he found himself on a floating island with a reverse-flowing waterfall.

---

D still felt a bit dizzy.

He remained calm, observing the surroundings while beginning to ponder: "Changed positions…"

"This should be the reason my position in reality shifted."

The last time he drank poisonous mushroom soup, he was over by the sofa.

Now, he should have been moved to the bed in the bedroom by the Indeedee using psychic powers.

D tried to move two steps and was surprised to find himself able to walk freely in this world?

He wanted to walk to the edge of the island and carefully observe the situation here.

Meanwhile, in reality, Koraidon and the Indeedee sibling watched D rise from the bed, step to the bed's edge, then crouch down to inspect the situation under the bed.

Koraidon, Indeedee sibling: "?"

Koraidon was concerned: "Ah ga si?" (Is D okay?)

The sister Indeedee shook her head lightly: "Ai yi..." (So strange…)

The brother Indeedee remained relatively calm.

He couldn't help but speculate: Perhaps because D drank poisonous mushroom soup, he could see things normally invisible?

The three Pokemon felt their minds were somewhat confused.

Just then, they saw D start moving randomly around again.

Seeing he was about to fall off the bed, the brother Indeedee hurriedly used telekinesis to support him, placing him gently on the floor.

...

"Amazing."

D had just felt his body float up, seemingly unaffected by gravity here.

At this moment, he had already reached the other side of the sky island.

Here, there was a small waterfall flowing upwards.

D looked up, curious, he couldn't help but reach out to touch the waterfall, but ended up touching nothing at all.

"It seems I haven't truly entered the reversed world."

D thought slightly, that mushroom soup should have just let him "see" here, but couldn't touch anything.

What's this? The world's backside?

D suddenly recalled an ancient saying from the Hisuin Region — strong light casts shadows.

It balances and coexists with the real world…

He looked at the relatively small waterfall, and the information about the waterfall jumped into his mind.

[Water of the Reversed World: Mythical Level ingredient, when used to make dishes, can grant them the effect of "Reversing the Universe", originating from the reversed world, guarded by Netherworld Dragon Giratina, extremely difficult to obtain, an invaluable ingredient.]

D: "?"

What's this?

Just then, D suddenly felt a chill as a gaze, impossible to ignore, was watching him.

The gaze was so intense, carrying an ancient, chilling sense of oppression.

The source of the gaze...

Behind him!

D spun around and saw a massive, ancient creature in the void behind him.

Its body resembled an enormously huge serpent, with its chest and belly covered in alternating black and red stripes, six golden spikes extending from its sides and six ghost-like black wings on its back!

Those six pairs of striped wings glowed darkly, their tips blood red, beneath the golden mask lay a pair of lonely, cold blood-red eyes.

At this very moment, those fear-inducing eyes were silently watching below!

Giratina! Master of the reversed world! And it's in Origin Forme!

D felt chills run down his spine, struggling not to collapse to the ground.

This sense of oppression was something he'd never felt before!

Even with Koraidon at full power, he hadn't felt such oppression!

At this moment, time seemed to stand still.

Between heaven and earth, there were no other objects, only two figures, one large, one small.

The enormous Giratina looked down indifferently at the tiny human below.

Its gaze and expression revealed no hint of its thoughts.

D felt panic inside.

Because he didn't know if Giratina could hit him in his current state!

But he still forced himself to confront this Dragon of Destruction.

He didn't move, Giratina didn't move; the two were locked in stalemate.

Time ticked by, and after the initial panic, D gradually calmed down.

"No fear, no fear, in my current state I shouldn't be able to touch anything in the reversed world, Giratina can't touch me!"

Yet, D still dared not provoke the Netherworld Dragon.

Because who knows if it might come to the real world through mirrors like lakes or rivers, using a Shadow Force to wreck the new home he'd just built.

After calming down, D tried his best to express goodwill and began to speak dishonestly:

"Well… I mean no harm, nor have I come to compete for territory! I just accidentally stumbled in here!"

However, as soon as he finished speaking, he noticed Giratina showing a disdainful look.

D: !!!

Ha, looked down upon.

"In the movie, you embarrassingly got captured and controlled by human machines!" D couldn't help but grumble inwardly.

But these words remained only in his thoughts; under Giratina's dominance, he could only offer a polite yet elegant smile.

Then, he felt someone feeding him Pecha Berry Juice.

D's vision immediately returned to the real world, his heart relaxed.

He looked at the worried faces of the Indeedee siblings and the very wounded Koraidon before glancing down at his current position.

What happened? Wasn't he supposed to lie down on the bed after drinking poisonous mushroom soup?

Could it be… his movements in the reversed world also correspond with movements in the real world?

D hurriedly questioned the three Pokemon before him.

As expected, he received a positive answer from them.

"Indeed, the world's backside…" D gradually freed himself from Giratina's influence.

After all, in Giratina's eyes, he as a human was still too small.

The other party would never chase him to the real world to kill him, nor deign to attack him.

Thinking about this, D felt his body gradually relax, accepting the Pecha Berry from the sister Indeedee, munching it, and fearfully explaining to them:

"You don't know what I just saw."

The siblings exchanged a glance: "Ai yi." (Just now, you looked at Koraidon, showing a terribly frightened expression.)

D: "..."

He immediately turned his gaze to the injured-looking Koraidon, suddenly realizing, no wonder!

"No, how could I be afraid of Koraidon?"

D patted Koraidon's head, seeing Koraidon cheer up, then smilingly explained the effects of the poisonous mushroom soup to the Pokemon:

"Drinking the poisonous mushroom soup allows me to see another world."

The sister Indeedee widened her eyes: "Ai yi?" Another world?

D nodded: "Namely, the backside of the real world."

However, unfortunately, the Pokemon seemed to have difficulty understanding.

Seeing this, D gave an example.

He closed the window, then turned on the light, placing a book under the lamp.

Under the bright light, a shadow appeared behind the book.

The three Pokemon looked down, as D explained:

"It's like the relation of this book, you see the book, but can't see the area behind it."

"The world we live in now, in places we don't see, is like this book, similarly casting a shadow, that's the world's shadow."

"After drinking the poisonous mushroom soup, I can see things normally invisible, namely the world's backside, the realm shrouded in shadow."

Koraidon and the Indeedee sibling listened, half comprehending, half not.

The brother Indeedee pondered: "Ai yi?" (Is there anything terrifying there?)

D: "It is home to a Pokemon named Giratina; it is the master of the shadow world, humans call the world the destroyed world, the reversed world."

"The reason I was scared just now is because I saw it; it suddenly appeared, and its appearance is simply terrifying."

Seeing D wasn't afraid of him, Koraidon immediately cheered up, pouncing forward like a big golden retriever, happily licking D with its tongue.

"Alright, alright." D was splashed with saliva, helplessly pushing Koraidon's face aside.

He recalled the waterfall he'd seen just before Giratina appeared.

Water of the Reversed World… capable of granting dishes the effect of "Reversing the Universe"...

This thing just sounds advanced by its name!

Intuition told him it was an ingredient even more precious than Zapdos' feathers.

D's mind was stirred.

He wanted it!

---

---

---

---

# 205: Chapter 202: Giratina Strategy Plan

A large bowl of Poisonous Mushroom Soup remained.

D sat at the dining table, not too eager to drink it this time.

Before that, he had to figure out a few things.

From the Indeedee Sibling and Koraidon, he learned that after drinking the Poisonous Mushroom Soup, he didn't just collapse on the bed immobile; his consciousness entered the Reversed World.

Correspondingly, if he moved in the Reversed World, his physical self in the real world would also move.

Moreover, the lack of gravity in the Reversed World couldn't affect him. He thought he was floating, but actually, the brother Indeedee used Psychic to hold him, preventing him from falling off the bed.

"It feels... a bit like wearing a VR headset," D muttered to himself.

But in that case, things became much simpler.

This meant he wasn't truly entering the Reversed World but "seeing" that place through some special means.

"If I move to a more spacious area to drink, will the explorable places increase?" D began to imagine.

He decided to try it on the ranch's lawn later.

But before that, he needed to develop a detailed plan.

Acquiring the Water of the Reversed World clearly wasn't simple.

Since he wasn't truly entering the Reversed World, even without Giratina's presence, he couldn't take that water out.

Moreover, as the ruler of the Reversed World, Giratina might not allow anyone to intrude on its domain at will.

Ultimately, everything circled back to Giratina.

To obtain the Water of the Reversed World, a mythical-level ingredient, he must first make contact with Giratina.

Improve its favorability, build a good relationship, and let it "invite" him into the Reversed World.

Entering openly is certainly better than sneaking in.

But how to do this remained a problem.

Giratina is a violent, destructive Lagendary Beast; its strategy difficulty is certainly not as simple as strategizing a little Eevee.

"What about exchanging with food?" D pondered deeply: "No, that probably won't work."

Legendary Beasts like Giratina typically don't need to eat; they can sustain themselves by absorbing surrounding energy.

At their level, eating is not a necessity; while gourmet food is appealing, its impact might not be as significant as imagined.

Moreover, he currently had no way to bring food into the Reversed World.

"Maybe try that out when the chance arises."

Aside from that...

Perhaps he could familiarize Giratina with his presence first, let it get used to it.

Just like some human interactions.

He could visit the Reversed World daily, greet it, chat with it.

Once Giratina could accept his presence and grow accustomed to it, when he suddenly stopped doing this one day...

D began to imagine the results of such actions in his mind.

If this Giratina had a more introverted and insecure personality, it might suspect it did something wrong, feel uneasy, and wish to continue the interaction, allowing him to take the opportunity to advance further.

—But this was obviously an impossible scenario.

With Giratina's temperament, it might react violently, directly coming after him, and then...

D quickly halted his imagination.

No matter the plan, it couldn't be completed in a day. To obtain the Water of the Reversed World, he had to endure this lengthy implementation stage.

D named it the "Giratina Strategy Plan."

Of course, there was a second method.

For instance, he could recklessly provoke Giratina in the Reversed World.

When Giratina became enraged enough to lose its senses, it would break through dimensions to enter the real world.

At that time, D could rally the entire ranch's strength to attack this big boss, subdue it, and then use its power to retrieve the Water of the Reversed World.

But D didn't believe he had that capability, and even if successful, it would end in mutual destruction.

The Water of the Reversed World wasn't something he absolutely needed, so he preferred to adopt the first plan, which would hardly bring any damage to the ranch.

After brief contemplation, D had already devised a complete and detailed plan in his mind.

He called upon the Indeedee Sibling, asking them to watch over him, then drank the now-cooled Poisonous Mushroom Soup once again.

Immediately, the entire world turned vibrant.

Amidst the spinning and dizziness, D collapsed to the ground with a thud.

When he opened his eyes again, he found himself back in the Reversed World once more.

Only this time, he was no longer on the previous Floating Island but was instead hovering in the void.

"As expected, changing position in reality also changes position in the Reversed World," D silently thought.

He looked around and didn't see Giratina.

But D wasn't worried because he knew Giratina had sensed his arrival.

This time, he didn't go anywhere, just sat cross-legged in the void, quietly waiting for Giratina to arrive.

So when Giratina flew over, it saw the human who had visited its domain thrice calmly meditating in the void.

Remembering the person's previous behavior, Giratina's gaze sharpened, and a nameless rage surged up.

---

What a bold human! How dare they provoke it!

What do they take this place for? And what do they take it for?

This is its territory!

Giratina's massive body swept through the void, the terrifying energy in it turned into a torrent of energy, blasting out from it.

D: "???"

He instinctively raised his hand to protect himself, but soon realized that the energy storm couldn't harm him at all!

D sat in the energy storm, utterly confused.

What on earth is going on? Why is Giratina acting crazy?

Seeing this human sitting safely in place, Giratina couldn't help but feel a big question mark in its heart.

"?"

"Hello?" D tried waving at Giratina.

Giratina immediately snapped back to reality.

It felt even more furious, he must really be provoking it, right?

Hmph!

Giratina immediately swung its Dragon Tail in disbelief.

However, the massive resilient tail once again passed through him.

Seeing this, D immediately realized something was amiss.

Perhaps in Giratina's eyes, his actions were seen as provocation!

Just like when someone is hitting you hard, and you're there asking if they've had their meal.

Goodness! D felt terribly wronged, he definitely didn't mean to do that!

D looked at the terrifyingly powerful energy storms capable of tearing through space aimed at him, his expression shifting from initial fear to total calmness.

Yet seeing this, Giratina fought even more fiercely.

At the same time, the two neighbors living next to Giratina both opened their eyes.

They looked curiously at the space wall vibrating with energy, a hint of doubt rising in their hearts.

What's that guy going crazy about?

*

D's mind was racing.

What should I do, what should I do?

After Giratina's Shadow Ball passed through him again, D's eyes lit up.

Got it!

He lay down on the ground, closed his eyes, held his breath, and pretended to be dead.

Seeing this, Giratina stopped, panting heavily, its chest rising and falling, not knowing if it was from anger or exhaustion.

Watching the "corpse" of the human disappear from its domain, Giratina gradually calmed down.

This is the price of provoking it!

It twisted its enormous body, flying towards the distant void.

After fifteen minutes, his sister Indeedee fed D a Pecha Berry.

D opened his eyes, smacking his lips as he got up from the grass.

Oh well, that's Giratina for you, always so intense!

"No wonder it dragged Dialga into the Reversed World after their big showdown."

D recalled how in the movie, the battle between Dialga and Palkia in Alamos Town affected Giratina in the Reversed World.

Enraged, Giratina took revenge by dragging Dialga into the Reversed World on its way home, accidentally bringing in a Shaymin too.

Using Shaymin's Seed Flare, Dialga escaped the Reversed World, and by rewinding time, sealed the Reversed World, trapping Giratina inside.

This incident led to the movie "Giratina and the Sky Warrior Shaymin".

Interestingly, even after that movie ended, Giratina didn't give up on revenge, persistently chasing Dialga all the way to Midina.

It wasn't until Arceus awoke from its slumber that they returned to their realms.

With such a temperament, if he hadn't played dead earlier and exactly fifteen minutes hadn't passed, Giratina might have kept attacking him until exhausted.

However, D couldn't understand why Giratina suddenly attacked him this time.

"Could it be because I've been entering and leaving the Reversed World repeatedly?"

D thought this was quite possible.

After all, the Reversed World is Giratina's domain, and his actions might seem provocative to Giratina.

"Sigh, looks like the strategy to deal with Giratina involves a lot more work!"

D couldn't help but sigh.

He looked at the leftover Poisonous Mushroom Soup, giving up the idea of drinking it further.

Better wait until tomorrow to see what happens!

The time had already reached the evening.

To continue this plan, D once again headed to the back hill with his basket, intending to gather more poisonous mushrooms from there.

The autumn back hill is like a massive treasure trove for farm workers running a farm.

The hills grow all sorts of berries, wildflowers, mushrooms, hazelnuts, and a variety of herbs.

This time, D met another person on the hill who was also collecting mountain goods.

D had seen him before at a festival; it was Toff, the owner of the neighboring farm.

Toff had a very distinctive appearance, with a long face, a classic goatee on his chin, and he always wore brown farmer overalls.

D recognized him from afar.

The two met, greeting each other.

Toff energetically said, "Good afternoon, D! What a coincidence, you're here picking mushrooms too!"

D smiled and nodded: "Yes!"

He glanced at Toff's basket, seeing it filled with mountain goods, including mushrooms, berries, and some hazelnuts that some squirrel Pokemon love.

At the same time, Toff also glanced at his basket.

Toff was startled at what he saw.

He pointed at the mushrooms in D's basket in shock: "You can't eat those! They're all poisonous mushrooms, eating them will send you to the hospital! You must get rid of them!"

D: "..."

He earnestly replied, "Don't worry, Mr. Toff, I know. These mushrooms are meant to feed my Poison Type Pokemon, they absolutely love them."

Toff had never been to D Ranch, so he assumed D indeed had Poison Type Pokemon there.

Hearing this, he breathed a sigh of relief: "You scared me, I thought you were planning to make soup with them."

"How could I?" D smiled.

"That's good." Toff laughed: "You know, I heard from Mayor Thomas that it's your first time running a ranch with no farming experience, you might not tell which mushrooms are poisonous or safe."

D immediately responded: "Mayor Thomas gave me a book on ranch management, which has sections about mushroom types. On this trip, I specifically came to pick poisonous mushrooms, see!"

With that, D showed him the mushrooms in his basket.

Toff took a look and indeed saw a basket full of poisonous mushrooms, not a single edible one.

"Your Poison Type Pokemon are in for a treat!" He pointed in a direction: "Over there, there are a lot of poisonous mushrooms. If you need, you can pick them all."

Hearing this, D's eyes lit up: "Great, I'll go check. Thank you so much, Mr. Toff!"

Toff laughed heartily: "We're all neighbors, and thanks to your shrine to Calyrex in the ranch, my farm's crops are doing so well this year, go ahead!"

D: "..."

Calyrex truly is a benevolent deity!

He smiled and thanked Toff, then headed in that direction with his basket.

There lay a thick tree trunk, hollow inside, dark and damp all around, perfect for mushroom growth.

D glanced around and, indeed, found many poisonous mushrooms there.

After filling his basket with poisonous mushrooms, he contentedly returned to the ranch.

He planned to keep these poisonous mushrooms to eat slowly, and whatever he couldn't finish would be dried and stored.

D intended to collect as many poisonous mushrooms as possible this autumn, using them as a bridge to the Reversed World.

Since they won't kill him, he'll just continue eating them!

So the next morning, after finishing work at the ranch, D once again drank the Poisonous Mushroom Soup.

This time, he learned from past experiences and didn't act too familiar; instead, he stood respectfully in place like a guest, waiting for the true master of the Reversed World to arrive.

A few minutes later, just as D expected, Giratina's massive body appeared before him again.

"?"

Giratina was dumbfounded, looking down at this human in shock.

W-what happened?

Wasn't this intruder already defeated by its Shadow Ball?

---

---

---

---

# 206: Chapter 203: Giratina Favorability +1! (Two Chapters Combined)

"Good morning, great Giratina, dragon god of destruction and shadows!"

This time, D decided to change his strategy.

He had learned from an experience with Calyrex's proselytizing event—to pretend to admire and like Giratina.

This would help to possibly eliminate hostility and prevent it from attacking.

However, upon hearing these words, Giratina simply stared at him silently without any reaction.

But on the bright side, this time Giratina didn't hastily unleash an attack on him.

Seeing this, D couldn't help but wonder to himself, "No way? Could it be that Giratina doesn't use telepathy? Or does it disdain communicating with humans?"

At that moment, a cold and slightly hostile voice echoed within his mind.

"Do you want to destroy the world too?"

Just like years ago, when a human sought it out to join forces, causing the Space-Time Gods to go berserk and opening the time-space rift.

Yet what puzzled Giratina was that it didn't sense any crazed ambition from this human before it.

This kind of person didn't resonate with it—it wouldn't lend its help to him.

D: "?"

He keenly noticed Giratina's choice of words.

Too?

Has someone else sought Giratina out before? D was a bit slow to realize.

But soon, he figured out whom Giratina was referring to!

It was Volo! The merchant of the ginkgo from the ancient Hisuin Region!

He had joined forces with Giratina in the Hisuin Region, using its power to send Dialga and Palkia into chaos, triggering a massive space-time disturbance.

He wanted to use the rift opened during the Space-Time Gods' conflict to enter the realm of Arceus, the Pokemon known as the Creator God!

D couldn't help but ask, "Was it Volo?"

Giratina: "You know him."

"Not really," D admitted, "I just didn't expect... didn't expect such a deep connection with the Hisuin Region."

Giratina didn't continue with this topic.

With its blood-red eyes, it looked at the human in front of it, and said irritably:

"Although I don't know how you barged in! I don't like it, leave my territory!"

D: "..."

He could only confess honestly, "Well, the reason I came in was because I wanted some of the water here."

Saying this, he pointed to a lake on a Floating Island not far away.

Giratina turned to look at it.

"But I'm not asking for it for free," D said sincerely, "I can trade for it."

"You?" Giratina looked dismissive.

D pretended not to notice, "... I promise, once I get it, I won't bother you again. In return, I can provide you with all kinds of food."

Giratina initially wanted to refuse, as human food held no appeal to it.

But thinking it wouldn't have to see this annoying and untouchable little insect anymore, it decisively nodded, "Fine."

D's eyes lit up, "So how can I come over to get it?"

Giratina stared at him, "Aren't you here now? Just take it directly."

D, "To be honest, in my current state, I can't touch anything here."

Giratina fell silent.

This was precisely what was puzzling it.

"Where are you physically?" Giratina asked, "I can open a gateway for you to come and retrieve it."

Seeing Giratina's expression, D became wary, "You won't attack me at that moment, will you?"

But at these words, Giratina instantaneously became agitated, exclaiming, "Am I that kind of Pokemon?!"

D thought to himself, maybe; after all, you did chase Dialga for blocks after it unintentionally affected the Reversed World.

Seeing his expression, Giratina, who indeed considered it briefly, turned its head away sheepishly, looking around.

"Ha! You were planning on it, weren't you?"

Giratina glared, "What nerve! You're the first human to speak to me like that!"

"Heaven as my witness!" D shook his head, "I never intended to cause trouble; I merely wanted to behold the legendary Giratina and say hello, but it was you who attacked me."

"That was because you provoked me first!" Giratina didn't believe it had done wrong; it thought it had been quite lenient.

If it were anyone else, it would have chased them to the ends of the earth and beaten them to a pulp.

"Alright, my fault, my fault, I shouldn't have bothered you," D went along with Giratina, appeasing it.

This creature clearly preferred the soft approach over the hard one.

As expected, Giratina's attitude wasn't as violent as it initially was.

Seeing this, D grinned and said, "Giratina, shall we make a deal? I want the water here, and while providing you with human food, I can also reveal some of your future to you."

Giratina narrowed its eyes, "My future?"

Could this human really possess such an ability?

"About you, about Dialga, about Palkia, about the God that created everything," D teased a little, "Are you interested?"

Though Giratina originally wasn't very interested, upon hearing this bit of info, its curiosity was piqued. It seemed this human knew quite a lot about them!

---

"Speak!"

D turned his gaze towards the nearby waterfall.

Giratina, exasperated, conceded, "I agree to your terms!"

"As expected, truly a hot-tempered dragon!" D mused inwardly, feeling the shift in Giratina's demeanor, sensing that this time Giratina wouldn't attack him again.

D felt a sense of relief internally, and then slowly began to explain:

"In the future, a person named Cyrus will collect the red chain from Azelf, Mesprit, and Uxie to control Dialga and Palkia, aiming to destroy this world."

Giratina interjected, "Cyrus?" This human's actions sounded quite pleasing to it!

D continued, "…But at the critical moment, you appeared from the shadows and dragged him into the Reversed World."

Giratina was quite surprised, "Why would I drag him in?"

It never allowed others to trespass into its domain!

D shrugged, "Who knows? Since then, he hasn't been seen again. Some people think he disappeared into the depths of the Reversed World, while others think you ate him."

Giratina was furious, "Nonsense! I've never eaten a human! I don't need to eat like you humans do at all!"

Giratina was extremely angry, poised as if it wanted to immediately find the person who said this and attack them for ten days and nights with all its might.

D said, "That's not important."

However, Giratina's attention wasn't easily swayed, "It is important!"

"Yes, whoever said that deserves to be flayed and dismembered!" D added, trying to stroke it a little.

Hearing this, Giratina glanced at him with surprise.

D: "…"

Ignoring Giratina's gaze, he continued, "Before this, a few other things happened."

"Dialga and Palkia's realms will collide in the future timeline, leading them into a fierce battle."

"Their energies are too immense and will disrupt the time-space continuum, affecting your Reversed World. You will angrily chase Dialga and drag it into this place."

Pausing, D gave Giratina a sidelong glance, speaking cryptically, "At that time, a human wanting to live in the Reversed World took advantage and used a machine to capture you, trying to replicate your power to open a way in, and then…"

Once again, before he could finish, Giratina interrupted with a loud shout.

"That's absolutely impossible!"

D smiled slightly, "Indeed, with your strength, it's absolutely impossible for such a thing to happen; that human must have used some treacherous means."

Giratina cast D a glance, suddenly finding this human more and more agreeable.

D continued, "After that, you chased Dialga to a place called Midina, where the god who created everything awakened from slumber. For some reason, it was enraged and wanted to destroy the entire world."

"At that time, you would team up with Dialga and Palkia to stop the enraged Arceus."

Hearing this, Giratina fell into a rare silence.

It knew about the slumbering one.

But it also knew full well it wouldn't be a match for that being.

"Was the world destroyed?" Giratina asked.

D shook his head with a smile, "No, you succeeded, not just you, some humans were also making efforts. You held back Arceus, giving humans the chance to change everything."

Hearing these words, Giratina couldn't help but reevaluate the human before it.

He was definitely not just any ordinary human!

To know so much about their secrets!

Initially skeptical about these futures, but now…

Seeing the turbulent dragon fall silent, D felt somewhat uneasy.

"Don't you want to say something?" he asked.

Giratina tilted its head again to look at him for a while and then said, "I agree."

D earnestly said, "Thank you very much, you truly are a kind god, the friendliest Pokemon in the world!"

The "friendly" Giratina recalled yesterday's moments when it bombarded the humans for over ten minutes, feeling somewhat guilty.

Fortunately, D didn't bring up old grievances, merely asking, "How should I take it?"

Giratina replied, "Tell me your location, and I'll open a passage to the Reversed World."

D nodded, revealing the location of his ranch.

Giratina peered from the Reversed World into the real world, indeed seeing a human ranch.

It felt utterly baffled, "How did you enter here? No… Your current state is very strange, I don't understand."

D shook his head, "That's my secret, but I can't say now because we aren't friends yet. Let's wait until our relationship grows closer before talking!"

Relationship…grow closer? Giratina was bewildered.

D thought for a moment and then asked, "What about the offering I wanted to give to you?"

Giratina, returning to its senses, said, "I saw a lake in your ranch, place it on the lake surface."

"Alright."

Coincidentally, at that moment, the effects of the Poisonous Mushroom Soup wore off.

Everything before D gradually blurred, shifting his vision back to the real world.

After finishing a Pecha Berry on the spot, he returned to the living room, with the help of his sister Indeedee, packing all the snacks like Lucky Pudding, Honey Bun, Secret Sandwich, and Berry Milk and placing them on the lake where the Dragonair lived.

As soon as he and his sister Indeedee arrived, ripples emerged on the previously calm lake surface, as if Giratina had been watching the real world from the Reversed World.

Sunbathing Dragonair were startled by this scene, quickly leaving the water surface, hiding behind D, cautiously poking their heads out to observe the situation.

"Don't be afraid, it's alright!"

D comforted them with a word before turning his gaze to the lake face.

In the gentle purple light, a temporal vortex slowly emerged, swallowing the heap of food on the lake surface.

Soon after, a torrent burst from the vortex, transforming into a fountain.

Seeing this, D hastened to shout for a bucket!

Half a minute later, his brother Indeedee, following instructions, ran over controlling a bucket with Psychic.

D placed the bucket under the fountain, and even after it was completely filled, the fountain continued without stopping.

Wow, Giratina was indeed generous!

Seeing this, D promptly asked Koraidon to bring over the big milk container.

Only after filling both large milk containers did the fountain cease.

The purple vortex gradually vanished, everything around returning to calm.

Gazing at the three brimming buckets of mythical ingredients beside him, D felt a sense of harvest joy.

[Water of the Reversed World: Mythical Level ingredient. When used to make cuisine, it grants the dish the effect of "Reversing the Universe," born in the Reversed World, guarded by Netherworld Dragon Giratina. Extremely difficult to obtain, it's a highly precious ingredient.]

It's not in vain that he ate so many Poisonous Mushrooms, yielding such results at last!

D couldn't wait to see the specific effect of "Reversing the Universe."

Decisively, he put Giratina out of mind, taking Indeedee and Koraidon to carry the three buckets of water back home, preparing to cook some noodles first using them.

*

In the Reversed World, amidst silence and void.

Giratina quietly watched everything happening in the real world.

At this moment, a sense of curiosity arose in its heart towards this human.

A god's intuition told it, this person was definitely not an ordinary human!

Seeing them enter a house, Giratina turned to look at things floating in the void around it, things clearly not belonging to this world.

Were these…what they called food?

Is this what those insignificant humans usually eat?

Giratina showed disdain.

With its massive body sweeping through the void like a big fish eating small fish, in the next second, all the floating food vanished without a trace.

Giratina's pupils narrowed.

This…

A special power suddenly appeared in its body!

What is going on?

Giratina was initially stunned, but soon caught on.

It was those human foods it just consumed!

---

---

---

---

# 207: Chapter 204: Reversal Series Cuisine (2-in-1)

D returned to the kitchen with three buckets of Reversed Water.

He opened the cabinet, humming a little tune as he took out the ingredients.

The Indeedee sibling standing by curiously asked, "Ai Yee?"

D chuckled and shook his head, "No, I don't need your help this time. I'll handle it; it's an experiment."

Hearing this, the Indeedee sibling nodded. It thought D wanted to try developing a new dish, so it quietly stood by its brother, ready to assist when needed.

However, D wasn't planning to make anything too complicated.

For the first try, he decided to make a simple bowl of clear soup noodles with this water.

He started by heating the pot, and once the water boiled, he added the noodles. Then, he added sesame oil, salt, chicken essence, soy sauce, and finally some scallions, a few slices of ham, shredded fungus, two slices of baby bok choy, and cracked in a poached egg.

With that, a completely prepared bowl of clear soup noodles was done.

The amber soup kept steaming, with bouncy, flavorful noodles lying in the glossy broth. A plump, white poached egg sat on top, sprinkled with a few green scallions for garnish.

Just the sight of it made one's mouth water uncontrollably.

"Smells amazing! No wonder it's me!"

D felt quite proud; his culinary skills were indeed getting more and more superb.

A single serving of noodles made up three bowls in total.

D lined them up on the stove and, looking down, the information on these three bowls of clear soup noodles appeared in his mind.

[Reversing Mask·Heart (S): A delicacy made with river water from the Reversed World and luxurious ingredients.

Cooking Effect: Reversing the Universe, reversing the inner hearts of humans and Pokemon after consumption. Good people become evil, evil people become good, cowards become brave, and selfish people become generous.

Rating: Use with caution!]

"Just as I suspected," D murmured.

For him and the Pokemon at the ranch, trying this effect would really require careful consideration.

D hesitated internally, staring at the fragrant bowl of clear soup noodles, unsure whether to eat it or not.

He decided to check the other two bowls.

That was a surprise; upon closer inspection, he discovered some differences.

The names of the two bowls remained as Reversing Mask, but the suffixes behind them were somewhat different from the first bowl.

[Reversing Mask·Techniques (S): Reversing the Universe, the effect is to reverse the abilities, aura, and authority of the Pokemon, applicable only to Legendary Pokemon.]

[Reversing Mask·Body (S): Reversing the Universe, reverses the physical capabilities of humans and Pokemon who consume it, making the strong become weak, and the weak as strong as an ox.]

"Huh? Heart, Body, Techniques? Three different effects?" D was surprised.

Heart and Body are relatively easy to understand.

But what does Techniques mean?

Only effective on Legendary Pokemon, reversing their abilities, aura, authority...

Could it be that feeding it to a Legendary Beast like Giratina would reverse it from the Dragon God of Destruction to the Dragon God of Creation?

Besides that, there's also the Life God Xerneas and the God of Death Yveltal.

If given to them, would their attributes swap?

D was a bit confused.

But his intuition told him that if he really did that, it could potentially cause an ecological imbalance, attracting Zygarde's attention.

However, when it came to the most suitable Pokemon for this dish, his first thought wasn't those famous Legendary Beasts, nor the Pokemon on his ranch.

Instead, it was the four Ruinous Pokemon sealed in the Paldea Region—Chien-Pao, Ting-Lu, Chi-Yu, Wo-Chien.

Wo-Chien absorbs energy from plant life, causing the surrounding forest to wither instantly and farmland crops to fail.

Ting-Lu causes earthquakes. When it swings its head like a giant cauldron, massive cracks over 50 meters deep appear in the earth.

Chi-Yu is naturally mischievous, controlling 3000-degree flames to melt rocks and gravel to form a lava sea, swimming in it leisurely.

Chien-Pao, innocent and carefree, causes avalanches with a hundred tons of snow just to play freely in it.

If reversed...

Wo-Chien could repel pests and weeds, Ting-Lu could make soil fertile, Chi-Yu could boost fisheries, while Chien-Pao could control snowfall, helping crops to survive winter safely.

Wouldn't that turn them into the new Bountiful Quartet?

D couldn't help but be tempted.

He had always had thoughts about these ancient Pokemon, especially the fluffy Chien-Pao. This Ice Type, fluffy snow leopard was perfect for cuddling in the summer.

But he'd held back these thoughts because they brought calamity.

Now it seemed, these could indeed be changed!

Excited, D gradually calmed down.

Because he still didn't know the current location of these four Pokemon, only that they are sealed in the Ruinous Shrine.

To unseal them, one would have to pull stakes in various places.

Too complicated...

"There's no hurry for this," D thought to himself.

Currently, the most important thing is to figure out the specific effects of these three bowls of noodles.

He gave the [Reversing Mask·Techniques], which has no effect on normal Pokemon, to the Indeedee sibling in front of him to taste.

---

Then he ate the "body" himself.

With a bowl of noodles down his throat, D clearly felt his body undergoing a transformation.

Muscles built from years of farm work shriveled, strength continuously ebbing away...

When everything returned to calm, D was astonished to find himself turned into a skinny dog, standing like a bamboo pole.

D: "..."

Seeing his transformation, the Indeedee Sibling was shocked.

D twitched his mouth, turned silently to boil water, planning to brew a cup of tea using Water of the Reversed World.

Soon, the tea was brewed, and its information appeared in D's mind.

[Reversed Red Tea·Heart (s)]

No, not heart!

D, unconvinced, brewed another cup.

This time it came out as technique.

D: "..." It seems this thing is completely random.

After brewing eight cups of tea in a row, he finally got a cup of body.

D quickly drank it, his body then returned to normal, changing back from a skinny dog to normal physique.

The Indeedee Sibling sighed in unison.

And as D looked at the remaining pile of heart and technique reversed recipes, a sudden idea popped into his mind.

What if he gave them to Giratina to eat?

Everyone knows, Giratina represents shadow and destruction.

And the opposite of these two...

The words light and creation flashed in D's mind.

With this thought, he immediately took the tea and hot soup noodles to the lakeside, then drank a mouthful of Poisonous Mushroom Soup, once again seeing the domain of Giratina.

Only surprising to D, this time as soon as he entered, he saw the figure of Giratina.

It seemed the other party had never left, staying in the same place all along.

D looked around, not seeing the pile of food he had previously offered, thinking they must have all been eaten by Giratina.

Seeing this human's action, Giratina glared at him: "Human, what are you looking at?"

D smiled gently: "Are you satisfied with the food I just offered?"

For this irritable dragon, he had found the way to get along, which is to stroke it the right way, never go head-to-head.

Giratina was satisfied, but still said: "Human food, just so so."

D smiled silently, and then said: "I just made some more food using the water from here, wanted to bring them for you to taste."

Giratina understood.

It thought this human was quite sensible, although not as compatible as Volo, but having another human who admired it was always nice.

Just like long ago, some humans built statues and towers for Dialga and Palkia.

Then it casually opened a time-space vortex.

Then, D let the Indeedee sister pour the hot soup noodles and tea in.

The gravity of the Reversed World is very special.

It's a bit like space here, things like tea and soup wouldn't randomly spill, but float in the void.
