The cooking effect that D brought over this time only had two kinds, changing personality of the heart and changing ability of the technique.

He wanted to see what changes Giratina would have after eating these two.

"Try it!"

Giratina glanced at him, then its massive body swept through the void.

Those hot soup noodles and tea immediately disappeared.

This way of eating... is really rough! D couldn't help but bellyache.

He watched Giratina closely.

After consuming those foods, at first, Giratina didn't feel anything wrong.

But soon, it felt something inside its body was changing.

Giratina's eyes widened.

A strong white light erupted from its body.

"Normal Type glow?" D was a bit uncertain.

Before, he once grew glowing crops, and the food made from those crops had a chance to let Pokemon comprehend a glowing move.

At that time, only Pawmot comprehended it, and it looked like this when using the glowing move.

A moment later, when the white light that enveloped Giratina completely dispersed.

A completely renewed dragon appeared before D.

Its eyes were no longer blood-red, but became a very sacred gold.

The golden parts on its body had completely turned into a holy pure white, and the original blood-red stripes had also turned blue.

Its massive body floated in front of D, but the aura it emanated was entirely different.

D's eyes widened.

This is... Shiny Giratina?!

No, it's a bit different from ordinary shinies!

Perhaps it can be called "Luminous Giratina", like Dark Mewtwo, Dark Lugia!

At this moment, Giratina looked calmly at the human in front of it: "What did you feed me?"

D cautiously tested: "Some human food with magical effects?"

On Giratina's ferocious face, there was actually a hint of a smile—It could be called a grin:

"I can feel, they have caused some magical changes in me, this feeling I have never had, anyway, thank you very much, and you are very welcome to visit my domain."

D opened his eyes wide in disbelief, his heart couldn't help exclaiming: It changed, it changed!

This is definitely not the Giratina he knew!

And Giratina seemed to sense his shock, thought for a moment, and then with a faint smile said:

---

"No need to be so surprised, I quite enjoy this feeling. Come on! Let me take you on a tour of my domain!"

Giratina wanted to use its Psychic powers to take D flying for a tour, but there was no reaction, so it couldn't help but turn its head in confusion.

D could only explain, "In my current state, I haven't truly entered the Reversed World..."

Giratina was slightly puzzled, "Then why not come in?"

D thought to himself, I'm afraid you'll bombard me for over ten minutes with your special moves again!

But he couldn't say that, so he just replied, "I haven't found a way to get in yet."

Giratina: "That's simple. I can connect to the real world through the water's surface. From now on, I can open an entrance to this place for you anytime."

D: "..."

Seeing he was silent, Giratina glanced around its territory and then sighed:

"Alas, compared to the world you live in, this place is simply terrible. Indeed, it's not a place for humans to live. If only flowers could grow here too."

Giratina's tone was envious. D felt that if it were the original Giratina, it would probably go beyond envy to jealousy and hatred.

That's why it teamed up with Volo to cause space-time rifts to destroy the real world.

It's like a neglected child taking revenge on its parents, and Arceus happens to be that parent.

At this moment, Giratina suddenly invited:

"I see you're skilled at planting flowers. Could you come here and help me plant some on those Floating Islands?"

D: "..."

He didn't know how to answer.

Help! If Giratina wakes up, there's no way it would let him go!

Just then, an aura of black energy suddenly emerged from Giratina in front of him.

The black energy swirled around its body, and in the next second, the "shining" Giratina returned to its original form.

It glared with wide eyes, angrily, "Damn it, what did you do to me?!"

Seeing it like this, D actually felt relieved.

He sincerely said, "It's good to see you like this."

Upon hearing this, Giratina's fury was instantly extinguished.

It exhaled two heavy breaths through its nose, "Hmph, forget what I just said! I wasn't envious of you! Foolish humans!"

D was startled, "Are you still you in that state?"

Though the words were ambiguous, Giratina still understood the meaning.

It glared, annoyed, "I am me!"

D fell into contemplation.

"The Cooking Effect doesn't seem permanent. No, it's not necessarily so. It might be because Giratina is inherently the master of the Reversed World, so it can reverse itself back."

"It seems that after a change in temperament, the Pokémon remains the same Pokémon and doesn't switch personalities. It's just that due to the change in character, it might say things it usually wouldn't express."

In that case, what Giratina just said might be its true feelings? It really envies the vibrant real world?

D asked with a smile, "Does your earlier invitation still stand?"

What invitation? Giratina was momentarily stunned, then realized it was about inviting this human to plant flowers. A flash of emotion crossed its mind.

But at that moment, it refused to admit it was part of itself.

Giratina snorted irritably, "I never said such things!"

"Alright, alright." D felt it was almost time, so he said, "I'll head back for now. See you next time!"

Saying that, the effects of the Poisonous Mushroom Soup gradually faded, and D's figure left the Reversed World.

Giratina, which was about to rant, was taken aback.

He just left like that?

---

---

---

---

# 208: Chapter 205: Golden Berry, Teddiursa, Ice and Thunder Gods, Lugia (4kk)

Alliance Year 198, early August, autumn.

That morning, a major event occurred on the ranch.

The berry garden they had planted earlier had finally matured.

D stood in the berry garden, looking at the large and heavy fruits on the branches, feeling very happy inside.

"A bountiful harvest!"

The feeling of watching something you planted with your own hands grow is incomparable!

Since there was nothing else to do, he casually wandered around the berry garden with his sister Indeedee.

His younger sister Indeedee, who followed behind, felt equally joyful.

But thinking about D's previous schedule, she couldn't help but ask, "Indeedee?" (Aren't we visiting Mr. Giratina today?)

"Let's skip today." D shook his head, planning to stay at the ranch to stroll around the garden and to properly arrange things in the glass greenhouse as well.

The glass greenhouse had been completed for some time, and because he had been busy with Giratina and the Water of the Reversed World affairs recently, he hadn't had much time to visit.

To be honest, since he left the Reversed World of Giratina last time, he hadn't been there much, at most once a week.

Because Giratina wasn't easy to get along with, and the Reversing the Universe series of dishes currently didn't have much effect on the Pokemon at the ranch.

The three barrels of water were still unused, all stored by D in Glaceon's ice cellar.

"Indeedee."

His sister Indeedee nodded and didn't ask further.

Just then, D suddenly saw something extraordinary on one of the fruit trees.

Amidst the rustling green leaves, hung a plump and glossy golden berry!

That is…

D instinctively focused his gaze on that berry.

In an instant, information about the berry surfaced in his mind.

[Golden Razz Berry: A rare ingredient, a variant of the Razz Berry. Its aroma and nutrition surpass those of ordinary Razz Berries and are much loved by Pokemon. When given to wild Pokemon, they become much easier to capture. The Pokeblocks made with this berry also greatly enhance in taste and quality.]

Wow!

Clearly, such a rare variant berry can't grow on an ordinary berry tree; it was clearly nurtured by Gogoat using Grass Energy!

D decisively picked it.

Since this berry was already ripe, he might as well have the Pokemon at the ranch try it out to see what makes it different from an ordinary Razz Berry!

Seeing this, his sister Indeedee smiled and turned to leave.

In no time, she returned to the garden using telekinesis to control a wicker basket.

Because they had hired two wild Dragonite, D had to provide them with Poffins daily, so the house's berry stock was running low.

Previously, their solution was to buy from the town, but now it was different; the ranch had a bumper crop of berries, and they just needed to come here to pick them in the future.

D placed the Golden Razz Berry in the basket and continued to wander through the berry garden.

While picking fully matured berries, he also scanned the branches with his eyes to see if there were any other mutated berries.

Sure enough, it didn't take long before he found the second, third, and fourth mutated berries.

[Golden Pinap Berry], [Silver Nanab Berry], [Silver Razz Berry]

Once the basket was filled, it contained a few more golden berries.

Carrying the basket back to the lawn outside, D dumped the berries on the ground, piling them into a small mountain before rummaging through to pull out the golden and silver berries.

"One, two, three... six, seven, huh? So many?"

Three golden berries, four slightly less rare silver berries.

D was greatly surprised by this sight.

The mutation rate of these berries far exceeded that of the crops he planted!

After the surprise came a sense of regret.

This level of berry is best used for making Pokeblocks.

As a top-tier dessert in the Pokemon World, a Pokeblock can be considered a perfect all-round food.

It can provide ample nutrition and energy to Pokemon, making their fur oily and slippery, enhancing their maturity and allure, and filling their bellies.

Overall, Pokeblocks are more popular and beloved among Pokemon than Poffins.

But unfortunately, he has not yet mastered the method of making Pokeblocks from berries.

Allen mentioned last time that he would help him find a Breeder skilled in making Pokeblocks, but it seems he hasn't found one yet.

After storing these berries in the house's berry cabinet, D took out a dozen flower pots from the storage room and, together with his brother Indeedee, carried them to the flower field.

Time flew by, and it was already August. In the blink of an eye, the entire autumn would pass, followed swiftly by the cold winter.

Even though the season had yet to arrive, a sudden cold wave had affected the entire world a few days prior.

The weather-related TV programs mentioned that the sea surface of the distant Orange Archipelago was frozen, causing wild Pokemon everywhere to riot, leaving their habitats and crossing the frozen sea to head there.

---

Regarding this matter, D guessed that it should be the start of the movie "Revelation Lugia."

The perverse collector, Lawrence III, wanted to collect Lugia as an exhibit, so he captured the Thunder God Zapdos. This action sparked a contest among the three legendary birds, which affected the weather everywhere, freezing the sea.

Initially, D was a bit worried.

But soon, the cold wave disappeared.

D guessed that Ash and the others had appeased the wrath of the three legendary birds and Lugia.

The Paldea Region was relatively far from there, so the impact wasn't that severe, only causing a drop in temperature due to the cold wave's arrival.

It was fine for humans, but not so friendly for plants.

The sudden drop in temperature affected many flowers in the flower field, making them wilt and appear lifeless, as if they were about to wither.

D dug them up from the flower field, transplanted them into pots, and then moved them into the glass greenhouse.

The newly built glass greenhouse was very clean and tidy, with bright sunlight streaming through the new glass, making the inside look beautiful and bright.

Compared with the outside, the temperature inside the glass greenhouse was slightly higher because Keri had installed underfloor heating beneath it, so it would remain warm even in the cold winter.

The energy for the underfloor heating came from electricity, all generated by Pawmot on the ranch.

Originally, there was also a Dragonite, but since Pawmo evolved into Pawmot, it had taken over Dragonite's job and monopolized the electricity supply.

The little Pawmi of yesterday had become more and more reliable!

D smiled with some emotion, then placed all the pots of flowers on the wooden flower stands.

The decoration of the glass greenhouse had already been completed, and D chose the rustic style once again.

On each side were light-colored wooden flower stands, with a small tea table and a soft sofa in the middle, alongside a bookshelf with an exquisite chandelier hanging above it.

The bookshelf was filled with books D had purchased from the town.

There were stories about young Trainers embarking on their journeys with their first Pokemon, eventually becoming world-renowned Pokemon Masters;

There were fairy tales about wild Pokemon building nests in tree hollows, rescuing other Pokemon, and then playing happily together;

And there were some quite dark ones, like a Caterpie that originally lived happily with its kind in the forest, but one day, a Pidgeot invaded, killing all the Caterpie, leaving only the protagonist.

The protagonist Caterpie was unwilling, and to avenge its kind, it trained hard alone and eventually evolved into a mighty Butterfree, using a combo of Silver Wind + Sleep Powder + Air Slash to defeat all the Pidgeot and successfully avenge its kind.

As for how D knew about this...

Of course, it was because he read some books in his free time.

After all, this world didn't have smartphones or computers that could play triple-A games.

Not reading was fine, but once he started, he found the local books of this world quite interesting.

Once the flower pots were all on the stands, the previously somewhat empty space instantly became full, combining nature with modern furniture to create a uniquely comfortable feeling.

Indeed, when the snow falls heavily, he could bring the Pokemon here, lounge on the soft little sofa, and browse through the books on the shelf.

If any Pokemon was curious, he could also tell them the stories from the books.

"Alright!"

D took out the Psyduck Spray Bottle to hydrate the flowers, then said to the Indeedee Siblings, "Can you help me call over the Combee and Butterfree?"

"Indeedee~" The sister Indeedee gladly agreed and turned to leave the greenhouse.

D looked at the solemn brother standing there and laughed:

"If you have nothing to do in the future, you can come over here too."

"Indeedee." The brother Indeedee, with an expression always resembling a little adult, nodded seriously and calmly.

D: "Just treat this place like your home. If nothing unexpected happens, we'll be living together for decades!"

"Indeedee." The brother Indeedee's expression became solemn, nodding his head once.

D: "…" Oh well, he always has this expression by nature.

Before long, the sister Indeedee brought the three Combee and Butterfree here.

D looked at them, smiling as he asked, "Do you like it?"

The Combee and Butterfree looked around their surroundings with pairs of innocent eyes.

The warm environment, beautiful flowers, comfortable house…

They liked it here!

"Bzzt~"

"Buzz~"

Seeing them, D felt even happier.

He calculated the time in his mind and said, "When winter comes, I'll move the beehive and wooden boxes here for you to live in!"

By that time, flowers would bloom in the glass greenhouse as well, so with flowers around, Butterfree and Combee wouldn't have to worry about food anymore.

After setting up the glass greenhouse, D stayed there a little longer to experience reading, drinking tea, and resting in this environment before getting up to leave.

On the ranch, the other Pokemon were still playing as usual.

Koraidon lay on the grass basking in the sun, but its gaze was directed this way.

---

Cyclizar raced across the lawn with Alcremie, followed by five Naclstack chasing them.

Two wild Dragonite had done something wrong and were being scolded by another Dragonite on the grass.

The Gogoats and Dolliv sat leisurely around the World Tree, chewing on Grassy Seed.

Glaceon seemed to have vanished, but seeing the rustling bushes nearby, D thought it must be wandering there.

What a lively scene! D felt so happy—life was complete.

Just as he planned to make some Poffin for the Dragonites, Allen suddenly rode a Cyclizar up to the ranch.

D looked at the letter in his hand and asked, "Oh? Is the town hosting another festival?"

Allen smirked, "The autumn horse race..."

D casually tore open the envelope, and sure enough, it invited him, the Rancher, to participate in the autumn horse race.

"Oh?" D raised his eyebrows, but he didn't have a horse yet, so he couldn't go.

Allen was still hung up over the money he lost from the spring horse race: "This time, I'm definitely not betting!"

D: "..." Oh, so he remembers that!

Allen looked around, he hadn't been to D Ranch for a while, and it seemed like not much had changed.

He glanced around but didn't see what he was looking for, and couldn't help but ask, "Haven't you captured any Horse-type Pokémon yet?"

D: "Oh? No rush, there's still plenty of time until the spring horse race."

Allen sighed and covered his face in frustration. "Ah, it seems I'll completely lose those League coins, my money!"

D: "..."

Allen let out a long sigh, clearly without hope for this matter anymore: "Well, the ranch work is so busy, there's no time to go capture Horse-type Pokémon... Forget it, I won't win it back!"

"Don't worry." D comforted him: "Trust me, I'll find a way to win it back for you."

After all, they had agreed earlier—Allen would help him find a Housekeeper Pokemon, and he'd help Allen win back the money lost in the spring horse race.

Now that the Housekeeper Pokemon was found, it was time for him to fulfill his promise.

Allen thought to himself that there weren't any Horse-type Pokémon around in the winter, but he'd already given up hope, and moved on to talk about another interesting topic.

"D, have you heard?"

Seeing Allen wasn't in a hurry to leave, D asked, "Want to sit for a while?"

Allen: "Hehe, not at all!"

D invited him into the living room to sit.

His sister Indeedee thoughtfully brought over two cups of tea.

Allen gulped down his drink before continuing, "I heard big news while delivering a letter earlier! It's about the Legendary Pokémon!"

"Hmm?" Instantly, D's mind flashed with the Legendary Pokémon of the Paldea Region.

Koraidon, Miraidon, Terapagos...

But Allen's answer caught him off guard: "It's the legendary Sea God, Lugia!"

Upon hearing this name, D was slightly taken aback.

Allen continued animatedly, "Wasn't there a cold snap in the Paldea Region recently? That cold snap is linked to Lugia!"

"I heard from a tourist from the Orange Archipelago traveling in the Paldea Region that there's a collector over there trying to capture the legendary Sea God, and it even angered the Fire God, Ice God, and Thunder God!"

"The commotion they caused was huge; I heard the sea even froze over, like the end of the world, with many wild Pokémon running to the frozen sea to watch them battle!"

Indeed, hearing these words didn't surprise D much, as he had expected as much.

However, Allen's next words genuinely surprised him.

"What's more interesting is what that person said. During the battle of the Legendary Pokémon, two flying Teddiursa joined in, and almost stole the treasure guarded by the Fire God, Thunder God, and Ice God!"

"The Fire God, Thunder God, and Ice God, who were fighting, immediately stopped and chased after the two Teddiursa, but they couldn't catch up, they were fuming!"

"In the end, someone found those two Teddiursa and told them taking the treasure would cause a huge crisis, only then did they return the Treasure Pearl."

"It's strange, why couldn't those Pokémon, hailed as gods, even catch two Teddiursa, and nearly let them escape?"

"Could it be Quick Feet ability? No, their ability must be Pickup! Only Pokémon with this ability would be interested in peculiar little trinkets!" Allen mused to himself.

D: "?"

---

---

---

---

# 209: Chapter 206: Silver Wing, Get!

"It's truly amazing that there are actually flying Teddiursa in this world."

Allen clicked his tongue in wonder, but when he saw D's strange expression, he curiously asked:

"Don't you find this astonishing? I've only seen such an extraordinary Pokemon at your ranch." He was referring to the Pawmot that flew around the ranch all day with a red cape.

D: "It is indeed surprising..."

"Exactly!" Allen exclaimed: "There are so many secrets about this world that haven't been discovered by humans yet!"

"I heard that many Pokemon Doctors and researchers started investigating how Teddiursa managed to fly into the sky after hearing about this, even the very famous Professor Oak is among them!"

Upon hearing this, D was slightly taken aback: "Professor Oak?"

"Oh? You don't know about him?" Allen was quite surprised, as, in his view, Professor Oak was very well-known, having discovered many secrets about Pokemon.

"I know a bit." D thought to himself, he just didn't expect this matter to catch Professor Oak's attention.

This made him unsure of what to say for a moment.

Allen's face showed an expression of admiration: "Professor Oak, a legend in the Pokemon world, earned his doctorate from Celadon University at the age of 25, becoming a leading researcher in the Pokemon academic field at a young age."

"It's said that to conduct thorough research, Professor Oak often risks being attacked by Pokemon to observe them. He believes that only this way can he obtain real and reliable data, providing a wealth of wild Pokemon information for Trainers traveling outside!"

"If it's Professor Oak, he will definitely uncover the truth behind Teddiursa flying in the sky!"

D: "..."

"Do you have a lot of faith in Professor Oak?" he asked.

"Of course!" Allen nodded: "Because Professor Oak isn't just a theoretical doctorate, before becoming a doctor, he was a highly skilled Pokemon Trainer!"

D raised an eyebrow, somewhat surprised, and said, "You really want to become a Trainer."

Allen nodded earnestly, full of longing, and said, "That has been my dream since I was a child! To receive my own first Pokemon and travel the world with it, what a wonderful life!"

"Then why are you staying in the town as a postal worker?" D couldn't help but ask.

On hearing this, Allen sighed and said helplessly: "Because I have to take care of my grandmother!"

He organized his words and explained: "After my mother passed away, my father started drinking heavily, and one day when he went out drunk, he never returned. Since then, it's just been my grandmother and me living together at home."

D: "...I'm sorry."

Yet Allen grinned, showing a cheerful smile: "It's okay, it's all in the past. Although I can't travel, I'm already very happy to live with the Pokemon. Alright, it's getting late, I still have to deliver mail to the neighboring ranch, see you later!"

D nodded, seeing Allen out the door, watching his figure disappear down the path, he couldn't help but sigh.

Sigh, another unfortunate person!

D withdrew his gaze and looked up at the sky.

Mew and Teddiursa hadn't sent him letters for several days. From Allen's words, it seemed like they had gone to the Orange Archipelago.

The Orange Archipelago is located over the sea, not a complete landmass, with a significant distance between each island.

D guessed that Mew and Teddiursa might have been wandering between the islands, unable to find a landing point, delaying the time to send a letter.

But speak of the devil.

Just as D was about to go back inside to continue making Poffins, he suddenly heard a metallic scratching sound from the sky.

D looked up and saw a Pokemon covered in a layer of silver armor, with feathers as sharp as blades, land in the ranch.

"Skarmory."

The name of this Pokemon flashed in D's mind.

Clutched in Skarmory's silver talons was a letter.

It took a look at D with its sharp gaze and then opened its mouth full of sharp teeth, emitting a screech.

With a "snap," the letter fell onto the grass.

D was already used to this kind of thing; he walked up and picked up the letter, smiling as he said "thanks for your hard work" to Skarmory.

Skarmory seemed quite pleased, responding with another screech.

D waved, and his brother Indeedee walked up from not far away.

D said to him, "Help me bring some Poffins."

His brother Indeedee looked at the visiting Skarmory and the letter in D's hand, seemingly struck with sudden inspiration, and then turned around, going back inside to get a large box of Poffins from his sister.

"This is for your legwork." D handed the Poffin Box to Skarmory: "There are two portions inside, one for you, and could you also send the other one to Teddiursa and Mew, if you can catch up with them."

Skarmory gladly agreed, grasping the Poffin Box with its talons, and flapped its wings contentedly, flying away.

D took the letter back to the living room.

The envelope was quite heavy, which immediately made D think of the three Treasure Pearls that Teddiursa and Mew had picked up from the Three Legendary Birds' guardians.

---

But the Treasure Pearl had already been returned, so this letter must contain something else.

Let's see what Teddiursa and Mew sent back this time!

As the letter was being opened, the Indeedee Sibling, who heard the news, gathered around.

The younger Indeedee curiously looked at the letter in D's hand and asked, "Indeedi?"

D hadn't eaten Translation Nuts, but seeing the younger Indeedee's expression, he could roughly guess its meaning:

"That's right, this is what I told you about before, the things Teddiursa sent back from its travels."

"Its ability is Pickup, and Pokemon with this ability love collecting all sorts of little items. See the display shelf over there? All of those are treasures Teddiursa picked up outside."

The Indeedee Sibling looked over, and sure enough, they saw a pile of random things.

There was a Water Stone, ordinary pebbles, broken Poke Balls, and leftover Berry seeds...

D explained with a smile, "To us they might be useless, but to Teddiursa, they're treasures."

Hearing this, the Indeedee Sibling exchanged glances. They became even more curious about the mysterious Teddiursa.

Just then, the envelope was opened, and D finally saw what was inside.

A photograph and a feather radiating a silver glow.

Looking at that feather, D's heart skipped a beat.

This familiar feeling, could it be... Silver Wing?

Silver Wing, the feather of Lugia. It is said that the holder can summon the Legendary Pokemon Lugia at some place in this world through this feather!

But that's not the main point. The main point is it reminded D of the Lightning Steamed Bun he made with Zapdos's feather last time.

So the question is, can this Lugia feather also be used to make buns?

If possible, what kind of effect would the buns have?

D's heart was itching with excitement.

He didn't care whether he could summon Lugia with this feather, and even if he did, it wasn't like he'd be able to capture it.

Instead of using it or keeping it for a collection, it would be better to use it as an ingredient to make a dish, just like last time.

After all, only what's in your mouth truly belongs to you.

With this decision in mind, D set the Silver Wing aside and picked up the photograph taken by Teddiursa and Mew.

On the back of the photo were two small paw prints, which D recognized as belonging to Mew and Teddiursa.

Teddiursa's paw print was a typical bear's paw print, while Mew's paw print was very similar to a cat's.

This time, neither Teddiursa nor Mew appeared in the picture.

There was only a clear sky, with not a cloud in sight, and below it lay an endless ocean, reflecting the blue sky as they intertwined, forming a seamless horizon.

In the azure sky above, a graceful and extraordinarily beautiful silver-white Pokemon was floating.

On its back rode a human boy wearing a hat with the League's Poke Ball emblem.

D instantly recognized the boy.

That was Ash.

Behind Lugia, in the distance, three similarly sized islands lay in repose.

Three distinct figures were flying towards the islands.

Judging by their silhouettes and colors, D identified these three Pokemon.

From left to right, they were Zapdos the Thunder God, Articuno the Ice God, and Moltres the Fire God.

Seeing this scene, D suddenly wondered how Teddiursa managed to obtain the Silver Wing.

He thought carefully and realized there were only two possibilities.

The first possibility: it took advantage of the chaotic battles of gods and plucked it directly off Lugia.

The second possibility: during a fight, Lugia lost a feather, and Teddiursa happened to pick it up.

D thought the second was more likely, since Teddiursa was so cute, how could it become a lawless ruffian?

However, come to think of it, the gods in the Johto Region seemed to enjoy scattering feathers all over the place.

The Silver Wing of Lugia, it is said that this feather is only bestowed to humans it approves of.

The Rainbow Feather of Ho-oh, unlike Lugia, is a ticket to the Rainbow Hero's Trial.

Then there's Dream God Cresselia's Lunar Feather, which helps humans dispel nightmares.

Of course, Cresselia isn't from the Johto Region; it's from Sinnoh.

Thinking of this, D couldn't help but wonder, "I wonder if Teddiursa will ever meet Ho-oh."

However, what he wanted was not Ho-oh, but its feather.

And he didn't want the feather to participate in the Rainbow Hero's Trial, but to cook with it.

It's just that... D suddenly thought of Marshadow, who examines the heart of anyone holding Ho-oh's feather.

Once Ho-oh tosses its feather to a chosen human, Marshadow sneaks into their shadow to check if their heart meets the conditions to become a Rainbow Hero.

If Marshadow and the members of Ho-oh's guard saw him cooking with Ho-oh's feather, their expressions would surely be priceless.

Fortunately, his bottom line is never to cook Pokemon; otherwise, Marshadow would definitely annihilate him on the spot in a fit of rage.

D put away the photo, planning to frame it and hang it on the wall as a keepsake.

As for the Silver Wing that fell off Lugia... it looks like he's going to have to bother Keri again.

Leaving the house chores to the Indeedee Sibling, D rode Koraidon straight to Keri's little cabin.

---

Seeing him arriving in a hurry, Keri raised an eyebrow: "Is there another ranch facility you want to build? Go ahead, do you want to build a greenhouse or a watermill this time?"

D grinned and pulled out the Silver Wing from his pocket: "This time, I'm not building anything. I want to borrow your crushing machine."

At his words, Keri turned her gaze to the feather emanating a silvery radiance.

"This is...?"

D initially thought of Lugia, but upon reconsidering, he found it difficult to explain, so he changed his mind midway: "It's Swanna's feather."

Swanna, an elegant and beautiful Pokemon, evolved from Ducklett, dances gracefully on the lake surface at dawn, and it gets its name from this.

"Swanna, huh? No wonder this feather is so beautiful." Keri exclaimed, "That Swanna must have been well-bred, its feathers seem to glow."

"Follow me, then!" Saying that, Keri turned around and led D to the workroom where the crushing machine was located.

She didn't ask D about the purpose of crushing the feather, just stood aside and started the machine: "Just put it inside."

D nodded and placed the feather inside.

Keri pressed the switch, and the entire crushing machine started rumbling.

Along with the machine's sound, the Silver Wing was swallowed by the machine.

D and Keri stood silently waiting, but suddenly a fierce wind erupted from the machine's outlet.

The wind instantly swept through the entire room, blowing Keri's drawings like snowflakes fluttering in the air.

Seeing this, Keri was stunned and asked amidst the wind: "What happened?"

D shielded himself from the wind with his arm, guessing wildly: "Maybe it's the Flying Type Energy stored in Swanna's feather?"

Keri was dumbfounded: "That's too exaggerated, isn't it?"

But soon, D realized something was wrong.

Because he saw silvery glimmers being blown out from the machine's inlet and outlet by the strong wind!

It was the crushed Silver Wing!

Damn it! He should have known!

A Silver Wing capable of summoning Lugia, how could it be as normal as Zapdos's feather? It must contain a large amount of energy, energy that Lugia could sense!

D hurriedly reached to grab it, but caught nothing, only able to watch the silvery glimmers vanish in the wind.

Heartache! It's such a pity!

Keri hesitated whether to turn off the machine.

Luckily, the wind stopped at that moment.

The scattered drawings fell like snowflakes, leaving the room in a mess.

D's heart skipped a beat, and he quickly apologized to Keri, feeling deeply remorseful: "I'm very sorry, I didn't know the energy in this feather was so exaggerated!"

Most of the drawings here were casual sketches by Keri, not of great importance, but seeing D so tense, Keri couldn't help but joke:

"If you really want to apologize, bring me some pumpkins later! Halloween is coming, and I want to decorate the house with them."

Seeing Keri's lack of anger, D breathed a sigh of relief and promised: "Of course, there will be as many pumpkins as you want!"

He planned to have Dragonite bring them to Keri later.

Keri chuckled: "Alright, I'm just teasing you, it's my first time seeing you so nervous."

D was a bit embarrassed: "Because I don't like causing trouble for others."

Keri opened the machine's outlet and looked at the remaining feather powder inside, asking: "Do you still want this?"

"Yes!" D hurriedly stepped forward and carefully filled the glass bottle he had prepared with the remaining powder.

Then, the two tidied up the room together.

D bid farewell to Keri, and on his way riding Koraidon back to the ranch, he had the chance to examine the effects of the feather powder.

[Lugia's Feather Powder: A legendary ingredient containing a large amount of Flying Type Energy powder, perhaps can be used to make dishes, effects unknown.]

---

# 210: Chapter 207: Storm Steamed Bun (Double Chapter Combined)

"It really is the same series of ingredients as the feather powder of Zapdos!"

Seeing the information on these feather powders, D calmed down, surprisingly not appearing too shocked.

Upon returning to the ranch, he waved to his few Pokemon, saying, "You go play by yourselves, I have something to do."

The Naclstacks obediently ran off to play on their own.

D then turned back into the house, entering the kitchen to prepare the ingredients for this cooking session.

Firstly, the most important was flour, followed by the tools for kneading dough.

He planned to blend these feather powders into the flour like last time, to create buns.

However, it was regrettable that a considerable amount of flying type energy contained in Lugia's feather powder had dissipated during the grinding process.

The remaining amount...

D held the transparent glass bottle up to his face, gazing at the thin layer of silver-white powder at the bottom.

"Compared to Zapdos' feather powder, there's far too little, probably only a quarter?" D thought to himself, regretting this, but it's unavoidable.

Another issue arose.

Would the small quantity affect the cooking effect?

After a brief contemplation, D decided to test it first.

He began kneading the dough, then mixed the silver-white powder into the flour, pouring in water and starting to knead.

Like touching Zapdos' feather powder, which gives a tingly feeling.

Upon touching Lugia's feather powder, D felt a strong breeze circling around his palm, with flying type energy turning into milky white air currents, surging continuously in the container.

The energy sometimes intensified and sometimes softened; when the flour fully absorbed the feather powder, the milky white air currents didn't disappear, still lingering on the dough, unable to be dispelled.

As if special effects were applied.

D raised his eyebrows with surprise, "Amazing."

The dough was kneaded, but it needed some time to ferment.

D properly handled the dough, covering it with cling film, setting it aside for fermentation, which would take about three to four hours.

D wasn't in a rush, opting to lounge on the couch watching TV while waiting.

The Indeedee siblings stayed like loyal butlers on the side.

Sister Indeedee thoughtfully prepared a cup of hot tea for D.

D took a sip, feeling perfectly cozy.

He looked at the two standing Indeedee, saying to them, "Don't just stand there, take a seat and rest a while!"

"Indeedee~"

Sister Indeedee nodded happily, sitting on the sofa close to D, while brother Indeedee sat at the other end.

Meanwhile, in the depths of the ocean.

In a quiet, profound sea region, Lugia, having just returned home ready for some good sleep, suddenly opened its eyes wide.

It sensed that a feather had lost contact with it.

Filled with astonishment and confusion, Lugia's eyes widened.

Legendary Pokemon like it rarely let feathers fall casually.

Only kind-hearted human trainers would receive its feathers.

If they brought the feather to the deepest cave of the Whirl Islands and sincerely prayed, it would appear and meet them, signifying its acknowledgment of their courage and heart.

Those silver feathers contained its unique energy, which was why, wherever the feather was, it could sense it.

In ancient times long ago, it even promised a few human friends that if they faced danger and needed help, they could call upon it with the Silver Wing.

At that time, it would emerge from the sea to assist.

Yet now...

It lost contact with a Silver Wing, unable to sense the feather's energy waves anymore.

Lugia was grave.

What has happened to that feather?

*

At two in the afternoon, the dough had finished fermenting.

D uncovered the cling film atop the dough, forming buns to officially start steaming buns.

Due to the limited key ingredient, this time D barely managed to make six buns.

But he wasn't certain if these buns would possess the Thunder God's effect like the Lightning Steamed Bun.

Once the buns at the pot's bottom were steamed, D finally found the answer to his doubts.

[Storm Steamed Bun (S+): Legendary Cuisine, made from powder of feathers from a legendary Pokemon. It's said that consuming it allows one to harness storms and weather.

Cooking Effect: Sea God (1/6), upon consumption, gives Pokemon the power of weather control, able to control the weather as freely as the owner of the feather. Flying-type Pokemon exclusive.

Evaluation: Unique in the world. Feed your flying type Pokemon! However, due to some ingredient ratio issues, the cuisine's original effect cannot be fully realized.]

"Just as I thought," D murmured.

The Sea God followed by 1/6 suggested Pokemon must eat six buns consecutively to fully realize the effect?

Coincidentally, he just made six buns.

D immediately prepared to try it.

In his ranch, there happens to be a flying type Pokemon.

A vision of Dragonite's round figure surfaced in D's mind.

He sent Indeedee to call Dragonite, who was playing outside.

---

Soon, Dragonite came running over behind Indeedee brother.

It looked at D with confusion: "Roar?"

D cheerfully brought the six steaming buns in front of Dragonite: "Want to eat them all?"

Dragonite's nostrils flared; it smelled the buns but felt troubled because, as a Dragonite, it didn't like eating buns.

Seeing this, D raised an eyebrow: "Don't you want to become the strongest Dragonite in the world? Eat them, and you can achieve your dream."

Dragonite, knowing D's ability, immediately widened its eyes and looked over excitedly: "Roar?"

D nodded: "Of course, this is a dish I made especially for you."

Hearing this, Dragonite hesitated no more. Regardless of the taste, it opened its mouth wide and swallowed the buns one by one.

When Dragonite finished the last bun, D saw an undeniable stream of air emerging from its body.

The wind started blowing inside the house.

D's eyelids twitched. He quickly pushed Dragonite outside: "Go outside!"

Once Dragonite stepped outside, the energy in its body exploded forth.

The originally clear sky was quickly covered by a thick, heavy cloud.

The commotion here immediately caught the attention of the other Pokemon on the pasture.

Five Dragonairs and two wild Dragonites flew over, eyes wide with shock at Dragonite's powerful aura.

Cyclizar sensed a strong presence, immediately ran over without looking back to watch.

Only Koraidon, with a flickering gaze, calmly looked up and then continued to nap on the grass.

At this moment, D and a group of Pokemon made the same move.

They all looked up at the sky, gazing at the enormous cloud.

Dragonite, on the grass, also sensed the changes happening within its body.

Its eyes sparkled with excitement, recalling what D once told it — Someday, the whole world will be covered by your rain!

Dragonite felt that it might really be able to achieve that now!

It decided to give it a try!

Dragonite let out a long roar to the sky and used its signature move, one it had practiced countless times and used every day—Rain Dance!

With a slight movement of Dragonite's mind,

the next second, light rain began to fall from the sky.

The gentle autumn rain immediately enveloped the entire pasture and extended further.

Dragonite still felt unsatisfied. It simply spread its wings, flew high into the sky, dancing fiercely in the rain while fully releasing the energy the magical buns had given it.

At the same time, the rain covered Porto Marinada Town, Casseroya Lake, Cascarrafa City,

and even the usually hot and dry Asado Desert began to rain.

Rain fell onto a Sandile lying in the yellow sand, acting as a predator waiting for prey to pass by its front.

The sudden coolness left this Sandile dazed in place; it looked up, its big black eyes filled with confusion.

In Porto Marinada Town, people and Pokemon walking on the streets hurried to take shelter under shop eaves from the rain.

A tardy office worker arrived under an eave, shook the water off his briefcase, and wondered:

"Strange, didn't the weather forecast say it would be partly cloudy to sunny today? Why did it suddenly start raining?"

He wasn't the only one finding it strange. At the same time, weather researchers at the Paldea Meteorological Station, located north of Cascarrafa City, west of the Giant Pit, stood under the institute's eaves, looking at the unusual heavy rain with bewilderment.

"What's going on? Machine malfunction?"

"No, doesn't seem like it. The rain came too strangely; one moment it was a sunny day, the next it rained, without any sign."

"This phenomenon, doesn't it seem like a Pokemon move?"

"Are you suggesting... Rain Dance?"

"Meteorological satellites detected rain in Cascarrafa City, Asado Desert, Porto Marinada Town, Casseroya Lake... the entire northwestern part of the Paldea Region is raining!"

Upon hearing this, the weather researchers were dumbfounded.

After a long while, someone finally exclaimed in shock, "Is this really caused by a Pokemon?"

The power required for such a Rain Dance must belong to an incredibly strong Pokemon!

Could it be a wild Legendary Pokemon has come to the Paldea Region?

Just like the incident on the Orange Archipelago not long ago, where the rampage of a few Legendary Pokemon caused worldwide weather changes.

Pokemon League.

Geeta, the current reigning champion of the Paldea Region, stood with hands behind her back by the window, looking up at the drizzle outside.

As an experienced Pokemon Trainer, Geeta keenly sensed the abnormality of this rain.

It's definitely not a normal weather change, but rather artificial, to be precise, caused by some unknown Pokemon.

Her fingers gently curled, and she looked pensive.

"I wonder what kind of Pokemon it is."

Geeta recalled that, to her memory, there aren't any Legendary Pokemon in the Paldea Region capable of affecting the weather.

This means the Pokemon causing this rain might very well have been brought to the Paldea Region by some mysterious trainer.

A trainer capable of breeding...capturing such a powerful Pokemon, is truly a talent!

"I wonder if the Paldea League could recruit them." Geeta thought subconsciously.

But she quickly realized her hypothesis hadn't been confirmed yet.

Geeta chuckled to herself, shaking her head. She closed the window and stopped paying attention.

If such a powerful trainer exists, then they will surely appear before the world one day, and she'll approach them then.

*

In the pasture.

D and the other Pokemon, including Cyclizar, took shelter under the eaves, looking up at Dragonite in the sky.

"Is this the power of Lugia?" D murmured to himself.

Lugia, known as the Sea God.

But this Sea God is different from the Sea God.

Unlike the embodiment of the ocean, Kyogre, who expands the sea, Lugia leans more towards controlling ocean currents and world weather.

It possesses the incredible power to calm the raging sea; it is said that when Lugia flaps its wings, it can cause a forty-day storm.

From these legendary records, Lugia's abilities are remarkable.

Airflow, weather, cyclones, hurricanes...

"Overall, the storm bun's effect exceeded my expectations," D concluded internally. "It also fulfilled one of Dragonite's wishes."

Watching the endless rain, D raised an eyebrow.

It seems that he wasn't lying to Dragonite when he said those words before?

D reflected with sentiment. When he looked up in search of Dragonite in the sky, he suddenly found it had disappeared.

D was slightly stunned, and asked Cyclizar beside him: "Where's Dragonite?"

Cyclizar pointed to the sky: "Gero."

Meaning Dragonite flew even higher.

D was speechless, this guy, really...

Forget it, let it have its fun!

D sighed helplessly, turned around, and went back to the kitchen to wash the dishes.

By the time he finished cleaning the pot, the rain outside had finally stopped.

Under the sunlight, a brilliant rainbow appeared in the air.

At that moment, the Dragonite, having had enough fun, finally returned to the pasture.

It jubilantly rushed into the house, stretched out its large claw, and hugged D, who was wiping the table.

"Roar~"

D sighed helplessly: "Are you that happy?"

Dragonite nodded repeatedly: "Roar!"

Seeing Dragonite's expression, D feared Dragonite might become arrogant because of this.

He thought for a moment and reminded, "You can't become arrogant, you know? You still have to work hard, practice diligently, and truly integrate this power. Although you're already strong, there are many Pokemon in this world much stronger than us."

Dragonite blinked its eyes and nodded happily.

"Roar!"

It was well aware of this.

Dragonite's mind conjured the memory of when it once passed through a typhoon's eye, seeing that pitch-black, powerful, and mysterious Pokemon at the typhoon's top.

So, it would continue to train hard!

---

---

---

---

# 211: Chapter 208: Lugia, Your Ultimate Move Is Mine Now!

"Beside this, are there any other new moves?" D asked again.

New moves? Dragonite blinked its big eyes, paused for a moment, then had a sudden realization as a small light bulb lit up above its head.

"Roar!"

D raised an eyebrow slightly: "Alright then, let's see what changes you have!"

Dragonite immediately began demonstrating.

It flew to a relatively open grassy area, and D, along with a bunch of Pokémon, hurried over, standing in a row to observe Dragonite.

Dragonite focused and held its breath, carefully sensing the changes within its body.

After eating those few buns, it felt it could perceive the flow of air currents more clearly, and could manipulate these currents and Flying Type energy at will.

As for the newly generated changes...

Dragonite glanced at D, then suddenly flapped its wings, a gale sweeping forth instantly, forming powerful wind vortexes, blasting ahead.

Witnessing this scene, D was momentarily stunned.

"Is this... Hurricane? No, not quite."

D had seen Dragonite use Hurricane before, the preparatory motion of Hurricane was quite different from the current move.

"Compared to Hurricane, this seems more like the Flying Type's Gust." He murmured thoughtfully: "However, the power and appearance seem enhanced, possibly because the Storm Steamed Bun significantly boosted Dragonite's talent and control in the Flying Type."

D then recalled the Rain Dance move Dragonite had just performed.

Although he couldn't see the specific boundaries, the area covered by the rain curtain surely included Porto Marinada Town.

A question couldn't help but surface in D's mind.

Could the normal Pokémon's Rain Dance have such a large range?

Evidently not.

While pondering, Dragonite began to demonstrate its second move.

Its small dragon wings gathered together, seemingly brewing something.

The next second, D saw a storm surge from Dragonite's front.

The tightly compressed storm began rotating rapidly, eventually forming an air vortex, launched from Dragonite's front.

Dragonite deliberately controlled the direction of the move.

The air vortex exploded violently mid-air, transforming into a more intense cyclonic force, swirling and lingering.

One can imagine the scene if opposing Pokémon were hit by this move.

D: "..."

Seeing this, D fell into an unusual silence.

If he wasn't mistaken, the move just now seemed to be Lugia's exclusive move, Aeroblast, right?

Did Dragonite just copy over that exclusive move?

"Roar?"

Finishing the demonstration, Dragonite landed excitedly in front of D, wearing a look pleading for praise.

"...Well done." D could only say.

It's just unknown what Lugia would feel seeing this.

Dragonite immediately became pleased, cheering: "Roar!"

And that's not all it can do!

If it were on the ocean, it could, like its giant Dragonite predecessor, instantly stir up a storm, trigger a tsunami, and with the addition of thunder...

Such power was something Dragonite never previously imagined!

Amid its excitement, Dragonite didn't forget to give D a big hug.

It hadn't forgotten that everything it had today was thanks to D.

Even without those magical buns, it was very satisfied.

Seeing this, the two nearby wild Dragonite and five Dragonair were filled with envy.

They naturally understood the reason for Dragonite's growth.

So in the next moment, several large and small dragons all came surrounding, indicating they also wanted it.

Seeing their actions, Dragonite proudly flaunted: "Roar!"

See that? Such a good Trainer is its own!

All the dragons: "..."

D helplessly shrugged: "That's all. That kind of cuisine was a one-time existence, cannot be sought or obtained unless you bring me back Lugia's feather powder."

A Dragonair blinked, puzzled: "Roar?"

What is Lugia?

"Lugia..." D explained: "That's the legendary Sea God, asleep at the bottom of a deep trench, possessing incredible strength capable of subduing the turbulent sea, always guarding the ocean and the Pokémon dwelling within."

The two wild Dragonite and five Dragonair were filled with confusion, their expressions becoming somewhat troubled.

They had never heard of nor seen such a Pokémon, even searching would be impossible.

The legendary Sea God...

Suddenly, the shiny Dragonair linked this to an incident at D Ranch.

That was D always offering tribute to a legendary Pokémon, and not only that, he even referred to it as the God of Agriculture.

According to information it got from Butterfree, when D prayed to that God of Agriculture, the crops on the ranch would suddenly grow the next morning.

This was the blessing D received from that deity.

With this in mind, Dragonair pondered if they could do it like D, by offering cherished berries to the Sea God on Dragonite Island, would the Sea God respond to their prayers?

They did not want big pumpkins or corn; they only desired a trivial feather, just like the scales they occasionally shed.

Dragonair thought of a good idea and immediately whispered it to the other Dragonair and Dragonite.

Hearing this, the Pokémon's eyes lit up, their faces full of eager anticipation.

So they didn't stay to play at the ranch and immediately prepared to head back to Dragonite Island, not forgetting to grab their Poffin Box before leaving.

Seeing them leaving, D was quite surprised: "Leaving so early today? Not playing a bit longer?"

"Roar!"

The two wild Dragonite responded without turning their heads, while the five Dragonair followed closely behind, heading toward Dragonite Island.

D was speechless.

Having not eaten the Translation Nuts, he couldn't understand Pokémon speech, but judging by the appearance of this group of Dragonair, they seemed to have something important to do?

D didn't ponder too long; he turned back to his own Dragonite, summarizing the enhancements the Storm Steamed Bun had brought it.

"Overall, not many new moves were learned; rather, there was more enhancement in controlling Flying Type energy."

For example, although Dragonite used to influence nearby weather with mysterious aura releases, it couldn't freely manipulate airflow.

But now it can.

"I'd really like to know what Lugia's expression would be seeing Dragonite use Aeroblast."

Could this be considered a sort of ntr up front?

It's a pity D currently had no way to travel far, and even if he went to Johto, it's uncertain if he could meet Lugia.

Because the Silver Wing was already made into buns, it couldn't summon anything currently.

However, D wasn't discouraged, for in life, there'd always be opportunities!

*

Life on Dragon Island remained unchanged.

Leisurely, relaxed, and lively.

After returning to the island from D Ranch, Dragonair and Dragonite immediately rummaged through the shrubbery for their cherished berries.

Dragonair hadn't grown hands yet and couldn't hold them, so they had to hand them over for the two Dragonite to hug.

They then followed the Dragonite to the seaside.

With excitement in their hearts, they looked at the calm blue sea, and then sent the berries they hugged into the ocean.

Accompanied by the crashing waves, the berries bobbed up and down in the water, quickly being absorbed into the sea.

Dragonites stood on the shore watching them go, couldn't help but wonder: would the Sea God receive them?

Then, several dragonites from the island noticed their actions.

Curious, they gathered around, asking eagerly: "Roar?" (What are you doing?)

Dragonite cheerfully replied: "Roar!" (Gifting the Sea God. D said if we do this, the Sea God would hear our prayers and respond, gifting us better presents!)

Hearing this, the shiny Dragonair couldn't help but feel somewhat guilty.

For D never said such a thing; it was a guess on Dragonair's part based on D's daily activities.

Dragonair carefully recalled.

Their actions were all based on D's doings; surely it should be fine?

---

---

---

---

# 212: Chapter 209: Stay Steady

It's a brand new day again.

Ever since eating the Storm Steamed Bun made by D, Dragonite felt it had become the most powerful Pokemon on the ranch.

This made it incredibly excited, even its walk had a strut.

After delivering the Miltank's fresh milk to Mrs. Dante, it set its sights on Koraidon, the previous top Pokemon on the ranch.

Looking at the relaxed and lazy-looking Koraidon lying on the grass, Dragonite ran up and stood there staring at it.

Noticing this unavoidable gaze, Koraidon lifted its eyes and looked over, its tone puzzled: "Ah-gar-sii?"

Dragonite eagerly said, "Awwooo?" (Can I spar with you?)

Koraidon: ?

This early in the morning, did it hear wrong?

Are you sure? Koraidon cast a doubtful gaze at Dragonite.

Dragonite nodded repeatedly: "Awwooo!"

Koraidon fell silent.

It originally didn't want to fuss with this Dragonite, but after thinking about it carefully, it realized that Dragonite seemed to be on cloud nine since yesterday.

Because it thinks it's now the strongest Pokemon on the ranch.

As for how Koraidon knew about this.

It was because Dragonite had mentioned it to Cyclizar while watching the moon together last night.

At the time, Cyclizar didn't feel much, just very envious, but now...

How could this be? It was the most powerful Pokemon by D's side!

Koraidon's expression became serious, and it began to take on a competitive spirit against Dragonite.

D's strongest partner could only be it!

So when D just came out of the Glass Greenhouse, he saw Cyclizar rushing towards him, saying that Koraidon and Dragonite had started fighting.

D was taken aback for a moment, quickly dropped the Psyduck Spray Bottle in his hand, and followed Cyclizar to the Gogoat pen.

Beside the pen was the battle arena he had built to welcome challengers participating in the Grass Trial.

At this moment, the two Pokemon had already arrived there, each standing at one end of the battle arena, following the rules.

"What's going on?" D quickly asked.

"Awwooo!" Dragonite responded happily.

"Ah-gar-sii!" Koraidon's expression was also normal, as if this was just an ordinary match.

D initially worried, but seeing the two Pokemon like this, he gradually let go of his concerns.

Because in this world, other trainers would also have their Pokemon battle each other as sparring partners during their travels.

"Alright then, let me be your referee!"

To be honest, he was quite curious too.

In the games, while Koraidon is a Legendary Pokemon, its performance was nowhere near as strong as those Legendary Pokemon from previous games.

And now, would Dragonite, who ate the Storm Steamed Bun, be a match for Koraidon?

Seeing both of them ready, D blew a whistle and then announced, "The battle begins!"

The two Pokemon did not attack immediately.

Dragonite, full of fighting spirit, looked at Koraidon on the opposite side and shouted: "Awwooo!" (I'm going to attack now!)

Koraidon was speechless: "…Ah-gar-sii."

Seeing this, Dragonite moved.

It gathered its wings, starting to condense an air vortex in front of it.

However, just as Dragonite made a move, Koraidon also acted.

Its crimson figure moved like lightning, almost instantly, it charged up to Dragonite, abandoning defense and launched an assault.

"Such speed!" D was shocked, was this Koraidon's full power?

The punch, capable of splitting the earth, slammed into Dragonite, interrupting its move. Before it could react, Koraidon unleashed a Breaking Swipe sweeping across.

Dragonite only saw darkness in front of its eyes, and with a single encounter, it was knocked out by Koraidon, its eyes turned into spirals with stars circling above its head.

"It's over?" D was full of disbelief.

Koraidon, with an absolute dominant stance, turned its back on the fallen Dragonite.

It was the strongest Pokemon beside D!

Koraidon looked at D, and when its gaze met D's, it returned to its original simple and honest demeanor.

"Ah-gar-sii~"

"Amazing!" D exclaimed in shock.

Koraidon scratched its head sheepishly at D's praise.

It still had a long way to go!

Images from back in Zone Zero flashed in Koraidon's mind.

Back then, it was still a Cyclizar.

Standing on the dazzling crystalline ground, it gazed longingly up at the powerful Koraidon fighting one against four dragon-types without falling behind.

At that time, it made a wish in its heart.

If possible, it too wanted to become as powerful a Koraidon to protect D and always stay by his side.

Now, it had achieved that.

It had become the strongest Pokemon by D's side.

D stepped forward to treat Dragonite.

Dragonite slowly opened its eyes from unconsciousness.

With a grey, dusty look, it seemed rather pitiable.

D cleared his throat and said, "Any thoughts?"

Dragonite sheepishly scratched the back of its head: "Awwooo..."

It realized it was wrong, it shouldn't have been so arrogant.

Seeing Dragonite in this state, D couldn't help but speculate in his heart.

It was impossible for Koraidon to challenge Dragonite on its own.

Based on his understanding of the two Pokemon, this battle was most likely because Dragonite felt it became too strong, so it challenged the even mightier Koraidon.

Thinking of this, D smiled and ruffled its head: "Don't be upset, you've already done quite well."

He recalled how this Dragonite of his would go to Dragonite Island to show off whenever it got stronger, and couldn't help but educate:

"Also, as dragons, we need to learn to be low-key, you know? Be more composed, Koraidon is family living with us, losing to it wouldn't make you look weak, but if it were another Pokemon, it might ridicule you."

D began to give examples to Dragonite, like if it bragged about its strength to a wild Dragonite and then the other beat it and said, "Is that all? I thought you'd be more impressive!"

Hearing this, Dragonite imagined itself and its face immediately turned red, looking both embarrassed and angry.

"So what should we do in such a situation?" D asked, while taking out a Translation Nuts Cake from his pocket and popping it in his mouth.

Dragonite unhesitatingly said, "Awwooo!" (Challenge again!)

D: "And if you can't win?"

Dragonite thought carefully and said with difficulty: "Awwooo..."

It didn't know.

D: "That's why! Do you understand what I meant now? Being low-key doesn't hurt."

Dragonite nodded.

D continued, "It's not your fault because your past experiences led to this. I just want you to understand something."

Dragonite looked up.

D looked into its eyes: "Strength is for protecting yourself and your companions, not something to boast to others about."

Hearing this, Dragonite was slightly stunned.

It glanced at Koraidon nearby, as if it understood something, then seriously nodded at D.

"Awwooo!"

D smiled and patted Dragonite's head.

"Your strength is quite good now, and I have something I need your help with, but it'll be a while yet."

That's the Spectrier and Glastrier in the Galar Region.

D planned to take Dragonite to the Galar Region, and if taming those horses required battling as in the games, he could let Dragonite try its hand against Spectrier and Glastrier without Calyrex riding them.

Those two with a species strength of 580, he wondered if they would be able to defeat a Dragonite with a species strength of 600.

---

---

---

---

# 213: Chapter 210: Super Berry Platter (4k)

League Year 198, August 12th, Autumn.

After more than eight months of development, everything on the farm gradually stabilized.

With the addition of numerous Pokemon, the productivity of the farm was greatly liberated, and D no longer needed to personally handle everything like at the beginning.

The Indeedee Sibling managed the house and farm in an orderly manner, and aside from looking after D's daily life, their favorite place to be was the Pokemon Tavern.

Over in the fields, Diglett helped with plowing and turning soil, watering was still done by Dragonite, and even harvesting crops no longer required D to pick them himself.

Because the Psychic Type Indeedee Sibling used Telekinesis to control the mature crops, picking them and piling them in the farm.

This made D's daily work much lighter; he either played with the Pokemon or sat in the garden drinking tea and reading, living quite comfortably.

This afternoon, another batch of berries ripened in the Berry Garden.

Just as the berries at home had been eaten up, D carried a basket to pick them.

Walking through the fragrant orchard, even though he had seen it a few times before, looking at the heavy berries on the branches still filled D with wonder.

Because after the berry trees grew large, the berries grew too quickly.

The first batch picked would mature again in less than seven days.

"No wonder berries in this world are so cheap..." D thought about the market price of 20 League currency for one berry and couldn't help but say to himself.

This price, a bottle of ordinary MooMoo milk could buy a bunch.

Wild berry trees must be like this too?

Such plentiful berry resources must be the food source for wild Pokemon living in mountains and dense forests.

"Huh? More golden and silver berries appeared?"

At this moment, D suddenly noticed a golden Razz Berry hanging on a tree.

Not only that, a string of golden Nanab Berries was also hanging on the neighboring tree.

This surprised D greatly.

The last time he picked berries, he also encountered many golden and silver berries, all of which were very rare varieties.

Looking at it this way, the chance of rare berries appearing is quite high!

At least higher than the mutated crops in the field.

D stepped forward and picked them, staring at the berries, a new idea suddenly came to him.

——Berry Platter!

Based on his observations over the past few months, if the quality and rarity of ingredients used in cooking were enhanced, then the Cooking Effect would change accordingly.

The last few golden berries were too few, plus the Silver Wing attracted his attention, so D didn't think much about it then.

Now, perhaps he should give it a try?

If he used these rare berries to make a Berry Platter, would the Berry Platter's effect vary?

Thinking this, D immediately carried the basket full of berries back to the kitchen.

His brother Indeedee, who was cleaning, saw his actions and looked puzzled.

Why cook at this hour?

If it remembers correctly, D had several sandwiches and a large bowl of porridge for breakfast!

D noticed his brother Indeedee's gaze, looked up, and saw its puzzled face, so he explained:

"I suddenly thought of a new dish... Uh, not exactly new, to be precise, it's an improved version of a dish."

Upon hearing this, brother Indeedee instantly understood.

It nodded, didn't ask more, but still couldn't help but be curious about the dish.

So it continued to clean earnestly, while throwing little glances towards the kitchen area.

D didn't notice brother Indeedee's little actions.

He picked out all the golden and silver berries from the basket, washed them clean with water, then cut them into irregular berry pieces and arranged them on a white plate.

And thus, a simple Berry Platter was completed.

These berry chunks gleamed gold and silver on the white porcelain plate, looking very precious.

D cast his eyes towards them.

The next second, information about the improved Berry Platter appeared in his mind.

[Super Berry Platter (A-): A gourmet dish made with various berries, blending the flavors of different berries, favored by some Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Skill Refinement (large), after consumption, a Pokemon's move will randomly change, the chance of learning rare moves is enhanced.

Evaluation: A basic dish, easy to make, a good choice for home travel.]

"Sure enough, it changed!" D widened his eyes.

Next, he made a regular Berry Platter and placed them together to compare.

"The dish's level increased from a rather poor C- to A-, Skill Refinement also gained a large effect."

Looking at the final words, D murmured in thought: "An increased chance to learn rare moves? Does that mean expanding the move pool by adding more powerful moves?"

"Unfortunately, the outcome is still uncontrollable." This made D feel remorseful, as this dish didn't even provide a guarantee, not friendly to unlucky folks.

At this moment, sister Indeedee happily ran in from outside.

---

It immediately saw D in the kitchen, and its eyes lit up: "Indeedee?"

Looking at his sister Indeedee who was running over, D smiled and explained: "This is a Berry Platter."

Saying this, he noticed that not far away, his brother Indeedee was also looking in this direction.

"Indeedee?" Sister Indeedee looked down at the golden berries on the white porcelain plate.

So beautiful! Can she taste it?

Sister Indeedee looked over with hopeful eyes.

But this made D a bit troubled.

He thought for a while and carefully explained: "This is different from ordinary dishes. After a Pokemon eats it, there's a fifty percent chance they will learn a new move, but also a fifty percent chance they will forget a move they already know."

"Indeedee?" Sister Indeedee was confused.

D laughed, "Scared?"

Sister Indeedee quickly shook her head: "Indeedee!" No way!

She looked down at the Berry Platter, her eyes filled with strong curiosity.

"Indeedee!" She wanted to try it!

Seeing Sister Indeedee's choice, D was not surprised.

Because although this Indeedee was a girl, she was very lively and outgoing, with a heart that loved adventure.

However, her brother Indeedee immediately cast a disapproving glance at her choice.

"Indeedee..." (You don't have many moves to begin with, what if you become a fool from eating?)

Sister Indeedee angrily put her hands on her hips: "Indeedee!" (Brother is the fool!)

D: "?"

Why did they suddenly start arguing?

He signaled the siblings to stop and smiled while saying: "It's not a permanent forgetfulness of the move. It's only temporary. With diligent practice, you can relearn it quickly."

"Butterfree ate it and learned an Electric Type move, Electroweb; Cyclizar ate it and learned Dragon Tail; Dragonite ate it and learned Extreme Speed."

"But later on, because Dragonite wanted to learn more moves, he ate more Berry Platters. The result was not only failing to learn anything new but also forgetting the Dragon Tail he barely learned, and he nearly cried from anxiety." D started animatedly recounting Dragonite's embarrassing history.

This was something the siblings had never heard before.

Their attention immediately shifted, and from D's description, complemented by their understanding of Dragonite during this period, they could almost imagine what Dragonite looked like at the time.

"Indeedee?" And then? Sister Indeedee couldn't help but ask.

D smiled slightly: "Later, Dragonite practiced tirelessly day and night, and after spending a week, finally remembered Dragon Tail again, but he didn't dare to eat Berry Platters anymore because he was afraid of forgetting other moves."

"So, do you still want to try it?" D asked.

Sister Indeedee seriously considered it, then a bright light burst forth in her eyes.

"Indeedee!"

It sounded exciting; she wanted to experience it too!

Seeing his sister's expression, Brother Indeedee knew he couldn't stop her.

But since D said it wouldn't harm the Pokemon, he didn't need to worry too much.

Brother Indeedee sighed helplessly.

Yet, he was also somewhat curious.

Would his sister learn a new move or forget a move she's already learned?

"You can't cry when you forget a move, okay?" D said with a smile.

"Indeedee!"

Sister Indeedee immediately let out a protesting cry.

She was already a reliable Indeedee who took care of others, and wouldn't cry like a child!

"Great, let's give it a try then!" D nodded.

He had previously prepared information records for the two Indeedees.

Brother Indeedee currently knows the moves: Stored Power, Play Nice, Helping Hand, Telekinesis, meditation.

Sister Indeedee currently knows the moves: Stored Power, Play Nice, Telekinesis, Helping Hand, Metronome.

D silently recalled these moves in his mind, then watched as Sister Indeedee began to enjoy that Super Berry Platter.

One piece, two pieces, three pieces...

When Sister Indeedee had eaten the entire Berry Platter, a magical wave suddenly appeared around her.

D's heart stirred, and his gaze was instantly drawn to the changes happening in Sister Indeedee.

"Did you learn a new move?" he asked.

Sister Indeedee carefully sensed for a moment, then nodded with bright eyes: "Indeedee!"

Such good luck! She actually learned a new move the first time! D promptly asked, "Which move is it?"

"Indeedee..." As for what move it was, Sister Indeedee wasn't sure.

She looked somewhat troubled, and after a moment of hesitation, she started to demonstrate.

Suddenly, a circle of pale pink ripples appeared around Sister Indeedee.

Then, an ethereal crescent moon vaguely appeared behind her.

D vaguely felt that something extra was in the surrounding air, seemingly released from Sister Indeedee's body as a magical force.

Although it was released, this force didn't dissipate but instead lingered in the air.

As this force was released, Sister Indeedee's body began to weaken, resembling a Pokemon that had lost its ability to fight.

Seeing this, D was slightly startled.

---

What kind of move is this?

Moon, user loses the ability to fight...

Suddenly, an unbelievable name appeared in his mind.

Lunar Dance!

Dream God Cresselia's exclusive move, Lunar Dance!

After using it, although the Pokemon itself will lose the ability to fight, it can restore the full strength of the next Pokemon and cure all abnormal states.

D was stunned.

Is his sister Indeedee really so lucky?

She suddenly learned a divine technique?

The mysterious energy lingering in the air seemed unable to find a target to use on, and ultimately returned to the body of his sister Indeedee.

His sister Indeedee, who was originally weak to the point of almost losing the ability to fight, immediately regained vitality.

Seeing this, the worried brother Indeedee beside her quietly breathed a sigh of relief.

Together with his sister, he looked at D, waiting for his explanation.

D: "If I'm not mistaken, this move should be Lunar Dance."

"Indeedee?" Lunar Dance?

His sister Indeedee, looking puzzled, had never heard of this move...

"I must say, your luck is really enviable." D explained in detail: "Lunar Dance's effect is that after initiation, it will heal the target Pokemon's all injuries and abnormal states, but it requires you to spend all your energy and effort, causing you to lose the ability to fight."

"This move is the exclusive move of the Legendary Pokemon Cresselia, only it can use this move."

Cresselia...

His sister Indeedee didn't understand, but she understood Legendary Pokemon.

Her eyes suddenly became bright.

She actually understood the move that only Legendary Pokemon can use! This is so exciting!

His sister Indeedee looked at her brother with joy, proudly saying: "Indeedee?" (Am I awesome?)

Brother: "Indeedee..."

His sister Indeedee turned to look at D, with an eager expression on her face.

She wanted to try it again!

D hesitated a little, he thought for a while, and still said: "Actually, I don't quite recommend, because this move Lunar Dance is very rare, if you accidentally forget it later, this kind of legendary move might be impossible to relearn through recall."

"I originally thought that since you have few moves now, if you learn a powerful move, even if you forget some it wouldn't be distressing, but not now."

"Indeedee..."

Sure enough, upon hearing this, his sister Indeedee showed a hesitant expression on her face.

She pondered for a while, and eventually gave up the intention to continue eating the Berry Platter.

D didn't force her either.

He rubbed his chin, looked at the remaining Berry Platter, and couldn't help but think: if only Teddiursa were here.

Teddiursa knows all basic physical attack moves like Scratch, Furry Swipes, and Surprise Hit.

These moves are different from Special Attack moves, even if forgotten, they can be quickly relearned through muscle memory.

Plus, it naturally has Super Luck, maybe it really could learn some very rare and powerful moves through this Super Berry Platter.

However, unfortunately, Teddiursa left too early.

D fell into contemplation: "Hmm... if in the future there's a Pokemon that wants to try, perhaps I can give them a Lucky Pudding to boost their luck before feeding them the Berry Platter, maybe it will increase the rate of understanding moves."

---

---

---

---

# 214: Chapter 211: Barley Beer, New Addition to the Tavern (4k)

Early the next morning.

Upon waking up, D habitually glanced at the calendar. Today was August 13th, and on the number 13, there was a conspicuous red circle drawn with a red pen.

That was a mark D made more than a month ago when brewing beer.

It signifies that today is the day the beer is ready.
