Thinking of this, D, who was still a bit drowsy, was instantly invigorated.

He got up and left the bedroom, bringing Pawmot and Koraidon to the living room.

In the kitchen, his sibling Indeedee had already started preparing a fragrant breakfast for everyone.

The brother Indeedee was tidying up the mess made by Alcremie and other Pokemon in the living room.

Cyclizar lay lazily in front of the fireplace, squinting as though it hadn't fully awakened.

Seeing this scene, Koraidon couldn't help but chime in: "Ah gaa hiss!"

How did it become so much like a lazy pig?

Hearing Koraidon's voice, Cyclizar instantly woke up. It opened its eyes to find everyone looking at it.

"Gaa oof?"

Cyclizar panicked a bit, thinking it might have done something wrong.

D, speechless: "It's fine, it'll be a while before we eat. You can continue napping for now."

Only then did Cyclizar relax, but after such a scare, it no longer felt sleepy. Simply, it shook its body and ran outside to breathe some fresh air.

The morning pasture always brings relaxation.

The breeze carried a faint floral scent mixed with the fragrance of fresh grass, with crystal-clear dewdrops hanging on the crop leaves in the fields.

A strolling Glaceon, a Dolliv performing Synthesis, diligent Combee, and wild Bird-type Pokemon perched on the rooftop - all of these combined created a leisurely yet bustling atmosphere.

Cyclizar loved it and soon joined in, running energetically across the grassy field.

In the living room.

Alcremie stood on the cabinet, watching the sister Indeedee prepare breakfast.

Pawmo jumped onto the sofa, its fluffy body lazily wrapped in the soft cushions.

Sunlight shone in through the floor-to-ceiling windows, and as Pawmo moved, a few fine floating hairs drifted in the air, clearly visible in the light beams.

D: "..."

Looks like they need to feed the fluffy Pokemon in the pasture some chicken soup.

He remembered that chicken soup had an effect on preventing shedding and could make the Pokemon oily and slippery.

Not just D, even the brother Indeedee noticed this scene.

It came over with a tool, cleaning up the floating hairs on the wooden floor.

D felt satisfied and once again couldn't help but admire the renowned Housekeeper Pokemon!

While breakfast was still being prepared, D opened the door leading to the cellar.

The cellar was dim, with a faint fresh fragrance in the air.

D sniffed carefully, able to catch a hint of the unique aroma produced by the fermentation of hops and malt.

"It's really done!"

With anticipation, D went down the stairs to the cellar, approaching the nearest oak barrel, turned the valve, and the golden beer began to flow out in a stream.

Following closely was the rich and mellow aroma of barley beer.

D called out to Koraidon, who was guarding the cellar entrance: "Bring a glass over!"

"Ah gaa hiss!"

Koraidon responded, quickly turning to fetch a glass from the living room.

D took it, filled it with beer, and then looked down, letting the beer's information float into his mind.

[Barley Beer (B): Beer fermented with malt and hops, an essential part of the tavern.

Cooking Effect: Intoxication. After drinking, it puts Pokemon in a tipsy state, bringing them temporary tranquility, allowing their body and mind to relax, and speeding up recovery from injuries in this state.

If Fighting Type Pokemon drink it before a battle, it might lead to some unexpected outcomes.

Evaluation: Basic barley beer, easy to make, a fine choice for home and travel.]

"Intoxication?" D was a bit surprised by such a straightforward effect, but after thinking about it, it seemed quite normal.

It can bring tranquility to a Pokemon's mind, allowing their body and mind to relax...

Perhaps those extremely exhausted Pokemon would appreciate such a drink.

However, what D found interesting was that the beer seemed to have a special effect on Fighting Type Pokemon?

Drinking it before a battle, could it help them perform a Drunken Fist style?

Coincidentally, his pasture also had a Fighting Type Pokemon!

D glanced up at Koraidon.

But after a brief contemplation, he decided against testing it on Koraidon.

He was worried that Koraidon might get drunk and start rampaging.

If it were an ordinary Pokemon, it would be fine, but Koraidon was a legendary Pokemon.

If it went on a drunken spree, no one on the pasture could stop it.

Having finished the beer, D was delighted and promptly went to share this news with the Indeedee siblings who were helping run the tavern.

"This beer can effectively help Pokemon relieve fatigue and let them relax. If injured Pokemon drink it, their recovery speed will also increase."

"In this way, we can add another drink to the tavern's menu in the future!"

"Aiye?"

Hearing about the magical effect, the sister Indeedee showed an excited expression on her small face.

---

D smiled and nodded. After finishing breakfast, he asked the pair of Indeedee to use Telekinesis to move a barrel full of beer to the tavern's bar.

This way, if any Pokemon wants to give it a try, the Indeedee only need to turn the valve and pour a glass.

Recently, the Pokemon tavern has been thriving under the management of the two Indeedee.

They not only act as bartenders and host guests but also prepare food for hungry patrons.

Besides the milk and Berry Juice prepared by D, they also specially prepared simple and easy-to-make sandwiches, berry pastries smeared with cream, and creamy popcorn made from corn for guests to choose from.

With their efforts and the word of mouth from the town's Pokemon, this little tavern, renovated from an old house, has clearly become a gathering place exclusively for Pokemon.

Although it deviated somewhat from his original intention of creating the tavern, this seems not too bad.

D usually doesn't go over to disturb this group of Pokemon unless there are ones he has never seen before or is interested in; then, he'd rush over to watch and eavesdrop on their conversations.

Today, intending to observe the effects of the beer, D specifically came to the tavern during its opening hours.

He had almost ten pieces of Translation Nuts Cake and stood behind the bar with the sister Indeedee to attend to guests.

The brother Indeedee stayed aside, acting as the bartender, ready to serve meals or clean up the floors and tables messed up by guests if needed.

At 6:30 PM, the tavern officially opened for business.

Not long after, the tavern's loyal patrons, the two Machamp from Keri's family, arrived.

They initially planned, as usual, to order a Berry Milk but suddenly found a new drink in the tavern today.

Machamp couldn't help but ask, "Nika?" (What's that? A new berry juice?)

The sister Indeedee smiled and shook her head, "Ai ee!" (No, it's beer that can relieve fatigue when you drink it!)

"Nika?!" Machamp was incredulous. Is there such a thing?

Sister Indeedee: "Ai ee?" (Would you like a cup?)

The two Machamp exchanged a glance and, ultimately unable to resist their curiosity, ordered two cups with the money Keri specially gave them.

Seeing this, D amusingly filled two large mugs of beer and served them.

The two Machamp sat at the table, looking down at the golden liquid in the glass, thinking the smell was a bit strange.

They hesitated a bit but eventually lowered their heads and took a small sip.

As the beer went down their throats, the cheeks of the two Machamp turned visibly red within seconds.

They successfully entered a tipsy state.

Experiencing being tipsy for the first time, the Machamp felt as if they were stepping on clouds, their bodies light, unable to feel any trace of worry or fatigue.

Though the drink's taste was somewhat weird, it surprisingly wasn't bad at all!

The two Machamp raised their cups and downed the beer, then sat there burping, wobbling their heads while staring at each other's faces.

Nobody spoke; they just quietly savored the unique atmosphere.

D: "..."

Uncertain if it was the overpowering effect of the beer or the poor alcohol tolerance of the two Machamp.

But when Quaquaval arrived, it saw them swaying on their chairs with expressions as if entranced by a Jynx's Disarming Voice.

This left Quaquaval stunned for a moment, and it curiously asked, "Ga?" (What happened?)

The sister Indeedee's face showed a gentle smile, "Ai ee." (They drank beer.)

Beer... no wonder!

Quaquaval had heard about this drink, meant for humans, with a somewhat strange taste that it didn't like.

Thus, it declined the sister Indeedee's recommendation and ordered a Berry Juice, sipping it slowly as usual.

As time passed, many more Pokemon guests arrived one after another.

Most were from the town, while some were wandering Pokemon who came upon hearing the buzz for free food and drinks.

There were a Tandemaus, Wattrel, Pikachu...

The remaining seats in the tavern continued to fill up, and those Pokemon chattered away, creating a lively atmosphere.

The Indeedee siblings happily watched this scene.

D, however, felt a bit regretful.

Because these Pokemon felt that the smell of the beer was odd, so after the Machamp, he couldn't sell another beer.

Just then, the tavern door was pushed open.

A Meowth with a crestfallen face and a gold coin on its forehead walked in from outside.

D recognized it.

According to Quaquaval, this was a Meowth wandering in Porto Marinada Town, making its living by opening treasure chests around town.

It was said that a Trainer once attempted to catch it but was refused, as it claimed to love its life of opening treasure chests every day.

By opening treasure chests, it meant rummaging through trash cans.

D had seen this Meowth a few times before, and it was usually cheerful and lively.

Why was it showing such an expression today?

Meowth walked to the bar, downcast, preparing to order a Berry Milk as usual.

It felt that only the sweet and aromatic milk could soothe its wounded heart.

However, suddenly, the sister Indeedee's gentle voice reached its ears, "Ai ee?" (The tavern has a new product today! It's a magical beer that can help relax your body and soothe your soul. Would you like to try it?)

---

Upon hearing this, Meowth froze for a moment.

It then thought that its wounded heart did indeed need some solace.

So Meowth nodded and, not in the mood to chat with Indeedee, simply said, "Meow." (Then I'll have a drink, Meow.)

It placed a 300-value coin on the bar; this was a treasure Meowth found using Pay Day, round and shiny, which it really liked.

Seeing that finally another guest ordered a beer, Sister Indeedee was very happy, immediately filled a large glass for it, and asked Brother to bring it to the table.

Meowth sat at an empty spot, let out a heavy sigh, then picked up the glass and took a sip of beer.

Upon hearing its sigh, Quaquaval from the fire brigade turned its head to look over: "Quack?" (What's going on today?)

Seeing someone inquire, Meowth immediately began to express its heartache: "Meow..." (I'm so foolish, really, I knew that rich Delcatty wouldn't like me, but I couldn't help confessing my love to it, Meow...)

Quaquaval: "..."

A sudden spark lit up in Meowth's eyes: "Meow!" (What a beautiful Delcatty she is, Meow! She's so beautiful, so noble and elegant, Meow! The moment I saw her, I knew my love had arrived!)

Then, Meowth's expression became confused yet despondent: "Meow..." (But it rejected my confession, and once again I've lost my love, Meow...)

Speaking thus, Meowth took another big gulp of beer.

Its expression was dazed, its face flushed, smoothly entering a tipsy state.

Upon hearing Meowth's words, D was taken aback.

Goodness, this is truly a romantic Meowth!

A drunken Meowth seemed to have forgotten its recent heartbreak.

It only felt light all over, like it was floating among clouds, wrapped in soft and white clouds, as if time had stood still.

"Meow!" (This feels so good, Meow!)

Seeing this scene, the other Pokemon were shocked, what's happening to this Meowth?

Clearly, a moment ago it was sad about the heartbreak, but now it showed this expression.

D couldn't help but explain, "This is the effect of beer; it can help Pokemon temporarily forget sad things, relax their body, relieve fatigue. After drinking it, as long as they go home and sleep well, by tomorrow morning, everything will get better."

Upon hearing this, many Pokemon were amazed.

Does beer really have such effects?

Some Pokemon who weren't planning to drink now showed eager expressions.

Seeing this, D raised an eyebrow, "Could it be that you've also had a breakup?"

"Pikachu!"

"Squeak squeak!"

"..."

A group of Pokemon quickly shook their heads, they certainly hadn't!

Many Pokemon also covered their mouths and chuckled, creating a joyful atmosphere inside the tavern.

D thus chatted with them, but just then, the tavern's door was pushed open once more.

At this point, the night was deep; D thought it might be Miss Jenny with her Growlithe coming over for a patrol, but to his surprise, he looked up to see a large group of unexpected figures.

Squirtle, Venusaur, Pikachu, Meowth...

The Pokémon that pushed open the door was a Golduck, evolved from Psyduck.

Seeing so many Pokemon, D's face showed a stunned expression.

The group of Pokemon also saw him, but in the next moment, they all made way.

Behind them, a creature with humanoid form and feline Pokemon characteristics stood in the darkness, with eyes showing no discernible emotions, calmly looking over.

---

---

---

---

# 215: Chapter 212: Drunken Mewtwo (4k)

Mewtwo?

Seeing the figure of the newcomer, D froze on the spot.

At that moment, many thoughts raced through his mind.

Why did this figure suddenly show up on his turf?

Or rather, how did Mewtwo know about this place?

D suddenly remembered the photo that Teddiursa and Mew had sent back earlier.

The one taken with Mewtwo and those cloned Pokemon.

Thinking of this, D had a rough idea in mind.

He stood there for a long time without coming back to his senses, whereas his sister, Indeedee, after a momentary surprise, gave Mewtwo and the cloned Pokemon a warm smile.

"Ai yi~" (Welcome~)

Mewtwo said nothing.

Its gaze calmly swept over the Pokemon in the tavern, finally resting on the only human in the room.

Mewtwo thought to itself: "So this is the human chosen by Mew?"

"He doesn't seem any different from the humans I've seen before."

"No, perhaps I haven't discovered it yet. The memories from Mew and Teddiursa tell me his name should be D."

"D..."

Mewtwo silently repeated the name in its heart.

Seeing that this Pokemon seemed unfriendly, Indeedee couldn't help but feel a bit worried.

The presence it gave off was quite intimidating, and it was trailing a large group of menacing Pokemon behind it.

She cautiously glanced at D.

D gave her a reassuring look, smiled, and said, "Welcome to the Pokemon Tavern, a place specifically catered for Pokemon. May I ask what you would like to drink?"

Hearing this, Mewtwo was slightly taken aback.

It was only then that it recalled the purpose of its visit.

After the last encounter with Mew and Teddiursa, they had gone to a natural secret realm named Mount Quena to live in seclusion.

But soon after, some humans with ill intentions invaded, attempting to capture them.

In the end, with the help of Ash and others, they thwarted Team Rocket's plot, and Mewtwo erased those humans' memories.

After the Mount Quena incident was over, it intended to find a place to continue its secluded life with those cloned Pokemon like itself.

But for some unknown reason, midway, it suddenly remembered the memories shown to it by Teddiursa and Mew.

A human willing to help Pokemon selflessly.

Thus, carrying with it a reason even unknown to itself, Mewtwo changed its route, as if seeking some answers or simply wanting to see the place Mew had once visited.

Mewtwo remained silent.

Not only D, but even the other Pokemon patrons in the tavern noticed this peculiar figure.

D noticed that Mewtwo's expression was remarkably calm, not as confused or tormented, and didn't seem as antagonistic towards humans as depicted in the animation.

After a brief contemplation, D guessed that it must have undergone the Mount Quena incident.

At this point, Mewtwo had undergone significant changes in its personality compared to initially.

It no longer sought to prove its strength but instead wished for a peaceful life for itself and the cloned Pokemon.

With this thought, D decided not to say much to this "special" legendary Pokemon, simply suggesting:

"Since you're undecided, why not come in for a drink first?"

Mewtwo glanced at him, then stepped inside.

The cloned Pokemon behind it followed suit.

In an instant, the tavern became even more cramped.

The space was limited, so they had to crowd at a table with the town's Pokemon.

For those curious, bound Pokemon like Machamp and Quaquaval appeared very interested in them.

A few lively Pokemon stepped forward to enquire about their origins.

The cloned Pokemon exchanged glances and then responded somewhat nervously: "Pika pika..." (From a very, very far away place…)

Upon hearing this, the interrogating Pikachu let out an enthusiastic growl, much like the hospitable people of Porto Marinada Town.

"Pikachu!" (Then you must enjoy the drinks here, D and Indeedee's food is the most delicious in the world!)

The cloned Pokemon looked at each other, unsure how to respond.

Seeing this, D asked his brother Indeedee to entertain the curious cloned Pokemon, bringing them Berry Milk and sandwiches.

Despite their complicated origins and the many distressing experiences they've endured, these cloned Pokemon remained innocent and simple at heart.

So before long, they were eating and mingling with the town's Pokemon.

Mewtwo stood at the bar, silently watching this scene.

It mused to itself: "The memories from Teddiursa and Mew were true."

"The smiles on these Pokemon's faces are genuine; they sincerely believe this human is a good person."

"I never imagined such a place existed in this world. If I were a Pokemon, I might also like it here. No...I should also be considered a Pokemon."

Suddenly, a drink with a rather unappealing smell was pushed toward it.

Mewtwo's attention was immediately drawn to it.

It lifted its head and looked at the man named D.

Seeing Mewtwo's appearance, D couldn't help but wonder to himself:

"Shouldn't Mewtwo be capable of telepathy? Why hasn't it spoken, could it possibly, like Giratina, disdain speaking to humans? No, as far as I know, Mewtwo's personality shouldn't be like that."

---

D's mind was full of thoughts, yet he smiled and said, "Have a drink, the Pokemon Tavern offers it for free. It can bring a moment of peace to your heart."

"What is this?" Mewtwo finally spoke.

Its voice sounded somewhat like that of a cool, deep male tone.

However, Legendary Pokemon should not have genders.

D replied, "It's beer, a new drink just introduced at the tavern."

Mewtwo looked at the glass of golden liquor, under the yellowish light, the transparent glass cup was filled with an intoxicating aura.

For a moment, Mewtwo felt a bit dazed, it steadied its mind, didn't reach out, but used telekinesis to bring the glass in front of itself.

Mewtwo reached out and caught it, then lowered its head to take a sip.

With the first sip of beer down, it immediately felt something changing.

Its body was becoming lighter, as if stepping on clouds, its brain, which had been pondering endlessly, finally stopped, and its painful and confused heart since its creation found unprecedented peace at this moment.

Peaceful, serene...

Mewtwo's eyes were dazed, cheeks flushed.

It seemed to forget all troubles, just immersed in this peaceful atmosphere.

"No... this feeling... what is this feeling like?"

Mewtwo tried hard to shake its head, forcing itself to start thinking.

D looked at such a Mewtwo, feeling a bit tickled inside.

"You're drunk," he said.

Mewtwo looked into this human's eyes, it had human genes inside, coupled with the ability to sense psychic powers and emotions, this suddenly gave it a strange feeling.

The gaze from the other person, the tone of the speech...

"You know me." Mewtwo spoke with certainty.

Heard from that Teddiursa?

D slightly raised his eyebrows, did not deny, just asked, "Who are you?"

Who am I... Mewtwo subconsciously replied, "I am Mewtwo, a Pokemon created by humans using Mew's genes."

It seems Mewtwo has now begun to consider itself a Pokemon, D nodded.

Then he saw Mewtwo downing a large glass of beer in one swig.

The "brainless" Mewtwo nodded while looking at him, suddenly remembered the purpose of coming here: "Mew said you can help us."

"Hm?" D was stunned.

He pondered and asked, "Is there anything you need my help with?"

"No." The drunk Mewtwo thought carefully for a moment, then said, "No help, we are here to find an answer."

D: "... What answer?"

Mewtwo looked at the group of cloned Pokemon playing and chatting with the local Pokemon, but suddenly stopped talking.

D was speechless.

He recalled in his mind Mewtwo's experience, then said:

"Matters like this, what others say to you won't help, you can only experience, perceive, and think for yourself."

As he spoke, he also looked at the group of cloned Pokemon: "Look, you are also drinking here with them now, whether artificial or naturally born, what's the difference?"

Mewtwo was thoughtful, as it pondered, then fell asleep.

D: "..."

Legendary Pokemon's tolerance for alcohol is too bad?

He sighed helplessly, told his worried sister Indeedee, "Later you guys take it to the guest bedroom to rest."

"Indeedee." The Indeedee Sibling nodded.

The group of cloned Pokemon was startled by Mewtwo's appearance, only after D told them that Mewtwo was just asleep, did they breathe a sigh of relief.

At ten o'clock in the evening, the Pokemon in town gradually left.

Only when the last Pokemon went home did D close the tavern.

He arranged for the group of cloned Pokemon to sleep on a soft lawn in the ranch.

After finishing these tasks, D yawned and carried Pawmot and Cyclizar back to the room.

The next morning, D hadn't woken up yet, his brother Indeedee knocked on his door.

D rubbed his eyes and opened the door, his brother Indeedee looked at him, pointed outside: "Indeedee."

D was slightly startled, then walked outside to see Mewtwo and the group of cloned Pokemon standing in the ranch ready to leave.

Hearing the sound of the door opening, Mewtwo looked up.

The memories after getting drunk yesterday were still lingering in its mind.

Recalling its appearance, this Legendary Pokemon felt an emotion named shyness for the first time in its life.

At this moment, it saw that human walking towards itself.

"Leaving?" D wasn't too surprised: "Where are you planning to go?"

Mewtwo calmly said, "We plan to find a place of our own and live there."

A place like Mount Quena, a natural Secret Realm untouched by humans.

Maybe one day, humans might accidentally enter there, but they will no longer avoid it like the last time.

D nodded.

Mewtwo didn't say much else, its eyes shone with blue light, enveloping the surrounding cloned Pokemon, flying with them into the sky, they seemed to just come this way for a visit.

D stood on the grass watching their figures disappear, just as he was about to turn around and go back indoors, he suddenly heard Mewtwo's voice again.

It said, "Mew likes you a lot."

D froze.

He soon thought about the things Mewtwo said yesterday while drunk.

So, it probably came here to see why Mew likes him, this human?

This really...

For a moment, D didn't know what to say.

He turned to look at his little tavern.

Thanks to Mew and Teddiursa's promotion, even Pokemon like Mewtwo became guests.

The only regret was, he couldn't capture this Mewtwo.

With Mewtwo's personality, it probably won't get captured by humans.

However, that's no problem!

As a farmer, to see a Pokemon like this with his own eyes has surpassed 99% of humans in this world.

Satisfied.jpg

D grinned, just as he was about to turn around and go indoors, he suddenly saw a fountain appearing on the lake nearby.

D: "..."

Some infuriated old dragon started acting up again.

D went inside and brought out a barrel of beer, poured it loudly into Giratina's Reversed World, then turned back to the dining room.

His sister Indeedee had already prepared a very hearty breakfast.

Eating the toast baked to a soft golden brown and the fried eggs cooked to a tempting golden hue, drinking the sweet and delicious berry milk, D felt that life was truly happy.

Meanwhile, in the Reversed World.

Giratina angrily looked at the golden liquor floating in the void before it.

The revering it, liking it, thinking it's the most majestic and powerful shadow Divine Dragon in the world.

False, all false!

It had finally figured it out.

Ever since it got those waters, that human has become increasingly dismissive!

Feeling deceived, Giratina was furious,

Giratina couldn't contain its anger, deciding to retaliate, drag that guy in here, and imprison him forever!

The next second, its body swept past the sky, and all the liquor vanished without a trace.

D finished his farm chores, remembered Giratina who drank the beer, and brewed a bowl of Poisonous Mushroom Soup, entered the Reversed World.

When he arrived, he saw Giratina, the big fat dragon, sleeping soundly on a Floating Island.

In such a posture, it was clearly drunk.

"Mewtwo can't handle alcohol, and neither can Giratina!"

Giratina was there snoring, while D began wandering around again.

He still remembered Giratina mentioning earlier about inviting him to farm in the Reversed World.

Regarding this, D had also carefully considered it.

He has this plan, but the Reversed World's environment is very unique, although there is water and land here, it lacks the most important factor for plant growth—sunlight.

D stood on a Floating Island observing the condition of the soil.

This island had no trees, but from his angle, he could see the island floating above had a tree growing.

Does this imply that plants can grow normally here?

D fell into contemplation.

"I can try planting something later." If possible, he plans to develop here as his backup field.

If plants cannot grow normally, then it needs to see if Gogoat or Calyrex can make the plants here rapidly grow.

---

---

---

---

# 216: Chapter 213: Birthday Gift (Double-Length)

Calyrex... Everything still can't do without Calyrex.

Planting the World Tree requires Calyrex, developing the Reversed World also needs Calyrex, and although the horse race doesn't need Calyrex's help, it requires his steeds.

D stood on the Floating Island, pondering, and stared at the sleeping Giratina not far away.

Suddenly, Giratina drowsily opened its eyes.

The first thing it noticed was D standing opposite.

Giratina seemed still in a drunken state, its speech turning into a tongue-tied stutter: "You... you finally remembered to come!"

D: "..."

He looked at Giratina suspiciously, thinking how similar this sounded to something else.

He also had business to attend to; things in the Reversed World were over, and he couldn't spend all day here.

Moreover, the weakness brought about by drinking Poisonous Mushroom Soup wasn't fake.

Looking at the deeply resentful Giratina, D said speechlessly: "Did you drink all that beer?"

"Bee... beer?" Giratina squinted its eyes, barely using its intoxicated mind to recall what that was.

It suddenly got up and shouted: "Delicious!"

D: "..."

Giratina glanced at him, threatening in a domineering manner: "Br... bring me some more barrels, or... or I'll drag... drag you in here and lock... lock you up!"

D: "..."

Giratina suddenly leaned in, drunkenly saying: "Foolish hu...human, why aren't you... you speaking? Such br... bravery! Dare to ignore me!"

With those words, Giratina released a towering fury, as if it was going to unleash amazing power to crush the human in front of it.

But in the last second, it still gave up.

Giratina lay back down, resting its large head on its two sturdy forelimbs:

"Never... mind, if I kill you, no one will be left to talk to me."

For a moment, D was at a loss for words. Thinking of Giratina's legend, he felt touched, preparing to say something, but then he heard the dragon muttering:

"Although you're a tiny, insignificant human, not... not as ambitious as those humans before... timid as well... zZZ!"

Looking at the once again sleeping Giratina, D felt like he had something to say but didn't know if he should say it.

Being called timid, he was tempted to lead Koraidon and a fully equipped riding Calyrex to invade the Reversed World, making this Evil Dragon work on the ranch!

D raised his fist at Giratina, grumbling as he called for his brother Indeedee to feed him some detoxifying Pecha Berry Juice.

After bidding farewell to Mewtwo and the others, life on the ranch returned to peace again.

At five in the afternoon, Zack prepared to drive the big truck to the ranch for goods pick-up.

After loading several barrels of MooMoo Fresh Milk and goat milk onto the truck, D prepared to take him home to inspect the cellar full of beer.

Standing in front of the brand new house, Zack couldn't help but stop and look up.

He looked at the new house, then at the distant old wooden cabin, and couldn't help but sigh:

"Wow wow wow, you're really making the ranch more and more prosperous!"

D looked at the group of Pokemon next to him, smiling knowingly: "It's not just my work alone."

Upon hearing this, Zack also looked at the group of Pokemon bred on the ranch:

"They're all great! Honestly, the first time I saw you, you were so skinny, Mayor Thomas and I couldn't help but worry if you could manage ranch work."

Hearing his words, D remembered what he looked like when he first came here.

He couldn't help but laugh: "And now?"

Zack: "It turns out our worries were unnecessary. Now you're as strong as a Tauros!"

Thinking of the group of black Tauros in the Paldea Region, D couldn't help defending himself: "It's not that exaggerated."

"Hahahahaha!" Zack burst into laughter, then looked at the stubble outside D's mouth and said: "But you need to shave properly. Otherwise, you'll look like a thirty-some-year-old man, and girls about your age on the street might call you uncle."

D: "To be honest, that already happened."

It was in summer; he often did farm work outside, which tanned his skin and grew a beard. When buying vegetables at Porto Marinada Market, he forgot to shave, and a sixteen or seventeen-year-old girl called him uncle.

Zack showed a face of 'I told you so,' amusing the nearby Cyclizar and Koraidon.

Their chat over, the two went to the cellar, where Zack, the professional, examined the beer's quality.

D patiently waited beside, waiting for Zack to set the beer price.

"Hmm, the fermentation is spot on, the aroma smells rich, with a natural oak flavor." Zack poured a glass, sipping it as he finely tasted: "Rich malt aroma mixed with the bitterness of the hops... It tastes smooth, with a clean aftertaste, very nice!"

Saying this, Zack couldn't help but regret: "Too bad the fermentation time was too short; if longer, it would be better, and the price of aged beer would be higher, the taste more rich."

D modestly sought guidance: "Aging? And how long would that take?"

Zack explained: "That's up to you. Take wine for example, naturally fermented wine usually needs about 20 days."

---

"According to the winemaker's intention and the style of wine being produced, if it is aged, the time can range from several months to several years."

"However, it's not necessarily true that wine must be aged to be good. It depends more on the drinker's preference; some people like wine aged for several years, while others prefer fresh wine."

"Of course, the market price for wine aged several years is much higher than that of normally fermented wine."

Zack said a whole lot, but D only caught one sentence from it — Aged wine sells for more!

"It's just right that there's a heap of Berries in the Berry Garden. After selling this batch of beer, we can use them to brew some fruit wine," D thought to himself.

At this time, Zack had already completed his evaluation: "3000 Poké Dollars per liter. This season is not beer-drinking season, so the market price fluctuates a bit. If it were summer, the price would be higher."

3000 Poké Dollars per liter... D calculated in his mind.

The specification for one oak barrel is three liters, and there are a total of thirty oak barrels in the cellar, which means this batch of beer can sell for around 270,000.

Compared to the price of Miltank's milk, the profit from winemaking is also a considerable income.

D nodded in agreement with the price.

Earlier in the summer, he had accumulated a large stock of hops, and once this batch of brewed beer is sold, the hops he stored can come into play.

Once he saves a bit more money, the greenhouse can be built.

Seeing him nod, Zack thought for a while and said, "Then I'll come back tomorrow to pick up this batch! It's a bit late today, and my truck is already full of goods."

"No problem."

D had no issue with this.

Having arranged for Zack to come by tomorrow, Zack drove off in his truck.

It was evening, with colorful clouds covering the lake surface.

The Dragonite and Dratini, happily with their day's wages, flew off to Dragon Island.

After seeing them off, D began wrapping up his day's work.

Together with Pawmot, they herded the hens back into the Chicken House and secured the doors and windows.

Filled the feed trough for Miltank with fresh hay and Berries.

Refilled Glaceon's water bowl with water.

Then he picked a big pumpkin from the field; the weather was getting colder, and tonight D planned to cook a pot of pumpkin porridge with rock sugar to warm himself and Cyclizar.

Speaking of Cyclizar.

D suddenly remembered something.

He went back to the bedroom and flipped through this month's calendar, indeed finding a mark on the 20th of August.

"Sure enough, I didn't forget; Cyclizar's birthday is in a few days!"

D returned to the kitchen, processing the pumpkin while glancing at Cyclizar yawning in the living room.

Cyclizar's birthday is important, but at the same time, there's another important matter.

That's the dessert he made earlier, a special dish that only takes effect on a Pokémon's birthday.

If he remembers correctly, that cake's effect is called "Birthday Gift"?

The specific effect is that if a Pokémon eats it on its birthday, it gains a blessing, permanently increasing one of its Hp, Attack, Special Attack, Speed, Defense, or Special Defense.

"Interesting."

D was very curious about the described effect.

But there was still some time before Cyclizar's birthday, and until then, he had to be patient.

A pot of sweet, soft pumpkin porridge was devoured by a group of Pokémon.

After dinner, his sister Indeedee went to wash the dishes while D cozied up on the sofa with several Pokémon to watch TV.

It was the weekend, the tavern wasn't open, and they didn't need to stay there, so everyone stayed home.

The living room was illuminated with warm light, and the TV was broadcasting the weather forecast for the next day.

The elegantly dressed female host was reading the script to the camera: "...Affected by cold air, it is expected that some areas in the northwest of Paldea will see rain around midnight. Please be prepared in advance and close all doors and windows."

Upon hearing this, his brother Indeedee silently turned to check if all the doors and windows in the house were properly closed.

D lazily remained on the sofa, leaning against the furry Pawmot, squeezing its paw pads.

This was his favorite moment of the day.

With all the work on the farm completed, the world outside was shrouded in darkness, and maybe a cold wind would blow through, but inside, it was brightly lit and incredibly warm.

The Pokémon were also gathered together, nestled with him on the sofa like cats huddling for warmth in winter.

On the wooden coffee table in front of him were snacks and milk that every Pokémon loved, along with a cup of steaming hot tea.

The old-fashioned TV across from them played news programs from the Paldea Region.

They would watch the weather forecast for the next day, and after learning tomorrow's weather, they would switch to the news program.

This show helped them learn about the outside world.

For example, the Paldea Academy was holding inter-school competitions again, some Trainer reversed a battle with an unexpected Tera Type, or large-scale events being held in certain places.

The antennas in the Paldea Region could occasionally pick up news programs from other regions.

Like Professor Oak's Pokémon lecture from the Johto Region.

Though Koraidon and the others couldn't understand human programs, they still accompanied him to watch them.

---

Every time it comes to this moment, D always thinks maybe the Pokemon like this kind of atmosphere and feeling, just like him?

D turns his head to look at his group of Pokemon.

Cyclizar is lying down on the soft carpet, resting with closed eyes.

Alcremie stands on the coffee table, swaying leisurely amidst a pile of puddings and little buns.

Pawmo is being used as a pillow by him.

Sister Indeedee, after cleaning the dishes, comes to the sofa to sit and watch TV together.

Its brother stands by, like a loyal butler, always ready for the master's call.

As for Koraidon...

Koraidon is watching him.

Seeing he's been noticed, Koraidon doesn't look away or act embarrassed. Instead, its eyes light up, appearing very happy.

D looks at Koraidon.

At this moment, he feels if Koraidon were a big dog, it would definitely wag its tail.

Thinking of that scene, D suddenly feels amused.

Koraidon is indeed a very cute Pokemon!

"I wonder what Miraidon would be like in this situation." D imagines the personality of Miraidon in the game.

Maybe it would turn its head arrogantly and pretend not to care?

However, to him, compared to such a tsundere Pokemon, Koraidon, who would lick you warmly without any hesitation, is much more lovable.

D feels that if he continues to look, Koraidon might pounce on him to lick him.

He quickly averts his gaze, continues to watch TV, and sneakily glances at Koraidon's reaction.

Upon seeing D look away, Koraidon immediately droops its head, looking completely dejected.

D: "..."

He thinks for a moment and simply lies horizontally on the sofa, resting his head on Pawmo and draping his legs over Koraidon.

Sure enough, Koraidon instantly perks up happily.

It's still a big dog that loves snuggling up to people.

In the early morning, it indeed begins to rain outside.

The autumn rain pitter-patters, mixed with a hint of chill, as it falls on the roof and eventually drip onto the soil.

D gets startled awake by the rain, yawning as he listens to the sound of the rain, turns over, and hugs the soundly sleeping Pawmo, continuing to sleep.

The next morning, the rain hasn't stopped, and the sky is still gray and gloomy.

D stands under the eaves, looking at the rainwater dripping down and realizes that there's probably no way to go out today.

Even the Gogoats have stopped their activities and are lying quietly in their sheds, eating Grassy Seeds.

At this time, the Dragonite and Dragonair working on the farm, as usual, arrive at the farm braving the rain.

Upon seeing them, D is momentarily at a loss for words.

As a kind-hearted farmer, he thinks and says, "In such bad weather, you can rest on the island for a day and come over when the rain stops."

However, the Dragonite and Dragonair immediately widen their eyes, indicating a refusal.

"Roar!"

How can that be? Otherwise, they wouldn't get to eat the delicious Poffins!

D: "..."

Look! It's definitely not that I'm deliberately exploiting the workers! They themselves want to work!

But indeed, for Dragonite and Dragonair, this level of rain hardly affects them.

D sighs helplessly, saying, "As long as you don't feel tired, it's fine."

"Roar!"

A chorus of Pokemon cheer, then take the work satchel and the fresh bottled milk for them from D's hands, flying towards the direction of Porto Marinada Town.

Watching this scene, D suddenly thinks maybe he can print "Dragonite Delivery, Mission Accomplished!" on Dragonite's parcels.

---

---

---

---

# 217: Chapter 214: Cyclizar, Little Angel (4k)

League Year 198, August 16, Autumn.

Four days before Cyclizar's birthday, D started thinking about what kind of birthday gift would be good for it.

This was his first time celebrating a Pokemon's birthday, so there must be some ceremony.

However, D thought for a long time and couldn't figure out what kind of gift Cyclizar would like.

According to his understanding of Cyclizar, it didn't seem to have anything it particularly liked or wanted.

How about making Cyclizar its favorite food?

"No, that's not right." D quickly dismissed this idea since giving Cyclizar the food it eats every day doesn't really count as a birthday gift.

"Then what about the training equipment that helps Pokemon become stronger?"

D carefully recalled, finding that Cyclizar's pursuit of strength was far less exaggerated than Dragonite's.

It might like it, but that's only because the gift comes from him, not because it likes the gift itself.

So, what better choice is there?

D sat under the eaves, listening to the melodious sound of the wind chimes overhead, while his eyes were directed towards Cyclizar playing in the sunlight.

Koraidon lazily lay down nearby, taking a nap with closed eyes.

It seemed to notice that D hadn't spoken for a while, so it opened its eyes slightly and glanced over, only to see D staring off into the distance.

Koraidon's gaze followed his line of sight and saw that the past version of itself was tearfully clutching its tail.

A Naclstack stood apologetically nearby, nervously shaking its head, seemingly saying it didn't do it on purpose.

Upon noticing its "mech" not moving, the Alcremie, who had been riding on Naclstack's head, peeked down curiously.

"Ah gasi?" Seeing this scene, Koraidon was somewhat confused.

D began to explain: "Naclstack accidentally stepped on Cyclizar's tail, I'll go have a look."

Speaking of which, he stood up and walked over.

As D left, Koraidon also stood up, shook itself, and followed.

Alcremie had already jumped off Naclstack and was blowing on Cyclizar's wound.

The commotion here had caught the attention of several Pokemon, and Glaceon and Butterfree had gathered around.

"Stack..."

Seeing D coming, the Naclstack that had accidentally stepped on Cyclizar's tail looked at him apologetically.

"Don't worry, let me see." Seeing that it was very worried, D first soothed its emotions and then took Cyclizar's tail from its hand.

The originally slender tail tip was now swollen like a pig's trotter, and the white skin was slightly flushed.

Naclstack's weight is over two hundred and thirty pounds, so Cyclizar's pain is imaginable.

Tears had been forced out of it from the pain.

After a brief examination, D said to his brother Indeedee who had rushed over: "Help me bring the bandages and medicine from home, just on the cupboard in my bedroom."

"Indeedee." Brother Indeedee nodded calmly, glanced at Cyclizar's tail, and then turned to leave.

D held Cyclizar's tail, just about to say something to comfort it, when suddenly the weight in his hand lightened.

He was stunned for a moment, then looked down and saw a section of pale Shed Tail lying quietly in his hand.

D was dumbfounded.

And the nearby Alcremie, Butterfree, and other Pokemon were terrified when they saw Cyclizar's tail had broken, their mouths immediately emitting exaggerated screams.

"Mama!"

"Eevee?!"

"Stack!!!"

Naclstack was nearly scared to death, it really didn't do it on purpose!

After a brief moment of shock, D looked up at Cyclizar, who seemed to be feeling better.

He tentatively asked: "How do you feel now?"

"Gao..."

Seeing everyone worried about it, Cyclizar felt a bit embarrassed.

It shook its head, confusedly looking at its missing tipped tail.

Now that the tail was broken, it seemed... suddenly not as painful.

D's brow twitched.

Could Cyclizar have inadvertently stimulated its potential through the pain and learned Shed Tail?

Shed Tail, a Normal Type move, the effect is to reduce stamina, create a substitute, and return to the Poke Ball, switching with a backup Pokemon.

In games, only Cyclizar and Orthworm can learn this move.

D checked the cross-section of Cyclizar's Shed Tail and found it wasn't as bloody as he'd imagined.

He couldn't help but ask: "Is this a new move?"

Instinctively, Cyclizar knew this was a move called Shed Tail, so it nodded, somewhat embarrassed: "Gao..."

Now it couldn't use it again, only waiting for its tail to grow back before it could use it normally.

D had a rough idea, just as brother Indeedee came jogging over with the medical kit.

D took out the prepared bandage and Super Potion, sprayed a few times on Cyclizar's Shed Tail, and then tied a bow with the bandage.

"Alright, it'll be healed very soon."

The recovery ability of Pokemon is really impressive, and according to D's understanding, the Shed Tail could grow back in three days.

Upon hearing this, the few Pokemon finally relaxed.

Naclstack lowered its head apologetically.

If it weren't for it, Cyclizar wouldn't have had to get hurt.

In response, the kind Cyclizar shook its head, smiling slightly, and indicated it was okay.

---

The harmony between the Pokemon had returned once more.

Cyclizar was a bit tired and found a comfortable place to lie down and sleep.

Although Cyclizar had forgiven it, Naclstack still felt uneasy inside.

It turned its head to check on Cyclizar from time to time and was extra cautious even while walking, fearing it might step on any small Pokemon and accidentally hurt them.

D and Koraidon retreated under the eaves.

Recalling Cyclizar's reaction just now, D couldn't help but say, "Cyclizar really is a little angel."

"Gaaagh~"

Koraidon had long gotten used to D praising Cyclizar.

Now, besides acceptance, it also felt a sense of shared honor and disgrace.

D laughed, "I'm praising Cyclizar, why are you so happy? I remember at the beginning, you seemed to not quite... not quite like it."

Koraidon had a silly expression on its face: "Gaaagh."

"Nevermind, it's good that you two can get along!" D didn't press further, as Koraidon is a Pokemon with a strong territorial instinct, just not fighting with Cyclizar was already good enough.

Supporting himself on the ground of the corridor with his hand, D let out a deep breath and remembered that Koraidon often hung out with Cyclizar. He then asked:

"By the way, has Cyclizar ever told you what it's interested in?"

Hearing this, Koraidon tilted its head in curiosity: "Gaaagh?"

D explained, "Isn't Cyclizar's birthday coming up? I'm thinking about getting it a birthday gift, but I still don't know what to choose."

Koraidon understood and couldn't help but recall the past when it was still Cyclizar and received gifts from D.

At that time, D gave a scarf and custom-made thermal clothing for Cyclizar.

Koraidon remembered the clothes were made from Wooloo's wool, like a little vest, its limbs could stretch out from holes, and the wool would protect its body and tummy, along with a scarf, it was very warm in the winter.

It loved that outfit, but unfortunately, it wore it for so long that it eventually broke after many patches, and D finally placed it at the bottom of the wardrobe.

Therefore, Koraidon provided an answer without hesitation: "Gaaagh!"

Just then, a chilly autumn breeze blew past.

An idea suddenly formed in D's mind.

Warm, protective clothing!

He remembered that the town sold scarves and clothing for Pokemon!

With winter soon approaching, he could give them to Cyclizar in advance so it could be ready for the upcoming winter.

"Got it!"

D suddenly stood up, ready to head into town.

Cyclizar had injured its tail and wasn't suitable for carrying him to town. Moreover, D intended to keep it a secret from Cyclizar, planning to give it a surprise later.

So, for this trip, he chose to ride the more inconspicuous Gogoat into town.

Before leaving, D reminded Koraidon, "Don't tell Cyclizar what we talked about today!"

Koraidon: "..."

Seeing Koraidon nod, D mounted onto Gogoat's back and set off towards Porto Marinada Town.

Having not visited for a long time, Porto Marinada Town was still bustling and lively as ever.

Following his memory, D headed straight to the Pokemon Beauty Salon.

Only here would they sell Pokemon accessories.

A Pokemon Stylist dressed in brightly colored clothes and stylishly dressed greeted him.

"Good afternoon, young man! Are you here to style this Gogoat? Oh my! Its fur is so lush and soft! Hmm, it would perfectly suit the hairstyle I recently designed."

Saying this, the stylist slid a depiction of a Furfrou with a mohawk hairstyle toward D.

He covered his mouth with a light laugh, "Would you like to try this trendy hairstyle?"

D: "..."

Gogoat understood what this strange person was saying.

It leaned in to take a look and its eyes widened immediately.

"Baaah!"

Green light began to surge on Gogoat's body, and with a displeased look, it glanced at the human planning to shear its wool, appearing ready to unleash a Grass Sacred Sword if the person moved.

"Ohoho, don't be mad!"

D couldn't accept these trendy hairstyles and styles, finding them too non-mainstream and trendy.

He pushed those thoughts aside, asking, "Do you have any clothes suitable for Cyclizar to wear? I want to get it something for winter."

"Cyclizar?" The stylist seemed surprised, "No, we only have clothes for small dog Pokemon."

Realizing he had come to the wrong place, D politely bid farewell and rode Gogoat to a store that specialized in clothing.

However, this time, they were left empty-handed again.

"Indeed, large stores only sell human clothing."

Standing in front of the store, D didn't know where else to look for a moment.

Just then, a young female store employee called out to him from inside the store.

D turned around, looking puzzled.

The girl asked, "Are you looking to buy warm clothes for Cyclizar for the winter?"

D nodded, "Yes."

The girl said, "I'm afraid there are no such clothes in Porto Marinada Town because clothes would affect Pokemon's movements, so aside from some wealthy ladies who pursue Pokemon fashion, it's rare for anyone to dress Pokemon."

---

"I see..." D pondered and nodded.

The girl smiled softly and suddenly said, "However, I have an idea! I see you've been running up and down this street for a while, and you must really like that Cyclizar. In that case, let me offer you a direction!"

"What?"

The girl replied, "Make it yourself. Near Porto Marinada Town, there are ranches that breed Wooloo, which produce high-quality wool. You could purchase some wool from those ranchers, then find someone skilled in tailoring to make custom clothing for your Cyclizar."

It was indeed a good option.

D nodded, thanked the girl, and while it was still early, rode his Gogoat to visit the neighboring Thunder Ranch.

Ray, the slightly older rancher, welcomed him. After learning about D's purpose, he took him on a tour of his ranch.

"Follow me!" Ray said with a smile.

D responded and observed the ranch.

Thunder Ranch was located at the foot of a hill, with lush pasture growing on it. A group of Wooloo and Mareep from the Galar Region were leisurely grazing there.

"Quite different from your ranch, isn't it?" Ray asked.

D nodded, "Yes, it is."

He noticed that there were only a few fields here, growing a few scattered pumpkins, and there were no Miltank or similar milk-producing Pokemon.

Ray proudly said, "My ranch focuses mainly on livestock, specializing in raising wool-producing Pokemon. The wool from Wooloo is the best material for making clothes, and in the Galar Region, clothes and carpets made from this wool are famous premium products!"

"The wool from my Wooloo can be sold all across the Paldea Region!"

"Impressive!" D genuinely complimented.

Compared to him, Ray was indeed an outstanding rancher.

He managed his ranch to its current success with his magical cuisine and various Pokemon.

Meanwhile, Ray ran this Wooloo Ranch skilfully with just his own two hands.

"Haha!" Ray laughed, feeling slightly embarrassed by the compliment, "You're here to buy some wool, right?"

D: "Exactly."

Upon hearing this, Ray waved and called over a chubby, adorably-eyed Wooloo.

"Just in time, it's shearing season again."

Ray took out a special pair of scissors and, while shearing the Wooloo, said:

"The wool on a Wooloo grows continuously throughout its life. Even if you shave it completely, it will grow back to its original state in just three months."

"If you don't shear it for a long time, these little ones would end up unable to move because of the wool growing too long."

Ray's shearing skills were very proficient, and soon, a complete wool fleece was freshly ready.

Ray held it up, "Look, isn't it amazing? The fleece is all intact and very elastic. If you wear it, even falling off a cliff wouldn't hurt you."

As he spoke, he patted it, and the wool bounced a few times.

D widened his eyes incredulously.

This is so amazing?

If I put it on Lycanroc, wouldn't it just become a wolf in sheep's clothing?

"I'll give you a discount, 2000 League Coins per pound. Hmm, this is 6 pounds total, which would be 12000 League Coins." Ray weighed the wool.

12,000... To be honest, for wool of this quality, this price wasn't expensive.

D thought it was just right to buy it all, planning to use it for clothing later. He paid and headed back with the wool.

Next up, I need to find someone skilled in tailoring to customize warm clothing for Cyclizar.

---

---

# 218: Chapter 215: Pumpkin Cookies (4k)

If he could mend clothes and weave fabric, he could make them for Cyclizar himself.

But unfortunately, he hasn't mastered that skill.

With several days left until Cyclizar's birthday, D looked at the woolen bundle that was almost bigger than himself, feeling a bit troubled.

After thinking for a while, he decided to first take the wool back to the farm, and then slowly find someone in town who could help make the clothes.

The speed of Gogoat wasn't as fast as Koraidon's, but it was more stable.

At three in the afternoon, D returned to the farm. He jumped off Gogoat, which then returned to the sheep pen on its own.

The farm was just as bustling as when he left.

On the grass, Dragonair was frolicking, Combee danced among the flowers, and Pawmot was napping on the Chicken House roof...

Seeing this, D smiled slightly and patted Koraidon's head, which had run up to him, then looked towards Cyclizar, who was still asleep in the distance.

This was a unique behavior of Pokemon.

Whenever they were injured, they'd curl up and stop moving, healing through sleep or rest.

Koraidon opened its mouth to say "Ah," but D quickly shushed it.

Koraidon immediately closed its mouth, its eyes revealing a hint of grievance.

"Let's go back inside."

D helplessly took Koraidon back to his room, placed the wool on the sofa, and asked the following Koraidon, "Are you afraid of the cold?"

Koraidon immediately nodded, "Aga-sii!"

"Eh?" D was a bit surprised.

Because in his daily interactions, he didn't feel Koraidon was particularly afraid of the cold, and based on what he knew, the King who could crack the earth with a punch wasn't very afraid of cold.

Koraidon's scales and dragon skin were much thicker than Cyclizar's.

This might be the nature of Dragon Type Pokemon? D thought.

After all, double weakness to Ice, type matchups are too crucial.

"Alright, then I'll find someone to make you a winter coat with this wool as well," D said.

He worried that only Cyclizar having one might cause unease among the other Pokemon.

Just like raising children, favoritism is wrong.

"Aga-sii~"

Koraidon let out a rather cheerful growl.

Seeing this, D's face showed a smile, and he couldn't help but reach out to pat the head of this Pokemon with a big dog-like personality.

A trip to town took nearly two hours, and D felt a bit thirsty and decided to have a drink of water first.

He had just picked up the cup when he heard a knock at the door from outside.

"Is anyone there?"

Hearing the familiar voice, D was suddenly very surprised.

He went to open the door, and sure enough, he saw Mrs. Dante from town standing at the door with a parcel.

Under the autumn sun, the elderly woman, her hair a mix of gray and white, had a kindly smile on her face.

Upon seeing D, she lifted the bag in her hand and spoke warmly, "Good afternoon, D, here are some pumpkin cookies I made. I accidentally made too many, and Riot and I can't eat them all, so I came to give you some."

D looked at the small bag of cookies but didn't refuse, smiling as he reached out to take them: "Please come in and sit!"

He welcomed the elderly lady into the living room.

Without needing D to speak, Indeedee served a cup of warm, aromatic tea for the guest.

Mrs. Dante took in the furnishing of the room, which, while not luxurious, was filled with a sense of life and traces left by Pokemon.

Mrs. Dante was genuinely happy for D: "It's wonderful! This place is even more prosperous than when your grandfather ran it!"

"Hmm? Was the farm quite backward back then?" D's mind contained only sparse memories, with very vague impressions of that time.

Mrs. Dante nodded, recalling as she spoke: "Back then, there weren't big houses here, just a few dilapidated wooden huts. Your grandfather was a tenacious man; he worked very hard to run the farm."

"There used to be many farms here, and every year the farm run by your grandfather would win first place in the crop evaluation contest!"

"It must have been very lively then," D imagined aloud, unable to resist.

"Indeed!" Mrs. Dante's eyes were filled with nostalgia, "Porto Marinada Town was quite famous as an agricultural town in the Paldea Region back then!"

As she spoke, a regretful expression appeared on her face: "Unfortunately, now there are fewer and fewer people farming, and young people either move to big cities or become trainers."

D: "Times are changing!"

In another ten years, the world is likely to change again.

From the era of slow horse-drawn travel and distant correspondence to an era where a single internet connection links the whole world.

"We're getting old!" Mrs. Dante sighed deeply, "If your grandfather saw how capable you are, he'd be very comforted."

D: "..."

He thought to himself that if the original owner's grandfather could see all this, he'd probably be fuming with anger.

"It's getting late, and I should head back," Mrs. Dante said kindly.

As she spoke, she stood up but suddenly caught sight of the big bundle of wool D had placed in a corner of the living room.

Mrs. Dante was quite surprised: "Those wool? Your farm started raising Wooloo?"

---

"Oh, right!" Mrs. Dante's question reminded D of the matter of finding a tailor to make clothes.

"No, it's almost Cyclizar's birthday, and I'm planning to make it a warm coat using this wool," D explained. "The town doesn't sell Cyclizar clothes, only Cyclizar saddle covers."

"A birthday gift, huh? Cyclizar will definitely be very happy." Mrs. Dante was quite surprised. "I never expected you to have such a skill."

D was momentarily taken aback, understanding Mrs. Dante's meaning. He shook his head helplessly: "...No, I don't know how. I was thinking of asking someone in town for help."

Upon hearing this, Mrs. Dante understood easily and offered warmly, "In that case, leave it to me! I have a loom at home and I often use it to make clothes!"

"Hmm?" D was slightly surprised.

Mrs. Dante proudly said, "When I was younger, I was a very popular tailor in town! But later, many clothing factories emerged, and our handmade tailor shop slowly got phased out."

Upon hearing this, D thought seriously for a moment, then thanked the kind elderly lady: "Then I'll be troubling you."

"Don't worry, this is my specialty," Mrs. Dante said enthusiastically.

The amount of wool was too much for her to carry alone, so D had Dragonite help transport Mrs. Dante and the wool over.

Seeing Mrs. Dante, who is very kind to him, Dragonite was very happy, gladly taking the wool from D's hands and following Mrs. Dante to the town.

*

At five in the evening, Zack arrived at the ranch right on schedule to pick up goods.

After loading the barrels of MooMoo Milk onto the truck, he wiped the sweat on his face with a towel and said:

"Next month will be Thanksgiving. Have you prepared the pumpkin cookies?"

"Pumpkin cookies?" D was a bit confused.

He knew about Thanksgiving because Zack had mentioned it to him before.

This day marks the last day of autumn, where people gather to celebrate the festival and give thanks to Calyrex.

But what did pumpkin cookies mean?

Seeing his puzzled look, Zack was quite surprised: "You don't know?"

D was even more baffled: "What do you mean?"

Zack thought carefully, muttering to himself: "Makes sense, you left Porto Marinada Town at a young age, so it's normal you don't know."

"In that case, let me explain!" Zack said. "It's a tradition that has been passed down in Porto Marinada Town for a long time. On the last day of autumn, to celebrate the harvest, people living near the town give each other small cookies made from crops."

"In the evening, children and Pokemon would go door-to-door exchanging their small cookies with the homeowners."

Upon hearing this, D roughly understood why Mayor Thomas and others would celebrate Calyrex on this day.

"I understand now, I'll make more later." He nodded, especially since the pumpkin cookies Mrs. Dante sent in the afternoon had already been eaten by Dragonite and the others.

"That's good!" Zack laughed heartily. "I just let you know in advance so that when the town's kids come over asking for cookies, you won't be caught unprepared."

D thought seriously for a moment and asked, "In that case, can I use one child's cookies to exchange with the next arriving child?"

Working on a loop reuse.

Zack flustered: "That's never happened before."

D chuckled: "I'm just curious, not planning to actually do it."

Zack breathed a sigh of relief. To him, D wasn't the kind to pull such a stunt.

After loading the goods, he drove off from the ranch.

D, meanwhile, picked a golden-yellow large pumpkin from the ground and carried it back into the house.

As dusk approached, his sister, Indeedee, began preparing dinner.

D cleaned the pumpkin, then carried it to the Cream Workshop prepared for Alcremie.

Though called the Cream Workshop, it was actually a room dedicated to making cakes, snacks, and desserts.

Alcremie, sharp-eyed, noticed D preparing to make desserts and quickly came running.

However, due to its small size, it couldn't keep up and could only issue a growl asking D to wait.

"Maa maa!"

D stopped and watched the small Alcremie scramble across the sofa to reach him. He lowered his body as Alcremie hopped onto the large pumpkin he held.

"Maa maa!" It wanted to make desserts together!

Seeing Alcremie's expression, D said with amusement, "Even after a whole day outside playing with Naclstack, you're not tired?"

"Maa maa~" Alcremie shook its head happily, jiggling its little body, a light sweet aroma wafting from it.

"Come to think of it, I've seen other Alcremie having timid personalities. Except for their Trainer, they hide from everyone else. Why are you so courageous?" D was curious.

Not only does it hang out with a much larger-than-itself Naclstack, but it dares to squeeze onto the same sofa with Koraidon.

"Maa maa!" Alcremie appeared proud, because it was a brave little Alcremie!

"Do you know what usually happens to courageous Pokemon?" D raised an eyebrow and deliberately asked.

"Maa maa?"

---

As expected, Alcremie was intrigued.

D: "The first type, the bold Pokemon that fears no existence, their lively and cheerful nature makes them friends with everyone."

Alcremie listened intently, then cheerfully said, "Maa maa~" That must be talking about it!

"Don't rush, there's also the second type." D smiled slightly, seeing Alcremie listening attentively, he continued, "But their size is too small, and they emit a very sweet cream scent, which might attract some sweet-toothed Pokemon and end up eating them whole."

At first, Alcremie was listening seriously, but gradually, the more it listened, the more something felt off.

This made it feel a bit scared.

Alcremie: (๑ŏ﹏ŏ๑)

"I'm going to start making pumpkin cookies, why don't you go wait over there for a while."

While speaking, D went to the Cream Workshop.

He placed Alcremie on the windowsill, then started making the pumpkin cookies.

Alcremie had already realized that D was trying to scare it.

It exclaimed loudly, "Maa maa!" Bad D!

D prepared to chop the pumpkin into pieces, steam it in the microwave, and then mash it.

Hearing Alcremie's voice, he didn't look up, continuing with his work while speaking seriously:

"It's not just me scaring you, these things really happen. You all have delicious sweet cream all over you, and wild Pokemon without a Trainer's guidance might just eat you directly."

Upon hearing this, Alcremie felt that D had a point and began to feel scared again.

D comforted, "Don't be afraid, Naclstack will protect you well! Once they evolve into tall and large Garganacl, with five together, most Normal Pokemon can't defeat them."

Standing in the hallway outside, brother Indeedee wore a complicated expression as he checked if D needed any Helping Hand.

Does comforting someone like this really work?

It turns out, this comfort worked well for Alcremie.

It visibly breathed a sigh of relief, sat in the Cream Workshop for a while, and seeing that D didn't need its assistance, Alcremie quietly left.

It went downstairs, ran to the living room, and out the door, heading straight to the Underground Cave where Naclstack lived.

Looking at the five Naclstack walking around the cave, still full of energy after a whole day of play, Alcremie took a long sigh of relief.

Seeing Alcremie suddenly come down, the Naclstacks wore confused expressions.

Alcremie earnestly asked, "Maa maa?" (Are we best friends?)

The Naclstacks nodded repeatedly, it was beyond doubt that their bond was the strongest in the ranch.

Alcremie continued to ask, "Maa maa?" (Then what if I face danger one day? Will you protect me?)

"Stack!" Of course!

Alcremie: "Maa maa!" (Then you need to work hard and evolve into Garganacl quickly, D said Garganacl is a very powerful Pokemon!)

Regarding evolution, the Naclstacks certainly desired it.

However, this matter was beyond their control!

After leaving the underground cave, Alcremie was still a bit uneasy.

It thought carefully and realized apart from relying on the Naclstacks, it needs to strive harder itself.

"Maa maa!" It's decided!

Alcremie's heart suddenly filled with fighting spirit.

It wants to train too!

Like Dragonite, transforming into the strongest Alcremie in the world!

---

---

---

---

# 219: Chapter 216: Go, Metronome!

The process of making pumpkin cookies isn't complicated.

Plus, D has specially studied how to make these sweet treats.

Now, he can make cakes, puffs, puddings, and cookies.

First, blend sliced pumpkin into puree, then mix it evenly with previously prepared MooMoo Milk. Next, pour Dolliv's olive oil and sugar into the eggs, and whisk until fluffy.

Step three, pour in the pumpkin puree, add flour and cornstarch, mix the three evenly, and pour into a baking tray with round molds.

Finally, put two fresh red cranberries on top as decorations, and bake in the oven for about ten minutes.

Soon, with a ding sound, the oven stopped working.

D donned oven mitts and pulled out the baking tray, seeing neat, round cookies exuding a rich fragrance.

It's the unique flavor created by the combination of the sweet scent of milk and the aroma of pumpkin.

"Done!"

Seeing these cookies looking good, D felt very pleased in his heart. He really seems to have some talent in making desserts.

D glanced down, and the information of these little cookies appeared in his mind.

[Pumpkin Cookies (B+): Cookies made from pumpkins grown on the farm, a treat full of rural charm.

Cooking Effect: Lucky lv2, when used by Pokémon, the abnormal status hit rate of moves like Sleep Powder, Hypnosis, Stun Spore, and Ice Beam is moderately increased, probability of powerful moves during Metronome rises, duration: 10 minutes.

Review: Delicious cookies born to celebrate the harvest, it is said to bring good luck to Pokémon.]

"Hmm?"

Lucky?

At first glance at this effect, D thought he had made a dish similar to Lucky Pudding.

But upon closer inspection, he discovered this effect only seemed effective when Pokémon used moves?

D curiously picked up a still hot cookie.

The increased abnormal status hit rate of moves like Sleep Powder and Ice Beam was fine.

But the following Metronome is interesting.

It's well-known that Metronome is a move that heavily relies on luck.

In the game, Metronome is described like this—waving fingers to stimulate the brain, using any move from all moves.

This means, moves like Groudon's Precipice Blades, Kyogre's Origin Pulse, Palkia's Spacial Rend, and Dialga's Time RoarRoar of Time can all be rolled out!

To roll out these skills, the only requirement for Pokémon is to be lucky enough.

Speaking of Metronome, D first thought of that Togepi of Misty's in the animation.

He couldn't help but imagine a Togepi rolling Arceus's Judgment in battle.

That scene, just thinking about it makes one excited!

D excitedly licked his lips.

This pumpkin cookie is interesting!

But unfortunately, it doesn't have much effect on the Pokémon in his ranch.

And the duration is very short, only maintaining for ten minutes. Unless specifically used for battles, it can't be used in daily life.

Belonging to a case of something is better than nothing.

D poured the baked pumpkin cookies into a plate and carried them downstairs.

Among his Pokémon, not many like sweet food.

Besides Alcremie, it's only his sister Indeedee, the others are not against it, but not particularly interested in sweet food.

Therefore, D originally intended to find Alcremie to help him with the tasting.

But looking around the living room, he didn't find Alcremie's presence.

D couldn't help but ask his brother Indeedee closest to him: "Where's Alcremie?"

"Ai Eedy."

His brother Indeedee looked towards the door.

Gone outside?

D was somewhat surprised, checking the outside sky, it was already dark, why was Alcremie still outside at this time?

He left most of the cookies for his sister Indeedee and the others to share, while he carried the plate to the ranch.

He wandered around the ranch, eventually finding Alcremie under the guidance of Butterfree.

Alcremie was talking spiritedly to Dragonite.

Curious, D took out a pack of Translation Nuts Cake from his pocket.

Next, he heard Alcremie milkily say: "Mama!" (Dragonite! Please teach me how to become stronger!)

Dragonite looked troubledly at the tiny Alcremie in front of it: "Awoo..." (My method may not help you, as you don't have wings and can't fly...)

Alcremie looked puzzled: "Mama?" (Does becoming strong have anything to do with flying?)

Dragonite's eyes brightened as it explained: "Awoo!" (Because only by flying can you make deliveries! D says, the delivery process is exercise that helps Pokémon become strong, I owe today to deliveries!)

Upon hearing this, Alcremie looked down at its body.

It felt very sad.

Because it doesn't have wings like Dragonite and can't make deliveries.

Alcremie said sadly: "Mama..." (I want to become the strongest Pokémon in the world...because then I won't be eaten!)

Dragonite was already bewildered.

D then stepped forward with Butterfree, comforting the disheartened Alcremie: "Don't worry, no one will eat you, everyone on the ranch will protect you."

---

Alcremie and Dragonite looked over.

Seeing D, Dragonite cheered, "Awooo!" (D!)

D nodded at it and then smiled, saying, "Open your mouth."

"Aw!" Dragonite immediately obediently opened its mouth.

D tossed a few cookies over, then looked at Alcremie.

Alcremie heard D's comforting words but seemed conflicted: "Maa-maa…" (But I still want to become stronger...)

D: "..." In hindsight, he shouldn't have told that story to scare Alcremie.

Recalling what Alcremie had just said—becoming the strongest Pokemon in the world.

The strongest... Even he wasn't sure of that, but becoming the biggest was definitely possible.

His family's field could grow pumpkins of any size, which could change Alcremie's form.

Moreover, apart from pumpkins, he could also help Alcremie Dynamax, though they'd need to make a trip to the Galar Region.

Thinking of this, D handed the cookies in his hand to it: "Alright, I'll think of a way to help you later, but for now, have some snacks! I just baked these little cookies, give them a try!"

Alcremie started eating the pumpkin cookies.

Watching this scene, D suddenly thought of something.

That Alcremie could learn Metronome!

Directly helping Alcremie grow into the world's strongest Pokemon was difficult, but at times like this, smart people learn to solve problems indirectly.

For example, first let Alcremie eat the Lucky Pudding, and then eat the pumpkin cookies while the good fortune from the pudding is in effect.

Combining double luck might just allow Alcremie to perform some incredible moves.

But the problem was, Alcremie didn't know Metronome yet, and in his entire ranch, only his sister Indeedee knew this move.

After a moment of thought, D said to Alcremie, "I have a way to help you become stronger."

Upon hearing this, Alcremie's eyes lit up, and it quickly asked, "Maa-maa?" (How?)

Is he going to teach it to fly?

"Metronome." D pointed to the pumpkin cookies in front of Alcremie, explaining the cookie's effect: "Although it can't make you stronger directly, it can help you borrow the power of other Pokemon to use those powerful moves."

"Maa-maa..." Alcremie was a bit confused.

D thought for a moment and gave an example: "You know Koraidon, right? Its most powerful move is jumping high into the sky, then unleashing a fierce Tackle towards the opponent."

"If you grasp Metronome and then eat one of these cookies, you might borrow Koraidon's power to use this move that only it knows to defeat the bad guys who want to eat you."

"Maa-maa!" Alcremie looked hopeful; could it really perform such powerful moves?

D smiled: "Not just Koraidon, Metronome can unleash many powerful Pokemon's moves."

How amazing! It had to learn this move!

But, how could it learn it? Alcremie looked puzzled.

Seeing this, D laughed: "That will depend on Indeedee's help."

But it was already time for dinner.

They first went home to fill their stomachs before finding his sister Indeedee.

D looked expectantly at his adorable sister Indeedee: "You know how to use Metronome, right?"

"Ayee~" His sister Indeedee nodded happily, then looked over curiously, what was going on?

"I'd like to ask you to teach this move to Alcremie." D explained, "The pumpkin cookie you just ate has the effect of enhancing Metronome."

His sister Indeedee instantly understood, looking down at the expectant Alcremie, nodding willingly.

"Ayee~"

So, D took a few Pokemon to a secluded grass field.

D looked towards his sister Indeedee: "The pumpkin cookie also works on you, and during its effect, maybe you could use some very rare moves."

His sister Indeedee gently nodded, having understood the cookie's effect.

Seeing this, D didn't say much more and stood at the back, watching his sister Indeedee's demonstration.

"Ayee!" (Watch closely, I'm going to demonstrate now!) His sister Indeedee looked at Alcremie.

Alcremie nodded excitedly: "Maa-maa!"

Then, his sister Indeedee gently waved her fingers.
