Her hands were instantly enveloped in milky white light, a magical power emanating from within her.

All the white light converged at a point, and when the energy reached its peak, it transformed into a dazzling Flash Cannon, fiercely blasting forward.

Feeling the terrifying power contained in this move, Alcremie's eyes shone: "Maa-maa!" Amazing!

"Steel Techniques, Flash Cannon." D murmured the name of the move shaken out by Metronome in his heart.

Seeing D and Alcremie's reaction, his sister Indeedee felt a bit helpless.

"Ayee..." Right now, shouldn't the focus be on the action of using Metronome?

D scratched his head apologetically: "Sorry, sorry, it's my first time seeing this move, and I found it too fascinating."

He looked at Alcremie, thinking its focus must also have been on the Flash Cannon rather than Indeedee's actions.

D: "Want to try again?"

His sister Indeedee nodded helplessly.

---

D looked at Alcremie and instructed, "This time, focus on Indeedee's fingers. Observe its movements closely, then try to imitate them."

"Maa-maa!" Alcremie responded, indicating it understood!

"Very good, let's begin!" D signaled.

Lil' Indeedee shook its finger once again.

The intense white light enveloped its hand once more, and suddenly, a powerful current surged out, instantly covering Lil' Indeedee's entire body.

For a moment, the night was illuminated by the intense electric light, as if it were daylight.

Amidst the surging current, Lil' Indeedee charged forward.

But without a target, it hit nothing.

Moments later, the electric light vanished, and Lil' Indeedee returned to its previous state.

But this time, a look of astonishment appeared on its face as it looked down incredulously at its own hands.

"Ai-yee?" What was that move just now? It felt amazing!

Lil' Indeedee looked at D with a seeking gaze.

D: "If I'm not mistaken, the move you used should be Bolt Strike."

"Ai-yee?"

D was somewhat excited: "It's not ordinary! This is the exclusive move of the Legendary Beast known as the Dragon of Ideals, Zekrom. In short, you were very lucky!"

It turns out pumpkin cookies really are effective!

D looked at Alcremie again and asked, "Did you see Indeedee's movements clearly?"

Alcremie nodded with bright eyes, "Maa-maa!"

D: "Want to give it a try?"

Alcremie responded, first looking down at its own hands, then imitating Lil' Indeedee's movements, it gently started to shake.

But after shaking for quite a while, no white light indicating the successful activation of a move appeared.

Seeing this, Lil' Indeedee stepped forward and demonstrated for Alcremie once more.

This time, it didn't shake out the exclusive move of the Legendary Beast, only a Normal Swift.

"Ai-yee— Ai-yee." Lil' Indeedee started to share its insights on using Metronome.

Alcremie listened intently, and under Indeedee's teaching, slowly began to practice.

First time, failed.

Second time, failed.

Third time, failed.

Gradually, the sky had completely darkened.

Pokémon coming to the Pokémon Tavern for a drink also gradually arrived at the ranch.

They were drawn by Alcremie's practice, and for the moment, didn't go to drink, instead gathering around to watch the excitement.

Fortunately, Alcremie wasn't disturbed by these Pokémon; it was fully immersed in practicing Metronome.

Finally, on the fifty-seventh attempt, the two little hands covered in cream lit up with intense white light.

Following that, Alcremie's eyes, as beautiful as rubies, shone.

"Maa-maa!" It succeeded!

At this moment, the gaze of all humans and Pokémon present concentrated on its hands.

The strong white light eventually coalesced into a bright, radiant orb.

That orb seemed to house tremendously powerful energy, causing what seemed like an ordinary orb to tremble slightly, as if unable to contain it.

Witnessing this scene, D's heart rose with anticipation.

What move would it be?

The next moment, the answer to this question emerged in his mind.

He saw that orb suddenly launched by Alcremie into the sky.

It burst like fireworks in the dim night sky.

The scattering points of light resembled meteors, igniting crimson tails as they fell rapidly.

Then— it hit the ground with a resounding crash.

Just like a meteor shower.

---

---

---

---

# 220: Chapter 217: Alcremie Pilots a Gundam? (4k)

Judgment Beam!

That's the Judgment Beam of Arceus!

D took a sharp breath, exclaiming in horror!

Getting the exclusive special move of the Creator God Arceus with a single try, Alcremie's luck is sky-high!

Not just him, but even all the Pokemon watching nearby were astounded by this scene.

They looked up at the night sky, where light trails with a red tail flame streaked by, reflecting in their eyes.

After the move ended, over a dozen craters appeared in the pasture, fortunately just in an open area, causing no harm to the ranch itself.

The Pokemon spectators were all visibly shocked.

How could this seemingly weak Alcremie use such terrifying moves?

Even the Alcremie itself didn't know the answer to this question.

It stood there dazed for a long while.

It took quite some time for Alcremie to regain its composure, looking down incredulously at its own small hands.

This kind of power...

Was it really something it used on its own?

"Why not try shaking it again?" D suggested tentatively.

Alcremie blinked and used Metronome once more.

Littles fingers gently shook, and amid the dense white light, a thick beam full of destructive aura shot straight into the hole.

Grass in the intense light withered and vanished into dust, leaving a clear path on the once lush green lawn.

Watching this, the name of the move sprang to D's mind.

Hyper Beam!

A powerful Normal Type move with a power of 150 in the games!

So strong!

"Too bad the effect of the Pumpkaboo Cookies isn't permanent, lasting only ten short minutes."

If only it could last forever...

D fell into thought, recalling all the dishes he had made since his arrival.

The only one that seemed to have a permanent effect was the Lucky Pudding he made for Teddiursa earlier.

Then comes the question, can the effect of the Pumpkaboo Cookies become permanent like the Lucky Pudding?

Emotion...

What's most important is the emotion involved in preparing the food.

"Clap clap clap!"

Seeing Alcremie successfully grasp Metronome and pull out such powerful moves, the Indeedee Sibling clapped happily for it.

Alcremie looked at the gentle Indeedee Sibling, its face turning slightly red with shyness.

D asked with a smile, "How is it? Are you satisfied now?"

"Maa maa!"

Alcremie nodded repeatedly.

D thought for a moment and said to Alcremie, "Actually, more than being suited to direct battles, I think you're more suited for support roles, aiding other Pokemon."

Hearing this, Alcremie looked up, puzzled: "Maa maa?"

D gave it an example: "For instance, when Naclstack evolves into Garganacl, you can stand on their shoulders and fight alongside them."

"With Garganacl as the attacker, you can use moves like Decorate, Aromatic Mist, Aromatherapy, Helping Hand, or Baby-Doll Eyes from its body."

Decorate can greatly boost Garganacl's Attack and Special Attack, Aromatic Mist can enhance its Special Defense, Aromatherapy can remove all its abnormal statuses, and Helping Hand can increase the power of its moves.

As for Baby-Doll Eyes... it can lower the opponent's Attack.

This tactic, D called it the "Mecha Tactic."

Calyrex can ride on Glastrier and Spectrier to battle, and Dragapult can carry two Dreepy into battle simultaneously.

So Alcremie can ride on Garganacl to engage in fights too.

"Maa maa..."

Alcremie's eyes brightened; this was a path it had never envisioned.

Seeing this, D knew Alcremie's interest was piqued.

"Alright, it's getting late, time to head back and rest!"

"Maa maa!"

Alcremie returned to its previously innocent and lively demeanor.

The Indeedee Siblings went to the tavern to entertain the Pokemon in town.

Pawmot and Glaceon also followed to join the fun.

D then took Alcremie back to the living room.

He placed the contented Alcremie on the cupboard, then went to check on Cyclizar's Shed Tail condition.

"How are you feeling? Does it still hurt?" D asked.

Cyclizar shook its tail, carefully feeling it: "Gaooo~"

D: "Glad it doesn't hurt, you go on sleeping, I still have things to do."

Cyclizar nodded obediently.

After checking on Cyclizar's condition, D returned to the Cream Workshop on the second floor.

He took the ingredients needed for making Pumpkaboo Cookies from the fridge.

Emotion... Integrating emotion into cooking might just extend the duration of the Pumpkaboo Cookies.

Despite saying so, emotion is intangible and untouchable, a rather mysterious thing.

D closed his eyes and began to feel, recalling everything that happened since meeting Alcremie, trying to find that familiar feeling.

The first time they met, Alcremie and Smoliv cautiously observed his appearance.

After living together for a while, Alcremie grew acquainted with him, its nature becoming lively and cheerful.

After the Nacli arrived, Alcremie began to help him like an older sister, taking care of the five Nacli and playing with them.

They made a cream cake together for the first time...

They gathered by the riverside on summer nights to watch fireflies...

They lived together from the spring of blooming life to the autumn of yellowing leaves.

They would live together for a long, long time, until the end of life.

---

D's mind conjured images of his old age, sitting in a rocking chair reminiscing with Alcremie.

Almost... It's time.

D opened his eyes, and with emotions surging, began making the second batch of pumpkin cookies.

Thirty minutes later, accompanied by a ding, the oven stopped working.

D pulled out the baking tray and lowered his head to check the information on this batch of pumpkin cookies.

[Pumpkin Cookies (A): Made with pumpkins grown on the farm, these cookies are infused with the feelings of the maker, a snack full of rustic charm.

Cooking Effect: Lucky Lv3, when used by Pokemon, greatly enhances the hit rate of moves like Stun Spore, Hypnosis, Sleep Powder, Ice Beam; greatly increases the chance of using powerful moves when Metronome is used; effectiveness lasts for 1 hour.

Evaluation: Delicious cookies born to celebrate the harvest, said to bring good fortune to Pokemon.]

"Is it no good after all?"

Perhaps since he had anticipated it in his heart, D wasn't too disappointed with the effect of this batch of pumpkin cookies.

Because he could feel that the emotions he tried to summon seemed too deliberate and weren't enough.

Unlike the natural emotions when bidding farewell to Teddiursa.

Perhaps, it would only be possible to make it when something major happens, like Alcremie leaving for a journey, or some special situation.

Nevertheless, this enhanced version of the effect is already quite good.

The cooking level of Lucky was raised from Lv2 to Lv3, the abnormal status hit rate increased from moderate to substantial, and the duration was extended to one hour.

It can be used barely.

If worse comes to worst, he can tie a little bag on Alcremie's body and put more pumpkin cookies inside; it can eat one when using Metronome.

*

Early the next morning, after breakfast, Mrs. Dantes called.

D answered, and Mrs. Dantes' gentle voice came from the other end.

"D, I still don't know Cyclizar's size. Do you have a tape measure? Could you help me measure Cyclizar's height, waist, and then tell me?"

D then remembered this matter: "It's my oversight. I'll measure it later and bring it back to you."

Mrs. Dantes: "Okay, I'll wait for you at home."

D hung up and found the tape measure he had bought earlier from the storeroom upstairs, then found Cyclizar, measuring its height, waist, and the size of the tire-like airbag on its chest, while it looked on with a perplexed expression.

"Ga-ow?" Cyclizar watched his actions with a face full of confusion.

D: "Just measuring your height to see if you've grown taller."

Upon hearing this, Pawmot ran over to have D measure it too.

"Alright, line up, one at a time!" D smiled.

Pawmot immediately stood behind Cyclizar.

Koraidon gurgled and promptly stood behind Pawmot.

Seeing this, the sister Indeedee happily came over.

She looked at her brother.

Her brother Indeedee's eyes were filled with hesitation, appearing moved but trying to maintain a composed butler image.

"Indeedee..."

The sister sighed and forcefully dragged her old brother over.

D noticed this scene and couldn't help but laugh.

The brother Indeedee immediately pulled a face, trying to maintain his mature image.

D, while measuring Cyclizar's waist, merrily said, "Sister, your brother's been like this since he was little. The first time I met you guys, he had the same expression."

It hasn't been long since then, so they both remember the scene.

When D came to see them, he also left them some tasty snacks.

The sister Indeedee nodded in agreement.

The brother Indeedee, recalling the scene of himself eating snacks with crumbs all over after D left, couldn't help blushing.

D found this personality quite amusing: "Actually, your brother is very cute. Despite being a baby Pokemon, he always tries to act mature."

The brother Indeedee's face turned even redder.

The sister Indeedee couldn't help but laugh.

"However," having teased them, D continued, "your brother is still very reliable. Thanks to him, I can be as relaxed as I am now. Before you came, I was overwhelmed every day!"

Upon hearing this, the siblings' eyes flickered.

They sensed a very apparent emotion of gratitude from D...

Both Indeedees instinctively used the horns on their heads to receive this emotion, which was immediately converted into a warm energy within their bodies.

Having received gratitude from their master, the sister Indeedee was very happy: "Indeedee!"

The brother Indeedee nodded slightly: "Indeedee."

"Alright!" Having collected Cyclizar's data, D petted its head and then said to the following Pawmot, "Your turn!"

"Pawmot!"

Pawmot eagerly ran forward, head held high, standing upright.

D measured its height: "Well, 1.1 meters, it's a very robust Pawmot."

Normally, Pawmot stands between 0.7 meters and 0.9 meters; his Pawmot is already considered a large Pawmot.

If placed in Monster Hunter World, it would be a large gold Pawmot.

"Pawmot!"

Pawmot joyfully cheered and then moved aside.

Third up was Koraidon.

This big fellow required D to look up.

D stood on the sofa to measure the height of the battle-form Koraidon: "Um, 2.6 meters... Impressive! You're the tallest Pokemon on the ranch!"

Koraidon looked proud: "Gyro!" It was also the strongest Pokemon!

D understood its meaning and said helplessly: "Yes, yes, such an incredible Koraidon, can you please move to the side?"

"Gyro!"

Koraidon excitedly gave D, his favorite, a lick with its tongue and then moved aside.

D: "..."

D grimaced: "You taste like fried cutlet, go drink some red tea to rinse your mouth!"

Seeing D expressing disdain, Koraidon drooped its head and sadly went to the refrigerator to find iced red tea to rinse its mouth.

The fourth Pokemon to have its height measured was the sister Indeedee.

D first petted her fluffy head before starting to measure her height.

"Well, 0.8 meters, your body is very healthy within the normal range," D said, "But you haven't turned one year old yet, you might grow taller in the future."

The sister Indeedee displayed a joyful smile: "Indeedee～"

The last one was the brother Indeedee who had a serious face.

"Relax, don't be so tense," D said with a smile, "0.9 meters, 0.1 meters taller than your sister, you're also a healthy Indeedee. All done, go play now!"

Seeing D refraining from teasing him, the brother Indeedee relaxed inside.

He hurriedly escaped the scene, grabbed a broom from somewhere, and started doing chores.

D hesitated: "..."

Forget it, he decided not to tease him.

Leaving the household management to the two Indeedees, D went outside to find Dragonite, preparing to visit Mrs. Dantes.

Before setting off, D noticed something.

Not far away on the grass, Alcremie was using her magical cream to decorate a Naclstack.

Nearby, four Naclstacks were already coated with cream like flower cats.

D watched on with interest.

That move is likely Alcremie's signature move, Decorate?

Decorate, a Fairy-type move, he remembered in the game its effect was to raise the Attack and Special Attack of allies by 2 stages, a very powerful supporting move.

It seems Alcremie took in what he said last night.

After watching a while, D turned to Dragonite and said, "Let's go to Mrs. Dantes' house!"

"Gwoooo!"

Dragonite responded, then flapped its wings, flying towards Porto Marinada Town in the distance.

---

---

---

---

# 221: Chapter 218: Forest Wildfire

Porto Marinada Town, Blue Sea Street.

Dragonite slowly descended in front of a mansion.

D jumped off its back and stepped forward to knock on the door.

In no time, Mrs. Dantes appeared behind the door, smiling warmly.

She adjusted her reading glasses with her hand and said in a gentle tone, "Come on in!"

D nodded, and as they walked toward the living room, Mrs. Dantes said, "Half of the wool you gave me yesterday has already been made into cloth. At this rate, it will take about two more days to finish."

Two days, that is, August 19th, still in time.

D had no objections; under Mrs. Dantes' hospitality, he and Dragonite sat on the sofa.

Dragonite was too large, the sofa was too small, and its tail was quite in the way with no space to place it, causing Dragonite to huddle awkwardly, holding its tail.

Seeing this scene, Mrs. Dantes couldn't help but laugh, "Dragonite is just too adorable. Wait here a moment, I'll go get you some snacks."

"Rawr~"

Dragonite didn't feel awkward at all; instead, it felt very happy.

D politely said, "Thank you, Mrs. Dantes."

"It's just a little thing," Mrs. Dantes said with a smile as she went to the kitchen.

D sat patiently on the sofa, calculating in his mind how much it would cost to custom-make a Pokémon outfit like this.

Soon, Mrs. Dantes came over with a cup of tea and a cup of berry juice.

She placed the steaming cup of tea in front of D, "This is for D."

Then, she placed the cup of berry juice in front of Dragonite, "This one is for Dragonite, and here are some cookies I baked earlier, enjoy!"

"Rawr!"

Dragonite cheered, immediately using its claws to eat the cookies as if this were its own home.

D noticed this scene and couldn't help but ask, "Is Dragonite always like this at your place?"

Mrs. Dantes picked up on his meaning and quickly said, "Dragonite is a very well-behaved Pokémon. Don't blame it; I insisted on giving it to him."

D: "…"

Mrs. Dantes had always lived in this small town with her husband, Riot.

D hadn't heard any news about their children, and he didn't know the details, but looking at this old lady, she truly seemed to like Dragonite.

Oh well, that's just how it is. Later, he'll let Dragonite deliver more pasture products over here.

D sighed and didn't continue on this topic. Instead, he turned to the business at hand.

He handed over the measurements he took that morning to Mrs. Dantes, "Here is Cyclizar's height and waist measurement."

Mrs. Dantes glanced at it and kindly said, "Alright, I understand. I'll make the clothes for Cyclizar based on these measurements."

D: "And what about the price?"

Mrs. Dantes was slightly stunned, then chuckled and shook her head, "No charge, I'm happy to do you this favor."

D: "That's not appropriate. Business is business, we can't keep taking advantage of you."

"It's just a piece of clothing, nothing like taking advantage. Consider it a gift from me to the child." Mrs. Dantes was firm in her stance that if he insisted on paying, she would not make it.

D sighed in resignation, "Alright then, I'll leave it to you."

Only then did Mrs. Dantes' face break into a smile again, "That's more like it! Actually, in detail, it's us who should be thanking you. If it weren't for the goat milk you gave us, Riot wouldn't have recovered so quickly."

D wanted to say that the goat milk was paid for, but he noticed that Mrs. Dantes seemed to be alone, so he asked, "Where is Mr. Riot?"

Mrs. Dantes said, "Him? I heard that some auction stalls have opened in Porto Marinada Market, specifically for selling Trainers' items, so that guy went to join the fun."

D: "…Alright."

He didn't say much more, finished the cup of tea in front of him, and bade goodbye to Mrs. Dantes, riding Dragonite back to the pasture.

He had no choice; since Mrs. Dantes wouldn't accept money, he could only instruct Dragonite to deliver some special products from the pasture to them.

Looking at the crops, rock salt, berries, and fresh milk placed in front of him, Dragonite looked confused.

"Rawr?"

D explained, "Since Mrs. Dantes won't accept our money, we'll use these things to thank her for her help. Similarly, if Mrs. Dantes needs help in the future, and we extend a Helping Hand to her, I believe they will reciprocate in the same way. It's called mutual assistance."

So complicated…

Dragonite didn't understand and felt its head spinning. After struggling for a long time and still not figuring it out, it simply put these complicated thoughts aside, picked up the gift in front of it, and flew towards Porto Marinada Town.

*

Year 198 of the League calendar, August 18th, Autumn.

Early this morning, D was sleeping soundly with Pawmot in his arms, when he was suddenly awakened by a sharp alarm signal.

D opened his eyes, somewhat confused.

The sound continued, seeming to come from not far away at the pasture.

This sound…

It seemed like the fire department's alarm from the town?

Fire department's alarm!

Realizing this, D suddenly woke up.

Not just him, but Koraidon, who had been sleeping by the bedside, also seemed to sense the unusual atmosphere. It walked to the window, looking outside with a solemn expression.

---

Outside the window, the flames blazed, and seeing this scene, D widened his eyes and quickly walked to the window to look outside.

In the distance, the mountain behind was ablaze with fire, the roaring flames dyeing the night sky a deep red.

The thing Miss Jenny worried about has finally happened!

The mountain behind caught fire!

This season is precisely autumn when the leaves start to yellow, and the ground on the mountain is piled with flammable dead branches and leaves. Once they catch fire, the result is predictable.

"No, we have to help!"

D hurriedly got dressed, brought Koraidon and Pawmot into the living room, and said to Cyclizar and Alcremie, who were woken up inside, "You guys keep sleeping!"

D quickly ran outside and happened to see Miss Jenny and Growlithe riding a police motorcycle past the ranch.

Seeing the lights on at the ranch, Miss Jenny turned around, stopped the motorcycle, and looking at D from a distance, said seriously:

"The mountain is on fire, it's dangerous here, D. The fire will spread over soon. Leave quickly!"

Growlithe: "Woof woof!"

How could D leave? This is the ranch he worked so hard to manage with his Pokemon!

"Where's Dragonite?"

D shouted, and soon Dragonite flew over from the grass.

Just woken up, Dragonite was still in a sleepy state and drowsily approached D.

After seeing the serious expression on his face, Dragonite instantly shivered.

"Do you see that?" D pointed to the mountain where the flames were sky-high.

Dragonite turned its head to look and saw the crimson sky, and the whole dragon immediately became alert.

D: "Please, use Rain Dance, like last time, and make it rain around here!"

Dragonite sensed the gravity in D's words, nodded, then turned and flew towards the mountain.

Seeing this, Miss Jenny was slightly stunned, but she quickly reacted and said seriously, "If you plan to help, come with me!"

D nodded, riding Koraidon, followed behind Miss Jenny.

The two hurried through the ranch towards the mountain.

From afar, D saw a lot of humans and Pokemon gathered at the mountain's foot.

A firefighter, with an anxious expression, was reporting to his captain: "We can't, the fire has completely spread. Our firefighter Pokemon's power isn't enough, and the Rain Dance only covers a small area, unable to fully stop this fire!"

The captain's face was sullen, and he looked at the mountain, heavy-hearted but still calmly issuing orders: "Create an isolation barrier around the mountain to prevent the fire from spreading to the fields below!"

At this moment, another firefighter ran over: "Re-re-report! Our helicopter saw a group of trapped Pokemon in the center of the fire! They... they're working together to fight this fire!"

The captain's face grew grimmer: "Organize a rescue immediately!"

"Yes!"

The team member quickly turned and ran off, but at this moment, the clear night sky suddenly gathered a mass of dark clouds.

A vast area of darkness was sharply highlighted by the glow of the fire.

"Boom!"

Accompanied by a clap of thunder,

Large raindrops poured down, drenching the ground engulfed by the fire.

All the firefighters on the scene showed a look of joy on their faces.

It's raining!

Seeing this scene, D immediately breathed a sigh of relief.

Thankfully, they made it in time…

The rain was getting heavier, seemingly turning into a downpour.

The cold rainwater met the raging flames, and the whole mountain instantly hissed and crackled.

The fire stopped spreading, and amidst this downpour, it gradually slowed.

The ring of fire began to shrink, leaving only a wasteland of ashes on what was once a flourishing land.

Everyone took shelter under the canopy of the fire truck and looked up at the outside downpour.

D, with Koraidon, followed Miss Jenny to the crowd.

Some firefighters, witnessing this scene, couldn't hide their excitement, whispering with teary eyes, "Thank goodness, thank goodness..."

Miss Jenny remained as calm as at the beginning. She glanced around and asked, "How is the situation now?"

The firefighter captain's grim face slightly eased: "Casualties are unknown; we have to wait for the fire to completely subside to know."

Miss Jenny: "What caused this fire?"

Firefighter Captain: "A Trainer camping on the mountain forgot to put out the campfire while resting at night. When he woke up, everything around him was engulfed in flames. He hurriedly rode his Flying Pokemon and contacted the fire department."

Hearing this, Miss Jenny's face became very unpleasant.

Just as she expected, such an incident eventually happened…

At this time, a sudden exclamation came through the firefighter captain's headset.

This middle-aged man, with red eyes, thought another emergency had occurred. He immediately frowned and loudly inquired, "Report the situation!"

"It's, it's Dragonite!"

In the air, the firefighter piloting the helicopter, releasing cooling bombs, was shocked watching the scene not far away.

In the darkness of the night and amidst the storm, a mysterious and powerful Dragonite was overlooking everything below.

It flapped its wings, creating a strong airflow, which turned into a hurricane, sweeping the storm towards the still-burning area.

Witnessing this scene, the firefighter immediately understood everything.

---

This unexpected downpour was no coincidence; it was this powerful Dragonite helping them!

After a brief moment of astonishment, he quickly reported everything he discovered: "It's a Dragonite. It brought down this rainstorm to stop the fire! It's also creating a hurricane to control the direction of the rain. Such... such an incredible Dragonite!"

"What?"

The fire chief on the ground was stunned, an expression of shock appearing on his face.

Dragonite... where did this Dragonite come from?

No, no way, how could a Dragonite possess such power!

Is this scale of a rainstorm really something a single Dragonite can unleash?

Upon hearing this name, Miss Jenny was also momentarily stunned. She then turned to look at D, who was standing quietly on the side, not showing any hint of surprise.

"D?" Miss Jenny cautiously asked, "Is it the Dragonite?"

D nodded slightly: "It's it."

Miss Jenny widened her eyes.

This meant that it was D who stopped the fire!

Hearing their conversation, all the firefighters present were frozen in place.

One firefighter asked incredulously, "That Dragonite is your Pokémon?"

D: "...Yes."

"Wait a minute, I've seen you before, D from the D Ranch!" Another firefighter got sidetracked, "Aren't you a rancher? How do you have such a powerful Pokémon!"

D: "..."

How should he answer this question?

As far as he knew, in most people's eyes, a rancher is just an ordinary country farmer.

He actually understood why some people would be shocked to hear he was a farmer after all.

Because it felt like a farmer who lives by digging food from the ground suddenly started crafting missiles by hand.

"Watch your words!"

The fire chief was the first to come to his senses. He glared at that firefighter, then patted D on the shoulder with a broad smile, saying:

"Well done, D, on behalf of the Porto Marinada Town fire department, I want to express our gratitude!"

Miss Jenny also visibly breathed a sigh of relief.

She solemnly saluted D: "I also want to thank you on behalf of the police department, D. We owe it all to you this time."

"Just a small effort." D shook his head.

At this time, Miss Joy, who was in charge of emergency aid, arrived late.

She and Audino jumped out of the car, braving the rain to run under the car shelter, asking anxiously: "How's it going?"

Miss Jenny: "Due to this rainstorm, the fire is subsiding. We still don't know the situation regarding the wild Pokémon's injuries, but we have our work cut out for us."

"I see..." Miss Joy seemed to realize something too, her usually gentle expression turning worried.

D stepped aside, silently observing the expressions of these people.

Although the fire was under control, none of the people present seemed happy about it.

Because they all knew that compared to the fire, the rebuilding process after the fire would be more challenging.

Winter was approaching, and those trees that burned down wouldn't grow back in just a few short months.

The back mountain was home to many wild Pokémon, and even if they survived this disaster, it would be difficult for them to endure the cold winter without food.

This would mean that the wild Pokémon ecosystem around Porto Marinada Town would suffer severe damage.

D felt powerless in this regard too.

Suddenly, a figure flashed in his mind.

No, there was still a way.

If he invited Calyrex here, then the ecosystem of the back mountain could be restored to its original state.

Moreover, it could simultaneously draw human and Pokémon faith for Calyrex.

---

---

---

---

# 222: Chapter 219: The Wandering Pokemon

"Eh? Mr. D, why are you here?"

At that moment, Miss Joy noticed D and Koraidon standing to the side.

Joy knew D because the D Ranch was a well-known place in this seaside town, and she had previously gone there to check on the Gogoat's health.

Through repeated interactions, she became acquainted with this young, gentle rancher.

D explained, "The ranch is nearby, so I came to help."

The tall and sturdy firefighter captain said to Joy, "This fire was brought under control so quickly, thanks to D."

Miss Joy looked bewildered.

After Miss Jenny explained the situation, she reacted, "Oh?"

So Mr. D is such an amazing Trainer, Joy thought to herself.

Initially surprised, she then remembered the three Gogoat with an overflowing Grass Type Energy she saw at the ranch last time, and suddenly it didn't seem so strange.

After a brief chat, everyone again turned their gaze to the rain-shrouded mountains in the distance.

Miss Jenny frowned, her naturally stern face appearing even more imposing.

The firefighter captain and crew also wore worried expressions, ready to move into the mountains to start rescue operations as soon as the fire was completely extinguished.

Miss Joy clasped her hands together in a prayer position, "I hope the Pokemon living in the mountains are unharmed..."

There's still some time before the rain stops.

The crowd, looking weary, gathered under the canopy to rest.

D sat beside Koraidon, scanning the world's firefighting team in front of him.

As he expected, this world's firefighting team is composed of humans and Water Type Pokemon.

Among them were familiar Golduck, Poliwhirl evolved from Poliwag, the adorable Quagsire, the blank-eyed Slowbro, and the fierce-looking Gyarados...

D also saw a familiar Pokemon among them, the Quaquaval that often visited his tavern for drinks.

Miss Jenny, standing nearby, noticed his expression and said:

"Some of these Pokemon are wild, and after the fire brigade rescued them, they stayed on to help with the work."

D nodded and began discussing the upcoming rescue operation with Miss Jenny.

"Once the rain stops, you plan to go in to help the wild Pokemon, right?"

Miss Jenny nodded affirmatively, "For this magnitude of a fire, it's impossible for the Pokemon to overcome the disaster on their own, and nature takes time to recover to its original state, so human intervention is vital."

D initially considered mentioning Calyrex but thought better of it, since he couldn't guarantee Calyrex's presence yet, and changed the topic.

"I have a large stockpile of Berries at my ranch. If supplies for the rescue are insufficient, feel free to contact me."

It's something he could help with, even just a little.

Hearing this, Miss Jenny was slightly surprised before she smiled:

"Thank you, but you've already been of great help. The Paldea League will provide us with rescue supplies, and it feels like Chief Champion Geeta might also come over!"

Geeta...

D noticed the admiration in Miss Jenny's tone and thought, the people of Paldea likely really admire this Chief Champion?

At this moment, Miss Jenny's Growlithe let out a pitiful Growl.

D turned his head to see the fluffy Growlithe soaked by rain, its paws covered in mud.

Sitting dejectedly beside Miss Jenny, it lowered its head to look at its muddy self.

D couldn't help but comfort it, "Don't be sad. Once everything is done, we can wash you clean."

Growlithe looked up at him, then happily barked twice, "Woof woof!"

"It really likes you!" Miss Jenny was somewhat surprised.

D thought for a moment and said, "Perhaps it's because I treated it to some Berry Milk?"

Growlithe enthusiastically wagged its tail, "Woof woof!"

Seeing this, D felt a pang in his heart.

The warm, fluffy, and friendly Growlithe made him really want to have one.

However, Growlithe Pokémon seem to belong uniquely to the Jenny family.

In this world, Growlithe and Arcanine are symbols of the Jenny family, and nearly every Miss Jenny is paired with a Growlithe.

Is it a monopoly?

D thought it didn't seem that way since he'd never heard rumors of the Jenny family prohibiting Trainers from raising Growlithe Pokémon.

Moreover, some traveling Trainers are accompanied by Growlithe Pokémon.

After all, one type of Pokémon cannot be controlled by just one family. Look at Lance's Dragon Taming Clan; they haven't monopolized Dragonite worldwide either.

Perhaps it's because Growlithe Pokémon are loyal, reliable, and intelligent, making them perfect as police dogs.

It's just the Jenny family uses them often, leading to a stereotype.

Miss Jenny, being a police officer, was very sensitive to sensing stares.

Noticing D watching her, she turned her head to inquire, "Is there something wrong?"

D snapped back to reality and said, "It feels like all the Miss Jennys around the world look the same."

Hearing this, Miss Jenny couldn't help but chuckle, "That's indeed the case. The Jenny family is quite remarkable; every girl born into the Jenny family looks identical, maybe because our family's genes are identical? It's not just us, the Joy family is the same way."

---

D looked towards Miss Joy busy in the distance.

Miss Jenny continued, "However, it's not entirely the same, because some Jennys and Joys who live in tropical regions do have darker skin, possibly influenced by regional factors."

"It's remarkable."

As she spoke on this topic, Miss Jenny seemed lost in thought, "If possible, I'd rather not be like this because outsiders always mix me up with other Jennys."

D: "..."

This conversation made D think of Brock who traveled with Ash in the anime.

Because Brock has the ability to distinguish between every Joy and Jenny.

Gradually, the fire engulfing the back mountain completely receded, the torrential rain ceased, and the sky began to brighten.

In the morning light, Dragonite slowly descended from the sky to D's side.

The surrounding firefighters brightened up at the sight of Dragonite.

"Was it this Dragonite that stopped the fire?"

"What a remarkable Dragonite!"

"It's the benefactor of Porto Marinada Town! We must properly thank it!"

Even the Water-type Pokemon showed admiring gazes.

Hence, the fire chief, representing the entire fire brigade, walked up to Dragonite and solemnly saluted it, "Thank you for your efforts, Dragonite! We truly appreciate your help this time!"

Facing so much gratitude and admiration, Dragonite felt happy yet somewhat embarrassed.

"Roar roar!"

It called out to the people twice.

The fire chief appeared puzzled and turned to look at D.

D smiled and explained, "It might be saying that this is what it should do, because it is also a part of this place."

"Roar!" Dragonite nodded repeatedly.

Seeing this, the tough-looking man showed a look of appreciation:

"Good Dragonite! But no matter what, we still must thank you."

"Roar～"

Now was not the time for idle chatter.

The fire had receded, leaving devastation behind.

The once lush back mountain now had only some blackened ashes, with large trees charred into soot.

The firefighters began to get busy again.

They, along with Jenny and Joy, organized personnel to venture into the back mountain to search for surviving Pokemon.

D took Dragonite to watch for a while, soon seeing Pokemon being carried out on stretchers and rushed to Miss Joy's ambulance for emergency treatment.

Witnessing this scene, everyone present, both people and Pokemon, felt downhearted.

D sighed and said to Koraidon and Dragonite, "Let's go!"

They couldn't be of much help here anyway, and when they had initially come out, they had disturbed many Pokemon, who must be very worried now.

With Dragonite and Koraidon, D hurried back to the ranch.

As expected, upon arrival, he saw Cyclizar, the Indeedee Sibling, Glaceon, and other Pokemon anxiously waiting.

D quickly stepped forward to soothe their emotions, "It's alright, don't worry."

Only then did a few Pokemon finally relax.

D stroked each of their heads, just about to tell them to go to sleep, when he suddenly saw Pawmot flying over frantically from afar.

It pointed towards the Chicken House, "Paw paw!"

"Hm?" D was stunned, "What's wrong?"

Without Translation Nuts, he couldn't understand Pokemon speech, but seeing Pawmot's state, could it be that a hen was taken by a weasel?

Pawmot tugged at his arm, seemingly wanting him to go take a look.

Seeing this, D led a group of Pokemon to the Chicken House.

In front of the Chicken House was the river that flowed through the ranch.

At this moment, D saw something being washed down by the river from upstream.

He looked closely and saw that the object wasn't debris, but a group of filthy, disheveled Pokemon!

They were flustered, floundering repeatedly in the river.

Seeing this, D hurriedly instructed the Indeedee Sibling to aid them.

Both Indeedee stepped forward to stand by the river, extending their hands, while a layer of blue light appeared in their eyes.

Before the group of Pokemon were submerged by the current, a psychic power enveloped them, promptly rescuing them from the water.

Back on the shore, the Pokemon coughed violently, spitting out the water they had swallowed.

The female Indeedee tapped gently on a Greedent's back.

D scanned the group of Pokemon.

Besides Greedent, there were five other Pokemon here.

Bellibolt, Grafaiai, Tinkatink, Cramorant, Tandemaus,

Each Pokemon looked so disheveled, covered in burn marks, with black stains on their faces and fur.

It was obvious that these Pokemon had escaped from the fire.

They possibly tried to hide in the river to avoid the fire, only to be swept downstream by the rushing waters.

After pondering for a moment, D instructed the male Indeedee, "Go and bring some Burn Heal."

"Indeed."

The male Indeedee calmly nodded, quickly ran back to the house, and fetched several bottles of burn treatment spray from the medical kit.

D approached the weak but highly alert Grafaiai and said, "Don't be afraid, I mean no harm. You're injured, and I'm here to treat you."

---

D took out the Burn Heal and waved it in front of it.

Grafaiai seemed to sense that the human in front of it meant no harm and genuinely wanted to help, so it finally lowered its guard.

D sprayed the Burn Heal all over its body and then moved to the round Bellibolt.

Compared to the wary Grafaiai, Bellibolt hadn't yet recovered from its fear, and its body trembled slightly.

Feeling someone approach, Bellibolt immediately curled its chubby body in fear, and instantly, a strong surge of electricity erupted from the generator on its belly, enveloping its body.

D earnestly tried to soothe its emotions: "Bellibolt? Bellibolt, don't be afraid, there's no danger now, it's very safe here!"

Bellibolt tightly tensed its body, shivering and still looking very scared.

Seeing this, D was a bit troubled, not sure how to approach the electricity-wrapped Bellibolt for a moment.

Just then, Pawmot volunteered eagerly from the side.

It patted its chest, flew to Bellibolt's side, and released a stream of electricity from the sacs on its cheeks.

This current met Bellibolt's electricity and immediately made a sizzling sound.

Initially surprised by this scene, D quickly realized it was a major way for Electric Type Pokemon to communicate.

They exchange currents to communicate, often as a gesture of friendliness.

Indeed, under Pawmot's calming influence, Bellibolt gradually relaxed its tense body.

It slowly extended its previously curled-up body, using its pair of little yellow eyes to observe the surroundings.

D gave it a smile: "Relax, I'm here to treat you."

This time, Bellibolt didn't resist anymore, and the burns on its body were successfully healed.

The third one was the Cramorant with a tilted head and a dumb expression.

D originally thought that this silly Cramorant would be easier to approach, but before he could get close, it flapped its wings and flew away, disappearing quickly.

D: "?"

Left with no choice, he could only start treating the remaining Pokemon.

Greedent, having seen humans before, was relatively friendly towards them, so it didn't resist like Bellibolt did.

Its treatment went smoothly, except its fur was burned bare, looking quite unsightly.

Tandemaus consisted of two mice with pure white, large ears; one was big and the other small. The smaller one hid behind the larger one, with fewer wounds on its body.

The larger one's wounds were much more severe.

They glanced timidly at the human and the group of intimidating Pokemon before them.

Seeing this, D crouched down, extending both hands to show he meant no harm: "Don't be afraid, I'm here to help you heal."

The Tandemaus had just seen this human heal the other Pokemon's injuries, so they exchanged a glance and cautiously moved forward.

D sprayed Burn Heal on the back of the larger one, praising it: "You're a very reliable husband."

The Tandemaus squeaked in response.

"Alright, it's all good now."

D prodded the two little ones with his finger, then walked over to the Tinkatink.

Different from his impression of a Tinkatink, this one wasn't holding its hammer, perhaps lost while fleeing or hadn't had time to forge one.

At this moment, the Tinkatink was straining to lift its head and gaze up at him.

D smiled gently, just about to say hello, but unexpectedly, the next moment, the Tinkatink collapsed to the ground, fainting.

---

---

---

---

# 223: Chapter 220: The Grassy Terrain Enveloping the Entire Mountain Forest (5k)

D was shocked by the sudden collapse of the small blacksmith.

He quickly crouched down to help check the small blacksmith's body.

"There are signs of burn injuries on the body, and they're quite serious. It should be due to significant physical exhaustion caused by burns."

D noticed the unconscious small blacksmith's body was still trembling slightly. He touched it with his hand and, sure enough, found the whole body was icy cold.

"It's a bit hypothermic!"

For Pokemon, this is a very dangerous condition.

Their body's heat loss exceeds heat supply. If they remain in this state for a long time, they could even face a life-threatening situation.

They must be warmed up quickly!

Not only the small blacksmith, but D also noticed the Greedent and others were shivering, probably from being immersed in the river water for long.

Thinking of this, D was immensely grateful for having read several Pokemon medical books in his spare time before.

He originally intended to treat the Pokemon on his family's ranch, but unexpectedly, it came in handy here instead.

Thinking of this, D picked up the small blacksmith and said to the Grafaiai, Tandemaus, Greedent, and Bellibolt before him, "Follow me!"

Seeing that a few Pokemon were a bit hesitant, his sister Indeedee gently said, "Ai yiii~"

The still-frightened Pokemon cautiously glanced at the humans before them, hesitated for a few seconds, and then lifted their feet to follow.

D returned to the living room, took out a warm blanket, and wrapped the small blacksmith in it, then placed compressed wood into the fireplace and lit it.

In no time, the temperature in the room began to rise slowly.

D pointed to the front of the fireplace, "Warm up by the fire for a while."

This time, the Pokemon did not resist any longer. They moved to the warm flames of the fireplace, where the heat gradually warmed their bodies.

The Greedent even let out a comfortable growl.

After spraying Burn Heal and Super Potion on the small blacksmith, D put it near the fireplace as well.

In such a warm environment, the Pokemon gradually relaxed.

But there was still a trace of terror on their faces.

Last night's experience was too frightening; the wild fire burned everything they were familiar with to ashes.

Everyone in the forest gathered together, madly fleeing before the menacing flames.

Even the usually hostile Pokemon gave up fighting temporarily and fled together.

Some Water Type Pokemon tried to use Water Gun to extinguish the fire, but their strength was too weak to stop the disaster at all.

At first, there were many Pokemon fleeing with them, but in the middle of the way, many were forced to disperse due to the fallen trees.

In the end, only a few were left, jumping into the river when the fire was about to consume everything, and were carried away by the river to this place.

D stood aside, watching this scene. Although he didn't know what these Pokemon experienced last night, judging by their expressions, it didn't seem like anything good.

Koraidon and Indeedee glanced at these Pokemon, sympathy appearing on their faces.

At this moment, a sudden "Gumo~ Gumo~" sound rang out.

Upon hearing this sound, all the Pokemon around swiftly turned their gaze to Bellibolt.

Bellibolt hugged its belly with little hands, looking quite flustered.

D was the first to react.

That sound was actually not Bellibolt's growl but the sound from its belly, proclaiming its empty stomach.

It's said that in Bellibolt's habitat, the sound of "Gumo~ Gumo~" can be heard rhythmically at sunset.

D then remembered that they hadn't had breakfast yet.

After ensuring the small blacksmith was fine, he began preparing breakfast with his sister Indeedee for everyone.

Brother Indeedee stood by the fireplace, guarding this group of wild Pokemon who had experienced disaster.

For some reason, these Pokemon reminded it of the Meowth that had been abandoned by humans in the manor.

Last night, such an event happened, and the other Pokemon were in low spirits.

Seeing this miserable scene, anyone would worry about the situation in the hills beyond.

Koraidon stood in the living room, glanced at everyone, and comforted, "Agaasu!" (As long as D is here, everything will be fine!)

It always trusts D unconditionally.

Even though Koraidon is now a powerful Pokemon, in its heart, D is still an omnipotent existence.

Upon hearing this, Glaceon, Pawmot, and other Pokemon looked at Koraidon.

Seeing its reliable appearance, they too were infected and directed their gaze at D, who was cooking.

D didn't notice this scene. He swiftly took berries, sandwiches, eggs, steak, and milk out of the fridge.

He heated the oil in the pan, started frying eggs and steaks, then added them to the sandwich, juiced the berries, mixed them with milk, and placed them in the microwave to heat.

In doing so, a delicious breakfast enriched with nutrition was completed.

Sister Indeedee helped distribute breakfast to everyone.

Greedent, Tandemaus, Grafaiai, and Bellibolt each got a thick sandwich and steamy milk.

The aroma of food deeply moved the Pokemon already hungrily longing; they gratefully looked at the human.

---

D noticed their expressions and smiled, saying, "You're welcome. We're all lives living in this place, helping each other is what we should do."

The warm firelight restored some energy to Greedent, who wiped its tears with its tiny paws, then lowered its head to eat the food.

The rest of the Pokemon also silently lowered their heads to eat, thinking: when given the chance, they must properly thank this human who rescued them.

Tinkatink hadn't woken up yet. D stepped forward to check and found it seemed to have fallen asleep from exhaustion.

He didn't wake it up. After breakfast, D planned to go to the back mountain to see the situation, to see if there were any other wild Pokemon in need of rescue.

Unexpectedly, after hearing D's plan, Greedent and others expressed their desire to go as well.

Seeing this, D left the Indeedee Sibling to watch the house and take care of Tinkatink, while he led the group of Pokemon to the back mountain.

It had just rained, and the ground was not dry; the road to the back mountain was extremely muddy.

At first, the scenery on both sides of the road hadn't changed much, but once they formally entered the back mountain, they were shocked to find that the place had entirely transformed.

The formerly tall trees were now charred black, fallen in all directions, the shrubs and grass burned to black ash, and the air was filled with the damp smell of rain mixed with ashes.

Greedent and Grafaiai looked at the devastation, tears silently streaming down.

Greedent wailed sadly.

All the hazelnuts and berries it stored in the tree hole for winter had been burned by this fire; they were the result of its busy autumn!

Grafaiai wiped the tears from its face, feeling a sense of despair; the place it originally inhabited might also have turned into ruins.

The Tandemaus couple silently shed tears, thinking that if they hadn't run fast yesterday, they would have returned to dust and earth by now.

Bellibolt reacted a bit more slowly, but looking at the scene before it, it now only felt a sense of languish on its usually casual face.

Koraidon and Glaceon were also dispirited by the sight.

Pawmot couldn't help but wonder whether if it lived in this forest, it would now be homeless.

Thinking of this made Pawmot feel very sad.

D's mood was also quite heavy.

He remembered how not long ago, he rode Gogoat to gather mountain goods in this forest.

Back then, the trees were tall and lush, and when the wind blew, the sound of waves would echo through the forest, spreading into the distance like waves.

The yellowed leaves would fall from the treetops, layers blanketing the ground, to become nutrients for the soil, making the land fertile for the next year.

Many Pokemon also inhabited nearby shrubs; he had seen Tarountula, Nymble, Wattrel, and Caterpie here before.

But now, all those Pokemon had vanished without a trace, their whereabouts unknown.

D could only silently pray in his heart, hoping that they had sensed the crisis ahead of time and escaped to a safer place.

Seeing the crying Pokemon around him, D comforted them, saying, "Don't cry; as long as you are alive, one day your home will be rebuilt. I, along with everyone else, and the people living in the town will help you rebuild your home."

The few Pokemon felt somewhat better but were still very sad.

Seeing this, D could only say, "Let's search nearby to see if any other Pokemon need help!"

"Barom!"

Pawmot was the first to react, the red cloak D prepared for it on its shoulder. Its image flying through the ruins was like that of a superhero.

D hoped it could also bring new hope to the Pokemon affected by this disaster, just like a real superhero.

Glaceon, Koraidon, Cyclizar, Gogoat, Dolliv, Dragonite, Butterfree, Combee, two wild Dragonites, and five Dragonairs—whether Pokemon living on the ranch or those left to work—all were dispatched by D to conduct search and rescue.

Seeing this scene, the sad Greedent was also moved.

It thought of the group of Pokemon that fled together yesterday, thinking that they might still be in danger, so it actively joined the rescue operation.

Grafaiai said nothing but expressed its intentions through action.

It swiftly shuttled through the forest, smearing its color-changing poison saliva on its fingers, using it to paint on the ground and dead trees, trying to guide those Pokemon in need of help.

D was moved watching this scene.

Even Pokemon who are usually adversaries would unite and help each other in the face of disaster.

With everyone's joint efforts, they soon found the first Pokemon in need of help.

It was a Wattrel with injured wings, unable to fly.

It was hiding in a narrow cave, and by the time Pawmot led D there, the Wattrel had already fainted.

D quickly pulled it out of the cave, carefully checked its injuries, and then sprayed it with Burn Heal and Super Potion.

---

The injured Wattrel was handed over to D, and Pawmot flew away again.

At this moment, Wattrel woke up.

Its expression was somewhat dazed, as if it hadn't quite come to its senses.

D gently touched its small head and softly reassured it, "Don't be afraid, it's all right now."

Wattrel seemed to realize that the human before it had saved it, so it didn't struggle but just stared dazedly at this human.

D smiled slightly, took out a Leppa Berry from his bag, and placed it in front of Wattrel. "Eat it, and after a nap, everything will be better."

Wattrel didn't move.

Seeing this, D was about to say something when Koraidon came running over, holding a fainted Petilil.

D quickly handed Wattrel to Koraidon and began to treat the Petilil.

Unexpectedly, as soon as Wattrel touched Koraidon, it suddenly started to struggle in panic.

D: "..."

Koraidon tilted its head: "Ar-gahh?"

"It's not a bad Pokemon, it's my partner, Koraidon." D explained helplessly.

Upon hearing this, Wattrel gradually calmed down.

Only then did D focus on treating the Petilil.

"Severe burns, extremely weak body... things are in bad shape."

After thinking for a bit, D opted not to use Super Potion, but instead took out a bottle of Berry Milk and fed it to the Petilil.

Compared to market-sold Super Potions, the Berry Milk he made was more effective and faster in healing.

But this stuff wasn't easy to carry; this time D only brought a dozen bottles, intended for Pokemon in critical condition.

Sure enough, after drinking the Berry Milk, Petilil slowly regained consciousness.

D then used Burn Heal on it.

As Petilil's condition stabilized, D felt relieved, leaving Petilil and Wattrel where they were, waiting for the condition of other Pokemon.

He thought the rescue mission would go smoothly, but unexpectedly, Koraidon and the others hadn't made progress for some time.

"No way, we can't let this continue."

If it drags on too long, the severely injured Pokemon might lose their lives!

The most important thing now is to find a way to stabilize their conditions.

After some careful thought, D had other Pokemon continue searching, while he called back the three Gogoat.

He looked at the three Gogoat before him, already grown tall and strong, with an unprecedentedly serious expression, he detailed his plan to them.

"I need you all to give your best and create Grassy Terrain, just like you did at the ranch before, cover as much area as possible."

Grassy Terrain not only enhances the power of Grass Type Moves.

The more important point is that it can restore energy to any Pokemon on the ground!

"Can you do it?" D asked.

The three Gogoat were full of fighting spirit: "Baa!"

"Great." D said seriously. "Then I leave it to you, afterward I'll make even tastier grassy dishes for you."

"Baa~"

The Gogoat showed expectant expressions, then the three of them lined up side by side, their forelegs stomped heavily on the ground.

In the next instant, a powerful light burst from their bodies.

The vibrant green light merged into the earth, and between flashes of light, sprouts of illusory, glistening grass grew on the scorched ground.

In an instant, a green sea swept across the land, swiftly expanding towards the distance, gradually enveloping the entire forest.

Watching this scene, D was surprised.

During this period, it seems the Gogoat have grown even stronger when he wasn't paying much attention?

On the other side of the forest.

A rescue team, composed of the firefighter, Miss Jenny, and Miss Joy, was swiftly traversing the forest, rescuing those injured Pokemon.

Growlithe lowered its head, sniffing the ground, using its sense of smell to search for the injured Pokemon.

Suddenly, it seemed to detect something, sharply raised its head, and gazed into the distance.

"Woof woof!"

Hearing Growlithe's barking, Miss Jenny looked down at it: "Did you find something?"

Before Growlithe could respond, a firefighter nearby exclaimed.

"Look, what's that!"

Everyone immediately looked up.

In the next second, they saw in the far-off direction, a wave of green light spreading towards them.

Soon, the light reached their feet, and in an instant, they saw countless blades of grass gently sway on the scorched land.

"This is?" Miss Jenny was stunned.

Beside her, Miss Joy quickly came to her senses and shouted, "Grassy Terrain! It's Grassy Terrain that can restore Pokemon's energy! Someone is helping these Pokemon!"

As a Pokemon Doctor, she was the first to react; that person wanted to use Grassy Terrain to sustain the lives of these Pokemon!

The fire chief's eyes widened: "Is this really the Grassy Terrain I know? It's impossible…"

How can there be such an exaggerated Grassy Terrain? Its coverage area is too large!

Miss Jenny also felt extremely shocked: "What kind of Pokemon could this be…"

This coverage area... Elite, or Champion level?

But the Paldea Region doesn't have a famous Champion-level Trainer who specializes in Grass Type Pokemon!

Who could it be?

Everyone present was shocked by this.

Miss Joy looked at the familiar scene; it seemed she had seen such a scene somewhere before.

Where could it be?

Miss Joy tried hard to recall.

Soon, an image flashed in her mind.

It was a ranch, where three Skiddo were raised, the Skiddo would use Grassy Terrain, then jump and run on it as if they were dancing.

And the owner of that ranch…

"It's Mister D!" Miss Joy exclaimed in surprise: "It's Mister D's Skiddo! I've seen them at D Ranch!"

What?!

Everyone was stunned, then showed shock.

How could this be?!

Could it be that their familiar farmer is actually a hidden Champion-level Trainer living in Porto Marinada Town?!

---

---

---

---

# 224: Chapter 221: Homeland Reconstruction Plan (Double Release)

8:00 am.

Geeta arrived at Porto Marinada Town by helicopter.

As the current Chief Champion of the Paldea League, her duties are not only to recruit talent for the League but also to maintain the peace and stability of the Paldea Region.

Faced with such an incident, Geeta rushed to Porto Marinada Town immediately.

Upon learning of the Chief Champion's arrival, Miss Jenny, who was searching for the injured, immediately came to report last night's situation to her.

Jenny: "The fire occurred around 4 a.m., caused by a trainer camping in the mountains who forgot to extinguish the campfire, resulting in sparks landing on some dry leaves nearby."

"At 4:10 a.m., both the fire department and the police received emergency calls. By the time the fire department arrived, the fire had been burning for twenty minutes, and the flames had spread to further distances."

"The fire department immediately began firefighting efforts, but this season there are many combustible materials in the mountains, and the fire was fierce. We could only create firebreaks to prevent the fire from spreading, but couldn't extinguish the entire blaze quickly."

"In a moment of crisis, a..." At this point, Miss Jenny paused and then continued, "A trainer came to our aid, deploying a Dragonite, and that Dragonite used Rain Dance, thus the flames began to weaken."

"By six in the morning, the entire mountain fire was completely extinguished, and after that, we immediately began rescue operations."

Geeta listened to Jenny's report as she headed into the mountains.

Upon hearing that a trainer had dispatched Dragonite to use Rain Dance to extinguish the fire, she couldn't help but ask, "Doesn't the fire department have any Pokemon that know how to use Rain Dance?"

"They do have." Miss Jenny's expression was peculiar: "It's just that those Pokemon lack power, both in terms of the coverage area of Rain Dance and the rainfall, which were too small to completely curb the spread of the fire."

"And that trainer's Dragonite..."

Miss Jenny imagined it and described:

"It has mastered the move Rain Dance to an incredible degree. That wasn't a regular Rain Dance; it was a torrential downpour that enveloped the entire back mountain. Thanks to it, this fire was quickly brought under control."

Upon hearing this, Geeta showed a surprised expression.

A Dragonite that has practiced Rain Dance to such an incredible level?

That is indeed rare. Though Dragonite are quite rare, throughout her long career as a trainer, she has seen a few with other trainers.

However, unlike moves like Rain Dance which are terrain-type weather moves, those trainers focus more on practicing moves like Outrage and Dragon Tail.

Various past scenes flashed through Geeta's mind, and she nodded, saying, "It seems we must properly thank that trainer."

As they spoke, the two formally entered the back mountain.

Before coming here, Geeta had imagined countless horrific scenes.

However, when she truly stepped here, what she saw was a scene full of green grass and vitality.

Geeta was taken aback on the spot, but soon she noticed something was amiss.

That wasn't real grass, but a Grassy Terrain formed by Grass Type Energy!

Geeta suddenly lifted her head, and then she saw the entire mountain enveloped in a layer of green light.

As the wind blew, thousands of blades of grass swayed gently in the breeze.

Geeta: "?"

The Chief Champion's head was filled with a question mark.

Seeing this, Miss Jenny took the initiative to explain:

"This Grassy Terrain was also a move used by that trainer's Pokemon. Though I haven't seen it, Nurse Joy says it was three Gogoat."

Speaking of this, Jenny's face showed a grateful smile: "Thanks to them, the rescue team gained more time."

Even without explanation, Geeta understood.

Grassy Terrain... This is a move that can restore the stamina of Pokemon.

Her face remained calm, but inside she was very shocked.

What kind of Pokemon can use such a large-scale Grassy Terrain?

It happens she has a Gogoat in her lineup too.

Could her Gogoat do the same? Geeta asked herself.

She couldn't.

"What kind of person is he?" Geeta asked.

Miss Jenny recalled D, and even her usually stern face couldn't help but reveal a small smile:

"He's a very gentle person; he really loves Pokemon and runs a ranch here. Oh, and by the way, although I haven't seen those three Gogoat, I have tasted the milk they produced."

"Not just me; everyone in town has heard of those three Gogoat, because the milk they produce makes people's bodies healthier."

Geeta was stunned: "…A rancher?"

Jenny thought for a moment and said, "He came to town in the spring, and I heard that he traveled the world before, so maybe before running a ranch, he was a very skilled trainer."

Geeta tried to remain calm: "I see."

She must visit him later, Geeta thought.

But now, they have more important things to do.

*

"Come on! Keep it up!"

D watched the Gogoat in front of him still striving to maintain the Grassy Terrain, cheering them on as he fed them Grassy Seed.

In just half an hour, with the help of Grassy Terrain, many wild Pokemon regained some stamina.

---

They emerged from their hiding burrow and were discovered by the Pawmot and Koraidon on patrol, who then brought them to D's side.

Now, besides the initial Wattrel and Petilil, a number of injured Pokemon had gathered here.

Two Hoppip, a Kricketune, two Surskit, three Shroomish, and a Slakoth with its fur burnt bare.

Apart from them, Koraidon also brought back some Pokemon that had lost their lives in the fire.

Looking at the silently lying charred Venonat and Lilligant, everyone present, human and Pokemon alike, fell silent.

D tried using the strongest stamina-restoring dish he had crafted to heal the Venonat, but failed.

They had completely lost their lives, and unless Ho-oh was here, nobody could save them.

Koraidon and Cyclizar drooped their heads in sorrow.

Glaceon felt deeply distressed.

Pawmot floated in mid-air, its tone downcast: "Pawmot..."

D sighed and said, "What we can do now is to bury them properly, so they can rest in peace."

There was no Ghost Tower or Mt. Pyre in the Paldea Region for burying Pokemon; they could only find a suitable place in the forest to lay them to rest.

At this moment, Gogoat finally couldn't hold on anymore, the Grassy Terrain on the ground turning into dots of light and vanishing.

"You've worked hard! Now go back and rest well for a while!" D reached out and patted their heads.

The other Pokemon continued to search for injured Pokemon, but after searching for half a day, they found nothing.

D thought, maybe it was because the Pokemon nearby had already escaped early?

Seeing this, he called off the rescue operation, then found a piece of land untouched by the fire, dug a few pits, and buried the dead Pokemon there.

Wattrel, Shroomish, Kricketune, and other Pokemon watched the scene, their faces showing sadness.

D consoled, "Don't worry, they aren't truly gone; their souls have just left these bodies to start a new life. Their bodies will eventually return to this world."

D acted as a priest and held a simple funeral for these Pokemon.

After it was over, he looked at the Pokemon behind him and said:

"In the coming days, you can stay with me. I'll provide enough food to get through this winter."

Upon hearing this, the Pokemon lowered their heads, their expressions gloomy.

They knew that without someone's help, even if they survived the fire, they couldn't make it through the cold winter.

The fire had consumed everything.

No adequate food, nor warm nests.

Greedent's lip quivered, and it began to wail in sorrow again.

Its tree hole! Its hole full of nuts and berries!

D: "..."

He sighed and said, "Come with me! Head to my place, and when spring arrives, I'll plant trees here and help you rebuild your home."

Hearing that their home could be rebuilt, the Pokemon immediately lifted their heads with eyes full of hope.

"Chirp chirp?" Tandemaus asked.

D couldn't understand, but judging by their expressions, it seemed they were asking if it was true?

He nodded: "Of course it's true. By then, I'll plant saplings here, and it won't be long before they grow into towering trees. Then, you can return to the forest and live in your rebuilt home."

The expressions on the Pokemon finally eased a bit.

They looked at the human before them with emotions, their hearts filled with gratitude.

"Let's go!"

D started towards the pasture, with a long string of little tails following behind him.

Midway, D encountered Miss Jenny riding a bike.

She was on her way back to the Pokemon Center to fetch more medicine for Joy when she saw D and the dirty, dejected Pokemon, and her face showed an expression of realization.

Jenny expressed her gratitude: "Thank you so much, the Grassy Terrain won us more rescue time. The Champion is aware of it and will personally visit you after this is over."

D: "?"

Which Champion?

He was momentarily puzzled, realizing it was Geeta who would visit him?

Noticing his reaction, Miss Jenny smiled and said: "Don't be nervous, the Chief Champion isn't pretentious, she's quite approachable."

D thought he wasn't nervous, just a bit surprised.

Since there was more to do, Miss Jenny didn't say much further.

After a brief chat, she turned her gaze to the wild Pokemon behind D:

"The rescue teams from other cities have arrived, and the townspeople have organized a Pokemon Rescue Team. The League has dispatched lots of supplies, and until the ecosystem at the back mountain recovers, the rescue team will take care of the mountain Pokemon. You can send them to the rescue team."

D shook his head: "No, I'll take care of these Pokemon. They're more familiar with me, and the orchard's harvest this year was good enough to sustain them."

Jenny: "Alright then."

---

After speaking, Miss Jenny left in a hurry.

D also returned to the ranch with a group of Pokémon.

The hardest-working Pokémon today was undoubtedly Gogoat.

Upon returning to the ranch, D immediately filled their feeding troughs with Grassy Seed.

Gogoat smelled the Grassy Seed, bleating as it squeezed to the trough and began eating heartily, replenishing its Grass Type Energy.

D clapped his hands and put the basket away.

Next, he had to address the food and shelter issues for this group of Pokémon.

Including the Greedent, Tinkatink, Tandemaus, Grafaiai, and Bellibolt from before, there were a total of sixteen refugee Pokémon.

D originally wanted to include Cramorant, but that Cramorant flew off somewhere and couldn't be found.

The food issue was easy to solve. This year's orchard had a bountiful harvest of Berries, which could be stored to help these Pokémon through the coming winter.

As for shelter...

D thought for a moment, then went to the old house's storage room, found a hammer and saw, and began crafting sturdy and beautiful wooden huts for them using planks and nails.

These various-sized huts were set up by D in the Berry Garden.

Wattrel liked to live in high places, so its nest was fixed on a Berry tree by D.

The other Pokémon didn't have specific requirements, so D installed their nests under different fruit trees.

Walking through the orchard, every few trees you could see a small wooden hut designed specifically for small-sized Pokémon.

Moreover, D placed special windproof and cold-proof materials inside the huts and spread a thick, furry blanket inside.

Once a hut was completed, Greedent was the first to try it out.

It burrowed into the warm hut, its burnt and bare tail clearly feeling the softness and comfort of the blanket beneath, making Greedent let out a satisfied sigh.

This was even more perfect than the tree hollow it previously lived in.

It could almost imagine how comfortable it would feel hiding in this hut during a snowy winter outside.

Seeing the expression on Greedent's face, D couldn't help but smile and said, "If you like it, when the reconstruction on the back mountain is finished, I can make you another hut there."

Upon hearing this, Greedent's face immediately showed an expression of surprise and delight.

"Alright, have fun, I'm going to check on the other Pokémon," said D.

He made a round through the orchard.

Wattrel, Tandemaus, Surskit, Slakoth, Hoppip, Kricketune, Shroomish, and other Pokémon were very satisfied with these wooden huts, and they were also full of gratitude toward D for providing them with shelter.

Seeing this, D smiled but didn't say much, only encouraging them to settle down here peacefully.

Then he came to the Bellibolt.

Due to its size, Bellibolt was not suitable for living in the wooden huts.

Therefore, D could only send it to the Underground Cave where the Naclstack and Diglett lived.

The cave had an underground lake and, being a distance from the Ground level, even in winter, it remained quite warm, making it an ideal wintering spot for Bellibolt.

After settling these Pokémon, D finally found some free time.

Standing in the ranch, he suddenly remembered the sleepy Tinkatink at home and hurriedly made his way back to the house to check on Tinkatink.

---

---

---

---

# 225: Chapter 222: Geeta's Recruitment

Inside, the flames flickered, casting a long shadow of Tinkatink as it lay on a square cushion. The flames dancing in the fireplace made its shadow sway on the wall.

Tinkatink felt like it was dreaming.

In the dream, the forest was ablaze. The fire's tongues, like the claws of demons, constantly approached it.

The heavy flames consumed everything, and in an instant, the once-familiar sanctuary turned into a deadly inferno.

It saw Pokemon being swallowed by the flames, more and more Pokemon starting to flee.

Then, it too joined the tide of Pokemon, fleeing endlessly under the scorching winds and flames.

The temperature around grew increasingly higher, as if the flames were about to touch its body.

Tinkatink let out a terrified scream, then abruptly opened its eyes.

Suddenly, the all-consuming fire vanished, and the fleeing Pokemon disappeared as well.

In their place was a completely unfamiliar environment.

Tinkatink's face still held a trace of fear as it panted heavily, like someone on the verge of drowning.

At this moment, a voice came from the side.

"You've woken up?"

Tinkatink turned its head and saw an unfamiliar human.

Memories returned to its mind, and Tinkatink recalled everything.

– They had escaped the fire and were rescued by the human before them.

But what about those Pokemon that escaped with it?

In panic, Tinkatink looked around the room, searching for the figures of those Pokemon.

Watching its actions, D roughly guessed what Tinkatink meant.

He said, "If you're looking for Greedent and them, you'll see them outside."

Upon hearing this, Tinkatink struggled to stand, wanting to go see them.

Because it had accidentally fallen while fleeing, and was about to be scorched by the fire, it was Greedent that stopped to save it.

For this, the fur on its tail was burned bare.

D didn't stop it, after a nap, Tinkatink's strength had clearly recovered much, not as weak as before.

He followed Tinkatink outside and eventually found the figure of Greedent in the Berry Garden.

Tinkatink couldn't help but breathe a sigh of relief.

D said beside it, "Although your body is fine now, you still need to rest more. During this period, stay in my ranch."

"After this winter passes, I'll help rebuild your home, then you can return to the woods."

Not only him, but people from the Paldea League will also be stationed here to help until the ecosystem is fully restored.

Then, those rescued Pokemon will be released back into the wild, returning to their home.

Upon hearing this, Tinkatink exhaled softly.

It turned around and looked up at the human who had helped them, then whispered, "Ooh wa..."
