D smiled and asked, "I've made you a little nest, want to try it? Live together with everyone?"

Even though they were in a safe environment, Tinkatink's mood didn't immediately recover.

It lowered its head and whispered, "Ooh wa."

Looking at the fragile Tinkatink, it was hard for D to relate it to the formidable Pokemon known as the Paldea Bully.

The Paldea Bully refers to Tinkaton, evolved from Tinkatink.

This Pokemon is not only highly intelligent but also has a bold personality; although adorable and petite, it can wield a giant hammer to strike a Corviknight.

D led Tinkatink to the wooden cabin he had prepared for it.

Looking at the cabin in front of it, Tinkatink turned its head to look at D, and then, under his encouraging gaze, walked inside.

Seeing this, D breathed a sigh of relief.

Finally, the housing for all the injured Pokemon was perfectly sorted.

Even though it wasn't dark yet, this group of Pokemon didn't stay inside their nests but scattered throughout the garden, their spirits very low.

D understood this, as the tragic event was so recent, it was normal for them to struggle with adjusting.

He simply gathered this group of Pokemon and said:

"You can pick and eat any Berry in this garden freely. If you want to walk around, you can find Pawmot or Alcremie, but be careful not to eat Alcremie."

The ripening season for Berries is much shorter than normal fruits, enabling these Berries to sustain the Pokemon through the winter.

The group of Pokemon nodded, indicating they understood, and would certainly not eat the Pokemon who helped them.

Before they knew it, time had moved to 5 PM.

Zack came to pick up the goods in his truck.

Clearly, he too heard about the forest fire on the hillside.

"It's truly heartbreaking, sigh..." Zack couldn't help but sigh.

"Indeed!" D recalled the Pokemon that lost their lives in the fire, equally lamenting: "Not only does ecological restoration require time, but the hearts of these Pokemon must have endured severe trauma."

"All we can do is try our best to help them through this hardship," Zack sighed.

"Things will get better," D assured.

---

Upon hearing this, Zack smiled and said, "Yes! We'll get through this challenge together as a team!"

He handed today's milk money to D, waved his hand, and turned to leave the ranch.

After seeing Zack off, D felt a deep exhaustion spreading through his body.

From early morning until now, he had not rested for a moment. Immediately after extinguishing the fire, he took the Pokémon from the ranch to the back mountain, and once back, he started building wooden shelters for them.

D yawned. Although he was tired, he persevered to help his sister, Indeedee, prepare dinner.

After finishing dinner, he filled the water troughs for Miltank and Gogoat and herded the chickens back into the Chicken House, finally able to rest for a while.

The Indeedee siblings were also exhausted. Seeing their condition, D simply hung a closed sign on the tavern to allow them to rest well, too.

That night, the ranch turned off the lights early, and D, along with a group of Pokémon, quickly drifted into dreamland.

*

The next morning brought a brand-new day.

The weather was quite nice today, with temperatures slightly warmer than before.

Early in the morning, D started mowing the grass with a lawnmower, planning to collect some grass to make new Grassy Seed dishes for Gogoat.

It's worth noting that, to ensure Gogoat had access to fresh Grassy Seed throughout winter, D had amassed an entire warehouse full of fresh grass.

All this grass was sealed within clear, glistening ice blocks, stored in Glaceon's ice cellar.

This way, whenever needed, it can be thawed to make the Grassy Seed.

While D was busy on the pasture, a new visitor arrived outside the ranch.

Geeta, the leading Champion of the Paldea League, was standing on the country path, gazing at the bustling ranch ahead.

"Is this the reclusive Champion-level Trainer that Jenny mentioned?" Geeta murmured to herself.

As she stepped in, she saw three Dragonite flying over her head, each with a small satchel.

Geeta showed surprise, wondering if this person specialized in Dragon Types as a Champion-level Trainer.

She turned to look at the direction the Dragonite were flying—towards Porto Marinada Town.

Last night, she'd heard some rumors about this ranch from Jenny.

Seeing those Dragonite now, she thought they must be the ones Jenny mentioned to be working as delivery carriers for the ranch.

"Luxurious," Geeta commented softly, "But Dragonite are indeed suitable for the job."

They must be the primary Pokémon of this ranch's owner, Geeta thought.

However, when she turned her head, she spotted a Pokémon stretching its limbs on the nearby grass.

It was a tall and robust Pokémon, entirely crimson, adorned with magnificent head feathers and a pair of golden eyes.

The Pokémon seemed to sense something, lifting its eyes to look in her direction.

Their gazes met in midair, and Geeta was momentarily stunned.

Isn't that… the paradox Pokémon that Professor Sada and Professor Turo are researching?

The entity called Koraidon mentioned in the Book of Scarlet and Violet?

As the chief of the Paldea League, Geeta was aware of the existence of Zone Zero and the paradox Pokémon.

To enter Zone Zero, outsiders must first apply to the Paldea League and receive her approval as the chief before they can gain access.

"No, Koraidon can't be here," Geeta subconsciously denied.

To her knowledge, Professor Sada and Professor Turo had only summoned two powerful and ferocious paradox Pokémon to this place.

If Koraidon was here, then what was the Pokémon at the deepest part of Zone Zero's research facility?

Yet the look from Koraidon's eyes was undeniable.

Even if she didn't believe it, the Koraidon in front of her wasn't an illusion but reality.

So, who exactly is this rancher?

To be able to tame the fierce and aggressive Koraidon?

Just then, D received a notification from Butterfree that someone had intruded into the ranch.

He paused his work to check it out. From afar, he saw Geeta, dressed in a black and blue Champion's uniform, looking at Koraidon in astonishment.

D raised an eyebrow, approaching to greet, "Champion?"

Geeta came to her senses and turned her head, seeing a rather familiar face,

Geeta frowned slightly, then politely asked, "Have we met somewhere before?"

D: "..."

He thought carefully. Compared to before, he had become much darker and stronger, and he had changed his hairstyle. It was natural Geeta didn't recognize him.

D explained concisely, "Yes, it was when that giant Dragonite visited the Paldea Region before."

With that, Geeta immediately remembered.

She opened her eyes wide in disbelief, "Was it you?"

D: "It was me."

"No wonder…" Geeta muttered, "No wonder you could manage that giant Dragonite; it makes sense since you're a Champion-level Trainer."

Hearing this, D was momentarily stunned; his head filled with confusion, he asked, "What Champion?"

Geeta looked at him puzzled, "Weren't you the Champion-level Trainer who handled yesterday's fire?"

---

D was even more confused.

How did he not know he had become a Champion-level Trainer?

Seeing his expression, Geeta was also quite perplexed: "Isn't that so?"

D: "…No, as you can see, I'm just a rancher."

Hearing this, Geeta furrowed her brow slightly, as if in thought.

Then, she seemed to have thought of something, and her brow gradually relaxed, revealing a smile: "I understand now."

D: "?"

What did you understand?

Geeta hadn't forgotten her main purpose for coming here today. She straightened her face and said:

"Mr. D, regardless of everything, I must on behalf of the Paldea League express our gratitude towards you. The Paldea League owes you a debt."

"As thanks, we will fulfill one of your needs to the best of our ability, whether it's money, Pokémon, or a position within the League like a Gym Leader."

Of course, the League position was something she personally added, as before coming here, Geeta had considered recruiting this talent.

However, upon hearing this, D twitched his mouth.

So they wanted to recruit him to work for the League?

What a joke?!

He didn't want to become a corporate drone like Larry.

Therefore, D decisively rejected the third option: "I still prefer running the ranch here."

Geeta was quite regretful: "Really, you won't reconsider? The League offers generous salaries for outstanding Trainers, and you can also run your ranch while working for the League; they don't conflict."

D smiled politely: "Thank you very much for your invitation, but these Pokémon here can't do without me."

Upon hearing this, Geeta looked at the group of Pokémon eagerly waiting behind him and could only regretfully say: "Alright, if you change your mind in the future, feel free to contact me anytime."

"By the way, since you don't want to work for the League, the first two options are still valid. Please don't refuse again; it's a token of our appreciation, and it's a reward you deserve."

D: "…"

He began to ponder deeply.

As an ordinary person, facing this situation, it's really hard to make a decision.

As for money, well, he doesn't lack it now.

As for Pokémon, D's mind was filled with images of the fluffy Eevee family and the Growlithe he longed for.

Seeing his hesitation, Geeta said: "You can take your time to think about it. This token of appreciation is valid indefinitely, so you can let me know once you've made up your mind."

D nodded silently, realizing that this was indeed the case.

For Eevee, he could check in town to see if anyone was breeding them, and he could try his luck in the wild for Growlithe.

After concluding the main topic, Geeta turned her gaze towards Koraidon and then asked: "Is it your Pokémon?"

D looked at his own Koraidon, then at Geeta beside him.

At this moment, many thoughts raced through his mind.

As he knew, the Paldea Giant Pit is currently under the management of the Paldea League, and outsiders need permission from the Paldea League to enter.

And the research station and institute founded by Professor Sada and Professor Turo under the Giant Pit were presumably able to conduct their research smoothly with the League's support.

"…So, as the Paldea League's top Champion, Geeta should be aware of the existence of Paradox Pokémon."

After a moment of contemplation, D nodded and said: "Yes, it's called Koraidon. The name was given by Professor Sada. Eight months ago, it appeared at the ranch, and Professor Sada entrusted it to my care, hoping I could discover more ecological information from Koraidon."

---

---

---

---

# 226: Chapter 223: Is Geeta Challenging the Grass Trial? (4k)

"I see." Geeta nodded in understanding.

Although the Paldea League knew that the two professors were conducting research under the Giant Pit, they only had a general idea and didn't know every single thing they did.

Moreover, not long ago, Professor Sada and Professor Turo developed the Terastal transformation, making Pokemon battles more interesting.

Recently, the Paldea League has been promoting Terastal all over.

The first to bear the brunt were the Gym Leaders scattered across Paldea.

As she looked at the Pokemon named Koraidon, Geeta's mind once again conjured images of the Dragonite summoning storms and Gogoats spreading Grassy Terrain to cover the mountains and fields.

At that moment, she suddenly became more interested in this ranch.

"If it's convenient, could you take me to see those three Gogoats?" Geeta asked.

"Of course." D nodded with a smile, "Please follow me!"

The two walked ahead, and as Geeta moved forward, she observed the surrounding environment.

From the bountiful rows of crops and the perfectly trimmed lawns, the ranch was managed beautifully.

Some Pokemon were running and playing on the grass, and Geeta looked at them, softly reciting their names.

"Alcremie, Naclstack, Glaceon, Butterfree, Combee, Miltank..."

Walking in front, D noticed Geeta's gaze and purposely slowed his steps to introduce her:

"These Pokemon gathered at this ranch over time. Naclstack can produce nutrient-rich rock salt, Combee can collect nectar to brew honey, and Miltank can produce delicious fresh milk."

Upon hearing this, Geeta, who had never been exposed to this, nodded slightly and gazed at Miltank, softly saying, "I kind of get it."

In the Paldea Region, there's more than one ranch and farm, and the crops and fresh milk produced from these places are sold everywhere, thus nourishing batch after batch of Paldeans.

Geeta smiled, about to say something when a splash of bright red suddenly appeared in her vision.

She looked up, only to see a Pawmot with a red cloak around its neck flying in front of D, handing him a dirt-covered egg.

Seeing this scene, Geeta's face was full of astonishment.

Did she see wrong? Was Pawmot... flying?

It rained yesterday, and a hen laid an egg outside, getting dirtied. Pawmot happened to discover this egg while patrolling near the Chicken House and hence brought it to D.

"Pawmot!"

D roughly guessed Pawmot's meaning, nodded, and said, "It's fine, take it to your sister Indeedee, and I'll make an omelet for you later."

"Pawmot~"

Upon hearing there'd be an omelet, Pawmot happily took the egg and flew away.

D turned back and saw Geeta's gaze was tightly following Pawmot, her face showing a particularly surprised expression.

D: "..."

Only when Pawmot had entered the mansion did Geeta retract her gaze and look at D: "Is that Pawmot also your Pokemon?"

D: "Yes."

Geeta asked curiously, "How did you train it to learn to fly?"

D was momentarily at a loss for how to respond, so he earnestly looked at Geeta and asked, "Would you believe me if I said it's naturally gifted?"

Geeta blinked, revealing an understanding expression: "Since it's your secret, I won't ask further. Let's go; I want to meet the Gogoats."

D breathed a sigh of relief, thinking that this Champion was quite perceptive.

He didn't continue on this topic but instead led Geeta to the sheep pen.

At this time, the Gogoats were crowded around the trough, ravenously devouring the grassy seed inside.

Looking at the three robust Gogoats, Geeta praised, "You've raised them well."

D thought: It's due to eating all the Grassy Seeds.

Geeta asked, "I heard from Miss Joy that these three Gogoats once experienced a state of energy surplus, having to expend energy in Grassy Terrain every morning, is that right?"

D nodded, "Yes, at that time, they would dance every day like clockwork, and I was really worried, so I asked Miss Joy to come and check on them."

"Dance?" Upon hearing these two words, Geeta immediately had vivid and cartoonish images of Gogoats jumping on the Grassy Terrain, she couldn't help but curl her lips: "That does seem accurate."

"Yes!" D also laughed and continued, "The results showed their bodies were fine, just simply had excess energy, maybe because they eat and sleep, and sleep and eat every day."

"I didn't have a good solution then, so to let them release energy, I hosted Grass Trial battle activities at the ranch, allowing them to battle with passing Trainers to release energy."

Geeta listened with interest: "And then?"

"Then..." D said, "Later, I found a better way, which was to let them use Grass Energy to help me tend the crops on the ranch."

This not only let the Gogoats release excess energy but also allowed the crops to grow lushly, indeed a win-win solution.

---

Geeta felt this was the case, but then her thoughts turned to the spectacular scene she had witnessed yesterday—the Grassy Terrain covering the hills, with thousands of blades of grass swaying in the wind, resembling a sea of green.

A Gogoat of Championship Level was battling a Trainer passing by the ranch.

Grass Trial, she wondered?

Geeta, curious, asked, "Can you introduce me to this Grass Trial?"

Upon hearing this, D paused slightly, wondering what Geeta intended to do.

Geeta smiled and explained, "I'm just a bit curious, wondering what kind of trial it might be."

D: "..."

He thought about it, then decided to tell Geeta the details of the Grass Trial.

"The trial consists of three stages: the first stage is battling one Gogoat, then you can challenge the second stage by battling two Gogoats at the same time, and similarly, the third stage requires facing three."

"Trainers who pass all stages can receive a special dish prepared by me."

After hearing that, Geeta's eyes revealed a look of interest.

She wasn't particularly concerned about the trial's prizes; she just wanted to see what the result would be when her Gogoat battled these Gogoats.

"Can I give it a try?" Geeta asked.

D opened his mouth, feeling helpless, "...Sure."

It seemed that at this point, Geeta had just recently become the Champion, and neither her character nor presence was as mature and imposing as in the games.

D called over the three Gogoats and asked Indeedee to prepare a meal for significant recovery, then led Geeta to the simple battlefield.

Geeta took a Poke Ball from her waist, and with a flash of white light, a Gogoat just as strong appeared before her.

Geeta said, "This is the Gogoat I raised, let's have a match between them!"

Seeing the Gogoat, D suddenly felt a headache and quickly said, "Wait, wait!"

Geeta looked over in surprise, "What's wrong?"

D pointed to Geeta's Gogoat, "May I ask, what is its ability? If it's Sap Sipper, then there's no need for this battle."

Because one of his Gogoats also had the Sap Sipper ability.

Sap Sipper: When hit by a Grass Type Move, no damage is taken, and the Pokemon's Attack is boosted.

If the battle proceeds with these abilities, it might just end up as "Stop fighting, go fight in the dance studio."

Such a battle doesn't need to happen.

Indeed, Geeta had already thought of this.

She nodded, saying, "That was my oversight. In that case, I'll switch to another Pokemon!"

With that, she recalled the Gogoat into its Poke Ball and took out another one.

Watching this, D was curious.

Geeta's Pokemon...is it Espathra or Glimmora?

With a flash of white light, a Kingambit with a sharp gaze, radiating the aura of a general, appeared in front of Geeta.

D: "..."

Alright, it's neither.

Kingambit, a Pokemon with Steel and Dark types, evolved from Bisharp, excels at suppressing opponents with brute strength.

"Are you ready?" Geeta stood at one end of the field.

D nodded, signaling one of the Gogoats to step forward.

The other two Gogoats stayed behind to watch; both D and they were quite familiar with this routine.

Seeing this, Geeta paused, "Aren't you going to step forward to command?"

D: "...Gogoats can battle on their own."

He's really just a rancher, ah!

Geeta frowned slightly, murmuring, "This isn't the kind of battle I wanted!"

She pondered briefly and then took a few steps back, with a hint of regret, "So be it; if Gogoat doesn't need your command, then I won't command Kingambit either. That's fair!"

With that, Geeta's eyes sharpened, and she looked at D, "Let me see your talents, shall we? Kingambit, fight autonomously!"

"Hmph!" Kingambit glanced sideways, nodded slightly, then focused all its attention on its opponent.

The battle officially began!

In an instant, Gogoat started moving.

Immense Grass Energy flowed naturally from its body, and almost instantaneously, it formed a Grassy Terrain beneath its feet.

The next second, a massive and sturdy Vine Whip shot out from its body, aiming high at the Kingambit below.

Geeta's eyes narrowed.

Is that…Vine Whip?

In her impression, weren't Vine Whips supposed to be slender and flexible, like the ones from Snivy? But why was this Vine Whip so thick, like a giant python!

"No!" Thinking of the Grassy Terrain beyond common understanding, the Champion quickly judged internally, "You can't gauge that Gogoat with common sense!"

On the battlefield, Kingambit watched calmly as this unfolded, and then the huge sword atop its head emitted an intense white light.

The thick Vine Whip struck down, instantly being sliced in half.

The first round of confrontation, neither side backed down.

Kingambit's eyes locked onto Gogoat.

At the same time, Gogoat gathered more thick Vine Whips, hurling them towards Kingambit.

Faced with such an extreme scene, Kingambit didn't panic at all; it simply stood up suddenly, its claws transforming into sharp blades, charging straight out.

The massive Vine Whips intertwined, attempting to attack Kingambit.

With green light flickering, Kingambit's claws swung quickly and continuously, instantly slicing through the huge Vine Whips.

Gogoat seemed never to have encountered such a situation; it stayed rooted in place for a moment.

Yet, in the next moment, Kingambit broke through the encirclement and arrived before it.

Raising its arm, turned into a blade, high, the last Fury Cutter slashed down directly.

Gogoat was struck hard, collapsing to the ground.

Upon seeing this, D felt a twinge in his heart.

"As expected, there is still some gap between Gogoat and Champion Level Pokemon, and its battle experience is far more lacking compared to Kingambit."

Kingambit silently looked at Gogoat.

Seeing this, D murmured, "It's going to lose."

Will the first trial of the Grass Trial finally be passed?

However, in the next second, the situation on the field surpassed his expectations.

Suddenly, a surge of intense energy erupted from the Grassy Terrain, drawing Kingambit's attention, alertly turning to scan the surroundings.

And that energy flowed entirely into Gogoat's body.

Subsequently, Gogoat recovered its stamina, its determined gaze locking onto the Kingambit standing tall before it.

Powerful energy burst from its body, causing its pair of huge, sharp horns to suddenly grow larger.

"Meh ah!"

The next second, Horn Leech pierced into Kingambit's body, beginning to siphon its strength.

Kingambit sensed something was wrong and quickly retreated from its position.

It attempted to fall back, but Gogoat had already locked onto him.

Gogoat lowered its body slightly, shooting out like an arrow from a bowstring, a flash of white light, reaching Kingambit in the blink of an eye.

And in its mouth, a massive, gleaming blade of Grass had appeared out of nowhere.

Before anyone could even catch up with the sight, that blade of Grass had already sliced across Kingambit's body.

Kingambit was struck powerfully, its strong form flying backward through the air.

The violent impact forced a bit of drool from its mouth, and it emitted a guttural sound from its throat.

At that moment, time seemed frozen.

After a brief daze, D quickly snapped back to reality.

Again with that move, closing the distance with an Aerial Ace and pursuing with Leaf Blade!

Turned the tables? D paused slightly, then a thought that he could hardly believe emerged in his mind—Gogoat defeated a Champion Level Pokemon?

Not just him, even Geeta, witnessing the instant reversal in the situation, stood bewildered.

She gazed at the Gogoat, her mind replaying its attack with that massive Grass blade in its mouth.

What...move was that?

A question mark appeared above Geeta's head.

---

---

---

---

# 227: Chapter 224: Opportunity to Head to the Paldea Giant Pit (Supplement 1)

However, the Kingambit did not immediately lose its fighting ability due to Gogoat's attack.

It staggered to its feet from the ground, looking at Gogoat with deep-set eyes.

With a thud, the Kingambit knelt directly on the Grassy Terrain.

Seeing this, D was slightly stunned, and Gogoat blinked its eyes with a dazed expression.

What's this? Is the opponent surrendering?

No, it's not surrendering!

D's inner alarm bells rang out; it was Kingambit's exclusive move, Kowtow!

By kneeling, it tricks its opponent into letting their guard down and launches a sudden attack when the opponent is careless.

This is an extremely insidious and despicable move!

If the Pokemon had encountered this before, it would presumably be wary and avoid being attacked.

But the crucial point is, Gogoat had never encountered such a situation before!

D wanted to warn, but suddenly realized this was a self-directed Pokemon match, and he couldn't give a vocal warning.

"Maa?"

Gogoat, looking slightly confused, took a step forward, seemingly trying to see what was happening with the opponent.

Just as it reached the attack range, the giant blade on Kingambit's head suddenly emitted a strong malice.

Then, Kingambit suddenly lunged headfirst to the ground, and with its movement, the giant blade slashed directly at Gogoat.

"Boom!"

The Kowtow hit a vital point, and Gogoat let out a painful cry: "Maa!"

Taking advantage of Gogoat's pain, Kingambit abruptly stood up, its body flickering with a pale light, intending to end the battle before the Grassy Terrain could restore Gogoat's vitality.

At this moment, Geeta's voice came from behind it: "Enough, stop it!"

Kingambit paused in its action, then retracted its edge and retreated to Geeta's side.

Once again, a burst of vitality surged through the Grassy Terrain, nurturing Gogoat's body and restoring its strength.

Gogoat stood up with steadfast eyes, glaring angrily at Kingambit opposite.

"Maa!"

This Pokemon doesn't play fair!

The other two Gogoat also ran up, glaring at Kingambit opposite them.

Seeing these simple and honest Gogoat, Geeta suddenly felt a bit guilty: "Sorry."

D sighed, stepping forward helplessly. Although he knew Kowtow was Kingambit's exclusive move, it was indeed insidious and vile before Pokemon unfamiliar with it.

D reached out to pat Gogoat's head, calming them as he patiently explained Kingambit's previous actions.

"It wasn't Kingambit attacking you maliciously, it was a move, and you just hadn't encountered it before, so there wasn't prior experience. But now if you come across such a situation again, you'll know what to do, right?" D asked.

Gogoat looked at him, pondered for a moment, then nodded: "Maa!"

"Good kids," D said with a smile, ruffling their necks, "Go ahead, that's it for today, you can go have some Grassy Seed!"

At the mention of Grassy Seed, Gogoat ignored the sly Kingambit and promptly ran off.

D then stood up to look at Geeta.

Geeta looked at him with confusion in her eyes, commenting on the earlier battle: "… Gogoat's strength is quite formidable, but it doesn't seem to have much battle experience."

"Indeed," D nodded, "They've been training at the pasture and so far have only fought in a few battles, but they're not intended specifically for combat."

"For battles..." D glanced at a few Pokemon not far away, "Dragonite and Koraidon are more skilled."

Geeta realized: "No wonder."

A puzzled expression appeared on her face, "If I didn't see it wrong, that Gogoat seems quite talented in using moves."

As a champion with extensive battle experience, Geeta could tell that every Grass Type Move Gogoat used was extraordinary, like enhanced versions.

Grassy Terrain, not only had a wider coverage, but the vitality restored also seemed way beyond normal Grassy Terrain.

Vine Whip was another matter, those sturdy frightening vines were clearly not just any ordinary move.

As for the final move...

Geeta recalled the image of Gogoat holding a gigantic blade in its mouth.

She couldn't help but ask, "What move was that?"

D: "Leaf Blade."

Hearing this answer, Geeta was slightly stunned.

D noticed her expression, explaining, "It's essentially still Leaf Blade, just a different method of use."

A different method of use?

Geeta pondered slowly, and after a long while, she murmured, "I see."

With her inner questions answered, Geeta returned to her usual self.

Her eyes revealed a look of appreciation: "I'm glad to see your remarkable skills, beautiful moves are endlessly emerging. It seems the Paldea Region welcomes another great trainer."

"Every Pokemon Trainer is a treasure of the Paldea Region, D, I greatly welcome your return to the Paldea Region."

D: "…"

For a moment, he didn't know where to start his retort.

Whatever, she says so, then so be it!

D asked, "Do you want to continue the challenge?"

Geeta thought for a moment, then shook her head: "Better not; it's not quite fair to Gogoat. Perhaps, when they have enough battle experience, we can battle again."

D indeed had no intention to continue further.

Geeta: "Let's leave it here for today!"

Geeta was quite satisfied with today's harvest and before leaving the pasture, she left D with her contact information.

"What I mentioned before is still valid, the Paldea Region will fulfill one of your wishes to the best of its ability, as a reward for hero trainers."

"Of course, if you're ever considering working at the League, you can also contact me. At that time, I would certainly arrange a decent job for you."

She hadn't given up on inviting this trainer to work at the Paldea League.

Watching Geeta's figure disappear into the distance, D looked down at the number in his hand, speechless: "Really..."

*

In the evening, as D was making Grassy Seed and Green Grass Cake at home, he suddenly received a call from Professor Sada.

As the call connected, Professor Sada's face appeared on the video phone screen.

"Good afternoon, D!" Professor Sada's confident face revealed a smile.

D replied, "Good afternoon, Professor, Koraidon is doing well recently."

Upon hearing this, Professor Sada on the screen was slightly stunned, before the smile on her face grew wider:

"I'm glad Koraidon is getting along well with you, but today I'm calling not about Koraidon, but to talk about something else."

D was puzzled, "What?"

Professor Sada explained, "This morning, Geeta contacted us, and I heard about your affair from her."

"I really didn't expect you to be such an outstanding trainer, which means your strength should be adequate."

D was utterly confused.

Professor Sada didn't keep him guessing, smiling as she said, "If you wish to visit Zone Zero, you can seize this opportunity, as we just wanted to conduct a check on Koraidon."

"Generally, entry to Zone Zero requires approval from the Paldea League."

"With your strength, able to ensure your safety within the Giant Pit and stopping that fire, if you applied to the Paldea League now, you might get permission."

"However, the interior of the Giant Pit is vast and you'll spend a lot of time there, so if you come to visit, make sure to prepare in advance."

---

---

---

---

# 228: Chapter 225: Dreams

"The space inside the Giant Pit is vast, with a complex terrain. Although there are observation stations inside, each only has a simple bed for resting, so you need to bring a change of clothes and enough food."

"We're located at the bottom layer of the Giant Pit. Although you could ride Koraidon directly down from a high place, I don't recommend you do that."

"That's because there are a great number of ferocious Pokemon residing along the way, coming from the same space-time as Koraidon. We call them Paradox Pokemon."

Upon hearing this, D's heart stirred.

Paradox Pokemon, does that refer to Great Tusk, Scream Tail, Roaring Moon, Flutter Mane, Iron Thorns, and the others?

D asked, "Paradox Pokemon?"

Professor Sada nodded, "Those are other Pokemon from different timelines. In simple terms, they are certain familiar modern Pokemon in their ancient and future forms."

D furrowed his brows in contemplation.

All along, he had wanted to visit the Paldea Giant Pit to see those special Paradox Pokemon.

But unfortunately, he never had the chance to encounter them.

And now…

As Professor Sada said, this is a good opportunity.

Should he go take a look?

No need to think, D's mind already had an answer.

He wants to go and see, to see the Time Machine, to see those Paradox Pokemon, and if possible, he wants to capture one or two Paradox Pokemon.

Because they are just too cool!

For example, the Paradox species of Volcarona, Slither Wing, is a chubby, furry, cute big bug.

Or the Paradox species of Tyranitar, Iron Thorns, is a giant creature covered in metallic armor and highly intelligent.

Seeing D lost in thought, Professor Sada thought he was worried about the dangers on the road.

"As long as you follow the path established by the observation station, you will not encounter too much danger. That path was set up through specialized research and is relatively safe." She explained.

However, D wasn't worried about that problem.

Suppressing his excitement, he asked, "Professor, can those Paradox Pokemon be captured by humans?"

Hearing this, Professor Sada was slightly stunned, then a smile appeared on her face.

She nodded, giving D a positive answer, "Of course, as long as you can capture them, just like capturing Koraidon."

D's eyes instantly brightened.

In that case, he's not going to be polite!

He wants them all: Slither Wing, Iron Thorns, Scream Tail, Roaring Moon, Iron Bundle, Iron Valiant!

Looking at his expression, a hint of yearning and expectation appeared in Professor Sada's eyes, and she smiled as she said:

"To be honest, D, having Paradox Pokemon and modern Pokemon live harmoniously in the Paldea Region has always been Turo's and my dream."

"We hope they can integrate into this world, live with humans like modern Pokemon, travel together, battle together, becoming top-tier combinations."

"For this, Turo and I have been working hard here."

Hearing these words, D's previously excited mood gradually calmed down.

He knew this was the dream of the two professors.

But in the game's plot, this dream never came to fruition.

Because the primitive and foundational forces from the distant past were too ferocious, and the evolutionary forces from the distant future were too powerful.

The vigorous life force of the ancient and future Paradox Pokemon would destroy the modern ecosystem.

In the game, after the two professors accidentally died, the AI they created had to use the barriers in Zone Zero to control the situation to prevent Paradox Pokemon from leaving for Paldea.

But even so, Pokemon like Great Tusk and Iron Treads that can break through the barrier still appeared.

They broke into the Paldea Region, becoming the Titan Pokemon ruling different areas.

After contemplating for a moment, D silently nodded, "I understand, if it can be realized, this is indeed a great dream."

Professor Sada smiled slightly, "Then we'll be waiting here for you and Koraidon to arrive."

The call ended, and D's heart remained unsettled for a long time.

He thought about his previous warning to Professor Sada to be careful of a lab explosion.

If they took it to heart, then perhaps someday in the future, the two professors might survive the explosion safely.

At that time, would they abandon their dream?

D didn't know.

Things seem to be starting to move in an unknown direction.

"Forget it, everything will resolve itself in time. There's no point worrying about it now. With Geeta and the Paldea League's Champion-level Trainers around, it shouldn't cause too much of an impact."

With this thought, the heavy stone in D's heart finally settled.

Anyway, if the sky falls, Geeta will hold it up. Right now, he just wants to visit the Paldea Giant Pit and see those Paradox Pokemon.

Humming a little tune, he took out the contact information Geeta had left and gave her a call.

The League's rewards didn't interest him, he didn't lack money, and he wasn't very interested in Pokemon Eggs; compared to that, a visit to the Paldea Giant Pit seemed more worthwhile.

For him, capturing a Paradox Pokemon would be a big win.

Soon, the call connected, and after a brief greeting, D expressed his wish to Geeta.

"...I want to take a look at Zone Zero. Professor Sada just happened to contact me wanting to check Koraidon's physical condition, but she said going to the Giant Pit requires approval from the Paldea League."

Geeta seemed more agreeable than he expected.

After hearing these words, Geeta didn't say much, she only asked, "The interior of the Paldea Giant Pit is extremely perilous. Without Champion-level strength, it's impossible to take even a single step. Are you prepared?"

"Yes." D internally appreciated Geeta's beautiful misjudgment of him.

Champion-level, Koraidon has definitely reached it.

When the time comes, he'll bring plenty of Enhancing Cuisine and Recovery Cuisine to ensure his own safety.

"Alright." Geeta nodded, "I'll submit the application to the League. You will have to wait for a bit. Once the League responds, I'll contact you."

"Okay, thank you for your trouble." D said.

Geeta's face revealed a hint of a smile, "I'd be even happier if you're interested in working for the Paldea League."

D: "..."

The call ended, D walked out of the living room to the eaves outside, and as expected, he saw Koraidon's figure on the grassy field basking in sunlight.

Koraidon had already picked up on D's scent. It immediately opened its eyes and lifted its head to look over, "Ah, gah?"

"Koraidon!" D walked up, squatted in front of it, and while rubbing its face, he asked, "Have you seen any other Pokemon that come from the same place as you?"

"For example, a Pokemon like an elephant with a pair of gigantic tusks, as well as one crawling on the ground with huge wings that look like flames."

At first, Koraidon looked confused at these words, but after hearing D's description clearly, its face quickly showed a sudden realization.

It nodded, "Ah, gahs!"

D: "Are they powerful? Are they ferocious?"

"Ah, gah..." Koraidon thought carefully for a moment, then looked pleased with itself, "Ah, gahs!"

It didn't know if they were ferocious, but it knew for sure they're not stronger than it!

---

---

---

---

# 229: Chapter 226: Depressed Pokemon

D: "..."

All right!

Compared to Great Tusk and Scream Tail, Koraidon is definitely the most powerful Pokemon.

D simply sat down beside Koraidon to bask in the sun together.

Koraidon comfortably squinted its eyes, while D began to recall which ancient Paradox Pokemon existed.

Donphan's paradox form is Great Tusk,

Jigglypuff's paradox form is Scream Tail,

Amoonguss's paradox form is Brute Bonnet,

Misdreavus's paradox form is Flutter Mane,

Volcarona's paradox form is Slither Wing,

Magneton's paradox form is Sandy Shocks,

Salamence's paradox form is Roaring Moon.

In addition, there are the ancient forms of the three Pokemon from Ho-oh's guard.

By the time he traveled through time, two forms had already been announced: Suicune's paradox form, Walking Wake, and Lei Gong's paradox form, Raging Bolt.

Only Entei's paradox form hasn't been announced yet; its name and image are unknown.

"I wonder how powerful these Legendary beasts' ancient forms can be..." D murmured to himself.

They were surely summoned by the Time Machine like many other Paradox Pokemon.

If that's truly the case, then it means he might have a chance to encounter them in the Paldea Giant Pit.

*

After lunch, D went to the Berry Garden to check on the injured Pokemon.

The Berry Garden was very quiet, with almost no sounds from the Pokemon, contrasting sharply with the laughter of the Alcremie and Naclstack on the meadow outside.

Looking inside, D saw that all the Pokemon were hiding in the wooden cabin, seeming like they hadn't yet emerged from that shadow.

It was now 1 PM, nearly thirty hours since the fire incident occurred.

Although they were now in a safe environment with no worries about food and shelter, their spirits still appeared extremely downcast and listless.

Seeing D approach, some Pokemon poked their heads out of the cabin.

D made eye contact with them, giving a slight smile: "I'm here to check on how your injuries are healing."

"Chii!"

The lively Greedent was the first to hop out of the small cabin.

Its fluffy big tail wagged behind it, looking absolutely adorable, provided you could overlook the patch of burnt, bald fur.

Greedent cradled its injured tail, presenting it to D for inspection, looking pitiful: "Chii chii..."

D examined it carefully and couldn't hold back a smile: "You're fine now, the burn has healed, and by the end of this winter, the burned-off fur will regrow."

"Chii!"

Upon hearing that the fur would regrow, the carefree Greedent immediately stopped worrying.

Turning around, it waggled its big tail and ran back to its little cabin.

Following Greedent's path, D spotted a pile of berries behind it.

Those must be the ones Greedent stored for the winter, right?

Because this Pokémon's habits are very similar to squirrels in reality, hoarding food for later.

Thinking of this, D couldn't help but think about the scorched, barren forest on the hill in the back.

Could one of those trees have been filled with berries by Greedent?

But if those areas got burned down, Greedent would likely be very upset, wouldn't it?

Greedent was indeed very upset.

It was sad for an entire day yesterday until it stashed another batch of berries in its nest this morning, which made it feel better.

Seeing the piles of full berries in the wooden cabin made Greedent feel incredibly happy.

Sheltering the berries with its tail, it then lay down on them, looking utterly delighted.

Seeing that Greedent was fine, D moved on.

The first was the Wattrel on the tree.

Its wings had been burned yesterday, causing it to lose its ability to fly, and this Wattrel had been despondent for quite a while.

It kept trying to flap its wings to fly, but all attempts failed.

Seeing this, D stepped up to lift it down, examining its bandaged wings.

Sure enough, he noticed even more severe injuries.

D frowned and called his brother, Indeedee, to bring the medical kit while reapplying medicine to the Wattrel, saying:

"Until your wounds are fully healed, you shouldn't fly. Every flap of your wings worsens the injury, and continuing like this might mean they'll never recover."

Upon hearing this, a flash of fear appeared in Wattrel's eyes, and it immediately settled down, hanging its head to show it wouldn't do it again.

D stroked its head and said: "I know flying is your nature. Without your wings, you're scared you might not be able to fly again, which is why you've been trying so hard."

Wattrel looked up at the human.

"I promise you that as long as you don't flap your wings like before, they will heal. Before long, you'll be able to fly in the sky again. Will you trust me?" D asked.

Wattrel stared into his deep, dark eyes and hesitated before nodding.

"Good." D smiled, patting its head: "From today, I'll come to apply medicine to you twice a day."

D placed Wattrel back in the cabin and continued forward, seeing the figures of Petilil, Hoppip, Shroomish, and Surskit under several Berry trees ahead.

These Pokemon generally had very gentle dispositions. Even though they seemed dispirited, they greeted D proactively.

D smiled and approached them, checking their bodies: "Your injuries have improved a lot. With a good sleep, you should be fully recovered."

"Tala!"

Upon hearing this, the three Shroomish happily circled around D.

The two Hoppip also began to float around D.

Surskit and Petilil watched this scene with smiling faces from the side.

Seeing smiles return to these Pokemon's faces finally allowed D to breathe a small sigh of relief.

He glanced at the Tinkatink, Grafaiai, Kricketune, and Tandemaus gathering nearby after hearing the commotion and softly said, "Everything will be okay."

D checked their bodies, and seeing no significant injuries, he stood up and looked around.

Suddenly, he noticed one Pokemon was missing.

Bellibolt was in the Underground Cave, its body had already recovered yesterday and was fine.

And the one missing...

"Where's Slakoth?" D couldn't find it after searching around.

At this time, Tandemaus squeaked, pointing in one direction.

D walked over and soon saw Slakoth lying on the grass surrounded by berry trees.

A beam of sunlight filtered through the leaves, hitting the ground where Slakoth lay motionless.

Even though someone approached, it barely moved its eyes before lazily letting out a yawn.

D: "..."

It was clear that this Slakoth had fully adapted to its environment.

---

---

---

---

# 230: Chapter 227: Blossoming on All Fronts!

League Calendar Year 198, August 20th, Autumn.

Early in the morning, D received some woolen warm clothes and a scarf from Mrs. Dante.

This lovely elderly woman handed the items in a tote bag to D, while her gaze drifted to Cyclizar stretching lazily nearby.

"That kid still doesn't know you prepared a gift for it, right?" she asked.

"Of course not." D smiled, "If it found out beforehand, it wouldn't be a surprise anymore."

Mrs. Dante nodded gently, her tone warm as she said, "Since you've got everything ready, I won't disturb you both. Please say happy birthday to Cyclizar for me."

"Alright."

With that, Mrs. Dante turned and left.

After watching her figure disappear, D carried the tote bag back into the house.

He looked at the Indeedee Sibling with a grin and asked, "Have you got everything ready?"

"Indeedee~"

"Indeedee."

The siblings nodded in unison.

Seeing this, D said, "Then let's start decorating! Koraidon, I'll leave Cyclizar to you. Take it outside to play and come back when the sun rises."

"Graaa..."

Koraidon thought to itself that today was its birthday too, even though it had already gone through one before.

At that time, it was also taken out to play by Koraidon. Later, that Koraidon left it behind and ran off alone.

While obediently walking outside, Koraidon recalled what happened next.

What happened afterwards?

Logically, being left behind by Koraidon, it should have been upset, but at that time it didn't seem very bright, didn't think much, and just happily carried a few flowers back home.

Because Koraidon told it that D had bought a vase and wanted to put the prettiest flowers in it.

When it pushed open the door with the flowers, it saw the surprise D had prepared for it...

Now that it had turned from an insider to an outsider, Koraidon felt a twinge of nostalgia.

It came outside and found Cyclizar rolling on the grass, saying to it, "Graaa." (Let's go find some flowers.)

Cyclizar looked adorable and clueless: "Gao?"

Then it seemed to realize that Koraidon wanted to play with it, and its eyes immediately lit up, happily exclaiming, "Gao!"

Koraidon: "..."

It made up a serious excuse saying, "Gra, graaa?" (D bought a vase and wants to pick some flowers to put in it. Do you know where there might be some?)

The excuse was full of holes. If anyone on the ranch knew where the prettiest flowers bloomed, it would be D.

But Cyclizar at the moment didn't think about that much and was so happy it was about to jump with joy, turning around to start leading the way for Koraidon: "Gao!"

Koraidon quietly followed behind, and soon they came upon the flower field in the southeast of the ranch.

Mid-autumn was just the right time for these seasonal flowers to bloom in full.

These flowers were blooming so vigorously thanks to the diligence of the Combee and the Butterfree.

As soon as Cyclizar arrived, it plunged into the flower bushes, rolling around inside, then poked its head out of the flowers, looking at Koraidon behind it.

"Gao!" Cyclizar tilted its head in confusion: "Gao?"

Weren't they here to play together? Why was Koraidon still standing there not moving?

"So silly." Koraidon thought, rolling in flowers—this was behavior only childish Pokemon would do.

It sighed quietly, shaking its head, rejecting Cyclizar's invitation: "Graaa."

"Gao."

Cyclizar looked adorably at Koraidon; seeing that Koraidon really wasn't coming to play, it didn't mind too much and continued playing on its own.

The only thing that made Cyclizar feel a bit disappointed was that there were no butterflies in the fall, so it couldn't pounce on them.

Koraidon watched for a while, and contemplating Cyclizar's exposed belly, couldn't bear it anymore and sneaked away while Cyclizar was distracted.

After a while, Cyclizar noticed that Koraidon had already left.

Shaking off the petals from its head, it didn't think too much of it, preparing to play by itself, when it suddenly remembered something.

"Gao..."

Koraidon seemed to have said that D wanted to put the prettiest flowers in the house.

So in that case, it would take a good look for them!

Cyclizar turned and dived into the depths of the flower bed, searching for what it thought were the most beautiful flowers.

*

In the living room, D and the Indeedee Sibling were setting up the room.

They hung streamers and light globes on the walls, and for places out of their reach, they left it to the flying Pawmot.

In the Cream Workshop, Alcremie was vigorously making delicious fresh cream.

Even Dolliv, who was usually lazy, had been invited over by D to present fresh olive oil for Cyclizar.

Seeing the Pokemon doing their part diligently, D entrusted the task of setting up the venue entirely to them.

He himself went back to the Cream Workshop to start making today's birthday cake.

The process of making the birthday cake was something D had long memorized by heart.

Eggs, milk, oven...

And adding just a touch of emotion.

This was a conclusion D had discovered before—putting affection into cooking made the result much better.

This time, he didn't need to deliberately stir his emotions, because his feelings with Cyclizar had long been woven into the daily bits and pieces of life.

Just thinking back on these brought forth those emotions naturally and effortlessly.

---

One year ago, Cyclizar hatched from an egg.

What would a freshly hatched Cyclizar look like?

Thinking back to the baby forms of the Indeedee Sibling he had seen before, an image gradually emerged in D's mind of a small Cyclizar wobbling and balancing an eggshell as it crawled out of the Pokemon Egg.

Unfortunately, he didn't get to witness Cyclizar's birth.

The baby Cyclizar began to grow, reaching the normal size, and then it left its parents, brought here by Mayor Thomas, who entrusted Cyclizar to his care.

At this thought, D sincerely wished Cyclizar a life filled with health and happiness, with everything going smoothly and joyfully.

With these emotions, he placed the cake he had prepared into a mold and put it in the oven.

After some time, the fragrant cake base was freshly out. D split it in half, placed diced Berry pieces inside, and finally, together with Alcremie, evenly spread fresh cream on top.

Thus, an intricately prepared birthday cake was freshly completed.

Looking down, the details of the cake appeared in D's mind.

[Birthday Cake (S): A cake made with rich cream and carefully baked cake base, decorated with Berries, and contains a celebratory meaning.

Cooking Effect: Birthday gift, when given to a Pokemon on its birthday, it grants a blessing, permanently boosting Hp, Attack, Special Attack, Speed, Defense, and Special Defense.

Review: Gift it to a Pokemon as a present—it might bring unexpected surprises!]

"Wow!"

Staring at the freshly made birthday cake, a thought of 'just as expected' filled D's mind.

"The cooking level soared from B directly to S, and the effect changed from boosting one attribute to boosting all of them, so exaggerated!" exclaimed D, taking a cold breath.

But then, a new problem presented itself.

The six attributes boosted by the birthday gift—are they species strength, effort values, or individual values?

If they're effort values, then it's a bit mediocre; D thought the lower limit of S-level cooking shouldn't be that low.

Then, what about species strength?

If it's that, then it's indeed exaggerated!

Cyclizar's species strength is 501; if it continued eating like this for several years, its upper limit could surpass those of Venusaur, Blastoise, and Charizard, the Starter Pokemon.

If it ate like this for decades, centuries, surpassing the Legendary Pokemon wouldn't be a dream!

But here's another issue: reality world isn't a game; humans can't visibly see the increase in Pokemon's species strength.

And even a higher species strength doesn't guarantee anything, as this world has seen cases where Pokemon with lower species strength defeated those with higher species strength.

D couldn't figure it out, and after a brief moment of pondering, he decided to cast all these concerns aside.

After all, he's not a Trainer, and there's no need to battle. What's most important now is to celebrate Cyclizar's birthday.

At this moment, Butterfree flew in from the window to notify that Cyclizar was on its way back.

Upon hearing this, D hurriedly carried the cake back to the living room with Alcremie and Dolliv.

The setup was already complete; the living room was brightly lit with decorations, and various gift boxes were piled on the sofa and wooden floor as decorations.

At this moment, most of the Pokemon from the ranch had already gathered here.

The Naclstack crowded in the corner, Miltank stood by, and Dragonite and Dragonair squeezed together by the fireplace.

These Pokemon were notified earlier by D to come celebrate Cyclizar's birthday together.

D asked the Indeedee sibling to pull the curtains, and suddenly, the room fell into darkness.

Everyone stared collectively at the direction of the door, waiting for Cyclizar to enter.

At this very moment, Cyclizar was walking along the wooden path laid out by D.

It held several flowers in its hand, flowers it deemed the most beautiful.

With a focused mind, Cyclizar walked the route holding the flowers, but suddenly, it sensed something amiss.

The once lively ranch was now unusually quiet, devoid of any sound.

"Gah-oh?!"

Cyclizar was startled, looking around to find even the Gogoat usually staying in the sheep pen were nowhere to be seen!

Cyclizar panicked, thinking what might have happened while picking flowers.

Thinking of this, Cyclizar quickened its pace, wanting to get home to see D.

Upon reaching the house and pushing the door, it heard the sound of fireworks exploding, colorful streamers falling like snowflakes from its head.

Indeedee sibling clapped enthusiastically,

standing on Naclstack, Alcremie boldly posed with hands on hips: "Ma-ma!"

"Mee-ah!" Gogoat expressed joy, showing a fondness for the Pokemon that often fed them Grassy Seed.

Seeing the room full of Pokemon, Cyclizar was dumbfounded, just standing there dazed.

"Cyclizar, happy birthday!" D smiled and walked up, pulling the stunned Cyclizar forward.

Even upon reaching the cream cake with a single candle, Cyclizar still hadn't processed what was happening.

Seeing this, D helplessly said, "Did you forget? Today is your birthday; on this day last year, you hatched from a Pokemon Egg."

---

Cyclizar nodded dumbly: "Gaa-oo."

Its eyes widened as it finally realized the current situation.

D and everyone, are they celebrating its birthday?!

"These are custom clothes I specially ordered for you from Mrs. Dante." D took out the shopping bag and placed the clothes before Cyclizar: "With these, you won't feel cold in winter, and you can play in the snow with everyone else."

Cyclizar listened blankly, the sudden surprise almost overwhelming it.

At this moment, it felt as if its entire body was bathed in an ocean of happiness.

"Gaa, gaa-oo!" Cyclizar let out a silly growl, looking at everyone with emotion.

Koraidon: "..." Can't bear to watch!

"Blow the candles!" D led Cyclizar to the cake.

Under everyone's gaze, Cyclizar puffed up its cheeks and blew out the candle.

"Clap clap clap!"

The Indeedee sister started clapping again.

Under its lead, the Pokemon around burst into cheers.

After blowing the candles, it was time to cut the cake.

Cyclizar didn't know how to cut, so D helped.

He cut the cake into small pieces and handed them to Cyclizar, letting it share the joy with everyone.

Cyclizar obediently complied.

Koraidon watched this scene, its eyes sparkling.

It knew that although Cyclizar looked foolish now, it would forever remember everything that happened today and be grateful for it.

At this time, Cyclizar came to it with a piece of cake.

Koraidon looked down at it, then reached out a paw to take the cake, eating this long-awaited birthday gift.

"Gaa-oo~"

Seeing Koraidon eat the cake, Cyclizar felt very happy.

It didn't care at all that Koraidon had left it alone in the flower field and sneaked away just now.

After finishing distributing the cake, Cyclizar took its portion and began eating it with everyone.

Enjoying the sweet taste of cream in its mouth, Cyclizar couldn't help but think to itself.

How happy!

*

On August 21, D received a response from the Paldea League.

Geeta's voice came through the phone: "The application you submitted that day to enter the Paldea Giant Pit has been approved by the League. You need to first head to Medali Town, where staff from the Paldea League will take you to the Area Zero Gate leading inside the Pit."

"However, they can only take you to the Area Zero Gate, and everything afterward is up to you."

Thinking of everything in the Pit, Geeta couldn't help but remind: "The inside of the Paldea Giant Pit is extremely dangerous. Even Champion-level Trainers must be cautious there. I wouldn't want to see the Paldea League lose a talent."

D: "..."

Even though he wasn't sure when he became a talent for the Paldea League, he still took Geeta's warning to heart: "I'll be careful, Champion."

Geeta: "That's best. When do you plan to set off?"

Upon hearing this, D turned back, glancing at the few Dragonair playing in the living room.

At the ranch, there's currently nothing urgent. With the Indeedee Sibling here, even if he went out for a while, the ranch would be well managed by them.

The only concern was the group of wild Pokemon temporarily staying in the Berry Garden.

They hadn't yet moved past the shadow of that disaster.

Thinking of this, D said: "In three days!"

In three days, Wattrel's wings should be completely healed.

"In three days?" Geeta nodded: "Alright, I'll inform the staff of the time."

D asked: "How should I find him?"

"That's simple." Discussing the person, Geeta's tone held a hint of helplessness: "He is Larry, the Gym Leader of Medali Gym. At that time, you just need to go to Medali Gym to find him."

"If he's not there, you can find him at a place called the Treasure Eatery in town. He will take you to the Area Zero Gate."

---

---

---

---

# 231: Chapter 228: Counting Moons with Cyclizar

"Larry?"

Upon hearing this name, an image of a man who seemed like an overworked corporate slave appeared in D's mind.

Isn't he an employee of the Paldea League's operations department? How is he doing such basic work as guiding people?

"Yes." Geeta said, "He is an employee of the League's operations department and also serves as the Medali Gym Leader. Since the Area Zero Gate is near Medali Town, he took on this job."

Hearing this, D nodded silently, "I understand."

"Then, be careful with everything." Geeta gave a final reminder and then hung up the phone.

D stood there for a while, recollecting matters concerning the Paldea Giant Pit in his mind, until his sister Indeedee came to call him, bringing him back to his senses.

Looking at the sibling duo beside him, D said to them, "In three days, I have to leave for a distant trip, which will take about a week before I can return. In the meantime, I'll need you to take care of everyone at the ranch."

"Eevee." The brother Indeedee nodded, as helping the owner manage the ranch was their duty.

The sister Indeedee also cheerfully expressed that she would take good care of the Pokemon at the ranch.

Seeing this, D's face broke into a smile.

With two Indeedees around, he felt at ease.

Next, he detailed the daily tasks at the ranch, as well as the food and feeding times for each Pokemon, to the two Indeedees.

The sibling pair listened intently, committing everything to memory.

After handing over everything, it was time to select the Pokemon to bring into the Paldea Giant Pit.

First, Koraidon was a definite.

Though he was a transmigrator, D wouldn't underestimate anyone or any place just because he had knowledge and information.

For caution's sake, besides Koraidon, he needed to bring two other Pokemon with sufficient combat ability for self-defense.

Currently, the Pokemon capable of fighting at the ranch...

In D's mind appeared several figures.

Koraidon, Dragonite, Pawmot, Gogoat x3.

Naclstack hadn't matured yet, and the power of Cyclizar, Glaceon, Dolliv, and Butterfree was average, far from sufficient.

Therefore, D planned to leave them at the ranch.

So only those six remained.

But Gogoats are the ranch's milk-producing Pokemon, taking them would affect milk production.

After thinking it over slightly, D made a decision in his heart.

This time heading to the Paldea Giant Pit, he would only take Koraidon, Dragonite, and Pawmot.

However, before leaving, he had a few things to do.

The first was to apply medicine to Wattrel until its wing fully healed.

The second was to observe the changes in Cyclizar and test the effect of yesterday's birthday cake.

While there was still time, D first went to the Berry Garden to do Wattrel's daily check-up.

Ever since he mentioned that forcing itself to fly while injured might lead it never to fly again, Wattrel had become more obedient.

It hadn't tried flying again and would spend the day either in its nest on the tree or perched on a branch watching the Shroomish and Kricketune below.

Seeing D approach, Wattrel shifted its gaze to him.

D smiled and said, "I'm here to see how your injuries are healing."

Hearing this, Wattrel moved closer, displaying its wing for him to inspect.

D gently pinched it and asked, "Does it still hurt significantly?"

Wattrel carefully felt it, then shook its head.

"That means it's healing well," D said.

Wattrel's eyes sparkled at once.

D: "You should be good to fly again in a maximum of two days. Once you're healed, I'll be off on a trip."

At this moment, the surrounding Shroomish, Surskit, Greedent, Tinkatink, and other Pokemon gathered around.

D looked at them and softly said, "I have some matters to handle, so you'll stay here comfortably. When spring arrives next year, I'll help you all rebuild your home."

Today, it had been several days since that fire, and the Pokemon's state had gradually improved, no longer as depressed as initially.

After hearing his words, these adorable little creatures joyfully hopped around, letting out happy cries.

D watched this scene with a smile, suddenly remembering something, and said to the Grass Type Pokemon in front of him:

"Oh, right, the weather has been nice these days with warm sunshine. You can go play more in the flower field."

Especially Petilil.

This kind of Pokemon loves moist and nutrient-rich soil, making the land they inhabit flourish.

In contrast, Shroomish prefers humid places and mostly resides in the damp ground deep within the forest.

During the day, Shroomish stays still underneath fallen leaves in the forest's shade, and their favorite food is the moist decaying leaf soil in the forest.

Petilil agreed joyfully.

With everything settled, D went to find Cyclizar.

After eating the birthday cake yesterday, Cyclizar showed no special reaction, making D wonder whether the cake's effects needed time to manifest.

Cyclizar is sunbathing on the grass.

D came up to it and tentatively asked, "From last night to now, have you felt any part of your body change?"

"Gah-ow?" Cyclizar looked puzzled, as if it didn't understand.

D explained, "For example, running faster, becoming stronger..."

Upon hearing this, Cyclizar carefully sensed its body, then shook its head blankly.

"Strange..." D couldn't help but ponder.

Could it be that the birthday cake had no effect? No, that's impossible.

Recalling his thoughts from yesterday, a bold guess suddenly formed in his mind.

That is, the birthday cake increased Cyclizar's species strength, enhancing its species limit!

If that's the case, then it might take a long time before the changes become apparent.

Just like the Pikachu among the Pikas, now Cyclizar is undoubtedly a special Cyclizar.

"All six species strengths increased, wow, that's a bit terrifying!" D muttered.

This is just the first year; what if he gives Cyclizar a birthday cake every year from now on?

For instance, if the birthday cake increased each species strength by 1 point, then it's 6 points in a year.

Ten years would be 60 points, thirty years would be 180 points, and thirty years later Cyclizar's species strength would be 681, nearing the Creator God Arceus's 720.

"Gah-ow?"

Cyclizar noticed D's excited expression, looking even more puzzled.

D came back to his senses, chuckled and patted its head, explaining, "Nothing's wrong; I just realized the joy of nurturing. Cyclizar, you'll become a great Pokémon in the future."

Upon hearing this, Cyclizar immediately straightened up proudly, like a child who has been praised.

It will work hard! It will protect D in due time!

In a daze, D seemed to see the shadow of Koraidon on Cyclizar.

He remembered that when he praised Koraidon, Koraidon had the same proud expression.

"Birds of a feather flock together; Cyclizar must have learned from Koraidon," D thought.

*

Three days later, Wattrel's wing fully recovered, and D bid farewell to the ranch Pokémon, preparing to head to the Paldea Giant Pit with Koraidon, Dragonite, and Pawmo.

He stood in the ranch and bade farewell to the Pokémon.

Cyclizar, Alcremie, Naclstack, and other Pokémon all wore expressions of reluctance.

Especially Cyclizar.

It had grown up so much, yet never left D for such a long time.

Seeing this, D looked helpless: "Indeedee will take good care of you. I'm just going out for a few days and will be back soon."

Cyclizar's eyes were already brimming with tears.

"Be good," D stepped forward to pat Cyclizar's head; this child loves him so much.

Cyclizar felt downcast: "Gah-ow..."

"Next time, next time I go out, I'll definitely take you with me, okay?" D promised.

Incidentally, he plans to visit the Galar Region in winter, and then Cyclizar can accompany him on a spontaneous snowfield journey.

"Gah-ow..." Though a bit displeased, Cyclizar nodded in agreement.

"Good kid." D pointed at the batch of unripe pumpkins in the field, saying to Cyclizar, "I'll be back when the pumpkins in the field ripen. You have to keep an eye on them; don't let the wild Pokémon eat them secretly."

"Gah-ow." Cyclizar responded, looking at the pumpkins in the field, and remembered these words.

"Alright, it's getting late; we should head out."

D stood up, returned Dragonite and Pawmo to their Poke Balls, then rode on Koraidon's back and waved goodbye to the Pokémon.

"Goodbye!"

"Ayee!"

"Mama!"

"Meah!"

"..."

When Koraidon's figure could no longer be seen in the sky, the Pokémon slowly looked down dejectedly.

Without the backbone of the ranch, although everything seemed unchanged around them, they still felt a void inside.

Indeedee Sibling looked at everyone's downcast mood, feeling an unprecedented sense of responsibility.

From today on, all these Pokémon will be under their care!

Cyclizar thought about the words D said.

The day the pumpkins in the field ripen… But when will the pumpkins in the field actually ripen?

Being a simple Cyclizar, it didn't know such things.

After pondering for a while, Cyclizar found Brother Indeedee and asked, "Gah-ow? (When will the pumpkins in the field ripen?)"

Brother Indeedee kept a serious, tight-lipped expression, resembling a little adult.

Hearing this, it was confused for a second, then suddenly realized it didn't know the answer either.

The pumpkins ripening in the ground…

Brother Indeedee carefully recalled D's previous scene of harvesting pumpkins, then answered:

"Ayee." (When they turn orange, they are ripe.)

What color is orange? Cyclizar did not understand.

Brother Indeedee thought for a while, found a pumpkin lantern D had carved before, and pointed at it saying to Cyclizar, "Ayee." (It's this color.)

"Gah-ow." (Oh.)

---

The Cyclizar memorized that color in its heart.

However, it still wanted to know the time, so it went to the Underground Cave to find a Diglett who was knowledgeable about farming.

"Gao?" (How many days until the pumpkin is ripe?)

"Zhi zhi..." This question stumped the Diglett; it was only helping D with the farming and was unsure about such matters!

Diglett scratched its head, troubled, struggled for a moment, and said: "Zhi zhi?" (Maybe Koraidon knows?)

That Koraidon flies around the ranch every day, and nothing escapes its eyes; it should know when the pumpkin will mature.

So the Cyclizar went to find Koraidon.

Facing Cyclizar's question, Koraidon looked at the sun in the sky and then at the pumpkin in the field, replying:

"Mii." (When the sun sets and the moon rises, seven times back and forth, the pumpkin will mature.)

"Gao."

The Cyclizar remembered this word.

So starting that night, the ranch saw a Cyclizar counting the moon.

The Cyclizar didn't know when the sun would set or when the moon would rise.

It only knew that when it counted to seven, D would return.

*

Medali Town is located in Western Paldea, set beneath the Paldea Giant Pit, and is a culinary town with a nostalgic atmosphere.

The distance between the two towns isn't too far; D rode Koraidon without a moment's pause, and by the time he arrived in Medali Town, only three hours had passed.

Just as he landed, D heard the enthusiastic shouting from the town.

"Ma po tofu, shredded pork with green pepper, vinegar-dressed Toedscool cucumber, authentic ramen! The most delicious dishes in Paldea are here, come and taste them!"

"Churros! Your mouth's good neighbor, kissy churros! We have chocolate flavor, cinnamon flavor, and authentic original churros!"

"Lemonade, coffee, sandwich cookies, cheese pies; this is the Welcome Café, your perfect place to relax!"

"Seafood skewers, rice pudding, lemon-dressed sashimi, seafood pasta; this is the top seafood restaurant, no need to wait, deliciousness is right here!"

The streets were bustling with people, and D and Koraidon stood outside the crowd, quite amazed at the variety of restaurants in front of them.

Medali Town was more lively than he had imagined!

It was precisely noon, and D felt a bit hungry, so he planned to first feed the three Pokémon before setting off to find Larry.

It was his first time in Medali Town, and the town's complex streets and buildings were simply overwhelming.

D walked in a circle; not only did he not find the Treasure Eatery, he couldn't even locate the Medali Gym.

Helpless, he sought help from passersby, and eventually, with the guidance of a pretty young lady and her Skwovet, he found the legendary Treasure Eatery.

Upon entering, D saw a Pokemon battle happening in the middle of the restaurant's tatami area.

A man dressed in a black suit, his hair thinning, with dark circles, exuding the vibe of a corporate slave, was weakly commanding a Komala in a Pokemon battle against a student-like youth.

Seeing this scene, D raised his eyebrows slightly.

Well, what's this called? Early arrival beats a timely one?

During lunchtime, all the guests in the restaurant were eating while watching the tatami area battle.

D joined the rest in ordering a few wasabi rice balls, a meat steak, an omelet, and a bowl of clear soba soup at the bar.

He released Pawmot and Dragonite from their Poke Balls and sat with them in the wood-paneled dining area, eagerly watching the battle in front of him.

---

---

# 232: Chapter 229: Beneath the Clouds and Mist

At this moment, the battle on the field had reached a fevered pitch.

Larry's Komala and the young boy's Fuecoco were both nearly out of stamina.

Facing the Fuecoco charging towards him, Larry once again ordered Komala to use Yawn.

The Yawn successfully hit, and Fuecoco instantly felt drowsy.

But just as it was about to fall asleep, the young boy suddenly shouted for it to hold on.

Fuecoco snapped awake, broke free from the drowsiness, and gathered flames in its mouth, using Ember to defeat Larry's Komala.

"Great!" The boy's face lit up with excitement.

Larry, with a calm expression, quietly took out a Poke Ball and retrieved Komala:

"You're really good, but you only have a worn-out Fuecoco left, while I have a fully healthy Pokemon remaining."

"We won't give up!" The boy shouted spiritedly.

The dining patrons were ignited by his fighting spirit, loudly cheering him on.

"Hey! Kid! Hold on!"

"The hungry customers are waiting! Show us what you're good at!"

"Both you and Larry, cheer up!"

"That's right, Mr. Larry!" The boy said with determination.

Larry: "…I got it, if we chat too much my boss will get mad, let's start the battle quickly!"

With that, he sent out his last Pokemon.

It was a sharp-eyed, agile Staraptor.

The final battle officially began!

Facing this nearly unbeatable strong opponent, the boy still did not choose to give up; his fighting spirit affected Fuecoco on the field.

Fuecoco courageously overcame Staraptor's intimidation and used its best move, Ember!

Perhaps it was luck, or maybe it was Larry's intention, the Ember successfully hit Staraptor and luckily caused it to be in a burn status.

Seeing this scene, the boy clenched his fists excitedly: "Yes! Just like that, Fuecoco!"

In burn status, the Pokemon's stamina gradually dwindles; the longer they endure, the higher the chances of victory in this battle!

Some of the audience supporting Larry were very dissatisfied when they saw this.

"What's the matter, Larry! So easily hit by that kid's move!"

"Get serious! I didn't come here to see you intentionally lose the battle!"

Upon hearing these words, Larry looked utterly exhausted. He glanced at the Trainer opposite him and said weakly:

"…Excuse me, it's okay if I use the move that social persons excel at, right?"

The boy was slightly stunned.

And Larry had already given the command: "Staraptor, use Facade!"

Facade, a Normal Type move, has the effect of doubling its power when the user is in burn, paralysis, or poison status.

Upon receiving this command, Staraptor emitted a dazzling white light, then dived down from the sky towards Fuecoco.

Fuecoco was unsurprisingly hit, and the Facade struck it, rendering it unable to battle immediately.

The boy stood with his mouth agape.

The diners cheered enthusiastically.

"Wow! Ahhh! That's Larry for you!"

"What a spectacular battle, it really makes the meal enjoyable! I've unknowingly had two bowls of ramen!"

Larry ignored these voices and said plainly: "Unfortunately, you did not pass the Medali Gym challenge, so I can't give you the Gym Badge. Well then, goodbye."

Finishing his statement, Larry picked up his briefcase, left the stage, and headed to the bar counter to have his meal.

"This is Larry…" D said with interest, observing the man with the extremely high hairline.

Even though he was dressed in a sharp, spirited black suit, his face showed undeniable fatigue, with very noticeable dark circles around his eyes.

Thinking of his main purpose of coming, D asked the nearby Pawmot and Dragonite: "Have you eaten enough?"

"Pawmot!"

"Grr~"

Seeing both Pokemon had filled their stomachs, D returned them to their Poke Balls, and then walked forward with Koraidon, sitting next to Larry, and ordered two bowls of red bean soup for digestion.

Before he could speak, Larry had already noticed him and the Koraidon beside him.

Larry's expression didn't show any surprise; it was still very calm: "You've arrived."

D froze on the spot: "You know me?"

Could it be that Geeta had shown him a photo of me? No, when did Geeta have his photo?

Larry saw him overthinking and began to explain: "The time with the chieftain… an uninvited rare guest, from the first moment you entered, I noticed you and that Pokemon."

"An ordinary person like me wouldn't be recognized walking down the street, but you're different; no matter where you go, you're a noticeable presence."

D was speechless: "…Exaggerated."

But Larry calmly said, "The Giant Pit, such a dangerous place you're bold enough to go to; if it was me, I definitely wouldn't. Hello, I'm Larry, pleased to meet you."

"I'm D."
