Larry nodded: "Please wait a moment, when I finish my meal, I'll take you over."

D didn't say much: "Alright."

Larry seemed to very much enjoy his peaceful dining time. He ate unhurriedly, and when he had finished all the food in front of him, D and Koraidon had also finished the two bowls of red bean soup.

---

Larry stood up, picking up his briefcase, "Let's go, let me just finish this job quickly!"

D: "Alright."

The two left the Treasure Eatery. D initially thought Larry would ride a Cyclizar taxi or drive his car to take him to Area Zero Gate.

But unexpectedly, after walking out of the door, Larry headed straight to a bicycle parking area, pulled out a bicycle, and got on.

D: "?"

Larry turned around, intending for him to follow, but seeing that baffled expression, he thought for a moment and explained:

"This is my usual mode of transport, very ordinary, right?"

D nodded numbly: "Yeah."

Larry: "Normal type... ordinary... common... mundane... goes well with my lack of personality, I just enjoy this, simplicity is reality."

As he spoke, Larry began pedaling the bicycle.

D, helplessly, could only follow by riding Koraidon.

On the way, Larry started to explain another matter: "Originally, I wasn't the one responsible for taking you there, but that person had something come up at the last minute and took a leave, so I had to take you over."

D wasn't sure how to handle Larry's workaholic nature, so he woodenly replied: "Sorry for the trouble, thank you."

Larry: "You're too polite... it's the work of a corporate employee."

D was speechless.

Larry, seemingly from too many side jobs, couldn't hide his fatigue on his face. After a brief introduction, he no longer spoke.

The Area Zero Gate was constructed on the steep, towering cliff outside the Paldea Giant Pit. To reach there, besides riding a bicycle, one had to climb the mountain afterward.

Medali Town lies beneath the Area Zero Gate, and in the town, one can clearly see the towering cliff scraping the sky.

The two soon arrived at the foot of the mountain.

Larry got off the bike, parked it by the roadside, "Next, we'll have to walk the mountain, the bike can't be ridden up there."

D was quite concerned: "Do you want to take a break first?"

Seeing Larry's appearance, he was afraid he might drop dead halfway up.

Larry: "...Let's consider this helping with digestion after the meal, come on, let me finish this job quickly and go home to rest."

D: "Oh oh!"

Larry was walking; he could only follow suit, walking.

Fortunately, he used to do farm work often, so his body was pretty well exercised.

After a short walk, Larry was already out of breath.

D concernedly asked: "Do you want to ride Koraidon up with me?"

Larry glanced at Koraidon, shook his head, and refused: "Forget it, I'm not keen on experiencing something so novel."

"...Alright."

Perhaps feeling that idle chat would make walking less tiring, Larry asked: "Is its name Koraidon?"

D nodded: "Yes, Professor Sada named it."

"I see." Larry thought about the fact that Geeta allowed this person to enter the Giant Pit, and he said surprisingly: "I didn't expect the Champion would approve your request to enter the Giant Pit."

D muttered: "Maybe she thinks I have the strength to protect myself?"

The ability to protect oneself in the Paldea Giant Pit's interior, could this man be a Champion-level Trainer?

Indeed, Geeta has always been attracting talent for the Paldea League, perhaps approving this man's application was an attempt to make friends with him.

Larry turned his head to look at D, suddenly asking: "Did the Champion invite you to work in the League?"

D paused: "How did you know that?"

Larry had a face of 'I knew all along': "I guessed it all."

"The Champion is my superior, if you agree to her request, maybe you'll end up like me, assigned to a city to be a Gym Leader, and perhaps also enter the sales department reliant on performance, doing multiple jobs at once, she's capable of such things."

D: "..."

Larry continued: "If you willingly want that, then fine, if it were me, I wouldn't do it, even though I'm also serving as an Elite Four right now, but this is already my limit."

D quietly asked: "Taking me to Area Zero Gate isn't considered part of the duties of an Elite or Gym Leader, right?"

Larry sighed: "No choice, this is the life of a corporate employee, to avoid being disliked by the boss and for overtime pay, you end up doing tasks not meant for you; maybe you'll understand as you grow older."

D: "..."

During their conversation, the two had reached the halfway point of the mountain.

After passing through a dimly lit tunnel, a towering building equipped with a landing gear and helipad came into view.

"We're here." Larry said, "This is Area Zero Gate, pass through here to enter the interior of the Paldea Giant Pit."

Larry followed the procedure, walked ahead, used the card issued by the League to open the metal door, leading D and Koraidon inside the building.

D looked up at the technologically styled place in front of him and couldn't help but exclaim.

Larry said: "It's my first time here too, it's too dangerous below, if it weren't for work, I definitely wouldn't come here."

As he spoke, he opened the metal door leading to the helipad: "It's right here."

D walked in, and heard the roaring sound of air current.

He and Koraidon stood on the landing gear, facing a vast stretch of dense white clouds.

In an instant, D felt as if he had reached the clouds, immersed in paradise.

Even though he had entered the Giant Pit in the game, at this moment, D couldn't help but feel nervous.

Because beneath this milky white cloud cover lies the seemingly bottomless Paldea Giant Pit; if he accidentally fell off Koraidon, he'd be shattered to pieces.

Beside him, Koraidon also seemed a bit nervous.

D patted its head, then took a deep breath.

He turned around and saw Larry standing distantly behind, watching him.

Seeing him turn around, Larry said: "My work is done, good luck."

"Thank you."

D thanked him and then mounted Koraidon's back, gently patting Koraidon's head: "Let's go!"

"Ah gaa ss..."

Koraidon's feelings were inevitably complicated at the moment.

But as D wanted to go, it still raised its forelimbs and leaped.

Instantly, the biting airflow frantically roared past D's ears, enveloping him and Koraidon in thick white clouds.

Koraidon began to rapidly descend, D tightly hugged its neck.

Suddenly, the ornate feather on Koraidon's head transformed into wings, drastically slowing the descent, calming everything around.

D opened his eyes, and they seemed to have already passed through the clouds. Below appeared a vast landscape and valleys glittering with magnificent crystalline light.

Light cascaded like waterfalls, pouring down from the clouds.

That light was so pure and dazzling, making the grassland, trees, valleys, cliffs, and waterfalls all appear sacred.

Koraidon landed nearby on a green cliff rich with life.

D led it to the edge of the cliff, standing before a simple railing designed by the Paldea League, gazing at the indescribable spectacular scenery.

The air shimmered with radiant crystal light, and on the grass, Frosmoth's scales drifted by in the wind, resembling stardust falling from the clouds under the luminous glow.

In the distant canyon, under the towering waterfall, Girafarig was drinking with a group of Girafarig.

Above them, Hoppip floated gently with the breeze.

"So beautiful!" D murmured, feeling he would never forget this scene his whole life.

"Ah gaa ss..." Koraidon called out, puzzled.

D withdrew his gaze, not forgetting his real purpose for coming here.

He looked around, surprisingly not seeing any Paradox Pokemon nearby; thinking perhaps this was the uppermost layer of the pit, those Paradox Pokemon's habitats hadn't expanded here yet.

Professor Sada mentioned that walking down from the area below Area Zero Gate would lead to the first observation station.

He and Larry had taken over two hours to climb the mountain; now it's estimated to be three in the afternoon.

D didn't plan to ride Koraidon straight down to the lower layer through the large central hollow.

The danger mentioned by Professor Sada was one reason, but mainly, D wanted to see the Paradox Pokemon residing here and see if he could capture some that were fated with him.

Thus, they had to reach the first observation station before nightfall, take a brief rest, and then continue downward.

---

---

---

---

# 233: Chapter 230: Blood and Moon, the Primal Incarnation of Ferocity!

At four in the afternoon, D successfully reached the first observation station.

Unexpectedly, he didn't encounter any danger along the way, nor were there terrifying paradox Pokémon everywhere like in the game.

On the contrary, the upper layer of the Giant Pit gave him a sense of tranquility and beauty, not much different from the outside world.

Pokémon like Hoppip, Girafarig, Frosmoth, Talonflame, and Raichu live leisurely here.

Especially near the observation station, this building covered with climbing ivy sits below a hillside, with hardly any wild Pokémon visible nearby.

Perhaps, as Professor Sada said, the location of the observation station was designed as a safe zone after careful investigation.

Opening the door of the observation station, the layout here is extremely simple, the room is hexagonal in structure, with unused building materials on either side of the entrance, and a thick conduit for energy transmission standing in the innermost position.

The wall is pasted with the working schedule of the research personnel, and a solitary single bed is placed in the corner, judging by the dust on it, it appears that no one has visited for a long time.

In such an environment, D was not surprised, he placed his backpack down, prepared for a brief rest, and then planned to go outside and observe the nearby Pokémon ecology.

But at that moment, the communication device inside the observation station suddenly rang.

D went forward to connect, and heard Professor Sada's voice come from it.

"...Is that D?"

"It's me, Professor."

"I figured, I saw the notification of the observation station being used and knew it was you, seems like you've safely reached the inside of the Giant Pit! How is it? Has everything gone smoothly?" Professor Sada asked.

"I've not encountered any danger yet." D thought for a moment, and asked: "By the way, Professor, aren't there Paradox Pokémon near the first observation station? I haven't seen a single Paradox Pokémon along the way."

Upon hearing his words, Professor Sada's tone carried a hint of amusement: "So you're here for those Paradox Pokémon, huh? Seems like you really like them, Turo and I are very pleased."

"However, I suppose those Paradox Pokémon are hiding in inconspicuous places at this time?"

"Their living environment is from the distant ancient past, with vastly different living habits compared to modern Pokémon, some Paradox Pokémon prefer to be active at night. Perhaps when it gets dark, you'll be able to see them."

"I see..." D nodded thoughtfully: "I got it, thank you very much, Professor Sada."

Professor Sada: "After traveling all day, you must be tired, right? I won't disturb your rest then. I'm looking forward to which Paradox Pokémon you'll capture, bring it to the lab for us to see!"

"Alright."

After the communication ended, D released Dragonite and Pawmot from the Poke Ball.

He said to a few Pokémon: "You can go play outside for a while, but don't run too far."

Having come to this place for the first time, the two Pokémon couldn't help but feel curious.

So with permission granted, they immediately ran outside.

Seeing this, D felt a bit uneasy, afraid these two flyers would run wild everywhere, so he sent Koraidon to watch over them.

The three Pokémon left, leaving D alone at the research station to organize the items he had brought.

First were the essential pot and ingredients, which D took out from his travel backpack and placed aside, readying to cook a simple dinner later.

Then there were some clothes for changing and several empty red and white balls, along with some basic observation tools.

Like a compass for direction and a telescope for viewing distances, among others.

These were especially prepared by him for this trip.

Once everything was organized, D began preparing tonight's dinner.

Traveling outside made everything inconvenient, so for dinner he chose the simple yet nutritious sandwich.

At this time, the three Pokémon returned to the observation station, drawn by the smell.

D looked at them and asked, "Any discoveries?"

Pawmot's eyes sparkled: "Pawmo, Pawmo Pawmo!" This place is beautiful!

Dragonite wore an eager expression, sensing several strong presences nearby—surely powerful Pokémon reside here!

Koraidon felt it too, deliberately releasing some aura to prevent them from recklessly approaching.

The Pokémon kept chattering away, and while listening, D passed the sandwiches over.

Using their claws, they grabbed the sandwiches, devouring them in large bites, displaying a blissful expression.

After dinner, the outside sky gradually turned dark.

The Giant Pit at night was silent, occasionally accompanied by rustling sounds emanating from unknown places.

Unable to sleep, D took his telescope and went outside the observation station to search for the Paradox Pokémon Professor Sada mentioned, which appear during the night.

Today's weather was excellent, even at night, visibility remained very high.

An orb-like moon hung in the night sky, its soft moonlight cascading through the clouds, enveloping the Paldea Giant Pit in a veil of mist.

Koraidon and other Pokémon guarded beside D.

D's gaze reached far through the telescope, slowly moving it, and the cliff view opposite unfolded like a painting before him.

"Are those Phanpy and Donphan? They look quite cute sleeping under the rocks."

"As expected, Corviknight is out and about, the actual Corviknight looks really cool!"

"Hey? Is that Deino? A pseudo-legendary Pokemon from the Unova Region! What's it doing?"

Through the telescope, the Deino briskly emerged from the cave behind it, lowering its head as it sniffed all around, occasionally biting the grass on the ground, then spitting it out with a "phew phew."

"A Deino with the Sap Sipper Ability, huh!"

D was amused by this scene.

Deino is quite rare, evolved Hydreigon being both powerful and fierce, maybe he should consider raising one?

D fell into contemplation.

And just then, through the telescope, he noticed something strange suddenly appear near the Deino.

The Donphan and Phanpy resting nearby suddenly and hastily awakened, turning around and scattering away.

Deino, too, seemed to sense some terrifying presence, suddenly becoming alarmed and attempting to flee.

But before it managed a few steps, a shadowy black silhouette eclipsed over it.

D was momentarily stunned, then shifted his view upward, where he saw a rapidly zooming crimson figure.

Enormous body, crimson eyes, menacing claws, and a pair of fully spread wings behind, casting a vast shadow like a blood-red moon.

Suddenly, a peculiar roar echoed throughout the valley.

The brutal Pokémon swooped down towards Deino viciously, hunting and devouring the Deino below.

Under the moonlight, Roaring Moon's bloodstained ivory fangs appeared exceptionally terrifying.

It was engaging in the most primitive hunting and feeding!

"Damn!"

D widened his eyes, feeling his worldview collapse as his heart pounded.

"Is this a Paradox Pokémon?"

At this moment, D roughly understood the meaning behind the words from the game by Ai the researcher, "The exuberant life force of Paradox Pokémon will ruin the modern ecosystem."

With savage Pokémon like Roaring Moon, should they venture outside the Giant Pit, D couldn't dare to imagine what could happen!

---

---

---

---

# 234: Chapter 231: The Mysterious Creature Called Roaring Tail (4k)

D had a look of shock on his face, prompting Pawmot beside him to appear puzzled.

It moved closer, wanting to see what was happening over there, but D covered its eyes.

Pawmot asked, confused: "Paw?"

D shook his head: "Children shouldn't look."

The scene just now seemed to him like the Nergigante in Monster Hunter devouring small herbivorous dinosaurs.

Saying so, D again looked through the telescope toward the opposite hillside.

The figure of Roaring Moon was nowhere in sight; it seemed the hunt was over and the Pokemon had left.

The surroundings returned to a silent calm.

After Roaring Moon had left, the group of Donphan and Phanpy cautiously returned to the large rock where they had been sleeping.

"It seems that Roaring Moon is at the top of the ecological hierarchy in this area," D murmured.

What's more intriguing is the feeling that Roaring Moon gave him, completely different from other Pokemon.

Like a primal creature from ancient times, not yet fully awakened, full of wildness and ferocity.

Comparatively, modern Pokemon have evolved to be more complete than Ancient Paradox Pokemon.

Living with humans over the years, they've evolved to be more harmonious for coexistence with humans.

Their character differences are as vast as those between ancient wildmen living in dense jungles and modern office workers living in cities.

Not just Roaring Moon, if the setting holds true, other Paradox Pokemon are also far from benevolent.

"Indeed, primordial!"

Suddenly, D lost interest in further observation.

He felt he could no longer view these Paradox Pokemon with the same stereotypes he held for normal Pokemon.

Just then, intense explosions erupted from the deeper valley below.

Snapping out of his thoughts upon hearing the commotion, D quickly ran to the edge of the cliff with a few Pokemon, peering down from the fence.

Amid the blazing light, a group of weak Pokemon was scrambling in panic.

Suddenly, a beam of heat swept across them, igniting nearby trees and grass in an instant, creating flames that blocked the Raichu's path.

One Raichu raised its head in anger, looking at the sky, electric currents crackling furiously on its cheeks before transforming into a spark shooting upwards.

But it missed.

D moved the telescope.

In the night sky, he saw a mysterious Pokemon radiating fiery light, with red horns on its head, a tail shimmering with silver metallic luster, and three pairs of radiant wings floating behind it.

"Iron Moth… the futuristic form of Volcarona…" D thought of the Pokemon's name: "Is it feeding again?"

No, it doesn't quite look like that!

Like a UFO, the Iron Moth tightly pursued the group of Raichu from above, occasionally using heat beams to force them to move, seemingly trying to herd them somewhere while observing their behavior.

At this point, the Raichu were already driven to the edge of a waterfall.

Iron Moth's glow intensified, and then its wings fully spread, transforming into a deity of flame, gathering an overpowering heat ray.

As the Raichu watched this scene.

Perhaps, like humans facing crises, they too could unleash unprecedented power.

The Raichu suddenly emitted intense sparks.

Electric Type Energy resonated, storm clouds converging above, and in the next second, a thick lightning bolt struck directly at Iron Moth.

Iron Moth's attack was interrupted, smoke billowing like a damaged machine, then it plunged into the unfathomable Giant Pit below.

The crisis was averted, and the Raichu panted heavily, not staying for long, quickly running off into the complex terrain and disappearing.

Seeing this, D couldn't help but breathe a sigh of relief.

"Thankfully, these modern Pokemon aren't entirely defenseless to resist."

The scene below was witnessed by Pawmot and Dragonite, and seeing their expressions, D seriously warned: "Did you see? It's dangerous here, be careful not to run around recklessly."

"Paw!" Pawmot nodded repeatedly.

Compared to the wild aura of Ancient Paradox Pokemon, these futuristic forms seemed more troublesome.

Because the ancient type's intentions are easier to guess—the primitive hunting instincts.

Whereas the image of futuristic Pokemon, merging high-tech metal with biological traits, presents a chilling vibe, making it hard to discern their intentions.

"Let's call it a day. Tomorrow we must continue our journey."

D no longer wanted to watch, so he packed the telescope and headed back to the observation post.

*

The next morning, the Paldea Giant Pit regained its holy appearance full of light.

Seeing the breathtaking scene bathed in glow, it was hard to believe that it would transform into a brutal and terrifying hunting ground by night.

D packed up his belongings, slung his travel backpack, and set off again.

By noon, they arrived at a forest covered in colorful crystals.

Looking at the roots completely encased by crystals, D murmured quietly: "It seems the crystallization of energy has also affected these trees."

---

But speaking of which, the Terastalization of Pokemon seems to follow the same principle.

Pokemon undergoing Terastalization will be enveloped by a layer of special crystals, causing their bodies to shine like cut gems, emitting a dazzling glow.

According to D's understanding, the occurrence of Terastalization is closely related to the Tera Energy seeping from the ground in the Paldea Region.

Could it be Terapagos that is seeping out this energy? D speculated wildly.

But unfortunately, the DLC of the time he crossed into hasn't been released yet, leaving him as clueless about all this as the locals of this world.

But based on the information provided in the game, could it be that Terapagos is down here?

Thinking of this, D reached out and touched the energy crystals on the tree, faintly sensing a powerful force from within.

Koraidon guarded nearby, tilting its head to gaze at its reflected image in the crystals.

Suddenly, it saw something flying from behind through the crystal.

"Ah gasi!"

Koraidon immediately became alert, turned around, and swiped its claw, sending the thing flying away.

The opposing entity crashed face-first into a tree, awkwardly sliding down the trunk onto the grass.

"Hmm? What's that?"

D immediately stood up, his right hand gripping the two Poke Balls in his pocket.

"Ah gasi..." Koraidon shook its head, indicating it didn't know either.

After seeing the appearance of the mysterious creature, D was instantly stunned.

Jigglypuff? No! Not Jigglypuff! It's the ancient species Scream Tail of Jigglypuff!

Thinking of the ferocious Roaring Moon seen last night, D immediately became vigilant: "Koraidon, stay alert!"

"Ah gasi!"

As the words fell, the Scream Tail that had slid down the trunk staggered to its feet, angrily turned around, with a red mark across its cute little face that seemed like it was run over by a wheel.

"Owooowooooo!"

Scream Tail emitted a fierce wildness, jumping wildly and issuing a lofty battle cry, akin to a gorilla from ancient times beating its chest and challenging enemies.

Then it charged wildly at Koraidon.

Koraidon looked baffled, raised its hand to prepare to swat this savage little thing away.

However, the Scream Tail suddenly opened its mouth, revealing sharp fangs, and bit tightly onto Koraidon's paw.

"Ah gasi!"

Koraidon was shocked, quickly swinging its arm trying to fling Scream Tail away.

However, the Scream Tail bit tightly, refusing to let go no matter how Koraidon shook.

Seeing this, D also stepped forward and pulled at Scream Tail's body, trying to detach it.

For a moment, Scream Tail was stretched long but still wouldn't let go.

D pulled off a few tufts of its fur, falling onto the grass.

"Use Flamethrower to burn it!" Unable to pull it off, D immediately ordered to set it aflame.

Koraidon immediately used Flamethrower.

Scream Tail was instantly engulfed in scorching flames, letting out a shriek and finally willing to open its mouth.

Scream Tail dropped to the ground with its fur aflame, madly running around.

When the flames finally extinguished, Scream Tail erupted with a powerful wave of Psychic energy.

It screamed furiously: "Roar, oowooo!"

Afterward, a physical Mind Wave blasted out from it toward Koraidon.

Seeing this, D knew this conflict couldn't end peacefully.

Without hesitation, he gave Koraidon a command: "Use Flare Blitz!"

Dragon Type Moves are ineffective against Scream Tail, and Fighting Moves deal only a quarter damage. Rather than continuing the tussle, it's better to go all out.

"Swish!"

A surge of scorching flames erupted from Koraidon, which enveloped its whole body, transforming it into a fiery chariot charging straight at Scream Tail.

Perhaps the difference in their strengths was too great; Scream Tail's Psyshock was instantly shattered by Koraidon.

The next second, they collided.

With a loud bang, Scream Tail was sent flying, with the immense impact breaking a big tree as it fell to the ground, losing its ability to fight.

The flames on Koraidon receded into its body.

D stepped forward, giving Koraidon a bottle of Berry Milk with a [Large Stamina Recovery] effect.

Koraidon took it, guzzling the milk down in one go.

D stepped forward to observe the defeated Scream Tail.

"Apart from the large ponytail on its forehead, it doesn't seem much different from a regular Jigglypuff." D recalled the appearance of Jigglypuff, carefully comparing the two.

"No, Jigglypuff doesn't seem to have such sharp little fangs, nor is its size as large as Scream Tail."

This Scream Tail must be at least a meter tall, almost twice the size of Jigglypuff.

At this time, the revitalized Koraidon approached, looking at the Scream Tail with a hint of puzzlement.

"Ah gasi?"

"This Pokemon is too savage." D said.

But compared to the ferocious Roaring Moon from last night, this Scream Tail is much better.

Should he try to capture it?

After contemplating for a while, D took an empty Poke Ball from his backpack and pressed it against the forehead of the Scream Tail.

The next moment, Scream Tail's body transformed into red light, getting absorbed into the Poke Ball.

"Thunk thunk!"

The Poke Ball dropped on the ground, trembling violently while the central button flashed brightly red.

Once, twice, thrice...

It seemed the Pokemon inside struggled fiercely.

Shortly, the ball's shaking reduced, until it completely stopped, and the red light ceased to flash.

This signifies a successful capture.

"Succeeded?"

D picked up the Poke Ball, hesitated a moment, and pressed the button aiming at the empty space beside him.

A white light flashed, and the Scream Tail, awakened by capture, reappeared before him and Koraidon.

"Roar oowooo!"

Even in a state of severe weakness, Scream Tail still displayed violence and ferocity.

It opened its mouth, showing sharp little fangs, levitating in the air toward D, intending to attack him.

But before it could get close, Koraidon swatted it away.

"Roar oowoooah ah ah!"

Scream Tail jumped up frantically, howling loudly.

D was irritated by the sharp noise, picking up the Poke Ball to recall the still aggressive Scream Tail.

The surroundings returned to peace, D looked down at the Poke Ball, contemplating:

"Indeed, Poke Balls have no mind-control function... Even capturing a Pokemon, it can't be made entirely obedient."

He reflected on Scream Tail's behavior, suddenly feeling amused:

"No wonder it's the ancient species Scream Tail, being a Fairy Type and Psychic Type Pokemon, yet rarely using Psybeam, preferring physical and bite attacks instead."

"Such behavior indeed resembles a primal savage, fitting its identity. It's a shame, being so cute yet so wild and violent."

The next step is how to handle this Scream Tail.

D couldn't help but ponder deeply.

Obviously, if he can't completely tame Scream Tail, then it's absolutely impossible to bring it back to the ranch.

With its current character, if he and Koraidon aren't vigilant, the ranch might see several Pokemon losing their lives.

For instance, Alcremie being eaten, chickens in the Chicken House having their necks bitten off at night, Dolliv, and Glaceon might also get attacked.

Thinking of this, D couldn't help but sigh.

Ah, this is really troublesome!

---

---

---

---

# 235: Chapter 232: Paradise, Book of Scarlet and Violet, Attempting to Tame?

At noon, D smoothly arrived at the second observation station.

The setup here was almost indistinguishable from the first observation station.

D glanced around, then set down his backpack, planning to take a short break with Koraidon and other Pokemon here, and continue after filling up.

"Dragonite, Pawmot, everyone come out!"

D took out two Poke Balls, and with two flashes of white light, Pawmot and Dragonite appeared in the observation station.

But looking at the last Poke Ball in his hand, D hesitated a bit.

After pondering for a moment, he ultimately decided not to release Scream Tail.

With lunch tools all prepared, just as D was about to show off his culinary skills, the communicator within the observation station suddenly rang.

D froze and went forward to connect it; Professor Sada's voice came from within.

"I saw the notification that the second observation station was activated, it seems you've successfully arrived there. How was the journey? Was it safe? Did you capture any Paradox Pokemon?" Professor Sada asked with interest.

"It's been relatively safe." D truthfully reported his experiences from last night until now, Roaring Moon, Iron Moth, Scream Tail...

At the end, D tentatively asked: "Professor, don't you think these Paradox Pokemon aren't really suitable to live in the same place as modern Pokemon?"

Professor Sada was clever; she immediately caught D's implication.

Her face, full of spirit, revealed a thoughtful expression:

"From our current research, not all Paradox Pokemon are that dangerous."

"Moreover, from you, we've seen new possibilities, which indicate they too can live harmoniously in this world like modern Pokemon alongside humans."

D was puzzled, something seen from him?

But thinking carefully, Koraidon indeed seemed to be a Paradox Pokemon summoned by the Time Machine.

Thinking of this, D looked at his Koraidon.

Professor Sada smiled slightly and continued: "This means Paradox Pokemon can be tamed, if they integrate into this world, it will bring new opportunities to Trainers in the Paldea Region."

"The Pokedex ecosystem will expand, Trainers will become familiar with more Pokemon, and one day, we'll be able to ride alongside Paradox Pokemon across the vast Paldea Land."

"This is our dream; we want to create a paradise where all Pokemon can coexist peacefully, live together without violence or conflict."

D was speechless, not knowing what to say for a moment.

He could feel the intense desire and emotion from Professor Sada's tone.

"A paradise?" D asked.

Professor Sada's lips curled: "You'll soon be able to see it in person; I will be waiting for you in the paradise."

D: "..."

For some reason, he felt that Professor Sada was somewhat mad, how eerie!

"Oh, right." At this moment, Professor Sada seemed to think of something else, and said: "I left a Book of Scarlet and Violet in the drawer of the desk at the second observation station. If you want to learn more about Paradox Pokemon, take a look at that book!"

After saying that, Professor Sada hung up the phone.

D froze for a moment, then walked towards the dust-covered desk.

He opened the drawer, and sure enough, saw a slightly yellowed book inside.

On the cover, the words "Book of Scarlet and Violet" were prominently printed with red and purple paint.

D raised his head to look at the three Pokemon standing in front of him, and asked: "Hungry now?"

"Pawmot!"

"Agahsia!"

"Oowoo~"

The three Pokemon shook their heads.

"Good, then wait for me a bit longer." D sat on the simple metal bed, and began flipping through the Book of Scarlet and Violet he held.

"I am Heath, today is July 8, League Year 002; to explore the mysterious Paldea Giant Pit, I organized an Observation Team and set out towards the depths of the Pit."

"The Paldea Giant Pit is a dangerous terrain with almost no nearby towns for resupply; to enter there, my Cyclizar and I acted together, Helping Hand the Observation Team in transporting supplies."

"With Cyclizar's help, the Observation Team managed to enter the Pit successfully."

"At first, the Pokemon found at the upper layers of the Pit showed no difference with other Pokemon in the Paldea Region, but as the Observation Team delved deeper, strange and inexplicable sights gradually began to unfold before us."

"We found strange markings on the ground resembling an array, but based solely on its appearance, it's impossible to confirm if it's the work of explorers from the Imperial era."

"With doubt in mind, we ventured further and finally reached the depths of the Pit; my goodness! There are so many sparkling crystals here!"

"Yet at this moment, the Observation Team was suddenly attacked by a Pokemon resembling Donphan but wilder, Damn it! Several Observation Team members sustained fatal injuries!"

"The Observation Team's medical skills were insufficient, two brave team members perished, we gathered together to mourn, we will forever remember their names."

"The Observation Team continued onward, for some reason, upon reaching the deepest part, I briefly got separated from the other team members."

---

"I lost consciousness and fell into a coma. Strangely, during the coma, I vaguely felt that I was having a conversation with someone unknown, and in an unconscious state, I wrote down a large number of notes."

"Later, the comatose me was discovered and rescued by other Observation Team members. This expedition ended embarrassingly, and we planned to share our findings with the world."

"If you want to know more, keep reading!"

Filled with curiosity, D turned to the next page.

There, he saw the sketch of Scream Tail.

Below the drawing was the record observed by Heath.

"This is a mysterious creature named Scream Tail. Although its appearance is cute, its temperament is extremely aggressive. According to the investigation by the observational personnel, as soon as anyone approaches this mysterious creature, it will immediately initiate an attack."

Continuing to flip through, D saw the sketches of other Paradox Pokemon such as Koraidon, Miraidon, Great Tusk, Brute Bonnet, Roaring Moon, Walking Wake, and Iron Valiant.

Notably, he noticed Heath's special mark on the page about Roaring Moon.

"Judging by the behavioral patterns of this creature, it's speculated that its ferocious nature surpasses even that of Salamence. Even if encountered accidentally, it's crucial to avoid contact."

It was evident that Heath's Observation Team suffered significant losses when facing Roaring Moon.

"Aga hiss?"

Seeing D engrossed in the book, Koraidon couldn't resist bringing its large head closer to read with him.

D looked at Koraidon and then turned to the page documenting Koraidon.

"This mysterious creature's appearance is very similar to Cyclizar, but its temperament is even more resilient and fierce."

"The first time we saw it, it cracked the ground below with its fist. Such terrifying power—it surpasses all the dangerous creatures the Observation Team has encountered before!"

"We thought this expedition would end and the Observation Team would be wiped out, but this mysterious creature didn't attack us."

"Undoubtedly, it is wary of humans, yet its curiosity seems to be very strong. After observing us for some time, it even actively came to frolic."

"Such close interaction allowed us to further understand this mysterious creature. It occupies a top position in this area's ecosystem, perhaps because it is confident in its strength, allowing it to not immediately attack the non-threatening Observation Team."

"This is the confidence of an absolute strong creature. In the area it inhabits, there are almost no signs of other predators."

"Moreover, its temperament is very changeable, and like Cyclizar, it can transform into multiple forms."

"During the observation of this mysterious creature, we saw it use its strong legs to push off the ground, jumping over 20 meters high, then spread its wings to crash into the ground."

"Due to this ecological habit, the Observation Team named it King of Wings."

"King of Wings," D pushed Koraidon's large head away and silently pondered the content of the book.

He remembered that the Book of Scarlet and Violet sold on the market was incomplete. Besides being edited, some crucial parts had been obscured with ink marks.

From his perspective, Heath's written Book of Scarlet and Violet is an invaluable research material for the Paldea Region.

Unfortunately, because the content is too bizarre, these stories ended up being treated as urban legends, and the Book of Scarlet and Violet gradually found its way into the second-hand book market.

But there's another matter that's quite concerning.

It's well-known that Heath entered the Giant Pit 200 years ago, and from his Book of Scarlet and Violet, Paradox Pokemon were already emerging within the Paldea Giant Pit at that time.

Yet, Professor Sada and Professor Turo claimed these Paradox Pokemon were summoned by them using the Time Machine.

"Could it be that Professor Sada's summoning of Paradox Pokemon via the Time Machine affected time previously?" D couldn't help but speculate wildly.

But isn't this too paradoxical?

Or does the Paldea Giant Pit connect to two special worlds, allowing those Paradox Pokemon to come here because of Tera Energy even without the Time Machine?

D's head hurt from thinking and eventually he lay down flat in frustration.

At this moment, Dragonite's stomach suddenly emitted a growling sound.

D immediately sat up and looked at it.

Dragonite looked innocent, "Roar..."

"Sigh!" D sighed, stood up, and said, "Hang on, I'll go prepare food for you all!"

"Roar!" Dragonite immediately became joyous.

D took out a bunch of ingredients from his travel backpack. He planned to make fried eggs and fried steaks for lunch today, alongside a few Berries and bottles of MooMoo Milk, forming a nutritious and rich meal.

Heating the oil in the pan, once the oil was hot, he placed tender, firm steaks into the pan. Soon, the aroma of the steaks wafted around.

The oil turned golden brown and fragrant, with sizzling juices dancing on the steaks, making them look plump and juicy. With a bite, the juices would burst in the mouth, continuously stimulating the taste buds.

Once it was almost ready, D finally took out the Poke Ball containing Scream Tail.

Being an ancient primitive Pokemon, Scream Tail is naturally unable to interact with humans like normal Pokemon.

---

In that case, he decided to use the natural instincts of a predator, trying to tame it with food, much like ancient primitive humans tamed wild wolves into hunting dogs.

Accompanied by a flash of white light, the still-wounded Scream Tail appeared on the grass not far away.

As soon as it came out, Scream Tail let out a series of agitated howls: "Awoo awoo awoo awoo awoo!"

Koraidon: Glare!

D watched the scene unfold. Ordinarily, Scream Tail should have followed its biological instincts, avoided a confrontation with the stronger Koraidon, and turned to flee.

However, this Scream Tail behaved unexpectedly.

It showed no fear, glaring at Koraidon, then attempted to launch an attack.

Koraidon immediately lashed out, slamming Scream Tail into the dirt with a powerful claw.

Scream Tail cried out in pain, letting out a distressed, lengthy howl.

D: "..." He began to question his actions, because this Scream Tail seemed really brainless, wasting its adorable appearance.

D remained silent for a moment, then instructed Koraidon to hold the wailing Scream Tail down with its fist and placed a steak in front of it.

Scream Tail's attention was instantly drawn to the aroma, and it immediately opened its mouth, revealing those small sharp teeth, eager to tear into the steak.

Yet in the next instant, the steak was taken away.

Scream Tail bit down on air, angrily glaring at the human in front of it: "Awoo awoo awoo awoo awoo!"

D attempted to communicate with it, pointing at himself and then at the steak: "Listen to me, and I'll give you meat."

Scream Tail: "Awoo awoo awoo awoo awoo!"

D: "?"

He raised a finger, signaling: "Stop, don't howl!"

Scream Tail: "Awoo awoo awoo awoo awoo!"

It suddenly opened its mouth, lunging towards D's finger. Luckily, D was prepared and quickly withdrew his finger.

"Argh!"

Koraidon became furious, releasing its aura without reservation.

That overwhelming aura engulfed the Scream Tail like a tidal wave of terror.

Scream Tail finally began to show fear, eyes filled with alarm, emitting a wail like a small beast in distress.

"There we go." D was speechless. He looked at the Scream Tail, placed the steak in front of it, and tried to soothe its emotions: "Don't be afraid, as long as you don't attack me, we won't harm you. Eat!"

Biological instincts made Scream Tail shiver, submitting to Koraidon's oppressive might; it dared not eat.

Seeing this, Koraidon reluctantly eased up a bit: "Argh..."

Scream Tail immediately focused intensely, using sharp tiger-like teeth to tear into the steak, its eating manner wild and rough, contrasting sharply with its appearance.

D patiently waited for Scream Tail to finish, but as he approached, Scream Tail suddenly bared its teeth, growling threateningly.

"Grrr —"

"Sigh." D sighed, slightly disappointed.

This low-intelligence wild Pokemon might really be unfit for human taming?

From Scream Tail's behavior, it seemed only willing to submit to a Pokemon like Koraidon, who could overpower it.

But for D, an unarmed human, submission from the Scream Tail was absolutely impossible.

Ancient Scream Tails couldn't comprehend the relationship between Trainer and Pokemon, and naturally wouldn't see him and Koraidon as a unified entity.

D figured it out; if he wanted to tame this Scream Tail, make it submit, he'd have to conquer it with his own strength.

But the problem was, could a weak human like him defeat Scream Tail?

If this were the world of Monster Hunter, perhaps he could.

He could wear armor and wield weapons made from Koraidon's scales to hunt Scream Tail and Roaring Moon.

But unfortunately, this is the Pokemon World.

---

---

---

---

# 236: Chapter 233: Arrival at the Area Zero Research Institute! New Possibilities?

D looked at the Scream Tail from a distance.

"...Currently, capturing Paradox Pokemon is far more challenging than I imagined."

"It's not only because Paradox Pokemon are inherently fierce and aggressive, but also because they can't communicate with humans like normal Pokemon, and can't understand my intentions."

Is there any other way besides conquering it with force?

At that moment, D suddenly remembered that he once used the Water of the Reversed World from Giratina to make a series of reversing cuisine.

But another issue arose.

Though there are reversing cuisines that can change Scream Tail's personality.

But, Giratina quickly recovered last time; does this mean the cuisine's effect isn't permanent?

If he takes Scream Tail back, briefly reverses its temperament, making it obedient, but someday when he's not paying attention, Scream Tail reverts to its true nature...

D didn't dare to gamble.

He hesitated for a moment internally and then instructed Koraidon to hold tight, stepping forward to stroke Scream Tail hard.

Only after satisfying his tactile addiction did he release his grip and pressed a Poke Ball against Scream Tail, first capturing it inside, then activating the release function of the Poke Ball.

A beam of red light flashed, and Scream Tail's figure left the Poke Ball.

It growled, baring its teeth threateningly.

"Agha!"

Seeing this, Koraidon let out a loud roar, and its terrifying aura turned into a gust of wind sweeping towards Scream Tail.

Instinctively, Scream Tail immediately turned to flee and quickly disappeared from sight.

Koraidon was relieved to see this scene; it confirmed that it had never seen this Pokemon's presence on the ranch!

Meanwhile, D ran to Pawmot's side, petting his sweet-natured baby, unable to help but sigh internally: "Modern Pokemon are still cuter!"

Pawmot enjoyed the gentle stroking immensely, tilting its head back in pleasure.

Beside them, Dragonite saw this scene and also came over for a touch.

"Alright, everyone gets a pet!"

D started giving the group of Pokemon a simple massage.

After a brief rest at the second observation station, they continued onward.

This time, D didn't continue to walk on the ground; he climbed onto Koraidon, ready to ride it and break through Paldea Giant Pit, heading to the deepest part of Zone Zero.

Koraidon's descent speed was extremely fast, accompanied by the sound of wind, and the surroundings were quickly changing.

The deeper they went, the darker it became, with caves and cliffs visible everywhere, towering waterfalls spilling from above. For a moment, D felt as if he had entered another world.

At the same time, the number and scale of the strange Crystals were increasing visibly until Koraidon landed on a massive Tera Pillar.

Standing there, D saw the human buildings standing below.

Those must be the institutes built by the two professors in Zone Zero.

"Let's go! Let's head over!" D said.

Koraidon turned and looked around, its expression becoming somewhat glum, seemingly not fond of this place.

But looking at D's figure walking ahead, it still lifted its feet to follow.

"Just like in the game, Zone Zero has a lot of Crystals," D remarked, looking at the surroundings and talking to himself.

Logically, sunlight shouldn't be able to reach such a deep area.

But surprisingly, this vast hollow is so bright and spacious.

Without question, all this light comes from the colorful Crystals embedded in the ground and walls, illuminating the entire bottom layer of the Giant Pit.

As they continued forward, some rare Pokemon began to appear, difficult to encounter outside.

These were known as "Glimmet" and "Glimmora" Pokemon.

They blossomed on the Crystals, appearing mysterious and beautiful.

There was an enormous number of Glimmora; their roots adhered closely to the Crystals, as if sprouting from within.

As D and Koraidon passed by them, these Glimmora only slightly moved their petals.

"Beautiful and radiant Glimmora blooms on Tera, carrying Toxic..." This looked rather eerie.

D shivered inexplicably, deliberately bypassing the Glimmora and arriving at a winding downward tunnel.

A signpost at the entrance indicated that this passage led to the institute.

"Alright, there should be no danger on the path ahead!"

Finally reaching here, D couldn't help but breathe a sigh of relief internally.

He led Koraidon onward, but as they passed through the tunnel, they suddenly saw a mysterious Pokemon on a solitary, gigantic Tera Pillar ahead.

That Pokemon was covered in a layer of green metallic armor, with green spikes resembling thorns protruding from holes in the armor, forming a complete chain of thorns.

Wasn't that... Iron Thorns, the future variant of Tyranitar?!

D paused slightly, and as he noticed the Iron Thorns, it also noticed him, gazing over with its cold, emotionless metallic eyes.

Unexpectedly, Iron Thorns did not immediately attack like Scream Tail.

It just stood there motionlessly, its gaze shifting as D moved.

"Just as recorded, Iron Thorns has an extremely calm disposition, disliking unnecessary combat..." D murmured.

---

Just at that moment, footsteps were heard from nearby.

D turned his head to see Professor Turo, dressed in a white lab coat, walking towards him.

Smiling, Turo said to D, "I saw you on the surveillance from Zone Zero, so I came to pick you up. It's great to see you, D. Come, I'll take you and Koraidon to the research institute."

D glanced at the Iron Thorns and then walked over to stand beside Professor Turo. As he looked back, he noticed the gaze of the Iron Thorns was still fixed on him, moving as he moved.

Noticeable of this, Professor Turo explained, "That Pokemon is called Iron Thorns. Its behavior is quite different from the Roaring Moon you saw before."

"This Iron Thorns has an extremely calm temperament, doesn't like unnecessary fighting, and is a highly intelligent and smart Pokemon."

"Currently, I've found some ways to coexist peacefully with it, so it has been staying in Zone Zero for a while now."

Peaceful coexistence? This left D, who had witnessed the predatory instincts of the wild Scream Tail and the UFO-like observing of Raichu by Iron Moth, feeling very surprised.

"Peaceful coexistence?" he asked incredulously.

"Yes!" Professor Turo nodded. "However, achieving this was quite difficult. Before you came, we had to go to great lengths to prove human harmlessness to it and feed it regularly, which is why it accepted our presence."

D was baffled, "Then why can't the ancient species do the same?"

Professor Turo pondered for a moment and replied, "It might be because ancient species of Pokemon naturally possess a primal and inherent ferocity that makes them wildly untamable."

"Compared to ancient species, future Pokemon lack this wild nature. However, their unknown power has evolved to an extreme, and their thoughts and intentions are full of uncertainty."

"To us, their danger level is no less than that of the ancient Pokemon."

"For instance, there's a Pokemon called Iron Bundle, which doesn't attack humans but modifies the environment around it, turning it into an ice field."

D, whose heart had been affected by ancient species, suddenly felt a warmth. His eyes lit up, and he asked, "So, I can capture future Pokemon without worrying about them attacking me?"

"Just some of them," Professor Turo replied with a smile.

Then, as if thinking of something, his expression became serious:

"Furthermore, if you encounter a Pokemon resembling Gardevoir and Gallade, never make eye contact with it! Otherwise, it will see it as a taunt, and its extremely brutal nature will lead it to attack you without hesitation."

Professor Turo showed a look of relief: "I'm very glad it doesn't know the Move Teleport, otherwise, I wouldn't be able to stand here in front of you now."

"Is it that brutal?" D clicked his tongue.

"We didn't expect it," Professor Turo said bitterly.

Speaking of which, they, along with Koraidon, arrived in front of the research institute.

Only then did D realize that there seemed to be more researchers besides Professor Turo and Professor Sada, with many lab-coated individuals around.

At the moment, two people were at the door with telescopes and notebooks, observing the distant Iron Thorns.

They sounded very excited: "Oh wow! Look at the gaze of that Iron Thorns. It seems so rational and intelligent! Surely, it hails from a far future era of Pokemon!"

"Yes indeed! The technology of that place must be far more advanced than ours!"

Upon hearing footsteps, they quickly put down the telescopes and respectfully exclaimed, "Professor!"

"Mmm." Professor Turo nodded.

The doors of the research institute slowly opened, and D followed Turo inside.

Before the doors closed, he again heard the two researchers whisper:

"That Koraidon is number one, isn't it?"

"I can't believe Koraidon can be so obedient. It really likes that person!"

"Indeed! But Koraidon and Miraidon are quite unique, different from other Paradox Pokemon, or else the professor wouldn't have formed a relationship with them so quickly."

Gradually, their voices faded away until they were no longer audible.

Visiting such a place for the first time, D inexplicably felt a bit tense. Turning his head, he noticed that Koraidon was also quite nervous.

D rarely saw Koraidon like this, so he instantly felt less tense. He reached out and patted Koraidon's head, mouthing the words, "Don't be afraid!"

Koraidon made no sound but rubbed its head against the palm of his hand.

At this moment, Professor Turo led them into a spacious room.

The room was filled with various green plants, and a winding stream flowed through the trees.

Flowers bloomed on the grass, and an artificial sun hung from the ceiling, providing light and warmth to the vegetation.

This was like a small Eco Garden!

The area extended far into the distance, but D noticed a clear dividing line not far away, assuming it was using special projection.

Professor Sada was standing on the grass at the moment.

Not far in front of her lay a blue-purple Pokemon, calm and cool in demeanor.

That was… Miraidon!

As if sensing something, Miraidon's pupils slightly shifted, quickly locking onto the suddenly appearing Koraidon.

Upon seeing this Miraidon, Koraidon immediately became alarmed, looking at it with hostility and letting out a roar: "Gaaahss!"

Miraidon's expression did not change at all, appearing like an unfeeling, emotionless mechanical construct.

But the slight current that appeared on its claws demonstrated its intentions.

Seeing the two Pokemon about to fight, D quickly said, "Koraidon, stop!"

Professor Sada also stepped forward, reaching out to Miraidon while calming it: "This one is not an enemy. It lives far away and won't compete with you for this land."

Miraidon's pupils shifted, and the current on its claws disappeared.

And hearing D's voice, Koraidon also calmed down, lowering its head with a helpless look.

With no other option, D could only pat it: "Alright, it's okay."

For some reason, the scene between Koraidon and Miraidon just now felt like watching a large golden dog squabble with a cold cat.

Koraidon's mood gradually settled down.

D then stood up and looked across.

Professor Sada greeted him with a smile: "Welcome to the paradise, D."

"Long time no see, Professor," D said politely, "How's Arven doing?"

Professor Sada lightly nodded: "Since dining with you last time, Arven's relationship with us has improved significantly. He should be at the lighthouse in Cabo Poco Town taking care of Mabosstiff now."

"Alright, let's not say more here." Professor Turo checked the time and said: "It's already five ten. Time for dinner, and after that, I'll show you to your accommodation."

"Thank you very much," D said politely.

Before leaving the Eco Garden, he glanced back at Miraidon.

This Miraidon was unlike other Paradox Pokemon he had seen; it seemed to have fully adapted to humans and this world.

Professor Sada handed her work over to Turo and then led D to the dining area.

On the way, she gently said, "I'm truly sorry about your experiences along the way, but I hope you don't view all Paradox Pokemon that way."

D was slightly startled.

Speaking softly, Professor Sada continued, "They have just arrived in this unfamiliar environment and have yet to adapt to the world we live in."

"But I believe that someday, they will adapt to everything here, and like Koraidon and Miraidon, they will live with us."

---

---

---

---

# 237: Chapter 234: Terastallize, Koraidon! (4k)

The variety of food at the institute isn't extensive; it's evident that the researchers don't have a strong desire for indulgence.

Under the guidance of Professor Sada, D and Koraidon arrived at the institute's dining area.

It was mealtime, and this place was already filled with quite a few researchers holding trays, both men and women. They nodded and greeted Professor Sada upon seeing her.

D looked at this group of researchers and saw a sense of admiration in some of their eyes.

"It seems that Professor Sada and Professor Turo have a high level of prestige here! But they do indeed have the strength to back it up," D thought to himself.

Professor Sada walked with a commanding presence, responding to her team of researchers while she said to D:

"Due to limited ingredients, the dishes here might not compare to those outside; you'll have to bear with it for the next few days."

D didn't mind; he looked at Koraidon and said with a smile, "No worries, we're not picky eaters."

Professor Sada curved her lips into a slight smile, "After the meal, I'll have someone show you to your accommodation. Tomorrow morning, we'll take you on a tour of this institute."

D was very polite: "Then I'll have to trouble you a lot in the coming days!"

"No need to be so formal. By age, Turo and I are only eight years older than you, so the gap isn't that big."

Professor Sada found D to be quite interesting and continued:

"Also, if it weren't for you, we probably wouldn't have been able to quickly master the method of communicating with Paradox Pokemon."

D smiled but didn't speak. He thought that even without him, the two professors could have made Miraidon adapt to them.

Professor Sada took him to the dining area, introduced him to the chef at the window, and said: "In the next few days, you can come here to eat if you're hungry."

The bearded chef inside the window waved his ladle and flashed a welcoming smile at D and Koraidon: "Don't worry, Professor, I'll take good care of our guests!"

"Thank you for your hard work." Professor Sada nodded at him and then called over a relatively young researcher, telling him: "After your meal, show our guest to his accommodation."

"Yes!" The young researcher nodded respectfully in agreement.

After saying this, Professor Sada bid farewell to D and turned to leave.

And the young man then shot D a bright smile: "Hello, I'm the professor's assistant, Bucky. I'll be responsible for showing you around here!"

As he spoke, he looked at Koraidon and said: "This Koraidon must be number one, right?"

D was a bit puzzled: "Number one?"

Bucky explained: "It's the first Koraidon summoned by the professor. Besides number one, we also have a number two Koraidon and a number one Miraidon here at the institute, though their personalities clash, so the professor separated them."

Hearing this, D was slightly taken aback.

Does this mean there are currently two Koraidon and one Miraidon at the Area Zero Research Institute?

Wait, that doesn't seem right! How does this differ from what he knew?

According to logic, if this is a fusion world, there should be four in total, two Koraidon and two Miraidon.

If following the game's storyline, these four Pokemon would have a fierce conflict over territory, causing number one to flee the Giant Pit and be picked up by the game's protagonist.

"Could it be that the fusion of the Scarlet and Violet Version worlds caused some automatic changes in certain places?"

D was puzzled but did not show it.

He released Dragonite and Pawmot from their Poke Balls and took some Pokemon food for them before casually choosing a spot to sit down.

To his surprise, while the food for humans in the institute wasn't great, the variety of Pokemon food was quite rich.

For example, dessert poffins, the magical Pokeblock, delicious Pokemon cookies, and fresh berries, everything was available.

D guessed that the professor specially prepared these to tame the Paradox Pokemon.

Bucky was a cheerful young man, about the same age as D. As D ate, he sat across from him and shared a lot about Professor Sada and Professor Turo.

For instance, how they are incredibly talented, invented Terastal, and risked their lives to contact Paradox Pokemon.

D listened while recalling the events that happened after he met the two professors.

Putting aside their actions of summoning ancient-future Pokemon to the present for their dreams, they indeed were very good people.

Bucky's face showed yearning: "Everyone at the institute admires the professors! That's why I applied to become the professor's assistant; I also want to become a great researcher like them in the future!"

D: "..."

He was just about to say something when suddenly he heard a complaining murmur from behind.

"Really, those Paradox Pokemon are so terrifying, and Professor Sada and Professor Turo still plan to study them and let them live outside the Giant Pit. I've heard that many researchers have been attacked and lost their lives. I'm very scared; I can't die, my wife and daughter are still waiting for me at home."

Another researcher asked: "Do you want to quit?"

---

"So what can we do? Oh, I don't know what's going on up there, why suddenly restart the frozen Taile Plan and move the entire base to the Area Zero Research Institute..."

The man wanted to say something more, but Bucky stepped forward and interrupted them:

"Hey! Now is not the time to say such things! The research on Paradox Pokemon is at a critical stage, even if we lose our lives for them, it's worth it! At least we have contributed to the entire Pokemon science community!"

The two quickly shut their mouths and no longer spoke.

Bucky sat back down, grinned at D and said, "Relax, humanity has never stopped exploring, and sacrifices are inevitable in this process. It's because of the sacrifices of our predecessors that we have what we have today, so we must also try to leave something for later generations!"

D nodded, showing understanding on the surface, but secretly pondering:

"No wonder there's not a single researcher in that huge research institute in the game."

Due to an excessive lack of manpower, plus later on, the two professors became distrustful of newly recruited researchers, so they created their own AI to assist with work.

It seems signs are already there now.

D silently finished his meal and, under Bucky's guidance, went to his accommodation—a small silver-colored room, extremely simple for a person.

The room wasn't large, containing a bed, a desk, and a wardrobe, with a bathroom and restroom near the door.

The overall decor style leaned towards a cold-toned technology metal vibe, looking cold and devoid of any homely feel.

After arriving, both Dragonite, Pawmo, and Koraidon showed somewhat drooped expressions, clearly disliking the place.

Seeing this, D helplessly said, "Sorry, I didn't know it would be like this here, but I promise you, once business is finished we'll go home and never come back here again."

His curiosity about the place had been satisfied.

Moreover, the atmosphere at Area Zero Research Institute was quite strange, coupled with the overwhelming Glimmet and Glimmora outside, D felt this place was very eerie, not quite liking it.

Seeing D's expression, the Pokemon all let out gentle growls and nodded in agreement.

D smiled and patted each of them, then said, "Alright, time to rest!"

*

The next morning, Bucky came over and knocked on D's door.

Having washed up and eaten breakfast, D opened the door and went out after hearing the knock.

"Good morning!" Bucky showed a vibrant smile, "The professor wants me to take you to meet her!"

"Okay."

D nodded and followed Bucky out of the room with Koraidon.

After passing through a corridor and turning a corner, they arrived at a laboratory filled with various research instruments.

Professor Sada and Professor Turo, dressed in white lab coats, were there adjusting a machine.

Seeing D approaching, Professor Sada came forward, greeted D, and then said:

"Next, we want to do a body check on Koraidon."

"Gagrr..." Koraidon tilted its head in confusion.

Seeing this, Professor Sada smiled and said, "Don't worry, we're just checking your body to see your health status, it won't hurt you."

Koraidon looked at D.

D nodded to it, "Go on!"

Koraidon then stepped forward and stood on the machine.

Professor Sada stayed in front of Koraidon doing scans while Professor Turo turned to a large computer.

With Professor Sada's scan, Koraidon's body data immediately appeared on the computer screen.

Looking at the content, Professor Turo felt extremely surprised, "This Koraidon individual has very strong energy responses! Its strength definitely far exceeds Koraidon No. 2 and Miraidon!"

"Hmm?" Professor Sada was equally surprised.

"Clearly, this Koraidon has been cared for very well. Based on its muscle density, it's a very strong Koraidon!" Professor Turo looked admiringly at D who took care of Koraidon.

No wonder even the chief champion praises this champion-level Trainer, being able to cultivate even a Pokemon like Koraidon to such an extent!

D: "?"

At this moment, Professor Turo discovered something new, his tone showed considerable excitement: "Indeed, Koraidon is slowly adapting to this world! Its brain is highly active, indicating deeper thought processes! Just like those intelligent modern Pokemon!"

"Are you certain?" Professor Sada hurried forward to inspect it together.

Professor Turo nodded, "Compared to Koraidon No. 2 and Miraidon, that's the case, showing that Paradox Pokemon can indeed gradually adapt to this world and human presence!"

"Great!" Professor Sada seemed excited, "This is truly great!"

D quietly watched the scene.

He began to ponder whether his arrival had changed something.

"No, even without me, the two professors would sooner or later discover this situation."

After all, in the game, they never gave up their dreams until the end of their lives.

Even the AI Professor, inheriting the emotions of the two professors, eventually traveled through the Time Machine to where Paradox Pokemon lived.

At this time, Professor Sada came up in gratitude and expressed heartfelt thanks, "Thank you very much for taking care of Koraidon during this time."

D: "?"

What's the situation? The words seem to imply taking back his Koraidon?

D's heart sank, he absolutely would not allow that to happen.

However, Professor Sada didn't mean that.

She glanced at Koraidon, then at D, and continued, "It seems you've built a deep bond, well, it's about time."

D was taken aback, "What do you mean?"

Professor Sada clapped, and an assistant brought over a black orb about the size of a Poke Ball.

Professor Sada handed the orb to D, explaining, "This is the Tera Orb developed by Turo and me, from now on, it's yours. Through it, you can have Pokemon engage in Terastal battles, I'm sure you've heard of it outside."

"Ah?" D reached out and took the Tera Orb, feeling a bit incredulous, "Really giving it to me?"

"Of course, you are a superb Trainer, qualified to have it." Professor Sada smiled slightly.

D: "..."

Though he isn't, right now he can be.

Then, Professor Sada beckoned Koraidon over.

"Gagrr?"

Koraidon walked straight to D's side.

Looking at the person and Pokemon before her, Professor Sada eagerly said, "Now, let Koraidon undergo Terastal! Turo and I want to witness this moment firsthand!"

D was completely perplexed, he looked at the Tera Orb in his hand, utterly confused.

With both professors watching expectantly for quite a while, he hesitantly raised his head and asked, "How do I use this...?"

Professor Sada: "..."

Professor Turo: "..."

They exchanged smiles, then Professor Turo explained, "Just like you usually throw a Poke Ball, toss it towards the Pokemon, and as it approaches, the Tera Energy inside will automatically merge with the Pokemon."

Upon hearing this, D looked at Koraidon and then attempted to throw the black Tera Orb.

Koraidon's golden eyes fixed intently on the Tera Orb in mid-air.

As the Tera Orb approached directly overhead, it suddenly burst with dazzling Tera Energy, instantly enveloping Koraidon below, morphing into a massive crystal.

The next second, accompanied by a "crack," akin to the sound of glass shattering emanating from the crystal, the massive crystal abruptly shattered, transforming into starlight scattered around.

Within that glow, Koraidon's body immediately emitted a radiance as splendid as gemstones, as if covered in a layer of transparent crystal.

And on Koraidon's head, appeared the Tera Gem resembling a Crown. Above the Gem was a large fist formed from pure Tera Energy.

In the eyes of Professor Sada and Professor Turo was reflected Koraidon's current image.

"Successful! Koraidon can undergo Terastal too!"

"This Koraidon's Tera Type is Fighting Type! In this state, if Koraidon uses Fighting Type moves, the power of these moves will become much stronger, highly suiting Koraidon!"

---

---

---

---

# 238: Chapter 235: Homecoming (4k)

D looked at the Fighting Tera Koraidon in front of him.

Hmm, how should I put this?

Mega Evolution is really cool, and the symbolism is quite nice too.

Z Moves are equally flashy when performed.

Not to mention, Dynamax and Gigantamax have exaggerated visual effects.

But now, with Terastal here, why does it feel a bit pulled down?

Having a crystallized giant fist on top of its head looks imposing, but it always seems like it could affect the battle stance.

With something like that on their head, wouldn't Pokemon feel top-heavy?

At this moment, Koraidon noticed his gaze and couldn't help uttering a puzzled growl: "Ah-ga?"

D smiled and praised, "Very impressive!"

Having been together for so long, Koraidon has learned to discern some of D's hidden meanings in his daily words.

For example, right now, only when he's complimenting someone against his will would he show this kind of smile.

Koraidon felt a bit gloomy: "Ah-ga..."

The two professors nearby didn't understand the little gesture between the two. Their attention was entirely focused on Koraidon after Terastal.

Professor Sada began to introduce, "This is the research achievement we've obtained at the institute, Terastal is a capability that all Pokemon can use."

As she spoke, Professor Sada looked at the Tera Orb in D's hand: "After Terastal, the Tera Energy within the Tera Orb depletes, making the Tera Orb unusable."

Hearing this, D looked down and sure enough, saw that the black Tera Orb had dimmed.

"And then?" he asked.

Professor Turo picked up the conversation: "We've signed an agreement with the Paldea League. In the near future, Tera Energy replenishment devices will be established at Pokemon Centers around the world. If a trainer's Tera Orb runs out of energy, they can go to a Pokemon Center and ask Joy there to help recharge."

"However, there's another method besides this," Professor Turo asked, "On the way here, you've seen those crystallized growths on the rock walls and ground, right?"

D nodded.

Professor Turo continued, "As long as you approach those crystals and let the Tera Orb make contact with them, it can also recharge the Tera Orb."

"But these crystals are rare in the Paldea Region, so most of the time trainers need to go to a Pokemon Center."

D understood.

The two professors had more research to do. After helping Koraidon with the physical examination, they handed D over to Bucky, who would take D on a tour of the institute, and then hurriedly left.

"Let's go! I'll show you around the place!" Bucky grinned and led the way.

Seeing this, D could only symbolically follow behind him to tour the entire institute.

Just like in the game, the Area Zero Research Institute consists of more than one building, divided into different areas.

There are entertainment and dining areas for researchers to rest, an ecological zone for breeding and observing Paradox Pokemon, and a serious research institute.

Bucky took D around, and D saw a bunch of large machines he couldn't understand.

Finally, Bucky pointed at a very special building, shaped like a hexagonal crystalline tower, and said to D:

"See that? That's where the Time Machine is located! The power of science is truly amazing, even such things as a Time Machine can be built!"

Hearing this, D looked at that place, thinking: "That must be where players battle the AI Professor in the game, right?"

Koraidon, following behind him, also looked there, its expression very complex, because it was there...

"Want to take a look inside?" At this moment, Bucky suddenly asked.

D was slightly stunned, "Can I?"

Bucky grinned, "Haha, just kidding, that place is the most important place in the entire Area Zero Research Institute. Usually, aside from the professors and a few researchers, even we aren't allowed in there!"

D: "..."

Bucky gazed at it with longing, "It's that Time Machine bringing ancient and future Pokemon to our world!"

"The Time Machine is always running, more Pokemon will come through it into our world!"

"The professors plan to build a paradise for them, and not only that, they've tasked Koraidon and Miraidon with guarding this place, protecting the Time Machine from being destroyed."

D quietly listened to these words, and hearing this, he suddenly spoke, "Paradise Guardian Dragon?"

"Huh?" Bucky seemed surprised, after thinking carefully, his eyes lit up, "That nickname is very suitable for Koraidon and Miraidon!"

D: "..." Oh well.

The research institute tour came to an end, and after lunch, D began trying to develop Terastalization for Pawmot and Dragonite.

Fortunately, the Tera Energy at Area Zero Research Institute is abundant, so even without approaching those crystals, it can be replenished.

After a series of attempts, he successfully learned the Tera Type of the two Pokemon.

"Pawmot is Electric Tera, Dragonite is Flying Tera, both are their original types..."

"Both Pokemon were previously empowered by food, Lightning Steamed Bun enhanced Pawmot's electric ability, Hurricane Steamed Bun strengthened Dragonite's airflow, combined with same-type Tera, after Terastalization they become even stronger."

---

"Hmm, overall, quite good."

If two Pokemon were to battle together, they could form the "Thunder God and Lightning Mother" duo.

Unfortunately, the ranch doesn't require battles, and no matter how strong they are, there's no use for those skills here.

D pondered carefully for a moment and then turned to look at Pawmot and Dragonite, who were holding some Poffins to eat, a look of relief appearing on his face.

All this was their own choice, not because they could only live on the ranch, but because they liked it.

If, when first arriving in this world, D entertained thoughts of traveling around like Ash in the anime.

Then now, he just wants to nest in the ranch with the Pokemon, stay at home, watch the changing seasons outside, and pass every spring, summer, autumn, and winter together.

Thinking of this, a sense of homesickness rose silently in D's heart.

Unknowingly, it had been three days since he left the ranch, and he wondered how everyone there was doing.

The matters at the research institute were resolved; though he didn't manage to capture the Paradox Pokemon he wanted, compared to the uncontrollable Paradox Pokemon, the Pokemon back at the ranch were far more important.

So, it was time for them to go home!

Thus, that afternoon, D found Professor Sada and Professor Turo, telling them of his plans to leave the Area Zero Research Institute the next morning and return to the ranch.

After hearing this, Professor Sada was slightly taken aback: "Not staying a few more days here?"

"No, Koraidon is a bit homesick." D smiled and shook his head in refusal.

Upon hearing this, Professor Sada glanced at Koraidon beside her.

She nodded gently: "If that's the case, we won't keep you any longer; when you have the chance next time, you can visit our research institute again, and we will make you feel at home!"

Professor Turo also nodded slightly: "By that time, our research might already have achieved new results, and we'll surely have a way to communicate normally and coexist peacefully with these Pokemon."

Professor Sada: "Absolutely, when that time comes, you can bring them back to the ranch worry-free."

They both knew that D liked Paradox Pokemon, and one of the reasons he came to the Giant Pit was to capture them.

But because those Pokemon had overly aggressive personalities, hadn't adapted to the presence of humans, and couldn't communicate, he had to release the Paradox Pokemon he liked...

Hearing these words, D was slightly taken aback.

He could feel the intentions of the two professors.

One has to admit, if they didn't get fixated on details, they were truly competent Pokemon Doctors.

But, perhaps smart people just like to get fixated on details?

D sighed inwardly, knowing full well his persuasion would be futile given their obsession with Paradox Pokemon, and he simply asked:

"Arven is almost school age, right?"

Professor Sada nodded with a smile: "There's still two years, and then we'll send him to Paldea Academy for his studies."

D: "Eight years old... I remember when I was Arven's age, my mom said if she didn't keep an eye on me for a day, I'd be rolling around on the ground. Compared to me, Arven is really impressive!"
