Professor Sada and Professor Turo, being smart people, immediately grasped the hidden meaning in these words.

D was implying that children of this age need their parents' guidance and company.

Regarding such comments, the two professors did not feel offended, but rather pleasantly surprised and unexpected.

"Looks like D really likes Arven!" Professor Sada thought, otherwise why would he be hoping for them as parents to spend more time with their child?

Professor Sada suddenly realized she hadn't seen Arven for several weeks.

Her heart stirred slightly, wanting to go back to see Arven.

However... the research was currently at a critical stage, and they couldn't leave.

She smiled slightly and nodded: "I think you will become a great father in the future."

D was taken aback, then replied helplessly: "I temporarily have no plans to get married."

Professor Turo laughed and said: "That day will come eventually; if we were around the same age, I think we'd have been very good friends."

"Alright then." Professor Sada looked at Koraidon and said: "The ecological research on Koraidon is complete, once you return, you don't need to provide us with data on Koraidon anymore."

Koraidon, however, didn't look at her any longer, focusing all attention on D.

Seeing this, Professor Sada continued: "It's apparent it really likes you, from now on, live well together!"

D nodded slightly.

Seeing that Professor Sada understood his intent, he didn't say anything more; he merely sighed inwardly, hoping that familial bonds might pull the two professors back a bit!

Yet, D felt the hope was slim.

It was indeed a pity for Arven.

After bidding farewell to the two professors, D departed the next morning, dressed neatly, riding on Koraidon out of the Area Zero Research Institute.

This time they didn't slowly retrace their steps but flew directly towards the top of the Giant Pit.

D sat on Koraidon's back, looking down at the Area Zero Research Institute, which was gradually receding.

Afterwards, they should never come back to this place.

Neither he nor Koraidon and the other Pokemon really liked the atmosphere of this place.

---

In contrast, the peaceful and tranquil world outside is more suitable for them.

After all, there are friendly and intelligent modern Pokémon there, and compared to Roaring Moon, D feels that even the notoriously fierce Salamence is so gentle and cute.

Koraidon's flight speed is extremely fast. It quickly broke through the mid-level of the Giant Pit, reached the upper level, and then broke out of the Paldea Giant Pit, returning to the outside world.

Looking at the misty Paldea Giant Pit, D suddenly felt that this giant pit seemed to be smashed out by something.

Recalling the revelations about Terapagos he had seen before his journey, Terapagos lying on a giant planet, with other scattered planets around and the background of the cosmos visible.

Because of that picture, many people even think it's from outer space like Eternatus.

If this speculation is true...

"Ha, thinking about it this way, the space of the Pokémon World is really populated with cosmic beings," D thought to himself.

Triggering the Darkest Day and endlessly absorbing and releasing massive energy, Eternatus, now adding a Terapagos.

It feels like Rayquaza might be overwhelmed...

D carefully recalled the story of Rayquaza and suddenly realized that this Sky God didn't seem so diligent either.

In the Emerald version, when Groudon and Kyogre were fighting, players still had to call it.

In the Pokémon Rescue Team, it was also because a meteorite was about to destroy the Earth, and players and other partner Pokémon climbed into its home to call it, and it still didn't believe it.

The only time it took the initiative was when the alien Pokémon Deoxys descended on this planet, but it didn't manage to prevent it...

It's just unknown if there's only one Rayquaza in this world, Dragonite had seen a black one before.

While pondering, the Paldea Giant Pit had already disappeared far behind them.

D looked back but saw nothing, just a vast blue sky with floating white clouds.

Looking at this beautiful scenery, D swept away the gloom left by the Paradox Pokémon and couldn't help but feel happy.

Coincidentally, there was a flock of migrating Kilowattrel flying ahead, and D greeted them happily: "Good morning, Kilowattrel!"

The Kilowattrel were startled, turning to look at this strange human.

Some Kilowattrel couldn't brake in time and crashed straight into companions flying ahead, prompting an angry growl from the hit Kilowattrel who pecked the offender's head hard.

Watching this scene, D chuckled.

Indeed, this is the Pokémon World he knows!

"Be careful flying high up! Bye-bye!"

D waved at the flock of Kilowattrel and continued the journey with Koraidon.

Before noon, the familiar Porto Marinada Town finally appeared below.

Looking at the vast fields on the ground, D couldn't help but anticipate.

Soon, Koraidon landed in the pasture.

D jumped off it and, looking around at all the familiar things, couldn't help but sigh: "There's no place like home!"

Before he could turn around, he heard an excited "Mama".

D turned back and saw a large group of Pokémon excitedly running towards him.

Watching this scene, he couldn't help but grin, stepping forward to touch this one and kiss that one, then announced: "Everyone, we're back!"

Hearing the commotion, Cyclizar also ran out.

It looked at the pumpkins in the field yet to mature, then at D in front, and was instantly dumbfounded.

D is back? But the pumpkins aren't mature yet? Could it be that Cyclizar is so longing that it's hallucinating?

Cyclizar rubbed its eyes incredulously.

It opened its eyes, and D did not disappear, instead walking toward it.

Cyclizar had a dumbfounded face: "Gao?"

D laughed: "What's wrong? Don't recognize me after a few days?"

At this moment, Cyclizar finally confirmed it was D who had returned.

It showed a silly smile on its face.

"Gao!"

That's great!

---

---

---

---

# 239: Chapter 236: Kricketune and the Band

After calming the group of Pokemon that gathered around, D returned to the house with his bag in hand, and sat on the sofa, letting out a deep sigh.

Seeing D return, the Indeedee Sibling felt very happy.

The sister Indeedee placed a cup of tea in front of D, then turned and took two photos from the table.

D raised his eyebrow slightly, looking at the small claw prints on the back of the photos; it was obvious who sent them back.

He reached out to take the photos, casually asking, "When were these sent back?"

The sister Indeedee gestured a three and a one: "Ai-ai."

D understood, one photo was sent three days ago, and the other one day ago.

This time, what kind of photos would they be?

D flipped through the two photos with interest.

On the first photo, he saw a vast, boundless ocean; above the sea was a blue sky dotted with fluffy white clouds.

In the middle of the photo were Mew and Teddiursa, their faces looking troubled, seemingly vexed about not finding land to stand on.

Seeing this, D was quite surprised.

He remembered the last time these two Pokemon sent a letter, they were over in the Orange Archipelago.

They should have yet to leave the vicinity of the Orange Archipelago these days.

Although D had never been to the Orange Archipelago, he could imagine the landscape and environment there.

Everywhere is boundless ocean, with scattered islands forming the Orange Archipelago.

Clearly, Teddiursa and Mew were lost, and from their expressions, they hadn't found an island to rest on for quite some time.

"Two poor little guys..."

D's heart was eager to know what happened next, so he continued to look at the second photo.

The backdrop of the photo was still a vast ocean.

However, Mew and Teddiursa's location had shifted from the air to the sea surface.

They were standing on the back of a Lapras, riding it across the sea.

And in front of Lapras, appeared a lush green island.

Perhaps they finally found a resting spot, as both Mew and Teddiursa wore excited smiles.

Whatever they might have communicated to the Lapras, it also turned its head, looking warmly and amicably towards the camera.

D couldn't help but breathe a sigh of relief, happy for Mew and Teddiursa finding an island.

To say the least, they really have that adventurous and traveling spirit.

Looking at the friendly Pokemon in the photo, D murmured, "Lapras... this type of Pokemon is really rare!"

Lapras, also known as Lapras, possesses a gentle heart, enjoys carrying humans across the sea, and once nearly became extinct due to illegal human poaching.

However, that seems to have been a long time ago.

Currently, major Pokemon Leagues list Lapras as a key protected species, especially in Alola Region.

It's said that because of excessive protection, Lapras there has become somewhat overpopulated.

Unfortunately, drought can kill, while floods can drown.

Lapras are hard to see in the Paldea Region.

*

Back at the ranch, D's life returned to how it was before.

Every day he farmed the fields, basked in the sun, drank tea, and petted Pokemon.

The group of wild Pokemon he helped and raised in the Berry Garden gradually emerged from their initial shadows and adapted to their current life.

This morning, D took them to the back mountain to see what had become of the place they once lived.

As soon as they entered the back mountain, they saw a very unfamiliar scene.

The forest, ravaged by fire, was all in disarray; burn marks were everywhere, thick layers of ash remained on the ground, the scorched, barren trees appeared like an eerie, horrifying black forest, devoid of vitality.

The Wattrel, its wings fully recovered, flapped its wings and landed on a dead branch, lowering its head and sorrowfully viewing the scene below.

The Greedent, its tail fur regrown, swiftly climbed a tree, jumping back and forth on several sturdy trees, as if searching for something.

Petilil and several Shroomish looked sorrowful; they still remembered how vibrant and green this place used to be, covered with grass and towering trees.

The other Pokemon also looked downcast, appearing quite sad.

Seeing this, D sighed and said, "You know, these plant ashes contain a wealth of nutrients and trace elements that plants need."

A group of Pokemon stared over in bewilderment.

D: "These ashes will make the soil more fertile, as all plant life turns into earth nutrients, rejoining the natural cycle."

"Come next year, they will grow anew, becoming even more lush than before."

Deadwood shall eventually sprout new shoots; this forest awaits rebirth.

At this moment, the Kricketune among the group of Pokemon seemed to have understood D's meaning.

It swung its forearms like an inspired musician, producing a melodious, unusual tune from its mouth.

The melody initially sounded sorrowful and downbeat, but soon it surged like waves, becoming filled with enthusiasm.

D was slightly startled.

The other Pokemon, seemingly encouraged, no longer appeared downcast, their eyes turning resolute.

---

That's right! They will work together to rebuild their home!

The Kricketune's performance came to an end.

Applause suddenly sounded from not far away.

D turned his head and saw a long-haired man with a guitar and a Toxtricity approaching from the distance.

He looked at the Kricketune with admiration, then said to D, "Your Kricketune is very talented at performing!"

D: "…No, it's not my Pokemon, it's just temporarily staying with me right now."

He told the man about the previous fire and how he had taken in this group of wild Pokemon.

After listening, the man sighed regretfully: "The fire was ruthless, but people are compassionate. That fire was truly heartbreaking. Hello, I'm Bass, a traveling musician currently roaming the world in pursuit of my musical dreams!"

A musician? D had never met someone like that before. He glanced at the Toxtricity beside Bass and nodded, "I'm D, I run a ranch nearby. By the way, what brings you here?"

Since the fire, this mountain forest had become desolate; not even trainers come here, let alone tourists.

Upon hearing this, Bass explained, "I heard that someone here, along with Pokemon, is steeped in pain and sadness, so I came! To bring them hope and joy with my music!"

D: "?"

Perhaps his confusion was too apparent, so Bass said with longing:

"Music can express emotions and convey emotions to others, whether it's sadness, grief, joy, happiness, or excitement. This is my life's pursuit, to bring happiness and smiles to people through music!"

The Kricketune was touched by this scene.

D: "..." As expected from someone in the arts.

Bass looked at the Pokemon in front of him and suddenly had an idea: "May I perform a concert for you? Would you be interested in listening?"

The Pokemon looked bewildered.

Only the Kricketune looked excited and eager.

Seeing this, D thought for a moment and said, "Sure, come to my ranch!"

"Thank you so much! Mr. D!" Bass seemed very excited to have an audience for his performance.

The group returned to the ranch. It was Bass's first time visiting a place like this; he curiously looked around.

However, he quickly contained his curiosity, fully immersing himself in the upcoming performance.

In the ranch, a group of curious Pokemon lined up from tallest to shortest, lying on the soft grass.

D and Koraidon stood at the back, watching the scene with curiosity.

Once the Pokemon were ready, Bass tossed a Poke Ball into the air and shouted, "Everyone, come out!"

With flashes of white light, another Toxtricity and a Rillaboom appeared beside Bass.

Holding his guitar, his eyes full of passion, Bass shouted to the sky, "We are the Toxtricity Band! To bring hope and happiness to people and Pokemon! Everyone, ready, go!"

With that, the Rillaboom began beating the wooden drum before it.

"Boom boom boom boom boom!"

In the midst of intense and rapid drumbeats, the two Toxtricity emitted bass-like growls, followed by Bass's melodious guitar and passionate singing.

"Come on! ♬"

"Set out on a journey! ♬"

"...♬"

"Explore the cosmos and change the world! ♬"

Listening to this, Koraidon felt a headache coming on: "...Agaaah."

D: "..."

Forgive him; like Koraidon, he has no musical talent.

He looked around and found that other Pokemon also seemed pained, except for the Kricketune, whose eyes shone brightly, waving its arms to the rhythm.

Seeing the expressions on everyone's faces, Bass and Rillaboom stopped playing.

Bass said dejectedly, "As expected, I still can't make it…"

Rillaboom and Toxtricity also looked disheartened.

D stepped forward and asked, "Are you okay?"

Bass looked at him, thanked him first, "Thank you so much for listening to our performance, D."

"I still can't do it... In past performances, the Pokemon listening even fainted from the terrible music. I'm just an amateur musician, no, I don't even count as that."

For a moment, D didn't know what to say; he awkwardly said, "Actually, your performance just now was really good, very passionate."

Bass quickly asked, "Really?"

D: "..."

Seeing the expression on his face, Bass said, "You're really a good person, D."

"Well, a dream that can be realized isn't a dream!" Bass regained his spirits, "Even if no one likes us, we must persist! Because we are the one and only Toxtricity Band in this world!"

"Boom boom boom!" Rillaboom agreed and beat the wooden drum before it.

"Sss～♬!"

At this moment, a melodious chirping suddenly came from the side.

D and Bass turned to see the Kricketune looking this way as if inspired.

Bass looked at its eyes, paused, then asked, "What? You say you really liked our performance just now?"

"Sss～♬!"

The Kricketune chirped melodiously again in agreement.

"Thank you, Kricketune!" Bass smiled, "You're actually a very talented musician too! If possible, I'd love to invite you to join my band."

"Sss～♬!"

Seeing this, D raised an eyebrow.

A thought suddenly popped into his mind.

"Kricketune, do you enjoy performing music too?" D asked.

Kricketune nodded happily.

"Would you like to join Bass's band and travel with him?" D asked again.

"Sss!" Kricketune nodded excitedly.

Upon hearing this, Bass was slightly taken aback and looked at D in disbelief, "Mr. D, you're…"

D nodded softly, "Kricketune is just temporarily staying at my ranch. Rather than staying here, I hope it can do what it loves. Bass, would you take care of Kricketune for me?"

"I would!" Bass nodded repeatedly.

"Then capture it with a Poke Ball!" D said.

He could tell that the Rillaboom and the Toxtricity were well cared for by Bass, and he really liked these music-playing Pokemon.

And it seemed that Kricketune was touched by his heart to convey emotions through music and had chosen this human.

So, D was willing to respect Kricketune's choice.

Upon hearing this, Bass took out an empty Poke Ball and captured Kricketune. The Poke Ball wobbled a little and then stopped.

"It seems to acknowledge you," D said.

Bass released Kricketune from the Poke Ball and greeted it earnestly, "Kricketune, please take care of us in the future!"

Kricketune: "Sss～Oooh! ♬"

At noon, D invited them to have lunch at the ranch.

After lunch, Bass planned to leave.

He and a few Pokemon stood at the ranch entrance to bid farewell to D, "Thank you very much for your hospitality, Mr. D. Please rest assured, I will definitely take good care of Kricketune!"

D asked, "By the way, where do you plan to go next?"

Bass thought for a moment and said, "I plan to visit Porto Marinada Town and then head north to Casseroya Lake."

D nodded silently. He thought of various cities in the game and then offered a suggestion:

"I heard that in Spikemuth Town in the Galar Region, heavy metal rock is quite popular. Maybe you can find your fans there."

"Spikemuth Town…" Bass's eyes lit up, then he nodded, "I'll check it out there later!"

D: "Good luck! I hope that by the time we meet again, you'll have become a famous band."

Bass nodded emphatically, "Yes, we will work hard!"

"Sss～Oooh!"

Nearby, Kricketune also bid farewell to D and was very grateful for his care during this time.

D smiled at it, "Rest assured, set out!"

Perhaps this small gesture will change the course of Kricketune's life from now on.

For him, why not?

---

# 240: Chapter 237: Autumn Thanksgiving (4k)

League Year 198, September 29th, the second last day of autumn.

The footsteps of time have already reached deep autumn, with the grass in the pastures gradually yellowing and withering, and even the leaves of the trees in the Berry Garden falling gently, laying a thick layer on the ground.

This morning, D went out to check on the condition of the farm.

The season is about to change, and the cold winter is coming. Before the snow season arrives, they must make preparations in advance.

Walking through the pastures, a chilly breeze would occasionally sweep by.

Some Pokemon without fur shivered from the cold wind, then continued to play on the grass.

On the other hand, some furry Pokemon were completely unfazed. With winter approaching, they are growing a thick coat to keep warm.

D walked along, watching the Pokemon frolic back and forth in front of and behind him, shaking his head helplessly but letting them be.

"Now the temperature has already dropped to fifteen degrees. According to the weather forecast, a cold front will arrive in the Paldea Region next month, and the temperature will likely drop further." D silently thought about what needs to be done.

By the end of October, November, snow will begin to fall, and the snow season will last for two months. It's said that during this time, Ice Type Pokemon will become more active on this land.

A few days ago, he and a group of Pokemon harvested the last batch of crops from the fields and stored them in the cellar.

The cold winter isn't suitable for crop growth. Although there is winter wheat that is cold-resistant, D has other plans this winter, so he does not intend to plant it.

Besides that, there's the issue of the Pokemon on the farm getting through the winter.

The Miltank live in the Pokemon House, which D has equipped with air conditioning, and with Pawmot there, running it all day poses no problem.

The Gogoat have thick wool naturally suited to winter environments, but if they still feel cold, they can stay with Miltank.

Then there are the Naclstack and Diglett living in the Underground Cave; there is some distance from the ground, so the temperature isn't too cold.

As for the Glaceon, there's no need to mention it—an Ice Type Pokemon wouldn't be an Ice Type if it feared the cold.

As for Koraidon, Cyclizar, Pawmot, Indeedee and such, they all live in the house with him.

The wild Pokemon he rescued also live in specially made cabins, so he doesn't need to worry about them being sheltered from the cold.

The only ones he needs to inquire about are the clueless Psyduck living across the river.

D found them, ate a few pieces of Translation Nuts Cake, and asked, "Winter is coming, and it's going to be very cold outside. Do you want to come live on the farm with me?"

Upon hearing this, the Psyduck tilted their heads, then hopped a few times on the ground with a dazed look in their eyes: "Quack, quack?" (Winter?)

D: "... There will be snowfall, and the whole world will be covered in snow. The river will freeze, so you won't be able to swim in it."

This time, the Psyduck understood.

One Psyduck suddenly brightened and happily jumped twice: "Quack, quack!" (Psyduck isn't afraid of the cold!)

Another Psyduck touched its cheek and said: "Quack, quack!" (Ducks have feathers!)

D: "..."

He thought about it carefully and realized they were right.

The Water Type Psyduck naturally has a resilience to Ice Types.

In that case, he'll give each Psyduck a scarf as a winter accessory.

"See you later then!"

D waved to the Psyduck and turned to the beehive. He called over the strong Koraidon to help move it to the newly built Glass Greenhouse.

He had already transplanted many flowers there, and with the honey bought in advance, the Combee could safely spend the winter.

While they were moving the beehive, the Combee followed closely behind.

D and Koraidon placed the beehive in the Glass Greenhouse and said to the Combee in front of them:

"The weather is getting colder and the flowers outside have all withered. From today onwards, you can live here until next spring."

"Buzz ~"

The Combee swayed gently, looking very pleased.

Seeing this, D called over the flower-loving Butterfree to come live here as well.

After handling everything he could think of, D returned to his room and sat at his desk, calculating this year's revenue and expenses.

With the increasing number of Pokemon on the farm, household expenses have also grown.

In addition to the money spent on building various facilities and buying furniture earlier, his family's savings now amounted to six hundred eighty thousand League Coins.

"Hmm, this number is about right..." D mused over the accounts, forming plans for the future: "In some time, I can ask Keri to build a multifunctional greenhouse."

By then, he won't be limited by the seasons and can plant any crops anytime he wants. Coupled with Gogoat's plant cultivation abilities, maybe they could develop more vegetables and fruits with magical properties.

"But there's no rush. It won't be too late to build it next spring," D thought to himself.

---

Because next, he needs to go to the Galar Region and doesn't know when he'll be back.

*

In the evening, Zack came to fetch the goods.

As soon as he saw D, he cheerfully asked, "Hey D, are you ready with the pumpkin cookies? Tomorrow's Thanksgiving, and there might be a lot of kids coming over to ask for cookies!"

"Of course." D grinned, "I've been preparing for a month now, don't worry, there'll be plenty of cookies!"

"Then I won't worry. Oh, by the way, did Mayor Thomas come by to invite you to the festival?" Zack asked.

"Not yet." D shook his head.

Zack: "Really? I just saw the mayor heading this way, he should arrive soon. Since that's the case, I won't say much more, the mayor will tell you about tomorrow's arrangements."

D nodded, "Alright."

With that, Zack left with a load of goods.

D waited on the ranch for a while, and sure enough, less than three minutes passed before Mayor Thomas arrived at the ranch.

The old mayor greeted him with a kind smile, "Good afternoon, D!"

D: "Good afternoon, Mayor Thomas!"

"Ho ho ho! You've heard, right? Tomorrow is Thanksgiving." Mayor Thomas said, "By then, local ranchers and townsfolk will gather at Festival Plaza to celebrate the day! You should come too!"

D smiled, "Of course, I will definitely be there."

Mayor Thomas: "The time is tomorrow morning, and the event will last until noon. You can bring your Pokémon because the town will set up a large cooking pot in the square."

"By then, nearby ranchers, farmers, and townsfolk will bring their ingredients, and at noon, everyone will put them into the pot to stew and enjoy the delicious food together."

"If there are any ingredients you'd like to bring, feel free to share them with everyone!" Mayor Thomas said with a smile.

"?"

D was taken aback for a moment, this setup seemed so familiar to him?

Thinking for a bit, he asked, "Can it be any ingredient?"

"Preferably crops harvested from fields." Mayor Thomas said a bit awkwardly, "Also ensure they're not poisonous or anything strange, because there was once an event where someone accidentally put poisonous mushrooms picked from the back mountain into the pot, causing indigestion for many people and Pokémon."

D: "… I understand."

Mayor Thomas's expression relaxed, "Actually, in the past, we held this Thanksgiving to thank this land beneath us."

"But now, besides this land that nurtures us, we also need to thank Calyrex, The King of Bountiful Harvests. Everyone from the nearby ranches says the harvest has been great since they began to worship him!"

D nodded in agreement, "Calyrex is a great Pokémon."

"Such a kind being, I really wish to see him with my own eyes!" Mayor Thomas murmured, "But such a Pokémon must be as elusive as in the legends, hard to meet, right?"

D: Not necessarily.

Mayor Thomas sighed with emotion, then said, "Alright, let's not talk about this anymore. Remember to come and celebrate with everyone tomorrow!"

D nodded, "Yes, I will definitely go."

Mayor Thomas smiled kindly, then took his leave and turned away.

D watched him walk away until he was out of sight, then turned his gaze to the Calyrex idol placed on the ranch.

It really was quite touching.

Back then, he only wanted the two horses of Calyrex, but he never imagined he would end up indirectly spreading Calyrex's name in the Paldea Region.

Realizing Calyrex might be able to sense the situation here.

D thought for a while, then walked to the idol and began to offer a prayer:

"... Great King of Bountiful Harvests Calyrex, I will travel to the Galar Region next month, hoping to see you there."

Meanwhile, in the Galar Region, The Crown Tundra.

On the vast snowfield, Calyrex seemed to hear a familiar prayer, then opened his eyes from slumber.

"It's my little follower," Calyrex thought, "Let me see what he's up to now."

The next moment, a layer of blue light shimmered in Calyrex's eyes.

Within this light, his gaze traversed time and space, seeing a distant scene.

His little follower was standing in front of the idol, praying.

"... Great King of Bountiful Harvests, I will travel to the Galar Region next month, hoping to see you there."

Upon hearing this, any lingering drowsiness in Calyrex vanished instantly.

What?

His little follower was finally coming to visit him?

Just like back when he ruled the Galar Region, those who worshiped him came to pay pilgrimage?

At this moment, Calyrex's long-quiescent heart was filled with anticipation and joy.

Finally—he has waited long enough!

Thanks to this devoted follower, he has now regained part of his power after absorbing much of the Power of Faith, not as weak as before.

But it's still far from enough for him to tame those two horses; he still needs the help of his little follower.

After a brief moment of excitement, Calyrex regained his usual nobility and grace.

He looked at his devout follower, and like a king, he gently responded, "I will be here waiting for your arrival."

*

September 30th, on the day of Thanksgiving.

D got up early and went to his cellar to fetch two large pumpkins, preparing to take them to Festival Plaza.

It's his first time participating in such an event, D lacked experience, especially since this isn't Stardew Valley, but the real world.

He couldn't possibly do something as quirky as taking the mayor's purple shorts to the event and putting them in the pot, that would be too tasteless.

Therefore, after much contemplation, he decided to take two pumpkins to see the world.

Just like Larry's saying, simplicity is true.

At 9 AM, D headed to Festival Plaza with his family.

Among them were Gogoat, Miltank, Pawmot, Alcremie, Glaceon... He brought almost all from the ranch that he could.

On the way, D thought it might be inappropriate, so after some more thought, he returned to the ranch and had Koraidon carry two barrels of barley beer.

But when D arrived at Festival Plaza, he found that he wasn't the only one bringing a crowd.

Although it was still early and the venue was still being set up, a large number of townspeople and Pokémon had already gathered, it was a lively scene.

In the center of Festival Plaza was a gigantic cooking pot, even larger than the one in Big Bad Wolf's home used for boiling sheep!

Staring at this scene, D was completely stunned.

At that moment, Allen, who was on holiday, ran over to him.

D looked at him, shocked, "This… this is too much!"

Allen, however, seemed used to it, "Hey, it's actually not that bad, with so many people, a bowl of soup each should be about right, it's like this every year, oh, I forgot, this is your first time here!"

Saying this, Allen looked behind D, "Where's Cyclizar and the others? You didn't just bring Koraidon along, did you?"

D patted his backpack, "Right here!"

It was already filled with Poke Balls to the brim.

But… really let everyone out?

D was a bit hesitant, because there were so many people here, what if a Pokémon got lost, ran away, or was taken by someone with ill intentions?

Allen seemed to realize this too.

He thought for a moment and said, "Then don't let them out yet, later when the feast starts, you can let them out. The town specially prepared snacks and food for the Pokémon, and you can keep an eye on them."

"That's right." D nodded.

"Hehe, enjoy yourself!" Allen smiled, "This is the liveliest day of the year in town!"

---

---

---

---

# 241: Chapter 238: Sharing Mom (4k)

At 10 a.m., a group of people began arranging the area around the large cooking pot.

Mayor Thomas also arrived at this time, dressed in formal attire appropriate for the occasion, holding a gentleman's cane, accompanied by a Cyclizar.

Mayor Thomas, representing the entire Porto Marinada Town, stepped onto the platform, took the microphone from someone beside him, and said in a kind tone:

"Welcome, everyone, to this year's Thanksgiving festival. I also want to thank you all for bringing such delicious ingredients for the celebration. By noon, we will put everyone's ingredients into the pot; I believe it will cook an incredibly delicious stew."

"However, before the feast officially begins, please be patient as our staff is setting up the venue."

D stood in a less crowded area watching this scene, with Pokemon like Dragonite, Cyclizar, Koraidon, Pawmot, and Glaceon by his side.

The smaller Alcremie stood on Dragonite's shoulder.

D glanced at the Cyclizar next to Mayor Thomas and then asked his own Cyclizar, "Cyclizar, is that Cyclizar your relative?"

Hearing this, Cyclizar glanced over and happily nodded.

"Raaawr~"

Indeed, that's its mother.

Sure enough! D thought about how Cyclizar had left its parents when it was just a few months old to come to his ranch. Maybe, like human children, it also misses its mom.

So he naturally asked, "Do you want to go over and talk to her?"

Cyclizar blinked its eyes, then cheerfully said, "Raaawr!"

"I knew it." D smiled and said to it, "Wait here; I'll go invite Mayor Thomas over, Koraidon, can you watch over everyone for me?"

"Graagh!"

Just as D was about to approach Mayor Thomas, he saw that Mayor Thomas had already spotted him in the crowd and was bringing the Cyclizar over.

"Ah, it's D! You arrived quite early; the feast hasn't started yet, so you'll have to wait a bit longer," Mayor Thomas said.

D shook his head with a smile, "It's okay, there's not much work at the ranch right now anyway, I was just about to find you."

Mayor Thomas looked a bit surprised, "Did something happen?"

"No, Cyclizar just wants to see its parents."

As he spoke, D looked at the Cyclizar beside him.

By now, the Cyclizar's mother had recognized her child.

She looked at her child with a kind gaze, noticing how strong it had become. It was clear this human had taken good care of it.

The Cyclizar's mother was sincerely delighted, lifting her head to let out an excited cry, "Raaawr!"

Cyclizar also looked very happy, "Raaawr~"

It was reassuring its mother not to worry, expressing how much it liked its current life and D.

Just then, an untimely growl suddenly broke in.

"Graagh!"

Hearing this growl, the Cyclizar's mother turned to see a tall, strong, and fearsomely scary Pokemon staring at her.

She was startled and instinctively stepped back.

Seeing this, Koraidon looked deeply hurt, even its tone turned very dejected, "Graagh..."

"Does Koraidon miss its mom too?" Mayor Thomas, standing by, seemed to understand and softly comforted, "Though I don't know where you came from, I'm sure your mother loves you very much, because there are no parents in the world who don't love their children."

"Graagh..." Koraidon lowered its head, silent.

D reached out to gently pat its head.

Koraidon lifted its head.

D gave it a smile, "Mayor Thomas is right, your mother surely loves you dearly, don't be sad, we're here with you now."

"Graa!"

Koraidon regained a little spirit, turning back to the Cyclizar and gave it a call, "Graa... Raaawr!"

Hearing this, Cyclizar next to it looked at Koraidon with a silly, bewildered look on its face.

Meanwhile, the Cyclizar's mother was taken aback and then looked at the fierce-looking big Pokemon in disbelief.

"Raaawr?" she tentatively asked.

"Graa~" Koraidon's tone was cheerful.

The Cyclizar's mother's eyes widened.

She was just a very ordinary Cyclizar, living in this simple little town.

She knew very little about the world, and for her, the happiest thing was to be by her master's side.

And now, the big Pokemon was calling her "mom"...

Initially, the Cyclizar's mother doubted when she had given birth to such a child, but then the other party claimed they really missed her.

Following her maternal instincts, she vaguely felt a connection with this big fellow, similar to her feeling with Cyclizar.

Could it be... this Pokemon was also her child?

She was completely baffled, having no idea when she might have had such a child.

The Cyclizar's mother couldn't understand.

She looked at the strong, evidently battle-hardened Pokemon in front of her, hesitated for a moment, and then said, "Raaawr…" (Though I don't know what you've been through, you must have had some tough times all these years?)

---

In Cyclizar mother's view, it takes countless scars to forge such a powerful Cyclizar.

And those scars must have been earned from countless hard and difficult battles.

Hearing Cyclizar mother's words, Koraidon suddenly had a urge to cry.

But it held back.

Because now it was no longer a weak Cyclizar, but a mighty Pokemon who can protect everyone.

Cyclizar stared blankly at such a Koraidon, suddenly feeling that something was strange about Koraidon.

Could it be that it wanted to compete for mom like competing for D?

Cyclizar's heart was instantly alarmed, it really wanted to say this is its mom, but looking at Koraidon's appearance, it hesitated.

Therefore, it asked: "Gawoo?"

Koraidon looked at its former self, just like the former Koraidon looked at it now.

It pondered for a moment, then said: "Gaaaa…"

In response, Cyclizar immediately showed sympathy.

Koraidon is so pitiful…

After some inner struggle, it said to Koraidon: "Gawoo!"

Since that's the case, it was willing to share mom with Koraidon, so it could also have a mom!

*

Meanwhile, D had already arrived at the back with Mayor Thomas.

Looking at the two barrels of barley beer and two large pumpkins on the ground, he asked, "Mayor Thomas, where should these be placed?"

Mayor Thomas widened his eyes: "You brought so many things?"

D smiled sheepishly: "This year's harvest was good, we can't finish it at home, so I brought them here to share with everyone."

"Hohoho, I see!" Mayor Thomas laughed: "Everyone's brought items have been placed over there, later some people and Pokemon will handle these ingredients specially, after cleaning and cutting them they can be put into the big pot to stew."

Saying this, Mayor Thomas waved his hand, and two enthusiastic citizens came with Pokemon to carry away the two big pumpkins and beer.

At this moment, a noise suddenly came from another side of the plaza.

Attracted by the sound, D couldn't help but turn his head out of curiosity.

Initially, Mayor Thomas was also curious but quickly seemed to remember something, he grinned: "Oh right, almost forgot about this!"

"What?" D was puzzled.

Mayor Thomas said mysteriously: "You'll find out soon, I need to go over there."

D: "?"

What's going on, so secretive?

He returned to the group of Pokemon, watching Mayor Thomas leave with Cyclizar's mom.

Then, D saw six Machamps carrying a huge object, walking through the crowd toward the center of the plaza.

The object was covered with a layer of black cloth, obscuring its exact appearance.

Once the six Machamps placed the object in the center of Festival Plaza, with a boom, the heavy object landed, Mayor Thomas stepped forward and whipped off the black cloth.

With that, D finally saw the appearance of the object.

It was evidently a super giant Calyrex statue!

"Holy crap!" D couldn't help but exclaim.

And Mayor Thomas was already holding a microphone, starting to introduce this great King of Bountiful Harvests to the unaware townspeople.

"This year, Porto Marinada Town's farm products have yielded a bounteous harvest! Far surpassing the neighboring town! I'm sure you are very curious about this."

"Why, despite the same weather and planting methods, our crops grew better than those in the neighboring town."

"Well now, let me introduce you to this great King of Bountiful Harvests!"

D: "..."

Alright then, now he doesn't need to preach about Calyrex, these later-developed believers have already started to learn to spread Calyrex's name themselves.

Mayor Thomas's promotion proved quite effective, as he already heard some surprised exclamations from unaware townspeople.

"So, on today's Thanksgiving, we not only thank the land that nurtured us, but more importantly Calyrex who granted us abundance!"

D watched this scene with interest.

In fact, compared to other Legendary Pokemons, Calyrex can indeed be described as a rather laid-back deity.

It requires no offerings nor specific tribute ceremonies, merely having people know its name is enough for its existence.

Moreover, according to Calyrex's setting, the more people know it, the stronger it becomes.

It typically doesn't need to battle, but what if it can't exhaust those powers? Surely, it would share excess energy with farmers who know it, aiding them in farming.

This is a completely positive cycle.

Even if one day someone forgets it, it wouldn't take revenge on humans.

Because Calyrex is a Pokemon filled with a loving heart, it would even, without bitterness, help enemies after battles.

Such a wonderful Pokemon!

Pity it ended up being forgotten by the world, with even its steed leaving it.

D felt regret for Calyrex in his heart.

And with Crown Tundra being so cold, could Grass Type Calyrex endure it?

*

At noon, the banquet officially began.

Mayor Thomas stood on a stool before the big pot, putting the ingredients people brought into the pot to stew.

These mushrooms and vegetables were easy to cook, and soon, a fragrance wafted from the big pot.

D and a group of Pokemon sat at a long dining table.

Across from him sat some residents of the town.

Upon hearing the young man in front was the rancher of D Ranch, the children of those residents felt curiosity toward him.

One five or six-year-old boy, with wide eyes, looked at Koraidon and asked innocently: "Big brother, what's this Pokemon called?"

D explained with a smile: "It's called Koraidon, it's a Dragon Type and Fighting Type Pokemon."

"Dragon Type and Fighting Type…" The boy pondered for a bit, then asked: "Isn't it Type Matchup by Fairy Type and Ice Type Pokemons?"

"Correct." D nodded, then shook his head: "But, Type Matchups don't mean everything; some trainers and Pokemons can still achieve victory in battles even against type constraints."

The boy blinked, understanding only a little.

A girl hugging a Skwovet beside also cast curious glances over here.

D turned to look, the focus brightened immediately as the girl and Skwovet asked: "Big brother, can I come to your ranch this afternoon to exchange pumpkin cookies? Mom's cookies are so delicious! Skwovet loves them too!"

D responded with a smile: "Of course, my ranch has prepared lots of pumpkin cookies, you can exchange for as many as you want."

Exchanging pumpkin cookies is a local custom, somewhat similar to kids asking for candy on Halloween.

Upon hearing this, several children cheered.

One boy accidentally revealed his true intent: "Great! We can go see Pokemon over there!"

After saying this, he quickly covered his mouth.

D raised a brow, somewhat surprised.

Seeing this, the boy's mother smiled and explained:

"Because on the town you can often see Dragonites flying from the ranch delivering milk, Dick wanted to visit that ranch where Dragonites deliver goods long ago."

Hearing this, the boy named Dick had shining, hopeful eyes as he raised his little hand:

"Uncle Zack said, Mr. D's ranch raises many many Pokemons!"

Speaking excitedly, the boy pleaded: "Mr. D, can I visit it? I promise I'll behave!"

"Of course you can." D replied with a gentle smile, regarding kids who aren't troublemakers, he genuinely likes them.

Moreover, in those children's eyes, he could see a shadow of Ash.

Who knows, maybe one of these kids will grow to become a passionate Pokemon trainer like Ash someday.

Upon his agreement, the boy cheered loudly: "That's great!"

By now, the food in the big pot was fully cooked.

Some warm-hearted townsfolk actively moved forward to serve the soup, sharing it with everyone.

Soon, a bowl of soup was placed before D.

Looking down at the bowl, the information of the soup floated into his mind.

[Celebration Stew (No Level): A stew cooked to celebrate the harvest, using plenty of fresh ingredients, though it has no special effect, it allows people to taste the flavor of happiness.]
