# 242: Chapter 239: D Empire and the Dragonite Legion (4k)

"The taste of happiness..."

D raised an eyebrow and took a sip of the soup from the bowl.

One sip revealed the flavors of many crops mixed with the rich broth and the lively festive atmosphere, truly giving a warming sensation.

D looked around.

He saw a girl feeding soup to a Pokémon, saw a toddler cozily snuggling against their parents, and Machamp gulping down the soup with large swigs.

This atmosphere, this feeling, indeed made it the most lively day of the year.

Everyone gathered to celebrate the harvest season together.

What a beautiful, leisurely life!

D felt a slight emotion in his heart. He turned around to look at his large family of Pokémon.

The Indeedee siblings sat on a bench, eating Pokémon snacks prepared for them by the town.

The younger Indeedee was tasting them carefully, sometimes furrowing its brows, sometimes its eyes lighting up, as if saying which tasted good and which didn't.

Pawmot, draped in a red cape like a little hero, was surrounded by some Pokémon from the town.

In their envious and admiring gazes, Pawmot did a superhero takeoff on the spot, eliciting a burst of exclamations from the group of Pokémon.

The Gogoat were lazily standing on one side.

After learning that the milk they usually drank was produced by these three Gogoat, several children from the town gathered around them.

A daring boy even carefully reached out to touch the soft hair of one Gogoat.

Seeing the Gogoat wasn't displeased, he immediately widened his eyes, saying to his companions, "So soft!"

The Gogoat seemed to enjoy this atmosphere too, suddenly releasing their internal Grass Energy, creating a somewhat magical Grassy Terrain around them.

A chorus of exclamations followed from the children, who marveled, "So amazing!"

Watching this scene, D couldn't help but feel, "The Gogoat are really popular!"

"Ma-aah~"

*

At 2 PM, the autumn Thanksgiving came to an end.

The full and satisfied townspeople gradually left. After bidding farewell to Mayor Thomas, D also took his group of Pokémon and returned to the ranch.

With autumn deepening, the temperature slowly cooled, but today's weather was still nice. In the evening, temperatures were in the twenties, and the warm sun felt very comfortable on the body.

Not long after they returned to the ranch, a lively noise came from outside.

D stepped out to find the town's children bringing their Pokémon partners to exchange pumpkin cookies with him.

Among them was a boy he met earlier that morning named Dick, accompanied by a Rockruff with pretty blue eyes.

Seeing D come out, Dick's eyes lit up, and he handed over a small package wrapped in ribbons and wax paper:

"Mr. D, I'm here to exchange pumpkin cookies with you!"

"Alright!" D smiled and nodded, turning to have the Indeedee siblings bring out the pumpkin cookies he had prepared, and exchanged with him.

After receiving the pumpkin cookies, the boy shyly thanked him, "Thank you!"

The Rockruff standing beside him also barked enthusiastically, "Woof woof!"

"Mr. D! It's our turn too!"

Another girl with a Tatsugiri on her head waved her little hand eagerly.

"Don't worry, there's enough for everyone."

D exchanged pumpkin cookies with them. Once the exchange was complete, the group of children and Pokémon all thanked him in unison.

Watching this scene, D thought to himself, these kids are pretty cute.

He glanced at Dick, who was full of expectation, raised an eyebrow, and asked, "Would you like to take a tour of my ranch?"

"Yes!" they replied in unison.

D smiled and said, "Then follow me!"

He took the group of children to the pen for the Skiddo and Gogoat, explaining the ecology of these two Pokémon to them.

Next, he took them to the Pokémon House to show them the MooMoo Milk production process.

After leaving, everyone went to the Glass Greenhouse, where D explained how the Combee gathered nectar and made Honey.

Finally, they went down to the Underground Cave, where D explained about the helpfulness of Diglett in farming land and Naclstack in producing delicious and nutritious Rock Salt.

With every sentence D spoke, the group of children would exclaim with excitement, "Wow!"

They seemed very curious about everything and became interested as D explained.

At the end, a girl said with longing, "When I grow up, I want to be a rancher too, like Mr. D, raising lots of Pokémon here and living with them!"

D nodded with a smile, "That's a great dream, but being a rancher is very hard work!"

Another boy immediately shook his head, "I'm not afraid! Mom says I'm strong! Fidough will help me too!"

The Fidough beside the boy promptly nodded, "Woof woof!"

"That's great." D then looked at the other children and asked, "What about you? What dreams do you have when you grow up?"

As expected, at this moment, all these children expressed a strong interest in becoming ranchers, stating they wanted to be ranchers in the future too.

---

Only Dick stood excitedly on the side.

D glanced at him and asked, "What about Dick?"

Dick's eyes were bright, and he said with determination, "I want to become a Trainer as awesome as Mr. D!"

D: "..."

The other kids were amazed and looked at Dick, puzzled: "Eh? Mr. D is a Pokemon Trainer?"

Dick looked confused and responded, "Haven't you noticed? Mr. D is a Trainer with a Dragonite Legion! To make so many Dragonite behave, Mr. D must be really powerful!"

"Wow!"

The other kids showed expressions of enlightenment and gazed at D admiringly.

"He's amazing!"

"He's even better than the top Champion!"

"That's right!"

D: "..."

Heaven knows, he really isn't.

D smiled politely and said, "The top Champion is the most incredible Trainer in our Paldea Region, and she's quite a charismatic person."

Upon hearing this, the children's attention immediately shifted.

A boy quickly asked, "Eh? Does Mr. D know the top Champion?"

D thought for a moment and replied, "You could say I know her!"

"Wow!" He quickly followed up, "Have you had Pokemon battles against each other? Can the top Champion's Pokemon beat your Dragonite Legion, Mr. D?"

D: "..."

He said helplessly, "It's not as exaggerated as you say. I don't have a Dragonite Legion. There are only three Dragonite in the ranch altogether."

Dick looked confused: "But I saw there are five Dragonair here with you, Mr. D? They'll evolve into Dragonite in the future."

Another girl counted with her fingers: "Five Dragonair plus three Dragonite, that's eight Dragonite altogether! Wow, so many Dragonite, incredible!"

Another boy said, "I've watched a movie with my mom where a Trainer led five Dragonite to fight against a bad nation's army! That Trainer single-handedly devastated the bad nation with just five Dragonite!"

"And Mr. D has eight. He might even conquer a city and become King himself!"

D smiled politely: "...It's a time of peace now. Doing that would get you arrested by Miss Jenny and Growlithe."

The boy thought carefully and then nodded: "That's right!"

Somehow, it was already five in the evening, and Zack drove over with a big truck to pick up the goods.

Upon seeing Zack, D patted a boy's head and said with a smile:

"Alright, kids, it's getting late. Let's wrap up for today!"

The boys realized they had played here too long, and if they returned home too late, their parents would worry.

So the kids waved goodbye to D and his Pokemon: "Goodbye, Mr. D, goodbye Cyclizar, goodbye Koraidon, goodbye Dragonite!"

"Gao～"

"Ah-gah-shi～"

Several Pokemon waved goodbye to the children too.

Watching their bouncing figures leaving the ranch, D felt a bit uneasy and sent Dragonite to escort them home.

"Awoo～"

Dragonite obediently followed them.

From a distance, Zack saw this and laughed heartily: "Looks like you're really popular with the kids!"

D sighed with emotion: "Mainly because they're all well-behaved. No one dislikes obedient kids, right?"

"Haha! Good kids are certainly likable." Zack agreed and said, "Alright, today's goods... let me count, a total of forty-eight thousand League coins. Here, take it!"

D reached over to accept it, then helped Zack load the goods onto the truck.

"See you later!"

Sitting in the driver's seat, Zack waved through the glass at D, then drove away from the ranch.

After seeing the vehicle disappear at the end of the road, D turned back to his house to prepare the evening meal with his sister, Indeedee.

Midway, Dragonite returned to the ranch.

D looked up at it and asked, "Was everything smooth?"

Dragonite happily nodded: "Awoo～"

"That's good." D smiled gently, "You worked hard, go rest well, dinner will be ready soon."

"Awoo～" Dragonite agreed cheerfully.

D had prepared a particularly sumptuous feast for dinner today.

Pan-seared patties, chicken and mushroom stew, sandwiches, milk pudding, Berry cake, aromatic Baofen, stewed ribs, steak, pumpkin soup, pumpkin cookies...

Almost every food Pokemon loves was included.

The Pokemon ate happily, and D was pleased watching them.

After dinner, D thought about what the kids said this afternoon.

He first found his Dragonite and asked it, "By the way, Dragonite, do you guys hibernate during the winter?"

Dragonite blinked its big eyes, looking quite confused: "Awoo?"

What's hibernation?

D thought for a while and explained, "That means when it gets really cold, do you guys hide in caves and sleep?"

Some Pokemon have this habit.

It's hard to find food in the winter, so to conserve energy, these Pokemon eat well before winter comes, then hide in their warm nests to sleep until spring comes and everything revives.

After D's explanation, Dragonite understood and nodded its big head: "Awoo!"

---

Some Dragonites do know, but for them, it doesn't really matter whether they hibernate or not!

Because Dragonite Island is located in the middle of the sea, the climate doesn't get too cold even in winter, and there are plenty of small fish in the sea, so they don't have to worry about food at all.

Hearing this, D nodded thoughtfully.

Then, he found the two wild Dragonites preparing to take Poffins and five Dragonairs back to Dragon Island.

D: "Tomorrow is the first day of winter, and the weather will get colder and colder. It might even start snowing. If it gets too cold on the way, you can rest on the island for a while and wait until it gets warmer to start work again."

Upon hearing this, the Dragonites and Dragonairs immediately shook their heads, indicating they were not afraid of the cold!

D: "..."

He said helplessly, "Alright, then I'll check on things when it starts snowing. Go ahead, today's work is done, you should hurry home!"

Under the watchful eyes of the Dragonite, the group of Dragonites and Dragonairs happily bid farewell to D, then waved their increasingly plump claws and flew off in the direction of Dragonite Island.

In a blink of an eye, they disappeared from sight.

At times like this, D couldn't help but be grateful that most Dragonites on Dragonite Island still valued their dignity.

If all of them shamelessly stayed at his ranch for food like these few, he would have gone bankrupt long ago.

Speaking of dragons, D hadn't forgotten that there was also a dragon at his ranch.

He thought for a moment, then turned back into the house and brought out a basket of pumpkin cookies to the lake where the Dragonairs usually stayed, lightly coughed twice, and asked, "Giratina, are you there?"

No response.

"Seems like it's not here," D murmured.

The poisonous mushrooms he picked last time had already been consumed, and he could no longer use the Poisonous Mushroom Soup to see the Reversed World.

Furthermore, after the forest fire, it was difficult to find poisonous mushrooms in the back mountains.

Moreover, since Giratina might have felt bored, it wouldn't always be peeking here and occasionally wandered off to other places.

Now, he had unilaterally lost contact with Giratina.

Just as D was about to head back with the basket, a purple vortex suddenly appeared on the calm surface of the lake.

Has it returned?

D was slightly startled, then threw the basket he was holding into it.

The purple vortex gradually disappeared.

D didn't mind, got up, smiled, and returned to the living room.

At this moment, the sky outside had completely darkened.

Due to the festival, the Pokemon Tavern was closed for a day, and the Indeedee siblings were staying at home.

The brother Indeedee was sweeping the floor with a broom, while the sister Indeedee was boiling water nearby, preparing to make tea for D.

D called the siblings over and said, "I'm going on a trip soon to take care of some matters, and it might take about half a month before I come back. I'll be counting on you to look after the house."

Hearing that D was going on a trip again, the Cyclizar lying in front of the fireplace immediately stood up.

D noticed it and said with a smile, "Don't worry, you're coming with me this time."

"Gao!" Cyclizar was overjoyed.

The two Indeedees nodded gently, saying, "Ai Yi~"

Rest assured, they would take care of everything at home!

"Thank you, I'll bring you gifts when I return," D said with a smile, patting the sister Indeedee on the head.

The brother Indeedee's small face tensed up.

Seeing this, D also reached out and patted it.

In the next moment, he saw the brother Indeedee's face turn visibly red, seemingly embarrassed.

D raised an eyebrow, didn't say much, just withdrew his hand and said, "It's getting late, you should go rest! I'll watch over things here."

Dazed and confused from the pats, the siblings nodded and obediently returned to their room.

D stayed in the living room, waiting for the water to boil. Then he went up to turn off the heat, poured the hot water into a thermos, and went back to his room to rest.

Autumn was over, and when the clock struck midnight, winter would officially begin!

With expectations for the days to come, D closed his eyes and, accompanied by the cozy snoring of the Pawmot in his arms, quickly fell asleep.

---

---

---

---

# 243: Chapter 240: Hammer (4k)

League Year 198, Winter 8th.

With the arrival of a cold wave, the Paldea Region has officially embraced the chilly winter.

Although there's no snow yet, the temperature has dropped to the extent that one needs to wear autumn pants.

D got up in the morning to find a layer of white frost covering the yellowed grass outside, the north wind blew through the valley bringing a whimpering sound from afar.

"It's indeed getting colder!"

The weather forecast said the temperature would drop sharply these two days, advising citizens to prepare in advance, and it seemed to be true now.

D pulled the curtain and turned to sit on the sofa, watching the morning news of the day.

A fireplace was burning in the room, its bright red glow warmly illuminating the entire living room.

As it gets colder, the Pokemon are reluctant to play outside, led by Cyclizar, a group of Pokemon lazily warming themselves by the fireplace.

Pawmot even happily exposed its belly.

This is a sign of feeling very comfortable and secure for Pokemon.

D found it somewhat amusing.

At this moment, the Indeedee brother approached and asked, "Ai-ee?"

D ate two pieces of Translation Nuts Cake, "Go ahead."

The Indeedee brother repeated, "Ai-ee?" (Master, when do you plan to set off for the Galar Region?)

With the weather getting colder lately, it feared it might snow soon.

"I told you, don't call me master, just call me D." D was somewhat helpless, he had told the Indeedee brother many times now.

But the Indeedee brother was very firm in this regard, even the Indeedee sister had changed, yet he persisted.

The Indeedee brother obligingly responded, "Ai-ee." (Alright.)

The expression on his face clearly wrote the words 'dare again next time'.

D: "..." Forget it.

The Indeedee brother had always been a sensible and mature Pokemon, understanding that he and his sister were sent to the farm to serve as a steward for their new master.

Hence, he always played the role of a mature steward.

D thought for a while and decided not to continue the topic, instead answering, "Probably soon, I had previously ordered a batch of winter scarves from town. Once I've given them out to Psyduck and Greedent, I'll set off."

The Indeedee brother nodded in agreement.

In the afternoon, the scarf shop owner personally delivered a box of Pokemon scarves to the farm.

After signing for them, D immediately took the box of scarves to the group of Psyduck, tying them on each one.

The Psyducks were delighted with their new attire.

They joyfully looked at the scarves in front of them, bouncing excitedly by the river.

D smiled at the scene, and indeed, the Psyducks looked noticeably cuter wearing scarves.

Perhaps this is the charm of dressing up?

It's said that some noble ladies love to adorn their Pokemon with various items.

Due to this, Pokemon beauty salons have sprung up in major cities.

"Keep playing, I have things to do, see you later!" D waved to the group of Psyduck.

The Psyducks mimicked him, waving their little arms with cheerful smiles: "Psyduck!"

D chuckled, then turned and headed to the Berry Garden dotted with wooden cabins.

With winter here, these fruit trees no longer bear berries.

But surprisingly, they remain lush as before, not bare like other trees.

For this reason, D had previously looked up books on berry tree planting.

He still remembered how that book described it.

"The magical berry trees are trees blessed by nature, these magical berries have a very short growth cycle. If planted in sufficient quantities, they can even meet the needs of Pokemon in the entire forest."

"Berry trees don't wither in winter, but won't grow berries either. Scholars speculate this is because the cold winter causes nature to enter a dormant period."

"But don't worry, when winter passes and spring arrives, the trees will bear berries again."

"Berry trees have a very long lifespan; if not affected by natural disasters, human logging, or Pokemon moves, they will keep growing."

"Some explorers have even found a gigantic berry tree in a mysterious and ancient forest, bearing countless fruits, with many Pokemon residing nearby. It's speculated that the tree has lived for at least a thousand years."

"Amazing," D murmured.

He couldn't help but imagine that scene.

A tree taller than three Koraidon combined, with a sturdy trunk that three adults can't wrap their arms around, growing exceedingly lush, dense tree clusters, and branches hanging with one plump berry after another.

D's first thought was that it would be perfect to build a treehouse on it.

If only he could grow such a giant berry tree.

However, unfortunately, it's almost impossible in his lifetime unless these berry trees undergo genetic mutation themselves.

As D just stepped into the Berry Garden, Greedent happily darted out from the wooden cabin.

"Chirp chirp chirp!"

D placed the box on the ground and reached out to stroke its head.

Upon hearing Greedent's growl, other Pokemon also started peeking out of the cabins one by one.

---

Before long, a group of Pokemon had gathered around D.

Wattrel, Grafaiai, Tinkatink, Surskit, Petilil, Shroomish...

Predictably, that Slakoth did not come over; it must still be sleeping, right?

A long time had passed since that fire disaster, and this group of Pokemon gradually emerged from its shadow.

D crouched down, took out specially made scarves of varying sizes from the box, and tied them around each one's neck.

After putting on the scarves, this group of Pokemon immediately felt the cold wind blocked out, and the areas covered by the scarves were warming up.

D thought about how these Pokemon used to live in the mountains and probably hadn't seen scarves before, so he explained:

"These are scarves. They can help block the cold wind and keep you warm. With them, you can safely get through this winter."

Upon hearing this, the group of Pokemon seemed visibly moved.

That fire disaster destroyed everything they had.

Homes, friends, family... even they themselves almost died in the fire.

Fortunately, they were saved by D, who temporarily sheltered them, provided them with food, and personally built them little wooden cabins.

Furthermore, D promised to help them rebuild their homes in the future.

Now, worried about them getting cold, he brought warm scarves for them.

Even the most cold-hearted Pokemon can't help but be moved by this.

They could almost imagine what kind of scene they would face this winter without D.

No berries, no shelter, they would freeze to death in the icy snowlands.

Just thinking about it made some Pokemon nearly cry.

Not tears of sadness, but tears of gratitude.

"Alright." D petted Greedent's head: "Stop crying, settle down here comfortably. If you need anything, you can come find me, or if I'm not around, you can ask Indeedee and the others."

Greedent nodded heavily.

D then looked at Tandemaus, having not noticed earlier, he suddenly saw a small addition among them.

D was a bit surprised and then focused his gaze, finding a small white mouse behind the male Tandemaus.

Oh? Has Tandemaus evolved into Maushold?

The little white mouse seemed a bit worried, cautiously hiding behind its parents, watching D in front of it.

But its parents pushed their child forward, as if wanting the human to see their child.

The little mouse stood there timidly.

D raised his eyebrows, "Congratulations, Tandemaus, you've now become parents."

"Squeak squeak~" Tandemaus called out happily.

The arrival of new life is always so joyful, even the other Pokemon felt quite happy about it.

With the cold wind still blowing outside, D didn't stay here long. After giving a scarf to the peacefully sleeping Slakoth, he turned and left the Berry Garden.

After returning home, D began considering his journey to the Galar Region.

Initially, he planned to leave in November, but seeing how the weather would only get colder by then, it seemed better to depart early. Then he could snuggle with the Pokemon by the fireplace, enjoying the snowy view.

"Well... tomorrow seems like a good time to leave." D flipped through the calendar on his desk.

It was already October 8th, and he wasn't sure how long the trip to the Galar Region would take. After pondering, D decided on the departure date.

Tomorrow!

After the time was set, the next step was to decide which Pokemon to take to the Galar Region.

After brief contemplation, D finalized the travel list.

Koraidon and Cyclizar.

This time was different from the last time he went to the Paldea Giant Pit, so he didn't need to worry about safety and thus didn't need many battle-ready Pokemon.

Especially since this trip to the Galar Region wasn't for vacation, and it was simultaneously very cold in The Crown Tundra.

Dragonite would probably prefer staying in a Poke Ball over there; rather than that, it's better to have it stay on the ranch to deliver milk.

Gogoat would remain on the ranch for milking, Naclstack for Rock Salt production, and the other Pokemon with their respective duties.

"Let's leave it at that; next time there's a chance for a vacation, I'll bring everyone along," D thought.

Next was choosing the travel method.

Each region is separated by a vast ocean, and generally, there are three ways to travel to another region:

First, riding your own Pokemon.

Second, taking a ship.

Third, flying on a plane.

The first option was immediately ruled out; it's too cold to ride a Pokemon, and while Koraidon would be fine, he'd be frozen to death by the cold wind.

The second option was viable.

As for the third option...

"Koraidon can't fit into a Poke Ball... seems there's no way to fly," D thought.

From what he knows, current aircraft do not allow passengers to keep large Pokemon outside of Poke Balls, especially not ones like Koraidon, which aren't very tall but are quite heavy.

"So, that leaves only the ship as the option."

The only downside was the lack of a developed network, making it impossible to book tickets online.

For travel to other regions, one had to go to port cities to board a ship.

In the Paldea Region, the cities with ports...

It seems there's only Levincia City.

Levincia City is a technology-rich, commercially vibrant city, with the largest port in the Paldea Region located on its coastline. It's said that commercial ships dock there daily from other regions.

Thinking of this, D opened the Paldea map he previously purchased at the bookstore to check Levincia City's location.

Levincia City... is on the very eastern edge of the Paldea Region.

While he was in Porto Marinada Town, which is situated on the far west side of the Paldea Region, with an entire continent in between.

D: "..."

Oh well, with Koraidon's speed, he should reach it quickly.

After finalizing everything, D began packing the luggage he needed for the trip.

Cold protection gear, camping tent, fire-making tools, pots, blankets, water bottles, thermoses...

Just then, a series of clear hammering sounds rang out from the ranch, as if someone was hammering iron.

D paused and listened carefully for a while; the sound didn't stop.

Curious, he stepped out and followed the direction of the sound, soon finding the culprit in a secluded meadow.

Watching the Tinkatink hammering away at a block of iron, D was a bit surprised.

And at that moment, Tinkatink noticed his arrival and immediately turned around nervously.

D saw that the iron block being hammered had been formed from several nails and looked like it had been hammered for a long time, all flattened.

"Making a hammer?" D asked.

Tinkatink nodded cautiously, "Eep wah..."

Its hammer had been lost before, and it had been diligently collecting iron scraps and nails from the ranch's junk pile, intending to remake a hammer.

Afraid that D might misunderstand it for stealing from the ranch, it quickly said, "Eep wah!"

The iron was gathered from the junk pile, not stolen! It had observed for a long time to realize the junk in that bin was all discarded human items.

Of course, D knew this; he'd already recognized that those rusty nails were the ones he had thrown away after dismantling things from the old house.

Seeing Tinkatink's nervous expression, D couldn't help but say, "No need to be so tense. If you want to make a hammer, just do it. I only came to check because I heard the noise. It's okay, keep hammering!"

Upon hearing this, Tinkatink breathed a sigh of relief.

Cautiously turning around, it was about to continue its work when it noticed D hadn't left yet.

Tinkatink paused, turning back.

D curiously said, "I'm just interested in seeing how you create a hammer."

Crafting a hammer is a natural ability for Tinkatink-type Pokemon.

Before evolving, they are quite weak and need the hammer for protection.

But it still isn't enough because certain metal-eating Pokemon would specifically steal the hammer Tinkatink painstakingly forged and eat it.

Seeing D's genuine curiosity, Tinkatink hesitated for a moment, then turned back around, diligently resuming hammering on the iron.

D observed the scene.

Initially thinking Tinkatink was using its hands to hammer, the reality was a bit different from what he imagined.

In fact, Tinkatink was using its steel-type Metal Claw to constantly hammer the iron scraps, crafting the hammer.

---

---

---

---

# 244: Chapter 241: Departing for Galar! Target: Calyrex!

"Bang!"

The fist wrapped in Steel Type energy pounded on the block of iron below again and again, producing a crisp sound from the collision of metals.

Tinkatink was focused and serious.

Gradually, under its pounding, the iron filings and nails slowly integrated, transforming into a metallic block exuding a silvery sheen.

Tinkatink stopped and picked up the metal block, examining it from side to side, with a slightly furrowed brow, seemingly dissatisfied with its work.

It placed the metal block back on the ground and continued hammering away.

D observed this scene from the side; he could only call Tinkatink's skill of hammering in the void miraculous.

No high-temperature melting, no cooling solidification, just beating the iron into the desired shape by brute force.

This entirely contradicted the physics knowledge D understood.

"Could it be because of the Steel Type energy?" he guessed wildly.

Tinkatink hammered for a while longer, seemingly a bit tired, and stopped to rest with a weary face.

At this moment, Tinkatink turned its head and was startled to see D standing behind it.

It thought D had already left, so why was he still watching here?!

D, filled with curiosity, asked, "Have you ever forged a hammer before?"

"Ooo wow!"

When talking about hammers, Tinkatink's eyes lit up as it nodded.

Once upon a time, it had a hammer, made from metal it collected from an abandoned cabin in the wild!

That hammer had accompanied it for a long time, and it even used that hammer to chase away several metal-eating Pokemons.

Unfortunately, during a previous escape, it accidentally left the hammer in the fire and lost it.

Thinking of this, Tinkatink's expression turned very sad again.

D thought it was feeling sad because of losing the hammer and comforted, "Don't be sad, if you could forge the first one, you can forge the second one!"

Tinkatink thought it over carefully and realized this was indeed the case.

Its small face immediately filled with determination, and it clenched its fists: "Ooo wow!"

"Then keep busy, I won't disturb you." D patted Tinkatink's head, bid it farewell, and turned back to the house.

Soon after, the crisp sound of hammering echoed again in the ranch.

D stood in the living room listening for a while, then returned to the bedroom and took out a book from the desk.

It was a biography describing the wild Pokemon ecology in the Paldea Region, which he previously bought from the town bookstore.

D recalled it contained detailed records about Pokemon like Tinkatink.

He flipped through the book and indeed found content related to Tinkatink inside.

"Tinkatink is a very special Pokemon, like Miltank and Tauros, it is a single-gender species."

"This matches what I know." D still clearly remembered some Pokemon information from his past life.

"People think Tinkaton is a solitary living Pokemon, but that's not the case; they're genuinely communal Pokemons."

"The Tinkaton live with Tinkatuff and Tinkatink in places filled with scrap metal, possibly ancient castle ruins, or abandoned human ruins."

"There's metal everywhere in these places. Tinkatink, lacking strength, collects iron filings and metal pieces to forge hammers."

"When they evolve into Tinkatuff, these rusty metals can no longer meet their requirements, so they actively search for Bisharp to collect tougher metals from them."

"And when Tinkatuff evolves into Tinkaton, they seek higher strength metals by attacking the poor Corviknight, collecting feathers of the same material as steel to create the Final Hammer."

"The strength of Tinkaton is tremendous, ordinary hammers can't satisfy them. Researchers found through investigations that the hammer of every Tinkaton weighs no less than one hundred kilograms."

"Despite being so heavy, Tinkaton can easily swing it, showing their immense strength and truly being Huge Power."

"Thus, as Tinkatink evolves, the requirements for their hammer materials gradually increase," D thought to himself.

From ordinary scrap metal, to the metal on Bisharp, and finally the steel feathers of Corviknight.

The reason D was researching this information was that he wanted to make a hammer for his Pawmo.

Then letting it electrify the hammer, paired with that red cloak, all the props for Pawmo to cosplay as a Thunder God would be ready.

He hadn't had the chance before, but now D saw the possibility in Tinkatink.

Could he request Tinkatink to forge a hammer for Pawmo?

D fell into deep thought.

Perhaps he could help Tinkatink collect some Corviknight feathers in advance.

Some could be given to Tinkatink as payment for forging the hammer, and the rest to make Pawmo's Mjolnir.

"Conveniently, there are plenty of Corviknight residing in the Galar Region, I could try collecting some on my visit," D thought to himself.

The Galar Region and the Paldea Region are different; Tinkaton is absent there.

Without natural enemies, Corviknight are unmatched in the skies of the Galar Region, and everyone can see them soaring freely through the sky.

---

Not only that, some clever Corviknight have also learned to cooperate with humans, working at the Galar Transport Company, established by humans, to provide flying taxi services to transport guests to various towns.

There must be people collecting feathers shed by Corviknight in such places. By then, he can just buy some.

*

League Calendar Year 198, October 9th.

That morning, after finishing the work at the ranch, D said goodbye to everyone and rode Koraidon to the other end of the Paldea Region, to Levincia City.

In mid-air, after confirming the direction of Levincia City on the map, D directed Koraidon to fly towards that direction.

While riding on Koraidon, he observed the map of the Paldea Region.

The map in reality is not the same as the map in the game.

To be precise, the map here is larger and more expansive than the one in the game, with a more complex and diverse terrain.

Additionally, between some of the famous large towns, there are many small villages and towns not present in the game.

Places like Sunnytown, Dewford Town, and Bullfighting City.

"If I have the chance, I might come to visit here later," D thought.

According to the travel guide included with the map, Sunnytown is a rustic town surrounded by Sunflora Fields where humans and Sunflora live together.

As for Dewford Town, it's said to be named because a large number of Toedscool inhabit the area near the village.

And Bullfighting City, it's said to be a big city full of traditional customs of Paldea, where many passionate bullfighters gather, performing intense and exciting bullfighting techniques with their captured Tauros in the arena.

After a long flight lasting several hours, D finally formally arrived in the eastern region of Paldea.

There's still some distance to Levincia City, so he rode Koraidon and overlooked the fields and cities below.

Looking at the modern high-rise buildings, D suddenly felt that Eastern Paldea is more modern and urbanized than the west.

When Koraidon reached above Levincia City, this feeling grew even stronger.

Skyscrapers towered, cars flooded the city streets, and by now it was already evening, with shops on both sides of the road turning on their lights early, the flickering neon lights turning the entire Levincia City into a dreamy kingdom.

For a moment, D felt as if he had entered another world.

Compared to this place, Porto Marinada Town is simply a very backward rural town!

"This is incredible!"

D was dazzled by the radiant lights.

He watched beams of neon lights shoot up high from the city, softly swaying and intertwining in the night, making the whole city appear dreamlike and picturesque, like a perfect scene.

Wait a minute!

D suddenly realized what he was looking at.

This is one of Paldea's Ten Views, the Million Volt Skyline!

He couldn't help but admit how beautiful it is!

Koraidon seemed to be stunned by this sight as well.

D heard it let out a soft call.

"Ahh-rooo..."

D didn't speak, he and Koraidon just quietly gazed at the lights below.

Until someone else arrived, riding a flying Pokemon.

The person took a camera with their companion, looking excited: "As expected of Paldea's Ten Views, it's truly beautiful!"

The companion quickly urged: "Take the picture quickly! These are the memories of our trip!"

D jolted awake, remembering they still had important things to do.

"Let's land!"

"Ah-roo~"

Koraidon responded happily, then slowly descended into Levincia City.

Upon entering the city, that sense of disparity grew even stronger.

D looked around, noticing the fashionable people dressed in suits and stylish attire, some of whom cast glances towards him.

D: "..."

Could it be that someone might ridicule him for being a hillbilly from the sticks?

He remembered that Ash from the anime was mocked like that before.

However, no one did such a thing; they merely seemed curious about the sudden landing of D and Koraidon.

Seeing this, D felt relieved and shifted his focus back to the task at hand, preparing to go with Koraidon to the port to buy a ticket to the Galar Region.

They passed through the streets, following the city's road signs towards the port.

"Cross the Central Square located in the city center, then pass over the sea bridge to reach Levincia Light Tower; the boarding area is near the lighthouse," D silently summarized the route and recorded it in his mind.

He took Koraidon towards the Central Square, and when they arrived there, they suddenly found the Central Square to be very lively.

Many Trainers with Pokemon excitedly rushed in that direction.

"The battle at Levincia Gym is about to start!"

"Don't block my way, I'm going to see Lono!"

"Ahhh, who pushed me?"

A crowd swarmed over, and D and Koraidon were squeezed to the edges of the Central Square.

D: "..."

When the crowd finally settled down, D could see the scene around him clearly.

In front, a sea of people and Pokemon surrounded the perimeter of the Central Square, observing the spectacle inside.

The so-called Central Square is actually a massive circular Pokemon battle arena, surrounded by bright, dazzling neon lights.

At this moment, someone was having a Pokemon battle in the arena.

When he got a clear look at one of them, D was slightly taken aback.

Blue and pink mixed hair, cute Magnemite hair accessories...

Is that...Lono?

At this moment, the battle on the stage hadn't begun.

Lono's eyes swept across the spectators around the square, grinning, revealing a set of beautiful shark-like teeth.

"Everyone~! It's time for another Lono performance~! "

The crowd burst into a lively cheer.

Lono responded with accustomed laughter: "Good morning, afternoon, and evening, hello Ciao! "

The Trainer standing opposite her looked admiringly.

Lono grinned, and her voice was cute and playful:

"Are you all ready~?"

"Capture your attention with Electroweb! Who's here to see me? Lono's here!"

"Today's challenger is~? Riding high, the Trainer Komatsu who can even take down Wattrel flying in the sky~!"

"..."

"So it is Lono," D thought, filled with slight confusion.

The time was still early, and Lono had already become a Gym Leader?

If he remembered correctly, Lono seemed to also be a streamer.

However, now Lono didn't appear to be streaming, only conducting an exhibition match.

"It should be because the streaming industry hasn't emerged at this time," D thought.

At this moment, Koraidon, noticing D's expression, its eyes lit up with excitement: "Ahh-rooo!"

"Hmm?" D looked at Koraidon in confusion.

Could it be that Koraidon also likes watching Lono's type of live streams?

Perceiving D's gaze, Koraidon turned its head, still somewhat excited: "Ah-roo!"

D curiously asked: "You like her?"

It seems like it's Koraidon's first time seeing Lono, right?

D recalled that although Lono was a Gym Leader, she didn't seem very famous yet, her fame not having reached Porto Marinada Town in Western Paldea.

Although Lono is indeed quite cute...

In that flash, countless thoughts raced through D's mind.

For example, could Pokemon fall in love with humans? Can Pokemon feel love at first sight with humans?

If he remembered correctly, the Meowth in the anime seemed to have fallen in love with a human.

Under his suspicious gaze, Koraidon nodded its big head repeatedly: "Ah-roo!"

Yet, Koraidon wasn't thinking that much; it was simply happy to see Lono.

Because before it became Koraidon, Lono took care of it and everyone at the ranch.

More importantly, she had a little master.

D named the little master Shou, and he remembered that D even changed the ranch's name to "House of Trees," where everyone lived happily together.

The little master...

In Koraidon's mind, the scene replayed of when it left, the little master pitifully holding onto it, asking when it would return.

Yet it couldn't go back.

Thinking of this, Koraidon's mood inevitably turned somber.

"Ahh-rooo..."

D: ?

```

# 245: Chapter 242: The Crown Tundra!

Under D's curious gaze, Koraidon's not-so-bright brain began to think.

It looked at the lively Lono bouncing in the sea of neon lights, and then glanced at the low-key D in front of it.

Koraidon suddenly realized that this time is still too early; its little master hasn't been born yet, and D doesn't seem to recognize Lono.

"Ah gaaah..."

Koraidon's head drooped down.

D carefully recalled and discovered that Koraidon seemed to not have eaten much at noon.

It's already late now. Could it be that it's hungry and doesn't want to stay here?

Thinking of this, D asked, "Hungry?"

Koraidon's attention was slightly diverted, and it carefully felt, indeed hearing a growling noise from its stomach.

It nodded its big head, "Ah gaaah!"

D couldn't help but laugh, "Alright, then let's not watch anymore. Let's go buy boat tickets and eat on the ship."

"Ah gaaah~"

Koraidon gladly agreed.

So D didn't continue to stay, but turned and left this place with Koraidon.

Passing through the Central Square, D saw the towering lighthouse on the coast, with a brightly lit building nearby, and several giant ships docked on the sea on the other side of the building.

That's the departure point.

The departure point at night was still bustling, with tourists traveling to other regions and Trainers from other regions coming to the Paldea Region.

Just as D arrived there, he heard a melodious whistle sound from the sea, as if a cruise ship was about to dock on the shore.

D saw that the staff at the departure point started to get busy.

Soon, the passage at the exit was opened, and a lively group of humans and Pokémon carrying large bags came out from inside.

"One ticket to the Galar Region."

D approached the ticket counter and spoke to the young lady at the ticket booth.

The ticket seller glanced through the window at the young man outside and the large Pokémon beside him, and with a gentle smile, she said:

"Alright, the nearest cruise departs at 7:25 PM, with the destination being Hulbury Town in the Galar Region. The journey is expected to take three days. Would you like to take this route?"

Hulbury Town...

In D's mind appeared a lively port town with many shops and restaurants.

He nodded, "Sure."

Ticket Seller: "The total is 3200 League Coins."

D paid the money.

Now that the time had reached evening, D received the ticket from her, with half an hour left until the ship departs.

He didn't loiter at the departure point but followed the staff's guidance to the port, smoothly boarding the ship.

The ship wasn't large, but its facilities were quite complete.

Restaurants, gym, barbershop, battlefield, Trainer exchange area, etc.

By now, plenty of passengers heading to the Galar Region had gathered on the ship.

Business people in suits, elegant upper-class women, young and passionate Pokémon Trainers...

Some people stayed on deck admiring the distant neon lights, while others gathered at the restaurant, enjoying with their Pokémon.

D also sat at the dining table with Cyclizar and Koraidon.

Soon, a server in uniform brought them the menu.

"Grilled steak, Mapo tofu, soy sauce fried rice, omelet rice, sashimi, sushi, ham… quite a diverse range," D was somewhat surprised and casually ordered a few dishes as per his usual eating habits.

Shortly, the food was served, and D tasted it, finding the flavor unexpectedly good.

He looked up at the two Pokémon in front of him and, lo and behold, saw them eating happily.

Seeing his Pokémon happy, D was happy too. He smiled and said to them, "Eat up, we're going to stay here for three days."

"Ga-roar~"

"Ah gaaah~"

The two Pokémon didn't mind. To them, as long as they can stay by D's side, it didn't matter where he took them.

D smiled faintly and was about to focus on enjoying his meal when he heard a conversation from the table behind him.

"Ah, it's a pity to miss Lono's Gym Leader performance for catching this ship!"

Another person seemed a bit speechless, "Hey, aren't you obsessed? But honestly, how old is Lono this year? I somehow feel like she's just a Trainer starting out on her journey."

"Not at all! Hmm, I heard Lono became a Gym Leader two years ago, and back then she was only fourteen. Now she should be sixteen, almost seventeen, right? Such a vibrant teenage girl, who's not enamored? That's Lono's charm!"

"..."

D silently listened to this, feeling slightly surprised.

Sixteen years old? Four years younger than him...

Wait, is Lono really that old?

Wouldn't that mean by the start of the game she'd be twenty-five or twenty-six?

She totally doesn't look like it!

Could it be she looks youthful? Doesn't show age easily?

But thinking it over, the Pokémon World is based on the real world, and overseas live streams have age requirements, needing to be at least 18 years old to broadcast.

---

NPCs in the game have mentioned that Lono has been live streaming for many years...

Clearly, Lono was already over twenty at that time.

"That makes sense," D calculated carefully in his mind.

But none of this really mattered to him.

D didn't think much of it. After finishing his meal, he took Koraidon and Cyclizar to the deck, ordered a few glasses of juice, and lay down on a beach chair to gaze at the stars.

*

Three days later, this ship arrived at the Galar Region on time, docking at the port of Hulbury Town.

D, looking travel-worn, disembarked with Koraidon, taking an interested stroll through this port town.

The temperature here was a bit warmer than in the Paldea Region.

The town's roads were very neat, with railings on both sides to prevent tourists from falling into the sea, while a tall wall with a medieval style stood on the edge of the town.

The blend of medieval and modern architecture added a strong sense of fantasy to this town.

Along the way, D saw parks, shops, street stalls, flower shops...

On those clean and tidy lawns, he also saw several Pokémon unique to the Galar Region.

For example, a Yamper happily running while generating electricity from the base of its tail on the lawn.

Or a Blipbug quietly observing everything in the park below from a tree branch.

A Sobble playing with water beside the fountain.

"Sobble, Galar Region's Water Type starter, looks pretty cute," D thought.

Unknowingly, he walked Koraidon over to where the Sobble was.

The Sobble, playing in the water, suddenly sensed a terrifying aura. It looked up to see a large Pokémon it had never seen before glaring at it menacingly.

Sobble's body went stiff and it started sobbing loudly in fear, tears gushing out.

Seeing this, D quickly said: "Don't be afraid, it won't hurt you..."

But as he was about to finish, D suddenly noticed a slight change in his surroundings.

Next, his eyes started to get wet, and tears flowed uncontrollably.

D was bewildered for a second, and hearing Koraidon's growl behind him, he turned to see Koraidon also inexplicably crying.

D: "?"

Seizing this opportunity, Sobble's color and pattern rapidly changed, blending in with the surroundings and disappearing from sight!

Koraidon noticed this scene and exclaimed: "Aga-sis!"

D looked down and realized that Sobble had vanished.

At this moment, someone was drawn to the commotion over here.

It was a tall, model-like girl with brown skin, blue eyes, wearing a swimsuit that flattered her figure.

She stood a short distance away, observing the scene and assessing this foreigner, and said calmly,

"That's a Sobble. This type of Pokémon is a bit timid, and the tears they shed when they're scared contain a potent tear-inducing substance, causing everyone in the vicinity to burst into tears, giving them a chance to make their escape."

Hearing the voice, D and Koraidon, still teary-eyed, turned and saw a familiar figure.

Was it... Nessa?

Nessa, the Water-type Gym Leader of the Galar Region, who also worked as a model.

D was slightly taken aback.

Nessa, looking at the tears on his face, gave her advice: "Although that substance is harmless to the body, I still recommend that you go to a Pokemon Center to check it out, otherwise this state will last for a long time."

D: "..."

Ten minutes later, he and Koraidon, still in tears, arrived at the Pokemon Center.

The moment Miss Joy of the Galar Region saw them, she knew what had happened and quickly applied eye drops to their eyes.

Looking at the reddened eyes of the human and Pokémon, Miss Joy couldn't help but ask: "Trainers from other regions, right?"

D nodded. Koraidon was not a Pokémon from the Galar Region, which was obvious at a glance.

Miss Joy chuckled, "It must have been hard for you two. The substance released by Sobble has a tear-inducing effect equivalent to 100 onions; if you get caught by accident, you'll keep crying until your tears run dry!"

D: "..."

Now he finally understood why Nessa suggested he come to the Pokemon Center.

Thanks to the eye drops provided by Miss Joy, that horrible state quickly vanished.

D rubbed his eyes, thanked Miss Joy, and then left the Pokemon Center.

Returning to that park, Nessa was nowhere to be seen.

"I wanted to thank her," D thought with regret, but since he had important things to do, he decided to put it off until he had a chance later.

The next destination...

D checked the signs in Hulbury Town and quickly found the exact location of Hulbury Town Station.

Ten minutes later, D officially arrived at Hulbury Town Station.

The ticket seller lady smiled at him and asked, "Where do you want to go?"

D: "The Crown Tundra."

"Ah?" Hearing this name, the ticket seller lady was slightly taken aback, then she realized and smiled, "You mean Crown Tundra, right?"

D then realized he mentioned the name from the Sword and Shield DLC and, embarrassed, nodded: "Yes, Crown Tundra."

---

The ticket lady couldn't help but chuckle and said, "I've never seen a Pokémon like that before, are you Trainers from another region?"

"Yes," D replied with a smile, "from the Paldea Region, planning to visit a friend in The Crown Tundra."

"I see," the ticket lady said, "Welcome to the Galar Region. Here, this is the Crown ticket to The Crown Tundra. Please keep it safe, and once you enter the station, you can show this ticket to board the train."

"Alright."

D nodded, holding the ticket as he entered the station. Following the signs, he quickly found the steam train heading to The Crown Tundra.

He boarded the train with Koraidon, and soon, the train began to clatter along.

The scenery outside rapidly zipped past.

Having never been on a train before, Koraidon and Cyclizar pressed their faces curiously against the window, gazing at the view outside.

D looked around and noticed to his surprise that the entire carriage was quite empty, with only him as a passenger.

"Whoa!"

Is The Crown Tundra really that unpopular?

D seriously tried to recall The Crown Tundra from the game.

He remembered it as a harsh, cold land with towering snow-capped mountains, where people lived in small villages, relying on each other due to the extreme weather and terrain.

This kind of place indeed doesn't seem suited for traveling. D thought.

But that shouldn't be the case, right?

If he remembered correctly, The Crown Tundra was scattered with various Ancient Ruins and Shrines.

Places like these are favorites among explorers and ruin enthusiasts, so The Crown Tundra should be quite popular with them.

Could it really be because it's too cold?

D thought it couldn't be that extreme, could it? Isn't it just a tundra? As long as you wear enough layers, it should be fine.

However, it turned out to be just that extreme.

When the train arrived at its destination, D exited the station with Koraidon.

A gust of cold wind hit, and the next moment, he was shivering uncontrollably from the extreme temperature.

"Holy crap, it's this cold!"

D's teeth began to chatter; his winter clothes offered no protection against the cold!

He had indeed underestimated the harsh environment of The Crown Tundra!

But there were no stores selling winter gear around, just the station behind him.

D let out a puff of white breath and forced himself to calm down, surveying the area, seeing nothing but an endless expanse of white snow.

The tundra was covered with hardy trees, bearing thick layers of snow.

In the distance were a series of steep, continuous cliffs, the terrain was incredibly complex and treacherous.

"Calyrex should be in this place," D thought.

But the problem was, he had no idea where to find Calyrex.

The Crown Shrine? Or perhaps some insignificant cave?

At this moment, Koraidon looked over with some concern.

D reassured, "I'm fine, how about you? Can you still manage?"

"Argh!" Koraidon nodded its big head happily.

"Good." D patted it, feeling that Koraidon was quite warm.

In this environment, for a Pokémon as strong as Koraidon, it might not be much of an issue.

D looked at the road sign next to the road in front of the station, indicating the direction to Freezington Village.

After a brief moment of contemplation, D decided to head to the nearby Freezington Village to gather some information first.

So he set off with Koraidon, heading toward Freezington Village.
