# 246: Chapter 243: The Forgotten King

The sky began to snow, white snowflakes covering everything, blurring the distant scenery.

The blizzard raged on, with visibility less than ten meters. D and Koraidon hadn't walked far before they found themselves disoriented in this snowy landscape.

"Which way is east, which way is west..."

D kept rubbing his hands, trying to determine the direction by observing the position of the sun.

However, in this weather, the entire sky was gray; forget about the sun, he couldn't even see the road ahead clearly.

D immediately felt a bit overwhelmed, and the surrounding temperature was so low.

Having lived in a warm and humid environment, he found it hard to bear after being here for just a short time. How did Calyrex manage to live here?

"Azi...?"

Koraidon shook the snow off its head with force and looked at D worriedly, as if asking him what to do next.

The cold wind howled through the valley. D glanced at the road ahead buried in snow and said to Koraidon:

"We might have already deviated from our original path."

"Azi?"

"We can't go on like this." D shook his head and thought that if they continued, Koraidon would be fine, but he might not withstand the environment and something could go wrong.

After a brief moment of thought, D directed his gaze towards the nearby steep cliffs.

"Let's go over there, walk along the mountain, and see if we can find a cave. We'll set out again after the snow stops!"

"Azi!"

The two immediately ran to the base of the cliffs and began exploring along the icy mountain.

Perhaps they were fortunate from eating a few Lucky Puddings earlier; it wasn't long before Koraidon discovered a snow-covered cave entrance ahead.

Koraidon's eyes immediately lit up and hurried ahead, standing at the entrance waiting for D.

D quickly followed, cautiously observed inside, and seeing no trace of other Pokemon, he led Koraidon inside.

The cave wasn't deep, and there was nothing inside. It didn't look like a Pokemon's lair but rather a natural mountain cave.

Snow fell heavily outside, with occasional cold gusts sweeping in.

D breathed a sigh of relief, shook the snow off himself, then quickly set up a simple campfire and pot from his travel backpack, pouring a small bag of Charcoal to start a fire.

He then, with frozen fingers, pulled out two bottles of drinking water and a bag of noodles from his pack.

Koraidon sat in a position closer to the entrance, using its large body to shield D from the wind and snow.

D warmed himself by the fire while waiting for the water to boil, telling Koraidon, "Just a little longer."

"Azi～"

Soon, the water was boiling.

D added the noodles, seasoning them with portable spices. After waiting a few minutes, a fragrant aroma began to fill the air.

"All set!"

D took out two bowls, served the noodles evenly, and poured the broth, completing a simple hot noodle soup.

D lowered his head to observe, and the effect of the hot noodle soup appeared in his mind.

[Hot Noodle Soup (B-): A simple dish, yet a cozy choice to warm the stomach in the cold winter.

Cooking Effect: Cold Resistance lv2, effectively resisting severe cold, warming the body upon consumption for 2 hours.

Rating: Slight.]

"2 hours..."

Despite the effect of the hot noodle soup being somewhat reduced due to simple ingredients and rough cooking methods, two hours was still pretty good.

D looked up to see Koraidon already tilting its head back, sipping it in.

He too swiftly finished his small bowl of soup, feeling his body gradually warm up, letting out a comfortable sigh.

"Finally, it's not as cold anymore."

The blizzard outside no longer affected them at all.

D thought about seizing the opportunity to explore outside, maybe finding the location of Freezington Village.

But after thinking carefully, the snow might last for an unknown duration, and the anti-cold effect of the hot noodle soup only lasted 2 hours.

If the effect wore off midway and they hadn't found Freezington Village, that would be problematic.

After much consideration, D decided it was better to stay and wait for the snow to stop.

He and Koraidon sat by the fire, warming themselves.

Time ticked away, and the sky outside remained dim and gray, making it impossible for D to determine whether it was noon or evening.

He could only gauge the passage of time through the duration of the hot noodle soup's effect.

When the first round of noodle soup's effect wore off, D cooked two more bowls.

This time, he used enough ingredients and cooked more carefully, increasing the Cooking Effect from lv2 to lv3 and extending the duration from two to six hours.

The snow outside had not stopped.

"Looks like we'll have to spend the night here!" D sighed helplessly.

"Azi～" Koraidon had no objections.

And just then, there was a sudden rustle of footsteps amidst the heavy snow outside.

D turned his head and saw an elderly man, bundled up like a big bear, holding a lantern, quickly approaching the cave entrance from outside.

The old man's lantern illuminated the dim cave, immediately spotting the human and Pokemon inside.

---

He quickly walked into the cave, placing the down jacket he had with him on D, who wore a bewildered expression.

"You're from outside, right?" the old man asked.

Koraidon tilted its head in confusion.

D was equally puzzled. He nodded and asked, "And you are?"

"I could tell you were from out of town, wearing so little here is very dangerous. Even hiding in a cave is unreliable, you might freeze to death!"

The old man spoke with a serious expression before explaining his identity:

"I'm a villager from Freezington Village. A few hours ago, my Swablu found you lost in the snow and went back to the village to call for help."

As he spoke, the old man turned his head to look at his shoulder.

Only then did D notice a small, sky-blue bird-type Pokemon with fluffy, cotton-like feathers perched there.

Upon hearing its owner's words, the Swablu named Swablu flapped its wings joyfully.

Seeing this, D finally understood what had happened.

The old man and the Swablu had come specifically to rescue those lost in the snow!

Although they didn't need rescuing, D was still grateful: "Thank you very much, Swablu."

"Chirp chirp!"

The lively Swablu hopped twice in response.

Old man: "Follow me back to the village first! When the snow stops, it will get even colder here, and it will be very dangerous then!"

"Thank you so much!"

Just as they needed to go to Freezington Village, D readily agreed.

He packed up the items in the cave and then followed the old man with Koraidon in tow.

Guided by the Swablu flying in the air, they finally did not get lost again. After passing through the deep snow, D saw a small village nestled in a serene valley amidst ice and snow.

"This is Freezington Village!" The old man grinned widely, "Although I don't know where you want to go, you can rest here for a while before setting off again."

D nodded: "Thank you very much, may I know how to address you?"

Old man: "Just call me Riley. Come on, bring your Pokemon and have a good rest at my place!"

Riley took D and Koraidon back to his home, letting them warm up by the fireplace.

D felt slightly moved. To be honest, the people here were quite kind.

At this moment, an elderly woman with silver hair came over, carrying two cups of steaming tea.

Her smile was gentle: "Have some hot tea to warm yourself up!"

"Thank you!"

D reached out to accept it. Coincidentally, the effect of the hot soup noodles had worn off, but with the fireplace in front of him and the cup of hot tea, he didn't feel cold at all; rather he felt cozy.

The elderly woman gently said, "My husband is a very warm-hearted old man. He often rescues travelers like you who get lost in the mountains."

D nodded gently: "Mr. Riley is a very nice person."

At this moment, the Swablu landed on the windowsill, curiously watching them.

D noticed this scene.

The elderly woman followed his gaze and smiled, saying, "This Swablu was also rescued by my husband from the mountains. At that time, its wing was injured and it was lying in the snow. The poor little thing was almost frozen stiff."

"My husband brought it back, and we planned to release it after it recovered, but this little fellow refused to leave."

"Since then, it has been living with us, helping my husband rescue lost travelers from the mountains."

Swablu seemed to hear that the old woman was talking about it, so it immediately flew up joyfully, landing in the old woman's arms, chirping happily.

At this moment, Riley, having changed his clothes, came out of the room.

Once he changed his clothes, he looked completely different, from a big old bear to a spry, sharp little old man.

With heavy snow still falling outside, D couldn't go out, so they sat indoors and chatted.

Upon learning that he had come from the Paldea Region, Riley showed a very surprised expression: "Paldea Region? That's very far away!"

"Not really, it takes only three days by ship. If you're talking about distance, I think the Kanto Region is far away," D smiled.

Riley nodded, looking at Koraidon beside D. Although he was old and a bit out of touch with current times, he still understood the profession of a Trainer.

Thus, he asked, "That's true. Are you traveling in the Galar Region?"

Speaking of this, Riley couldn't help but show a puzzled expression.

Traveling in the Galar Region, shouldn't you go to those big cities up north? Why come to this remote little place like Freezington Village?

D saw his confusion and explained, "Actually, I'm not here for tourism. The reason I came to the Crown Tundra is because I'm searching for the legendary Pokemon, The King of Bountiful Harvests."

"What? Something about The King of Bountiful Harvests?" Riley pondered, struggling to recall: "I remember it's something like Calyrex? Or maybe Calygruntang?"

D: "… It's Calyrex, isn't it?"

Riley sheepishly scratched his head: "Oh, it's called Calyrex?"

---

Riley's wife, the old woman beside him, looked at her husband with a hint of helplessness.

She carefully recalled and said, "The King of Bountiful Harvests... I used to believe it was real. It's in the stories my grandmother told me when I was a child. She said that if you were naughty in the fields, the king would take your body away. But now, I think it's just something to scare kids, right?"

Indeed...

Although some people in Freezington Village have heard of Calyrex The King of Bountiful Harvests, they don't believe it truly exists, thinking it's just a legend.

Let alone faith.

For Calyrex The King of Bountiful Harvests, who derives power from faith, this is undoubtedly fatal.

Without faith, Calyrex's power weakened, even Glastrier and Spectrier abandoned it...

And the root cause of all this, was it the Galar Royal Family who claimed the credit of repelling the Darkest Day with Zacian and Zamazenta as their own?

D fell into contemplation.

Regarding this, the game does not explain in detail.

But judging from the actions of the Galar Royal Family, it is highly likely that in order to maintain their rule and image in people's minds, they also took away Calyrex's achievements.

As time moved on, and kingdoms changed, Calyrex disappeared from people's hearts and the river of history.

The King of Galar was forgotten by its subjects.

The loss of faith made Calyrex's power gradually weaken, and Spectrier and Glastrier abandoned it.

D's heart was very complicated.

At this moment, the snow stopped outside the house.

Riley and his wife said joyfully, "It finally stopped!"

Then, Riley looked at D and said to him, "If you're not in a hurry to leave, you can stay in the village for a while longer. The snow scenery in Freezington Village is quite worth seeing."

The old woman next to him also said, "During this period, you can stay with us! Usually, it's just me and the old man living here. It would be more lively with another guest!"

"Thank you very much." D expressed his gratitude.

The Swablu joyfully chirped as it perched on his shoulder.

Riley laughed and said, "Let's have Shelly take you on a tour of the village!"

D looked at the Swablu and smiled, saying to it, "Thank you too."

"Chirp~!"

The Swablu let out a happy growl.

D walked out the door with Koraidon and began to wander around the small village on the snow plain.

Overall, the village is not large, with only more than twenty households.

Several pieces of farmland are opened up in the center of the village, with some cold-resistant crops planted in them.

Snow-covered roads connect the houses together.

Perhaps because the snow had stopped, people started stepping out of their houses. In the blink of an eye, the village, which had been silent for quite some time, became bustling.

Farmers used Mamoswine to pull carts, which were filled with goods.

The Swablu perched on the farm fence, alongside several geometric snowflakes swaying in mid-air.

It's hard to imagine that there is such a vibrant scene in this frigid land.

At this moment, the Swablu suddenly left his shoulder and flew into the farmland, landing on a tall snow mound.

The Swablu hopped around.

D was somewhat surprised. He suddenly remembered something from the game and stepped forward to brush off the snow, discovering a fragmented stone statue beneath.

"Indeed..."

Just as D was about to say something, Koraidon turned its gaze to the distance.

"Ah-gah-shhh."

D lifted his head, his eyes peering beyond the statue, under a snow-covered pine not far away, he saw a small, elegant, yet majestic mysterious Pokemon.

D was slightly taken aback, the two gazing across the vast snowfield.

At this moment, time seemed to stop.

That was... Calyrex!

However, looking at Calyrex, an untimely thought suddenly popped into D's mind.

"What a big head!!"

---

---

---

---

# 247: Chapter 244: The King's Promise

"No, doing so might be a bit disrespectful to the Calyrex..."

D hurriedly shook his head, tossing that bold idea out of his mind.

However, he couldn't help but glance at the huge dark green bud on top of Calyrex's head, resembling a crown.

Below the enormous crown was a delicate, slender body alike a rabbit's.

Calyrex had a pair of majestic blue eyes, beneath which were a small blue nose and mouth.

Behind it were wings that resembled a cape, and beneath them was its grayish-white body and slender, pure white legs.

Calyrex quietly levitated over the snowy ground, gazing in this direction.

From its mouth came sounds humans couldn't understand:

"Caly... mooji ji..."

D: "?"

At this moment, an uncle wearing a furry hat and carrying a hoe came over, ready to work in the fields.

Upon seeing D and Koraidon standing in a daze, he was about to ask where this guy came from but then saw a mysterious glowing Pokemon levitating in the snow not far away.

The uncle, with a face full of shock, couldn't help but exclaim: "Such... such a big head!"

D: "..."

Good gracious, someone just said what was on his mind!

Calyrex was also drawn to the sudden arrival of the human.

A blue light flashed in its eyes, and then its entire body flickered to appear before the stunned uncle.

The uncle hadn't reacted yet when he felt his body losing control, his consciousness gradually becoming blurry. His feet floated off the ground, and his body became enveloped in the same faint glow as Calyrex.

Calyrex levitated beside this human, then turned to look at its little devotee, using its spiritual power to manipulate the human's body to speak:

"My devotee, you have finally arrived. I have been waiting for you here for a lengthy period."

D glanced at the poor uncle being used as a mouthpiece by Calyrex and couldn't help but say, "Actually, we could have a better way to communicate."

"Oh?" A hint of doubt flashed in Calyrex's eyes.

D took out a big package of Translation Nuts Cake from his backpack.

Calyrex's gaze was drawn to it, asking, "What is that?"

D explained, "A piece of human cuisine endowed with magical effects, eating it allows you to communicate with Pokemon."

Calyrex had never heard of such a thing.

In its curious yet majestic gaze, D chomped on several pieces and then said, "Alright, now I can understand what you're saying."

Upon hearing this, Calyrex ceased controlling the uncle.

Perhaps it didn't want humans to notice it had been here, so Calyrex used Teleport, leaving a message for D before disappearing.

"I will be waiting for you at the cliff over there."

With those words, Calyrex disappeared from its spot.

Freed from control, the uncle slowly landed, looking around in bewilderment, scratching the back of his head:

"What happened? Why am I here, ouch... my body feels so cold! Oh wait, who are you? How did you end up in the village? Are you a tourist from elsewhere?"

D casually cooperated, saying, "That's right, I'm here to observe the Pokemon ecology in the Crown Tundra."

The uncle looked enlightened: "I see!"

After saying this, the man shouldered his hoe and left.

D then looked at the Swablu on his shoulder, telling it, "You should head back first, we have something to do."

The temperature outside felt even colder than when it was snowing, and Swablu huddled in its fluffy feathers, pathetically nodding.

"Chirp chirp!" (I'll be off; you guys have to be careful! Don't get lost again!)

D chuckled, nodding, "Don't worry!"

Only then did Swablu turn and flew towards home.

After watching its figure leave, D rode Koraidon and flew to the cliff pointed out by Calyrex.

When they arrived, Calyrex was already waiting there.

It levitated on the snow-covered precipice, overlooking Freezington Village below, like a king standing on a castle gazing at his subjects.

Hearing the sound, Calyrex turned around.

Gazing at this devotee who held it in high reverence, it solemnly expressed its gratitude:

"I have felt your dedication; everything you've done for me on that land, I have seen it all."

Upon hearing these words, D felt somewhat embarrassed; his original goal seemed just to be for Calyrex's two steeds?

But saying such things at a time like this would ruin the atmosphere.

Thus, D earnestly said, "That's what I'm supposed to do, the people of the Paldea Region are very grateful for your power."

Thinking of those who crafted statues and established festivals in gratitude for it, a touch of emotion surfaced in Calyrex's eyes.

Its voice became gentle: "I know all of it... because of your presence, I was able to recover some strength."

Yet, comparatively, its subjects were entirely different.

Though unaware of external happenings, Calyrex could sense through its weakening power that it was losing human faith.

To this, the former King of Galar felt an internal sadness.

Scenes from the past floated in its mind.

---

At that time, under His rule, the Galar Region was so prosperous, with bountiful harvests and flourishing flowers.

The humans and Pokemon living on this land regarded Him as King, and He, in turn, treated these beings as His own people.

Humans and Pokemon adored their King, built shrines for Him, and offered tributes, while as King, He tried His best to bring abundance to His people.

Thinking of this, Calyrex felt a pang in His heart, unwilling to believe that His people had already forgotten Him.

Calyrex turned to D, "My faithful! I have a request for you!"

D could feel the sadness of Calyrex and nodded, "Please tell me."

Calyrex cast His gaze toward the human village below, "I want to ask you to go there and inquire for me if they still remember Me."

"As expected, Calyrex is very concerned about this..." D thought to himself.

He responded, agreeing to Calyrex's request.

"Thank you very much," Calyrex said to him, "I will wait for you here."

D nodded silently, then rode on Koraidon and flew down to Freezington Village.

On the way, Koraidon frowned, somewhat puzzled, and asked, "Ah gah si..." (He seems very sad...)

D could somewhat understand Calyrex's feelings and nodded, saying:

"Calyrex is likely saddened, right? It's like you and me, living together always, then being separated due to some circumstances, and when we met again, you found I didn't remember you..."

Before finishing his words, Koraidon hastily said, "Ah gah si!" (No separation!)

D was slightly taken aback and then smiled, patting its head, "I know we won't separate. I was just giving an example. Think about it, wouldn't you feel very sad?"

Koraidon thought carefully, then drooped its head, "Ah gah si..." (Very sad...)

But then, it earnestly said, "Ah gah si!" (I will never forget D!)

"I will never forget you either." D gently patted Koraidon's head and continued, "Compare Calyrex's people to me, and Calyrex to you, doesn't this make it easier to understand?"

Koraidon's thoughts returned to the matter at hand, and after some consideration, it nodded, "Ah gah si!" (I understand! Calyrex will come home with us!)

D thought Koraidon hoped that the forgotten Calyrex would return to live with them on the ranch, "It depends on Calyrex's own will."

At this moment, Koraidon landed in the midst of Freezington Village.

D focused his gaze on the villagers strolling around and posed as an out-of-town tourist, inquiring about Calyrex.

The first villager, an older man, was full of confusion, "Calyrex? What is that?"

D: "..."

The second, an elderly woman, after some memory searching, said, "I seem to remember a story my grandma told me as a child. It was said that if children didn't eat well, the King would take away their bodies and souls, and they'd never return home."

D: "That's something a Ghost Type Pokemon would do, right?"

The third, a white-haired old man with glasses, said, "Calyrex? Of course, I know! The village relies on it to attract tourists! That way, the village inns and shops can make a good profit!"

"What? How do I know this? Because I'm the village chief! I've heard the legends of Calyrex since I was a child, and my family has storybooks recording these tales!"

D: "..."

He returned to Calyrex with these responses.

After hearing D's recount, Calyrex's face showed a clear sadness.

He didn't immediately speak but stood in the snow, overlooking His kingdom, and closed His eyes, letting out a sigh.

After a long while, Calyrex opened His eyes and regained His previously majestic demeanor.

"It seems they no longer remember My existence. Humans have long forgotten the bonds we forged..."

D didn't know how to comfort, so he said, as if soothing his own Pokemon, "The village chief remembers you."

Calyrex heard the comforting tone in his words and shook His head, indicating He was fine:

"No worries, why would I lament over this? I am the King of Bountiful Harvests! I won't be shallow enough to rely on humans!"

"I can no longer depend on human faith to restore My strength. If only My beloved horses were with Me, perhaps things might be better."

With that, Calyrex looked at D.

D thought to himself, "Here it comes!"

Calyrex continued, "My once graceful steed, a four-legged Pokemon, whose statue stands in the village with Me riding it, long ago, they were partners who roamed the mountains and fields together with Me, but since My strength waned and we parted ways, I have no idea where they are."

"I wish to ask you again, if you could help Me find My two beloved horses, I will grant you a wish, whatever it may be, as long as it's within My power, I will fulfill it for you," Calyrex earnestly promised.

D had anticipated that Calyrex would say this.

---

Just like the story in the game, Calyrex now needs the Help of others.

In the plot, once the player helps Calyrex reunite with its beloved horses, they can start to capture Calyrex.

If D helps Calyrex find Spectrier and Glastrier, would Calyrex be willing to leave its realm and travel with him to the Paldea Region?

D looked up, meeting Calyrex's gaze.

"What a large crown," D couldn't help but think again.

Then, he thought about the plot concerning Calyrex.

Reuniting with Spectrier and Glastrier is not difficult; as long as you have carrot seeds that the two horses love to eat, and let Calyrex use its power to accelerate their Growth, the two horses will be drawn in.

Additionally, it's necessary to prepare the Reins of Unity that can unite Calyrex and the two horses, allowing It to tame them.

The Reins of Unity are not just ordinary ropes; they're intricately woven using Radiant Flowers and the mane of Horse-type Pokemon.

Long ago, the people of Calyrex specially offered these items in gratitude to the King for the abundance he provided.

After a brief contemplation, D spoke: "I promise you."

Calyrex felt gratitude and emotion in its heart: "Then I will always wait here for you. Though my power has not fully recovered, if you need my Help, you may come here to find me."

D smiled and nodded: "I understand."

He remembered the village chief mentioning that there were books in his home recording legends related to Calyrex and decided to visit the Freezington Village chief.

Thus, D bid farewell to Calyrex and rode Koraidon away from there.

Snowflakes began to drift in the sky again, and Calyrex stood in the snow, watching them leave.

Its little follower is truly a kind person. Calyrex thought.

It silently gazed at the back of the little follower, a thought flashed in its mind — perhaps it could re-establish Bonds with humans like this.

This time, it didn't want to be a King anymore.

*

Freezington Village.

The old village chief, upon hearing D's intention, took him back to his home.

"The King of Bountiful Harvests, huh? It's been so long since I've heard that name. If It really existed, that would be nice," the chief said. "The land here in Freezington Village is becoming more and more barren, making it difficult for crops to Grow."

D felt a bit confused: "Logically, Calyrex led the Galar Region in ancient times, so why do we rarely hear legends about It now?"

The chief looked at this tourist and shook his head: "Maybe it's because those stories were just made-up legends?"

"Just like some myths say we humans were created by God, if the King of Bountiful Harvests truly existed, why hasn't It appeared for such a long time?"

D: "..." Possibly, those who believe see, and those who don't believe don't?

The old chief continued: "When I was young, my history teacher taught me that the Galar Region has always been ruled by royals who protected the Galar Region from the Darkest Day's crisis."

"It's said that a long, long time ago, the Darkest Day broke out in the Galar Region, and at that time, two heroes used the legendary sword and shield to successfully drive away the Darkest Day, saving the Galar Region."

"If Calyrex truly existed, It should also come out to protect Its people, right?"

At this point, the old chief pointed to one side of the bookshelf in the living room.

"Alright, the books about the King of Bountiful Harvests are placed on the bottom shelf; go take a look yourself!"

---

---

---

---

# 248: Chapter 245: Planting Radishes

Faced with the words of the old village chief, D was at a loss for how to respond.

In the end, it wasn't their fault; the main culprits for Calyrex being forgotten were the Galar Royal Family during the kingdom's era.

So he simply said nothing, and after thanking the chief, he went to the bookshelf piled with books.

The covers of these books were worn, the pages yellowed, clearly several years old.

The old village chief explained from behind, "These ancient books have been passed down from my great-grandfather's generation. When I was young, I regarded them as stories. Although some are old, by their looks, they can be passed down for a long, long time."

D nodded, reached for a book on the shelf, and saw boldly written on its cover, "Crown Snowfield Expedition Diary".

"Not this one."

D set the book aside and continued to browse the shelves, and soon found an ancient book titled "The King of Bountiful Harvests".

Opening the first page, he saw the straightforward recording of the legend of Calyrex.

"It's the Pokemon that brings bounty to the land. When it raises its right hand, it can make flowers bloom and grass flourish. When it raises its left hand, it can enrich the fields and yield abundant crops."

"It emits a blue glow from its gigantic crown and gallops across the land with its beloved Horse-type Pokemon."

"Its arrival revitalizes vegetation, bringing bounty to the land. We regard it as king, worship, and believe in it."

"Huh?" Seeing this, D looked surprised; he thought Calyrex was born as the King of Galar...

But from the contents here, it seems Calyrex was not a king at first; humans, upon witnessing its abilities, made it their king.

"In this light, The King of Bountiful Harvests must also have been a name given to him by ancient humans."

D had a rough idea in his mind and then continued to read.

"The king has two beloved horses. They are not only gentle mounts for the king but can also inspire the king's power. When united as one, a forest can be nurtured overnight."

"Originally, the two horses were of very wild nature, even stealing crops from the village, making humans suffer greatly. However, the king persuaded them and took them as subordinates."

"Some say their color is as white as the freezing ice, while others say their color is as black as the midnight ghost, but no one knows which saying is correct."

"These must be Glastrier and Spectrier." D pondered upon seeing this.

The records here are entirely correct, two horses, one white and one black, capable of invoking different powers within Calyrex.

D continued to read.

"The king tamed the two beloved horses with reins infused with his own power, and those reins were offered to the king as gratitude for the harvest bestowed by him."

"Connected to the king through the reins are not only the beloved horses but us humans, who must not forget to offer these reins to the king at the beginning of the year."

"The reins must be woven, layer by layer, from the Radiant Flower and the Horse-type Pokemon's mane, and this crafting method must be passed down through generations."

"It matches exactly with what I recall." D thought.

From the looks of it, helping Calyrex find the two horses, the Reins of Unity are indeed necessary.

Thinking of this, D turned and asked the old village chief, "By the way, chief, do you know the Reins of Unity?"

Old village chief: "Reins of Unity? Of course, I know! My ancestors once followed the book's method to craft the Reins of Unity, though that was long, long ago."

Saying this, the old village chief looked with some surprise at the tourist in front of him: "Could it be that you want to make the Reins of Unity?"

D honestly nodded: "Yes."

"Although I don't know what use it is for you to make that thing..." the old village chief said, "Nowadays, no one remembers the traditional crafting method. If we could return to the era when my great-grandfather was alive, we would surely find someone to help with the crafting."

D nodded thoughtfully.

Meanwhile, the old village chief looked at him curiously.

Earlier, he heard from Riley that he rescued a stranded tourist from the snow mountains; apparently, the person sought Calyrex.

That stranded tourist, evidently, is the young man before him.

Braving the heavy snow to come to such a desolate place, nearly freezing to death in the mountains, yet still not giving up searching for Calyrex...

The old village chief had never seen such a person.

"You're quite a strange fellow. Do you truly believe in the legend of Calyrex and think it's real?"

"Yes, I believe." D nodded and further added, "Perhaps Calyrex is watching over you somewhere right now."

The old village chief shook his head helplessly; it seemed this tourist was already obsessed.

"That's merely a legend. If you wish to seek Calyrex, then try it yourself! But, no matter what, you must pay attention to your safety!"

"Don't lose your life searching for a nonexistent Calyrex; that would be too unfortunate."

Such a promising youth losing life here would be very regrettable.

Moreover, not just that, if news of a death in Freezington Village spreads, no tourists will come, and the businesses in the village like inns and shops will be affected.

---

D: "..." He just knew it!

Whatever, D shook his head and continued to flip through the contents below.

The following content is still related to Calyrex's two mysterious horses.

"The carrots of the Crown Tundra have peculiar growth patterns. In the fields of deeply snow-covered lands, they grow into cool Iceroot Carrots, while in the fields beside graves, they grow into Shaderoot Carrots."

"The legendary King of Bountiful Harvests' horses are said to love eating these carrots. They supposedly can eat up to a hundred at a time, stopping only when their bellies are round and full."

"A hundred..."

This is so exaggerated; aren't they afraid of eating themselves into pigs? D couldn't help but snark.

But this is indeed very useful information.

Because these carrots will lure Spectrier and Glastrier to them.

D began to formulate his next plan in his mind.

First, he needed to find carrot seeds, plant them in fertile soil, and then ask Calyrex to hasten their maturity.

Then, he could just sit back and wait for the two horses to come to him.

Having the information he needed, D bid farewell to the village elder, then got up and left with Koraidon.

But getting carrot seeds was still a problem.

Walking along the snow-covered paths in the village, D thought for a moment, then turned back to Riley's house to inquire about the carrots from Riley's wife.

"Carrots?" The village woman was slightly surprised as if she didn't expect a tourist to ask about such things.

D nodded: "Yes, because I heard the carrots grown on the Crown Tundra are different from those elsewhere, so I became a bit curious."

"Oh, that's why!" The woman had a look of realization, then smiled brightly, saying: "Carrot seeds can be bought from the village shop there. I happen to have a bag of unplanted seeds at home, and I'll give them to you!"

As she spoke, the woman got up, took out a bag of seeds from the cabinet, and handed them over.

"Thank you so much!" D's eyes lit up.

The woman smiled, saying: "These seeds are just ordinary carrot seeds, which you can buy in other cities. Only here in the Crown Tundra can they grow into Iceroot carrots, probably because it's a snowy land?"

With the carrot seeds in hand, D promptly rode Koraidon back to Calyrex's side to share his discovery.

After listening, Calyrex reminisced: "Indeed, as soon as they discover certain crops, they will gallop over, and I have to go to great lengths to make them behave. So, those things were carrots!"

D nodded earnestly: "Next, as long as we plant these carrots, once they grow, they can lure the unknown whereabouts of the two Horse-type Pokemon!"

Calyrex found that His little follower had truly considered His well-being.

His heart was touched, and even His authoritative voice softened:

"With fertile soil, cultivating crops is not a difficult task for Me. As long as there is a grain of seed, I can nurture it to maturity."

"As for the snowfield of Mine, the soil suitable for planting crops..."

Calyrex closed His eyes, and His spiritual power instantly swept across the entire tundra.

A moment later, He reopened His eyes and continued: "One place is the cold and clean snow-covered land's field, another is the field beside the tombstones comforting the deceased... I shall go with you."

"Of course!" D was about to take the seeds and move on, but he thought for a moment and said to Calyrex: "However, I have one more thing to do; please wait for me here for a while."

Calyrex showed a slight trace of confusion in His eyes.

Then, He saw His little follower ride the red Pokemon back to the village.

About ten minutes later, the little follower returned with a hoe on his shoulder.

This was a hoe borrowed from Riley's house. With the hoe on his shoulder, D excitedly looked at Calyrex in front of him and said, "Let's go!"

If he guessed correctly, the two places Calyrex mentioned are Snowy Valley and Ancient Tomb.

In the game, players would plant carrots there to lure Spectrier and Glastrier.

But he was new here, unfamiliar with the place, so he needed Calyrex to help guide him.

Calyrex naturally had no objections.

Thanks to the assistance from His little follower, His power had recovered somewhat, no longer as weak as before.

Therefore, Calyrex looked elegantly at His little follower in front of Him, and lightly nodded.

His body then emitted a blue halo and levitated into the air.

The next moment, D saw the surrounding scenery flash, and then he and Koraidon were taken by Calyrex via Teleport to an unfamiliar place.

This was a quiet valley surrounded by snowy mountains.

In this picturesque valley, some fields had been cultivated by someone.

Calyrex stood before a field, gazing at the soil below, and then slowly spoke:

"This field can grow Iceroot carrots... Although these are My old memories and now very blurred, I recall this was the favorite vegetable of the Ice-type beloved horse."

D stepped forward with a hoe, confidently patting his chest: "Leave it to me from here!"

---

As a farmer, no one is better at farming than him!

Seeing the eager little follower, Calyrex smiled, "Then I'll trouble you to do the sowing."

The soil in the icy snow was somewhat hard and tough, not easy to till.

But fortunately, D had ample experience to help him manage this situation; it wasn't too hard for him, just needed a bit of time.

Koraidon and Calyrex stood aside watching him work.

As D tilled the land, he asked, "Do you remember things from before?"

"Things from before?" Calyrex wasn't sure what he meant; he carefully recalled and then shook his head, "I have been sleeping here for a long time due to losing my power, and memories from long ago are all fuzzy now."

"Losing power?" D felt a sinking feeling in his heart, "Is it because people from Galar forgot about your existence?"

"I... I don't know." Calyrex tried to recall but simply couldn't remember anything: "I only remember something very bad happened, I used up a lot of energy, and then I've been dormant till now..."

"Hmm?" Hearing this unfamiliar information, D froze in place.

He carefully recalled the story, finally realizing something wasn't right.

Because in both the DLC storyline of The Crown Tundra and the Calyrex he saw now, they appeared to be in a near-amnesiac state.

Moreover, in the game, Calyrex only remembered having a Horse-type Pokemon but couldn't remember having two horses.

Thinking of this, D tentatively asked, "Then do you remember how many horses you have?"

"Two." Calyrex looked at his little follower in confusion, not understanding why he asked such a thing.

D was even more perplexed; this isn't right?

Seeing his expression, Calyrex carefully recalled and then said, "It's because of the belief you brought to me that I remembered; at first, I only remembered having a Horse-type Pokemon, and the rest was unknown."

"I see..." D thoughtfully nodded.

It seems his activities in the Paldea Region have indeed helped Calyrex quite a bit.

However, one concerning point was the "very bad thing" Calyrex mentioned, which led to consuming a large amount of energy and falling into sleep.

The bad thing, was it referring to the awakening of Eternatus?

The land was almost plowed, D placed radish seeds in the holes, covering them with soil, and asked:

"Do you know about Eternatus?"

"Eternatus..." Calyrex repeated this name, a hint of confusion in his eyes.

D asked again, "Then what about Zacian and Zamazenta?"

Calyrex still didn't remember.

D thoughtfully nodded, "I see..."

He carefully recalled the storyline and background of Sword and Shield.

If envisioning Zacian and Zamazenta as heroes of a fantasy tale, and Eternatus as the demon king.

Then would Calyrex play the role of a king in it?

A king coronates the heroes, then the heroes go to fight the demon king...

A very traditional hero versus demon dragon story.

If that's truly the case, then the reason Calyrex didn't appear during the Darkest Day makes sense.

He likely foresaw the awakening of Eternatus beforehand and used his power to massively relocate the Pokemon in the forest!

D remembered, the Pokedex entry for Calyrex in Shield described:

"It is said that it moved vast forests and the creatures dwelling within to other places in one night, saving the forest's creatures from the crisis of a meteorite's fall with the ability to see all events past and future."

Falling meteorite, wishing star, Eternatus from outer space...

Thinking of this, D's expression became even more complex, he looked up at Calyrex before him.

After Calyrex's slumber, sword and shield heroes Zacian and Zamazenta also retreated after fending off the Darkest Day.

The Galar Royal Family took the opportunity to claim their glory...

Sensing his little follower's gaze, Calyrex's eyes were filled with puzzlement and confusion.

"Calyrex, does your previous promise still hold?" D asked.

Calyrex solemnly nodded, "Of course, I would not break my vow!"

D smiled slightly, saying to Him, "Alright, I've decided what I want."

Calyrex looked at him, "Speak."

D seriously said, "After I help you find your horses, would you come with them to live with me in the Paldea Region, at my ranch?"

---

---

---

---

# 249: Chapter 246: Paldea Is My Home, but I Will Never Forget Galar (4k)

Upon hearing these words, Calyrex was slightly taken aback.

"I…"

For a king, there is only one situation where one leaves one's homeland, and that is when the kingdom is destroyed, and one becomes a captive.

As for Calyrex, this Galar Region is His domain.

Here lie His memories and His people.

Calyrex hesitated.

D quietly waited by His side for a response.

Calyrex gazed at the young devotee before Him, remembering how He had been forgotten by the world, thereby losing power and falling into slumber.

Then, in His mind, He envisioned His young devotee striving to spread His name in that distant land.

Calyrex's eyes were filled with confusion, uncertain of how to make a decision.

After a long moment of contemplation, He finally responded, "I need to think carefully."

The fact that Calyrex did not outright refuse meant there was hope!

D felt relieved, smiled, and said:

"Alright, the people and Pokemon of the Paldea Region have all told me that it would be great if they could meet you; they're all very grateful to you."

Calyrex was moved, "I understand their sentiments. D, please give Me some time."

"Okay." With his goal achieved, D changed the topic: "The radish seeds have all been planted; next, we rely on you."

Upon hearing this, Calyrex lightly nodded before drifting over to the farmland located in the Snowy Valley.

"Leave it to Me! See, I shall show you My great power!"

As His words settled, a deep blue radiance began to shine from Calyrex.

Soon, the freshly tilled farmland seemed to resonate with Him, bursting forth with a blue, wave-like energy ripple.

Specks of light emerged from the ground, drifting towards the sky.

The radish seeds buried in the earth sprouted at a speed visible to the naked eye.

In the blink of an eye, the field was brimming with white radishes.

D looked down, and details about these white radishes surfaced in his mind.

[White Radish: Rare ingredient, the favored crop of The King of Bountiful Harvests' beloved horse, grown in snow-covered fields.]

Beside him, Calyrex gazed delightedly at the white radishes, His eyes brimming with gratitude:

"Thanks to you! Without the faith you provided, My previous power might have only been able to grow a single radish!"

"Now, with so many white radishes, My beloved horse is sure to be attracted!"

"Alright, child, let us wait here for a while!" Calyrex turned and said.

D had no objections, and together with Calyrex, he looked into the distance, awaiting the appearance of Glastrier.

During this time, D chatted with Calyrex about the Reins of Unity.

Upon hearing this, Calyrex contemplated and said, "The Reins of Unity that can strengthen My power… With it, I could easily command My horse."

"However, over the years, humans have forgotten their faith in Me and no longer offer the Reins of Unity."

D recalled something, then turned his gaze to the circle of green petals around his neck.

Calyrex noticed his gaze, slightly confused: "Why do you look at Me like that?"

D shook his head and replied, "From the human books I read, to create the Reins of Unity, the petals of the Radiant Flower and manes from two horses are required."

Upon hearing this, Calyrex paused and then fell into contemplation and remembrance: "What? Flower… Flower… I see… It appears so!"

Soon, Calyrex seemed to realize something and said with enlightenment: "I understand now, I thought humans no longer offered the Reins of Unity because they forgot their faith in Me."

"As it turns out, it's because I didn't provide the necessary materials! It seems I've misunderstood humans for a long time!"

"However, to make the Radiant Flower bloom, vast energy must be consumed, and as My power weakened, it caused Me to be unable to make it bloom for centuries."

"I've just recalled some things, thank you very much, D. Next, I shall attempt to make the Radiant Flower bloom once more in this long-separated era!"

After speaking, Calyrex's expression suddenly became majestic.

The enormous Crown atop His head erupted with a dazzling blue radiance.

That light poured into the bud on His neck, and the next moment, a beautiful flower as deep and blue as the ocean blossomed forth.

The Radiant Flower slowly floated down from Calyrex's body and settled in front of D.

D reached out, and the Radiant Flower, emitting a dreamy glow, gently landed in his hand, resting quietly.

Calyrex: "I must ask you to create the Reins of Unity for Me! The descendants of the reins-making tribe should still be in the village."

D nodded; since he was here, he would certainly help to the end.

For his attitude, Calyrex felt a bit apologetic: "I am deeply sorry, unconsciously I have already asked so many things of you!"

D grinned: "It's nothing."

Koraidon beside them also nodded his big head joyfully: "Argh-ki—" (No problem—)

Calyrex wanted to say something more.

Just then, a sudden neighing sound came from afar.

D quickly turned his head and saw a Pokemon completely white in body, with an ice armor covering its face, and four strong and sturdy legs, galloping from the distance upon a chill air.

---

It's here!

Calyrex's expression was excited: "That fellow is my beloved steed... Glastrier?! That dazzling white coat! That wild galloping figure! It's exactly as it was when I first saw it. Ah~ So familiar, so nostalgic!"

Glastrier approached from afar, its gaze firmly locked on the icy carrots scattered on the ground, and let out an excited whinny from its mouth.

"Ice... neigh!" (Something delicious!)

Glastrier was extremely excited, with so many icy carrots, it could finally have a feast!

However, at that moment, Glastrier, galloping with all its might, suddenly felt something strange.

It raised its head and saw three figures lying in wait in a secluded corner.

When it recognized one of them, fear immediately appeared on its face.

It quickly stopped, its strong and sturdy forelegs lifted high from the snow, letting out a frightened whinny, as it abandoned the icy carrots in the field and attempted to flee.

Seeing this, Calyrex frowned, "This is not good, Glastrier was defeated by me once before, and it will not willingly approach anything threatening!"

Upon hearing this, D nodded and then shouted: "Koraidon!"

"Ah ga!" (I'm here!)

Koraidon understood and immediately leapt up from the snow.

It jumped high into the air, eyes locked on Glastrier below, while changing its form and charging towards it with all its might.

"Boom!"

With a loud roar, Koraidon collided fiercely with Glastrier, and a terrifying explosion of energy occurred.

The high temperatures caused by the energy melted the nearby snow, and a large amount of white steam rose into the air.

Calyrex looked at this scene in shock: "Such... such a powerful Pokemon! I've never seen anything like it before! Is it the ruler of that land?"

D: "...No, the Paldea region doesn't have a king anymore; it's now ruled by the Pokemon League. It's complicated with Koraidon, I'll explain it to you later."

Because right now, they had more important matters to attend to.

Hit by the heavy blow, Glastrier let out a painful whinny, stood up, and looked angrily at Koraidon opposite.

Its forelegs kept pawing at the snow, and two streams of white cold air spewed from its nostrils.

Koraidon tilted its head at the sight of Glastrier.

"Ah ga?" (What do you want?)

D and Calyrex had already rushed forward, "Watch out for its Ice Type moves!"

As his voice fell, a terrifying cold air suddenly released from Glastrier's hooves.

The snow under its feet instantly froze into ice, and Glastrier stomped on the ice, charging fiercely towards Koraidon.

The ice spread quickly over the snow, reaching Koraidon's feet in the blink of an eye.

Faced with such a brutal and aggressive Glastrier, Koraidon showed no fear, instead becoming more excited.

"Ah ga!" (Bring it on!)

It roared, and a burst of fiery flames shot out from its robust, crimson body, charging straight at Glastrier.

Ice and fire collided in the air, producing a large amount of steam.

The ice created by Glastrier melted instantly from the high temperatures, revealing the hard earth below.

In the thick fog, the two Pokemon collided once more.

A powerful energy storm swept outwards the moment they met.

D quickly shielded his eyes with his arm.

When the dust settled, he saw Glastrier already lying on the ground.

While Koraidon stood arrogantly in front of it, looking down at the opponent.

Calyrex looked at this scene in disbelief.

Its... its beloved steed was defeated just like that by that Pokemon?

However, Glastrier seemed not to have lost its ability to fight.

It awkwardly stood up from the snow, realizing that the enemy in front of it was not something it could defeat, so it turned and tried to run.

But Koraidon was quick to act, leaping up and landing accurately onto Glastrier's back.

The heavy weight instantly made Glastrier unable to stand up straight, its running speed greatly reduced, letting out a series of woeful whinnies.

"Ah ga neigh!" (Behave!)

Koraidon smacked its head with a paw.

Glastrier felt aggrieved, but now being ridden could only reluctantly follow Koraidon's wishes, carrying it laboriously to Calyrex and D.

Calyrex looked dazed, too shocked to utter a single word.

This, this, this!

Koraidon jumped off Glastrier, accidentally hitting it with the large tail during the process.

Glastrier snorted in displeasure but quickly lowered its once proud head when it saw Koraidon.

Hold it in, don't laugh!

D tried hard not to laugh as he went forward, skillfully took out a cup of Berry Milk from his backpack to give Koraidon.

Koraidon happily accepted, gulping it down eagerly.

"Ah ga neigh~" (Delicious~)

At this time, Calyrex finally came to his senses.

"I... I am truly shocked! Glastrier is a very proud and arrogant Pokemon, and it would never submit unless faced with overwhelming power!"

---

"It took me quite a lot of effort back then to tame them!"

D looked at Glastrier, whose face was practically screaming "I want to run but I don't dare," and turned to ask, "Can you restore your power like this now?"

Calyrex shook its head: "Not yet. Only by using the Reins of Unity to directly transmit my power to it will it once again swear loyalty to me."

This loyalty probably refers to the resonance of power between Calyrex and Glastrier, merging into the form of Calyrex riding a white horse, D thought.

The function of the Reins of Unity should be similar to Kyurem's DNA Splicers.

After a brief contemplation, D said, "Then let's go back to the village first and use its mane and the Radiant Flower to make the reins."

It was already evening now, and night was about to fall.

Calyrex nodded gently: "I will leave my beloved horse in your care for the moment. In the meantime, I will go investigate the whereabouts of my other favorite horse. This is all in your hands!"

After speaking, Calyrex glanced at Glastrier, who was bowing respectfully to Koraidon in front of it. After healing its injuries, Calyrex used Teleport to go investigate the location of Spectrier.

D stood in place for quite a while, then smiled helplessly: "It's really…"

Oh well!

He turned to Glastrier, about to say something, when he noticed it looking longingly at the Iceroot Carrots in the nearby field, as if it were about to drool.

D: "…"

A foodie Glastrier, huh?

Suppressing a laugh, he said to the horse, "Go ahead and eat; those were planted for you by Calyrex."

Hearing this, Glastrier's eyes lit up, but then it hesitantly looked at Koraidon again.

"Aghh…" (Such a scaredy-cat…)

Koraidon twitched its mouth and waved a claw at Glastrier, who then trotted happily to the field and began munching on the Iceroot Carrots.

Even after eating all the Iceroot Carrots in the field, it still had a look of unsatisfied hunger on its face.

Since there was other business to attend to, D pretended not to see.

He returned to Freezington Village with Koraidon and Glastrier, and as soon as they entered, they immediately drew the attention of the villagers.

An elderly woman was full of surprise: "Where did this Horse-type Pokemon come from?"

A young man, eyes sparkling, said, "This snowy white fur, this strong physique, it's an unprecedentedly fine horse!"

Riley, who was wandering around in the village, also noticed the commotion. He walked over with great surprise and asked, "Is this a Pokemon you just captured on the snowfield?"

D initially wanted to say no but then remembered that Calyrex seemed unwilling to reveal its identity, so he changed his answer mid-sentence: "That's right, I just happened to encounter it while wandering on the snowfield."

"That's amazing!" Riley praised.

D gave a polite, slight smile, then bid farewell to the villagers and headed to the village elder's house.

He left Glastrier outside in the snow, asking Koraidon to keep an eye on it.

He then found the old village elder and inquired about the Reins of Unity.

Hearing D's request, the village elder looked quite shocked: "What?! You want me to help you make the Reins of Unity?"

D nodded: "Yes."

The village elder seemed a bit troubled: "I've already told you before, no one remembers the traditional method of making it anymore."

"My ancestors did model after the legendary method, but that is a long-forgotten, ancient tradition…"

The village elder murmured thoughtfully, and then, as if struck by a realization, a look of shock appeared on his face.

"If this is true, then… then the legend of Calyrex is true too? No, no, how could that be?!"

---

---

---

---

# 250: Chapter 247: The Adaptable Glastrier

The old village chief instinctively dismissed the speculation in his mind.

After all, it was just a myth, and myths aren't real!

He looked at the tourist in front of him, who had come to Freezington Village for Calyrex.

Regardless of whether The King of Bountiful Harvests truly exists, it's the tourists' visits that are bringing prosperity and wealth to Freezington Village.

Thus, a smile appeared on the old village chief's face: "Since you've come all this way for this, I'll try making it for you!"

D felt a surge of joy and handed him the Radiant Flower and Glastrier's mane: "Thank you so much!"

Looking at these materials, the old village chief hesitated for a moment, then said:

"I don't know if I can craft a reins as impressive as my great-grandfather's, but it's time for the village's tradition to be revived in the world!"

This way, Freezington Village will further increase its appeal! It will attract even more tourists!

Thinking about this, the old village chief was suddenly filled with determination.

He quickly dashed into the inner room, fetched the needle and thread for sewing reins, sat upright on the sofa, focused his mind, and began sewing at a breathtaking speed.

D watched this scene in amazement.

This is way too exaggerated, isn't it?

It was unclear how much time had passed, but with a loud shout from the old village chief, a very rough reins had taken shape.

D glanced at the reins, feeling somewhat odd about it, and tentatively asked, "Is it finished?"

The old village chief shook his head seriously: "No, my eyesight isn't as sharp anymore, I couldn't sew the finer details properly. To finish this reins, someone with more refined skills is needed."

"As expected..."

D remembered that in the game, the final step was completed by Uncle Peony.

D went up to check the unfinished reins, hesitated for a moment, then said, "Do you want me to give it a try?"

The old village chief was very surprised: "You... you can sew too?"

"A little," D replied, explaining that while doing farm work, he would often tear his clothes and ended up sewing them himself.

Upon hearing this, the old village chief immediately stepped aside and handed over the reins to him.

D examined the reins, quickly thinking of how to sew it in his mind: "First this way, then like this, and finally that way, it should be complete."

He started sewing.

The old village chief, recalling the legends his grandfather told him as a child, murmured, "Seems like, while sewing, you have to sing with gratitude... only then can the reins be fully completed."

D was stunned: "What singing?"

"I remember the lyrics were..." the old village chief began to hum: "Flowers rolling round and round～♬ gathering the mane to honor our king～♬, seems like that's the tune."

D: "...Can't we do it without singing?"

"No, you can't." The old village chief shook his head, his eyes full of encouragement: "Come on, sing it!"

D felt the old man was being deliberate, but he thought, in the game, Uncle Peony indeed sang while sewing the reins...

Darn it, this awkwardness is as bad as doing the Z-Move dance!

But having reached this point, he couldn't just give up halfway.

After all, it wasn't like there were lots of people watching.

D gritted his teeth and awkwardly began singing.

"Flowers... rolling round and round～♬"

"Gathering the mane... to honor our king～♬"

"Yes, exactly!" the old village chief nodded repeatedly: "That's it, keep going!"

D looked numb, but this time it went much smoother.

"Flowers rolling round and round～♬ gathering the mane to honor our king～♬"

With the last stitch, a snow-white reins, glittering under the lamp and faintly exuding a chill, was finally sewn.

Upon seeing this phenomenon, the old village chief was greatly astonished: "It's finished! This is the Reins of Unity, it's glowing, no! Could it be the stories my great-grandfather told me were true?!"

The old village chief was bewildered.

He looked at the tourist holding the Reins of Unity and tentatively asked, "Are you... seeking Calyrex?"

D looked at him and nodded, "Yes."

The old village chief's heart skipped a beat: "Did you find it?"

D, unsure of Calyrex's intentions, thought for a moment, still shook his head: "Not yet."

The old village chief felt slightly disappointed; he hesitated for a moment, then said, "If... I mean if you really find The King of Bountiful Harvests, could you tell me? I'd like to know if those myths are real."

D nodded: "If I get to meet it."

He thought to himself: if Calyrex is willing.

*

After leaving the old village chief's house, D noticed there was an inn in the village, so he decided not to bother Riley's family and turned to the inn with Koraidon and Glastrier to stay there.

Glastrier was tied to a fence in the courtyard of the inn.

Perhaps due to Koraidon's dominance, the horse seemed docile the entire time they were back in the village.

The inn provided meals for staying tourists.

The hostess warmly brought steaming dishes to the table.

The warm glow of the lights inside made the place feel cozy.

---

D released Cyclizar from the Poke Ball and sat with it and Koraidon on the wooden tatami, enjoying their meal while watching the beautiful snow scene falling quietly outside the eaves.

"Calyrex hasn't returned yet..." D murmured to himself, deep in thought, "It seems it still hasn't found any trace of Spectrier."

According to the game's plot, both Glastrier and Spectrier actually reside in the Crown Shrine.

However, for various reasons, Calyrex has always been avoiding, so it doesn't know the exact location of the two horses.

However, D wasn't worried.

After all, it's the great Calyrex; it will inevitably find Spectrier's location.

All he needed to do was to wait patiently here.

After dinner, the snow outside had lessened considerably.

Looking at the vast expanse of white snow in the courtyard, the innkeeper's wife smiled and suggested, "In this kind of weather, it's not too cold. You can take the Pokemon out to make snowmen and have a snowball fight!"

D initially thought it was too childish, a game for kids, and he was already in his twenties, a full-grown adult.

But the moment he turned his head, he saw the eager expressions on Koraidon and Cyclizar.

D: "..."

Alright then!

He stood up, ready to take the two Pokemon out to the snow to play for a while.

"Ah gah hiss!"

Koraidon let out an excited shout and dashed outside.

Cyclizar tentatively stepped one paw onto the snow, then immediately drew back in fear, looking pitiful.

"Gah oww..."

The effect of the Translation Nuts Cake had already been used up by Calyrex, Koraidon, and Glastrier, so D couldn't understand what Cyclizar was saying.

But judging from its tone and expression, it seemed to fear the cold.

With its hairless body and bare skin, Cyclizar indeed dreaded the cold.

D thought for a moment, first comforting Cyclizar, asking Koraidon to wait for a little while, then approached the innkeeper's wife to borrow her kitchen.

The innkeeper's wife gladly agreed.

So, D made several bowls of soup with cold resistance effects in the kitchen and distributed them to the Pokemon.

Glastrier also got a portion, but it seemed uninterested in the food, snorted, and turned its head away.

D didn't mind.

After finishing a bowl of soup, Cyclizar's body warmed up, and finally, it could go out and play in the snow.

Cyclizar dashed happily into the snow, leaving a series of clear paw prints on the ground.

Meanwhile, Koraidon had already started rolling snowballs on the ground with its large paws, building two rough snowmen. It was anyone's guess where it learned that from.

Seeing D walk out, Koraidon immediately turned back and shouted, "Ah gah!"

"What's up?" D stepped forward.

Koraidon pointed at the smaller snowman, then at itself, "Ah gah hiss!"

Next, it pointed to the bigger snowman and then towards D.

D understood. He raised an eyebrow slightly and asked, "This one is you, and this one is me?"

"Ah gah hiss!" Koraidon nodded its big head happily; they would always live together!

Cyclizar was drawn over by this scene, looking at the two snowmen with a naive expression.

After a moment, it started rolling snowballs with its paws, also wanting to build a snowman.

However, Cyclizar had never built a snowman before. After trying for a long time, it couldn't roll out a decent snowball.

"Gah oww..." Cyclizar looked disheartened.

D, seeing this, walked up helplessly and said with a smile, "That's not how you do it. Watch closely—"

He then molded a small snowball in his hands and placed it on the ground, continuously rolling it around, and gradually, the snowball grew bigger and bigger.

Cyclizar watched this scene with admiration.

When it seemed big enough, D stopped and asked, "Would you like to give it a try?"

Cyclizar began to try it out.

It formed a round snowball with its paws and then placed it on the ground, rolling it constantly.

Soon, a large snowball came together on the snow.

D came over, rolling the two snowballs to where Koraidon had built the two snowmen, then put the smaller snowball on top of the larger one.

Thus, the third snowman was completed.

D pointed at the snowman and said to Cyclizar, "This is you."

"Gah oww～"

Cyclizar was delighted; now it could also stay by D's side like Koraidon!

Koraidon, standing nearby, watched this scene with a moved expression.

For once, it did not feel jealous.

Because this scene was something it had experienced a long, long time ago.

Back then, D had taught it to build a snowman just like now...

"Alright, you all continue playing!"

D stood up from the snow and walked over to Glastrier with the reins, but Koraidon unexpectedly followed him.

Looking at Glastrier before him, D thought inwardly.

"I remember that Calyrex transferred its power into Glastrier's body through the Reins of Unity, then merged with it."

So the question arises, if other Pokemon did the same, could they merge with Glastrier just like Calyrex?

Thinking of this, D, intrigued, placed the reins around Glastrier's neck.

Noticing the human's malicious gaze, Glastrier's heart skipped a beat.

Before it could react, the next second, it heard the human say, "Koraidon, try riding on it!"

---

"Ah gah?"

Koraidon, though slightly confused, still attempted to jump onto Glastrier's back.

Glastrier's back almost buckled under the pressure, and it used all its strength to support its body so it wouldn't fall.

Glastrier originally wanted to kick with its hooves, but constrained by the ferocity of the Pokémon on its back, it wasn't a match and could only endure with difficulty, letting out a strained whinny.

"Good, just bear with it for a while," D, feeling a bit sorry, patted Glastrier's head. "I'm just trying something."

Glastrier, sullenly, let out two snorts with a constipated expression.

D, worried about Glastrier being crushed, quickly said to Koraidon, "Hold the reins and try to inject your power into its body!"

"Ah gah ciss?!"

Koraidon widened its eyes, looking disbelief.

Its power... What kind of power does it have?

Hesitant, Koraidon tried to release its Dragon Type Energy from within.

However, nothing happened.

"Doesn't work?" D furrowed his brows slightly. "Seems like other Pokémon cannot fuse with Glastrier through energy transfer..."

Seeing Glastrier about to collapse, D hurriedly asked Koraidon to get down.

Glastrier was panting heavily with exhaustion.

D looked apologetically at the horse. "Sorry, once Calyrex returns tomorrow, I'll ask Him to plant your favorite carrots for you."

The aggressive and arrogant Glastrier was almost fainting with anger due to this human letting the Pokémon ride it.

But hearing this, its attention was involuntarily drawn to carrots.

D keenly noticed the change in Glastrier's expression, then extended five fingers. "I'll plant five hundred carrots! Enough for you to eat your fill!"

"Ice... ne-eigh!" Glastrier raised its neck and let out a whinny.

Hmph, that's more like it!

After all, it can't beat this Pokémon called Koraidon next to it, so why not surrender to this human offering carrots, just like it did with Calyrex years ago.

D: "..."

This Glastrier sure knows when to yield and when to stand firm.

When facing opponents far stronger than itself, it will surrender to them, willingly becoming their steed.

And if the opponent's strength ever weakens, it might just abandon its owner and escape, like how it once left Calyrex.

This really left D unsure of what to say for a moment.

Whatever!

It's getting late, and after running around busy all day, D felt a bit tired, so he returned early to the room at the inn to rest.

Early the next morning, daylight broke.

Just after breakfast, D saw Calyrex's figure appearing in a corner where no one noticed.

Calyrex glanced at him a few times, then turned around and floated away.

D understood Calyrex's intent — He wanted him to go outside to talk!

"Let's go!"

D returned Cyclizar to his Poke Ball, then grabbed the Reins of Unity, taking Koraidon and Glastrier to the cliff where the entire village could be seen.

Calyrex was already waiting there, and D took out the prepared Translation Nuts Cake and ate it.

Then, he heard Calyrex say seriously, "I have finally figured out the location of my beloved steed!"

"Turns out Spectrier has made the Crown Shrine its base!"

"That was the place where I once spent time with it; due to the fear of recalling those beautiful memories, I have always deliberately avoided it, only to find Spectrier has taken advantage of my weakened power and claimed it as its own..."

D listened silently, and seeing Calyrex frown, he spoke up, "Don't be angry, don't be angry. I made the Reins of Unity last night. Do you want to try it now?"

"I am not angry..."

Seeing the reins emitting a gentle glow, Calyrex gratefully said:

"Indeed, this is the Reins of Unity! I must once again express my gratitude to you! You have done enough for me; from now on, leave everything to me!"

As the words fell, Calyrex used Telekinesis to take the reins.

Once the reins were in Calyrex's hands, it flickered and appeared above Glastrier.

Calyrex swung the reins, wrapping them around Glastrier's neck.

In the next moment, the reins automatically merged with Glastrier, and a dazzling light radiated from both Calyrex and Glastrier.

---

---

---
